DiscoveryRSI: Recursively Learning to Discover Valuable Hypotheses
DiscoveryRSI：递归学习以发现有价值假设
Scientific discovery requires developing worthwhile hypotheses into practical investigations, yet learning from this evolving research process remains challenging. We introduce an operational definition of valuable and testable research hypotheses: evidence-grounded claims about consequential uncertainties, coupled with feasible investigations, explicit decision criteria, and alternative explanations. Our DiscoveryRSI framework develops hypothesis trees through multi-agent evolutionary search, compiles selected investigations into executable Harbor tasks, and recursively learns through three mechanisms: hypothesis credit optimization attributes scientific assessments to documented research contributions; harness replay identifies productive strategies and recurring failures to refine research workflows; and rediscovery applies accepted improvements to new investigations, generating further experience for subsequent learning. We also introduce DFM-Bench, which provides comprehensive assessment of hypothesis quality and cumulative research progress at the levels of hypotheses, revisions, development paths and research runs. Across four life-science domains, automated evaluation shows that hypothesis quality rises from 4.86 for the base model to 5.39 after two rounds of self-improvement, with the large gains in falsifiability and data resolvability. We further validate hypothesis quality through expert review, comparing our DiscoveryRSI-27B with other research agents. Preliminary human assessments show that DiscoveryRSI-27B scores higher than GPT-6 Luna on hypothesis importance and whether claims are plausible and evidence-bounded. DiscoveryRSI-27B achieved a 6.3% rate of hypothesis correspondence with research published after the model cutoff, largely outperform GPT-6 with 2.5% and Claude Opus 5 with 2.3%, while delivering greater human-rated improvement along research paths.
中文翻译
科学发现需要将有价值的假设发展为可行的研究，但学习这一动态研究过程仍具挑战性。我们提出了一个关于有价值且可检验研究假设的操作定义：基于证据的、关于重要不确定性的主张，搭配可行的研究方案、明确的决策标准以及替代解释。我们的DiscoveryRSI框架通过多智能体进化搜索构建假设树，将选定的研究整合为可执行的Harbor任务，并通过三种机制实现递归学习：假设信用优化将科学评估归因于已记录的研究贡献；回放机制识别有效策略与重复失败，以优化研究流程；再发现机制将已接受的改进应用于新研究，生成更多经验用于后续学习。我们还引入DFM-Bench，从假设、修订、发展路径和研究运行等多个层面全面评估假设质量和累积研究进展。在四个生命科学领域中，自动化评估显示，经过两轮自我改进后，假设质量从基础模型的4.86提升至5.39，显著提高了可证伪性和数据可解决性。通过专家评审进一步验证，DiscoveryRSI-27B在假设重要性、主张合理性及证据约束性方面优于GPT-6 Luna。初步人工评估显示，DiscoveryRSI-27B与模型截止后发表的研究论文的假设对应率达到6.3%，远超GPT-6的2.5%和Claude Opus 5的2.3%，同时在研究路径上的改进也获得更高的人类评分。
👤 Jianyuan Zhong, Chenwei Wu, Zhaochen Yu, Xixian Jiang, Xuewei Li et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Recognizing Study Lineage Is Not Enough: Over-Crediting Same-Trial Evidence in LLM-Based Medical Verification
识别研究谱系尚不足够：LLM医学验证中对同次试验证据的过度引用
Publication count is not study count. A model can identify shared trial lineage correctly in a direct task yet over-credit a second publication from the same trial when aggregating evidence. We evaluate this behavior in medical LLM verification using 14 source-audited clinical families and five model conditions. The families span provenance, clinical applicability, result direction, and composition; the provenance families provide the direct lineage tests. Recognition and aggregation are separate stateless calls, and repeated calls assess within-input stability. Across all three original provenance families, GPT-5.5 identifies the independent update in 10/10 recognition calls per family but over-credits the same-trial report in 5/5 separate aggregation calls per family. Selected Qwen3-4B and Ministral-3-8B conditions show the same cross-task contrast. A study-grouping instruction reduces GPT-5.5 over-credit from 4/5 to 0/5 in each later pure-provenance family. Other model conditions show mixed behavior, and rejected outputs limit several comparisons. The results identify a behavioral gap between elicited lineage recognition and evidence aggregation. The code and data artifacts are available at this https URL .
中文翻译
发表数量不等于研究数量。一个模型可以在直接任务中正确识别共享试验谱系，但在整合证据时却会过度引用同一试验的第二篇发表文章。我们使用14个经源审核的临床家族和五种模型条件评估了这一行为在医学LLM验证中的表现。这些家族涵盖来源、临床适用性、结果方向和组成等方面；其中来源家族用于直接谱系测试。识别与整合是两个独立的状态无关调用，重复调用用于评估输入内稳定性。在所有三个原始来源家族中，GPT-5.5在每家族10次识别调用中均正确识别出独立更新，但在每家族5次独立整合调用中均过度引用同一试验报告。选定的Qwen3-4B和Ministral-3-8B模型条件也表现出相同的跨任务差异。加入研究分组指令后，GPT-5.5在后续纯来源家族中的过度引用从4/5降至0/5。其他模型条件表现各异，且拒绝输出限制了部分比较。结果揭示了诱发的谱系识别与证据整合之间存在行为差距。代码与数据可在指定链接获取。
👤 Mukand Krishna, Anoushka Harit, Chenghao Xiao, Zhongtian Sun 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

SIT-Eval: Situation-Based Interactive Turn-by-Turn Evaluation of Conversational Agents
SIT-Eval：基于情境的对话智能体逐轮交互评估
The rapid proliferation of conversational AI agents necessitates evaluation methods that assess performance across diverse dialogue scenarios, utilizing multiple situational criteria, as appropriate behavior depends on context and encompasses various aspects. Existing dialogue benchmarks typically use static natural conversations, which underrepresent rare situations---a critical safety gap, as low-probability events become frequent failures at production scale. However, limited sample sizes force a choice: either maintain natural dialogues for realism or turn to adaptive situation sampling for comprehensive yet statistically reliable coverage. We introduce SIT-Eval, an adjustable framework for evaluating situation-based conversational agents. We formalize an agent's desired behavior as situational rules: if situation is P, follow rules Q. At each conversation turn, an LLM-interlocutor enacts a uniformly sampled situation. A context-aware LLM-judge leverages all conditionally applicable rules as criteria to evaluate the agent's reply for that situation. The judge is validated using human-verified synthetic golden dialogues. Benchmarking 8 LLMs on 1292 conversation turns reveals failure modes hidden by aggregate metrics: models with similar overall scores vary widely in their adherence to individual rules. All models struggle most with ambiguity clarification and Socratic questioning.
中文翻译
对话式AI智能体的快速普及迫切需要能够评估其在多样对话场景中表现的方法，结合多种情境标准，因为适当行为依赖于具体语境并涵盖多个方面。现有对话基准通常采用静态自然对话，未能充分代表罕见情境——这在生产规模下构成关键安全缺口，因为低概率事件在大规模部署中频繁发生。然而，样本量有限迫使选择：要么维持自然对话以保证真实性，要么转向自适应情境采样以实现全面且统计可靠的覆盖。我们提出SIT-Eval，一个可调节的情境化对话智能体评估框架。我们将智能体期望行为形式化为情境规则：若情境为P，则遵循规则Q。在每一轮对话中，LLM对话者随机采样一种情境。一个情境感知的LLM裁判利用所有条件适用规则作为评判标准，评估智能体在该情境下的回应。裁判通过人工验证的合成黄金对话进行验证。在1292轮对话中对8个LLM进行基准测试，揭示了聚合指标掩盖的故障模式：整体得分相似的模型在遵守个别规则方面差异巨大。所有模型在模糊澄清和苏格拉底式提问方面表现最差。
👤 Ravil Mussabayev, Saveliy Zavertyaev, Dmitriy Parpulov, Andrey Savchenko 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

EduWild: How Teacher-Configured Chatbots Fail in Real-World Use and How to Fix Them
EduWild：教师配置聊天机器人在真实场景中的失败原因及改进方法
Teachers increasingly configure educational chatbots through natural-language instructions, but how these bots fail in real-world use remains poorly understood. We introduce EduWild, a dataset of 1,726 conversations with 217 teacher-configured chatbots, each paired with the instructions and configuration behind it, including 900 conversations from the intended end users of 35 deployed chatbots. We develop a 29-code taxonomy covering factual, workflow, diagnostic, and pedagogical failures and apply it using a human-calibrated LLM judge. The most frequent failures are lapses in following teacher intent --- bots skip required workflow steps (53.8% of end-user conversations), omit expectation setup (33.4%), and overload learners with information or questions (37.1%). Guided by these findings, we design a prompt-based harness targeting the six most frequent failure modes. In a study with 200 college students across ten bots, the harness reduces the mean number of target failure codes per conversation from 1.406 to 0.489, a 65.2% relative reduction with bots weighted equally. These findings show how real-world failure analysis can guide practical improvements to educational chatbots.
中文翻译
教师越来越多地通过自然语言指令配置教育类聊天机器人，但这些机器人在真实使用中的失败模式仍不清晰。我们引入EduWild，一个包含1,726次对话的语料库，涉及217个由教师配置的聊天机器人，每条对话均配有背后的指令与配置信息，其中包括来自35个已部署聊天机器人中900次目标用户的真实对话。我们开发了一个包含29个代码的分类体系，涵盖事实性、工作流、诊断和教学法方面的失败，并使用经过人工校准的LLM裁判进行应用。最常见的失败是未能遵循教师意图——机器人跳过必要工作流步骤（占终端用户对话的53.8%）、遗漏期望设定（33.4%）以及向学习者传递过多信息或问题（37.1%）。基于这些发现，我们设计了一种基于提示的约束机制，针对六种最频繁的失败模式。在对十款机器人、200名大学生的实验中，该机制将每轮对话中目标失败代码的平均数量从1.406降至0.489，相对减少65.2%（按机器人加权平均）。这些发现表明，真实世界失败分析可有效指导教育聊天机器人的实际改进。
👤 Ziqi Liu, Ruiwei Xiao, Yuxuan Li, John Stamper 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Truth in Contrast: Calibrated Factual Evidence from Language Model Likelihoods
对比中的真相：基于语言模型似然性的校准事实证据
Large Language Models (LLMs) encode substantial factual knowledge. Yet, estimating confidence in factual claims reliably remains challenging. We study likelihood-based factual confidence from the complementary perspectives of discrimination and calibration across multilingual factual statements. Specifically, we instantiate contrastive generative likelihoods as Contrastive Factual Evidence (CFE), which compares the likelihood of the same claim under competing factual and non-factual conditions, and derive its probabilistic counterpart, the Contrastive Factual Posterior (CFP). We show analytically that CFP calibration depends on the differential error between the two surrogate conditionals, allowing errors shared across them to cancel and yield tighter calibration bounds. Empirically, CFP substantially improves calibration while maintaining factual discrimination comparable to its strongest competitor. Nonetheless, discrimination is uniformly weak across methods in lower-resource languages, which simultaneously undermines the utility of the measured calibration. To this end, we interpret a likelihood geometry, within which an evidence-bearing direction is induced, along with an orthogonal evidence-invariant direction. In our investigation, we selectively align target-language claims with stronger English evidence only along the factual-evidence direction, improving discrimination and open-ended factual generation without target-language labels.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）蕴含大量事实知识。然而，可靠估计事实主张的置信度仍然具有挑战性。我们从判别性和校准性两个互补视角，研究跨多语言事实陈述的基于似然性的事实置信度。具体而言，我们实例化对比生成似然性为对比事实证据（CFE），通过比较同一主张在竞争性事实与非事实条件下的似然性，并推导其概率对应物——对比事实后验（CFP）。我们从理论上证明，CFP的校准性取决于两个代理条件之间的误差差异，使得两者共有的误差能够相互抵消，从而获得更紧的校准边界。实证结果显示，CFP显著提升了校准性，同时保持与最强竞争对手相当的事实判别能力。然而，所有方法在低资源语言中的判别能力均普遍较弱，这同时削弱了所测校准的有效性。为此，我们解释了一种似然性几何结构，其中诱导出一个携带证据的方向，以及一个正交的无证据方向。在研究中，我们仅在事实证据方向上将目标语言主张与更强的英语证据对齐，从而在无需目标语言标签的情况下提升了判别能力和开放式事实生成性能。
👤 Ponhvoan Srey, Jason Lucas, Pittawat Taveekitworachai, Junyoung Lee, Anh Tuan Luu 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

MuninAI: A Self-Hosted Agentic Framework for Independent Research Groups
MuninAI：面向独立研究小组的自托管智能体框架
Research groups that want a language model grounded in their own literature face a choice between hosted services that receive their unpublished work and self-hosted deployments that keep the data local but give the model no access to the corpus. Munin is an open-source framework for a self-hosted scientific assistant that indexes a group's corpus and its citation graph, and wraps whichever open-weight model the group can serve in an agent harness with specialised reading, search, and computation tools. We claim that the harness, rather than the specific model, is what makes the system useful on domain literature, and we ship the evaluation suite that tests this claim with the software. On 199 paired literature questions, the harness more than doubles the accuracy of the base model, from 0.387 to 0.874 (+0.487, 95% CI [0.407, 0.568]), while naive retrieve-then-answer scores below the unaided model on every backbone tested. Under the harness, roughly twice as many answer claims trace to a passage the model read itself as with fixed top-5 retrieval. When the source paper for a question is removed from the corpus, the system abstains on about nine in ten of the questions it had answered correctly on the Qwen backbones and eight in ten on gpt-oss-20b, and it produces no fabricated local citation across 300 fabricated references. The pattern holds on three backbones from two developers, including a 20-billion-parameter model that runs on a single consumer-grade GPU. With all outbound network access disabled, both Qwen backbones still exceed the published PaperQA2 accuracy. The evaluation suite runs as a single command, so an adopting group can re-run it on its own corpus and model.
中文翻译
希望使用基于自身文献的语言模型的研究团队面临两难选择：托管服务会接收其未发表的工作，而自托管部署虽可本地保存数据，却使模型无法访问语料库。Munin是一个开源的自托管科学助手框架，可索引研究组的文献及其引用图谱，并将团队可部署的任意开源权重模型封装在一个带有专门阅读、搜索和计算工具的智能体外壳中。我们主张，正是这个外壳而非具体模型使系统在领域文献上具有实用性，并随软件附带了验证该主张的评估套件。在199对文献问题上，该外壳使基础模型的准确率从0.387大幅提升至0.874（+0.487，95%置信区间[0.407, 0.568]），而朴素的检索-回答方法在所有测试的骨干模型上均低于未辅助模型。在该外壳下，约有两倍多的答案主张源自模型自行阅读的段落，而非固定前5名检索。当问题来源论文从语料库中移除时，系统在Qwen骨干模型上约九成的问题放弃回答，gpt-oss-20b上约八成放弃，且在300个虚构引用中未产生任何虚假本地引用。这一模式在来自两位开发者的三个骨干模型上均成立，包括一个可在单个消费级GPU上运行的200亿参数模型。在完全禁用外网访问的情况下，两个Qwen骨干模型仍超过已发布PaperQA2的准确率。评估套件可通过单一命令运行，使采用该系统的团队可对其自身语料库和模型重新执行评估。
👤 Markus Fischer, Jens Meiler, Matthias Elgeti 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.23254]

Ignored, Not Refused: What Low Prompt-Injection Success Rates Hide in Small Tool-Using Agents
被忽略而非被拒绝：小规模工具使用型智能体中低提示注入成功率所隐藏的问题
Tool-using agents read untrusted external content, which exposes them to indirect prompt injection (IPI), where hidden instructions hijack the agent's tool calls. Benchmarks measure IPI security by the attack success rate (ASR), which treats every failed attack as a successful defense. To test this assumption, we audit 33,000 trajectories from eleven open-weight agents (2-14B parameters) on InjecAgent and AgentDojo. A label-blind LLM judge shows that most failed attacks are ignored (76-80%) rather than refused (17-18%). With identical model weights, changing only the system prompt shifts InjecAgent ASR from 0.2% to 43%. Our Attack-Task Outcome Matrix (ATOM) further shows that in 21.7% of AgentDojo episodes, the agent stayed safe but did not complete the user's task. A low ASR therefore mostly reflects agents silently ignoring injections, not refusing them. Evaluations should report how attacks fail and whether the user's task is completed, alongside ASR. We release the labeled corpus, judge prompt, and analysis code.
中文翻译
使用工具的智能体会读取不受信任的外部内容，从而暴露于间接提示注入（IPI）攻击中，其中隐藏指令劫持智能体的工具调用。基准测试通过攻击成功率（ASR）衡量IPI安全性，将每次攻击失败视为成功防御。为检验这一假设，我们审计了十一款开源权重智能体（2-14B参数）在InjecAgent和AgentDojo上的33,000条轨迹。无标签的LLM裁判显示，大多数攻击失败是被忽略（76-80%），而非被拒绝（17-18%）。在相同模型权重下，仅改变系统提示即可使InjecAgent的ASR从0.2%升至43%。我们的攻击-任务结果矩阵（ATOM）进一步显示，在AgentDojo的21.7%的回合中，智能体虽安全但未完成用户任务。因此，低ASR主要反映智能体静默忽略注入，而非主动拒绝。评估应报告攻击失败方式及用户任务是否完成，而不仅限于ASR。我们发布了标注语料库、裁判提示和分析代码。
👤 Farhan Zarif, Kazi Shahid Ahmed Galib, Synthia Akter Promi, Shabnam Sharmin Tarin, Ariyan Hossain et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

MultiAgent-OPD: End-to-End Training of Hierarchical Multi-Agent Systems with On-Policy Distillation
MultiAgent-OPD：基于在线蒸馏的分层多智能体系统端到端训练
In a hierarchical multi-agent system, a Leader assigns tasks and subagents carry them out. Jointly training the Leader and the subagents is difficult because a final answer gives only indirect feedback to the subagents. An LLM judge can score the performance of the subagents, but these scores depend on the judge's own assessment of the quality of subagents' responses. We present MultiAgent-OPD, which combines on-policy distillation with reinforcement learning to jointly train a shared-parameter Leader-Searcher-Answerer team end to end. We first train a Teacher in a Searcher-Answerer workflow using answer correctness. During team training, the frozen Teacher scores student-generated search and summary tokens to provide local process supervision for the Searchers, while the Leader learns from team success and the Answerer from candidate correctness. Across seven datasets, the trained system reaches 38.68% macro avg@4, exceeding Prompted Critic by 4.06 percentage points and C-MAS + RL, the trained cascaded multiagent system, by 2.61 percentage points. The team also solves more questions within four attempts.
中文翻译
在分层多智能体系统中，领导者分配任务，子智能体执行任务。联合训练领导者与子智能体困难，因为最终答案仅对子智能体提供间接反馈。大型语言模型裁判可评分子智能体的表现，但评分依赖于裁判自身对子智能体回答质量的判断。我们提出MultiAgent-OPD，结合在线蒸馏与强化学习，实现共享参数的领导者-搜索者-回答者团队的端到端联合训练。首先，在搜索者-回答者流程中使用答案正确性训练教师模型。在团队训练阶段，冻结的教师对学生产生的搜索与摘要token进行评分，为搜索者提供局部过程监督；领导者从团队整体成功中学习，回答者从候选答案正确性中学习。在七个数据集上，训练后的系统达到38.68%的宏平均@4，优于提示式批评者（Prompted Critic）4.06个百分点，优于C-MAS + RL（训练过的级联多智能体系统）2.61个百分点，且在四次尝试内解决更多问题。
👤 Yuhan Sun, Jiaxin Zhao, Yongdong Luo, Yibo Hu, Meiguang Jin et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

MAS-Bai: Linguistically Informed Adaptive Multi-Agent Translation for Bowen-Script Bai
MAS-Bai：面向鲍文文字白语-中文翻译的语言学启发式自适应多智能体框架
Large Language Models (LLMs) remain unreliable for extremely low-resource languages whose writing systems conflict with high-resource pretraining priors. We study Bai-Chinese translation in the Bowen script, where Chinese characters are frequently borrowed phonetically rather than semantically, causing severe literal-translation hallucinations. We propose MAS-Bai, a linguistically informed adaptive multi-agent framework for Bowen-script Bai-Chinese translation. MAS-Bai routes each input through either a lightweight retrieval-translation path or a deeper philological path with etymological and syntactic agents, and uses cycle-consistency with a lightweight reverse translator as an inference-time reliability signal. We construct a 6,536-sentence Bai-Chinese corpus and evaluate on a 1,536-sentence test set. MAS-Bai achieves 16.23 BLEU and 62.15 COMET, improving over Standard RAG by 8.11 BLEU and 6.85 COMET. Forward LoRA, expert ablations and controlled routing experiments distinguish the roles of supervised adaptation, Bai-specific expertise and conditional routing. The largest gains occur on Bowen phonetic loans and divergent Bai word order, while human evaluation and reverse-verifier analyses support the automatic results.
中文翻译

AMEND: Constraint-Guided Multi-Agent Text Detoxification with Isolated Critics
AMEND：基于约束引导的隔离批评者多智能体文本净化框架
Text detoxification requires balancing toxicity removal, semantic preservation, and fluency. Existing methods typically optimize an unconstrained scalar objective---enabling safety scores to mask semantic loss---or rely on open-ended iterative debate prone to semantic drift and high computational cost. We propose AMEND, an inference-time framework that decouples detoxification into role-conditioned proposal generation, isolated multidimensional criticism, and constraint-first selection. AMEND evaluates diverse candidates with role-blind critics and enforces strict semantic and fluency feasibility before ranking by utility. To maximize efficiency, AMEND Lite restricts subsequent search to at most one targeted repair round only when residual toxic spans remain. On ParaDetox, one-round selection over diverse roles improves the joint evaluation metric by $+0.0508$ over the best single role. On a frozen confirmation test set, AMEND Lite achieves a joint score of $0.6597 \pm 0.0062$, outperforming unconstrained multi-round loops by $+0.0489$ ($p < 0.05$) while reducing inference requests by 28.4%.
中文翻译
文本净化需要在去除毒性、保持语义和流畅性之间取得平衡。现有方法通常优化无约束的标量目标——这可能导致安全评分掩盖语义损失——或依赖开放式的迭代辩论，易引发语义漂移且计算成本高。我们提出AMEND，一种推理时框架，将净化过程解耦为角色条件的提议生成、隔离的多维批评以及以约束优先的选择。AMEND使用无角色偏见的批评者评估多种候选方案，并在按效用排序前强制执行严格的语义和流畅性可行性。为提高效率，AMEND Lite仅在残留毒性片段存在时限制后续搜索最多一轮针对性修复。在ParaDetox数据集上，跨角色的一轮选择使联合评估指标提升0.0508，优于最佳单角色表现；在冻结确认测试集上，AMEND Lite获得0.6597±0.0062的联合得分，比无约束多轮循环提升0.0489（p<0.05），同时减少28.4%的推理请求。

Chinese Decision: A Family of Language Models for Single-Pass Probabilistic Decision-Making
中文决策：面向单次传递概率决策的中文语言模型家族
Many language-based applications require probabilities over a finite set of decisions rather than open-ended responses. We present Chinese Decision, a family of Chinese-language models for single-pass probabilistic decision-making. Given context, an instruction, and candidate descriptions, the model extracts final-position candidate-letter logits in one forward pass and normalizes them over valid options, without autoregressive answer generation. A shared interface supports Boolean judgments, categorical choices, and ordinal scores. We report a completed 0.8B model trained with distribution cross-entropy on 13.048 million decisions and preliminary 2B checkpoints trained with an additional RLCD objective. On the full CJ-Bench of 307,900 decisions, the uncalibrated 0.8B model achieves 72.24% general-domain accuracy and 76.98% average accuracy across medicine, law, and finance using a single checkpoint, with 1.07% overall expected calibration error. The 2B model reaches 72.56% general-domain accuracy after 40.13% of one epoch but has not surpassed the completed 0.8B model on the professional-domain average. We further evaluate a validation-temperature-scaled deployment model and its Q8 quantization, finding close aggregate performance while documenting individual prediction changes. We release the training, evaluation, and deployment pipeline. The current results establish a reproducible approach to Chinese probabilistic decisions but do not isolate the effect of model scale.
中文翻译
许多基于语言的应用需要在有限决策集合上输出概率，而非开放式回答。我们提出中文决策，一个面向单次传递概率决策的中文语言模型家族。给定上下文、指令和候选描述，模型在一次前向传播中提取最终位置候选字母的logits，并在有效选项上进行归一化，无需自回归生成答案。统一接口支持布尔判断、类别选择和序数评分。我们报告了一个已完成的0.8B模型，使用分布交叉熵在1304.8万条决策上训练，以及初步的2B检查点，额外采用强化学习决策（RLCD）目标训练。在包含307,900条决策的CJ-Bench全集上，未经校准的0.8B模型在通用领域达到72.24%准确率，医学、法律和金融领域的平均准确率达76.98%，整体期望校准误差仅为1.07%。2B模型在完成约40.13%一个周期后达到72.56%通用领域准确率，但在专业领域平均表现尚未超越已完成的0.8B模型。我们进一步评估了验证温度缩放的部署模型及其Q8量化版本，发现总体性能接近，但记录了个体预测的变化。我们发布了完整的训练、评估和部署流程。当前结果建立了可复现的中文概率决策方法，但尚未分离出模型规模的影响。

Explaining Chess and Go in Natural Language
用自然语言解释国际象棋与围棋
Modern chess engines play far beyond human ability, often choosing moves whose underlying ideas are difficult for humans to understand. We propose an automatic metric for evaluating chess explanations, grounded in chess-engine evaluations. Evaluating state-of-the-art language models using this metric, we find that they perform surprisingly poorly, often relying on surface-level board observations rather than robust, transferable chess concepts. We then introduce BinProp, a training-free method that enables these same models to discover reusable chess concepts and use them to explain moves in natural language. BinProp improves explainability for every model tested, nearly tripling the average score compared with direct prompting. Beyond chess, the same method discovers useful concepts in Go, draughts, and shogi without additional tuning.

Disentangling Language Effects on Tool Admission and Post-Retrieval Safety
解耦语言对工具调用与检索后安全的影响
LLM agents increasingly use retrieval tools across languages, yet their safety evaluation remains English-centric. It remains unclear whether language affects tool admission, post-retrieval generation, or both. We independently vary request, retrieved-page, and required response languages in a controlled URL-retrieval setting across ten models and seven languages. We identify Safety Loss through Interlingual Processing (SLIP). Non-English harmful requests increase mean tool admission from 46.9% to 67.4%, with a positive mean change in every model. This pattern also persists in an autonomous retrieval setup across all four models tested in this setting. Requiring answers in the page language increases pooled harmfulness on matched tool calls from 50.3% to 57.4% across four closed-weight models, mainly in Chinese and Hindi. Translating pages alone produces no consistent harm increase in eight models with comparable inference settings. Automated audits show that a non-harmful response may be a genuine safety refusal, a deflection citing language difficulties, or an unusable answer. Harmfulness labels alone cannot distinguish these outcomes. Multilingual safety evaluations should therefore measure tool admission and response harmfulness separately and distinguish refusal from capability failure.
中文翻译
大型语言模型代理越来越多地在多语言环境下使用检索工具，但其安全性评估仍以英语为中心。目前尚不清楚语言是否影响工具调用决策、检索后的生成过程，或两者皆有。我们在一个受控的URL检索设置中，独立改变请求语言、检索页面语言和所需响应语言，在十个模型和七种语言上进行实验。我们发现了‘跨语言处理中的安全损失’（SLIP）现象：非英语有害请求导致工具调用率从46.9%上升至67.4%，所有模型均呈现正向变化。该模式在自主检索设置下也持续存在，覆盖全部四款测试模型。要求以页面语言回答时，匹配工具调用的综合有害率从50.3%升至57.4%，主要出现在中文和印地语中。仅翻译页面内容并未在八款模型中引发一致的危害增加。自动化审计显示，非有害响应可能是真正的安全拒绝、因语言困难而转移话题，或无法使用的答案。仅靠有害性标签无法区分这些情况。因此，多语言安全评估应分别测量工具调用与响应的有害性，并区分拒绝行为与能力失效。

Before Retrieval Begins: Safety and Tool-Use Decisions in LLM Agents
检索前：大语言模型代理中的安全与工具使用决策
Large language models increasingly act as agents that call external tools, yet most tool-safety research examines only what follows a tool call. The prior decision of whether to call the tool remains largely unexamined and can influence downstream safety. Across the seven open-weight models, the unweighted model-average harm rate after admitted harmful requests is 74.5%. To identify what governs this decision, we apply a factorial intervention to the input request. Within our generated construction, neutralizing the requested-operation component produces the largest admission contrast (19 pp). A linear direction estimated from this contrast influences held-out admission in four of seven models; effects on operation-neutralized requests in some models show that it is not an operation-specific mechanism. We then test whether written refusal is behaviorally and representationally coupled to the same decision. Written refusal accompanies 33.6% of admitted harmful calls. Plain-text refusal training reduces unsafe admission in models that retain benign tool use, showing that response-level safety training can transfer to the call decision. Together, these results establish a partial, model-dependent relation between written refusal and tool admission. They motivate direct evaluation and supervision of tool admission rather than treating text refusal as its proxy.

ParetoChat: An LLM-Based Interface for Interactive Multi-Objective Optimization
ParetoChat：基于大语言模型的交互式多目标优化界面
Multi-objective optimization models are often deployed in settings where decision makers (DMs) must balance some conflicting criteria. In practice, however, stakeholders must translate their qualitative preferences between different criteria into numerical weights, priorities, or reference points required by optimization methods, which can be difficult to specify and interpret. This paper proposes \emph{ParetoChat}, an LLM (large language model)-based expert-agent interface for interactive multi-objective optimization. The agent translates natural-language user input into structured scalarization parameters, invokes an optimization system, presents candidate solutions, and iteratively elicits feedback. We describe a multi-agent architecture that separates input processing, multi-criteria decision-making (MCDM) expertise, solution presentation, and feedback processing. We then present a simulation study using an LLM-based DM agent and benchmark ParetoChat against interactive methods implemented in DESDEO, including reference-point and NIMBUS methods. The computational experiments on multi-objective binary knapsack and set-partitioning instances show that ParetoChat can guide the search efficiently toward solutions that match the DM's preferences. An application to historical courier-order assignment instances illustrates how users can explore objective extremes and intermediate trade-offs through natural-language requests. The results indicate that LLM agents may provide a practical interaction layer between business stakeholders and optimization tools.

Same Person, Different Advice: Auditing LLM Financial Advice with Repeated Risk-Tolerance Responses
同一人，不同建议：通过重复风险容忍度回答审计大语言模型的金融建议
AI financial advisers set a client's stock allocation largely from one stated-risk question, yet people do not answer it the same way twice: in a national US panel, 41% of households chose a different answer two years later, and in a laboratory retest a third of people moved two or more points on a 0-10 scale after six months. Seven large language models from five providers gave one-turn allocation advice for 800 real household profiles in which only the risk answer changed. The recommended stock share moved by 10 to 16 percentage points on average, and by 24 to 35 when the answer changed, against 0.6 to 4.2 points for the same prompt asked twice; in the laboratory retest the rating alone moved advice by 11 to 18 points. Without any note, the models respond to the answer about as strongly as when told that it is very reliable (less so for the two Claude models). If that instructed response is how each model maps attitude to advice, discounting the answer for its measured reliability, as classical test theory prescribes, would cut the swings by a factor of 2.4 to 3.3. The swing is largest for clients under 40. A note that the answer is noisy steadies some models, but shifts their weight to the client's age and pulls cautious clients towards a generic middle allocation: clients unwilling to take any risk are advised 22% stocks instead of 4%. Three extra money questions reduce the swing for some models, with no statistically detectable change relative to how much advice differs across people. In a scripted test, one clarifying question removes nearly all of the swing from an answer the client then corrects (33.5 points to 1.9 on average) while keeping the response to a change the client confirms. We argue that same-person retest audits should be a standard check for AI advisers, and that what to do with an unreliable answer is a question of client autonomy as well as of statistics.
中文翻译
AI金融顾问主要依据客户一次陈述的风险问题来设定股票配置，但人们并不会两次给出相同答案：在美国全国性调查中，41%的家庭在两年后选择了不同的答案；在实验室重测中，六个月内有三分之一的人在0-10量表上移动了两个或更多点。我们使用来自五家提供商的七个大型语言模型，对800个真实家庭档案进行单轮配置建议，仅改变风险回答。结果显示，推荐的股票比例平均变动10至16个百分点，当回答变化时，变动幅度达到24至35个百分点，而同一提示重复提问时仅变动0.6至4.2个百分点；在实验室重测中，仅凭评分变动就导致建议变动11至18个百分点。在未加注释的情况下，模型对回答的响应强度与被告知该回答非常可靠时相当（Claude模型例外）。若按经典测试理论建议，根据测量可靠性对回答进行折扣处理，可使波动幅度降低2.4至3.3倍。波动最大的是40岁以下客户。添加关于回答存在噪声的说明可稳定部分模型，但会使其更依赖客户年龄，并将谨慎型客户引导至通用中间配置：不愿承担任何风险的客户被建议持有22%股票而非4%。额外增加三个金钱相关问题可减少部分模型的波动，但相对于人与人之间建议差异而言，统计上无显著变化。在脚本化测试中，一个澄清性问题能几乎消除客户随后纠正的回答带来的波动（从平均33.5点降至1.9点），同时保持对客户确认更改的响应。我们认为，同一人重测审计应成为AI顾问的标准检查手段，而如何处理不可靠回答，既是统计问题，也是客户自主权的问题。

KiranaBench: Can a Language Model Run a Neighbourhood Shop?
KiranaBench：大语言模型能否经营一家邻里小店？
Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tasks, including autonomously running a business. Existing benchmarks for this setting are almost exclusively English-language, Western in retail context, and single-agent, with the agent observing the business directly. We study how these results transfer to a multi-agent Indic setting. We introduce KiranaBench, a simulated Indian general store in which four roles (manager, cashier, helper, and bookkeeper) are each played by a separate LLM agent. Interactions with customers and suppliers occur in English, Hindi, or Hinglish, and product demand is calibrated to India's national Household Consumption Expenditure Survey. Evaluating four open-weight LLMs, we find that all fall far short of a fixed-rule shopkeeper baseline: the best model earns only one-eighth of the baseline's profit, and two models finish below an unmanaged shop. Performance is also consistently lower for Hindi and Hinglish than for English supplier communication. KiranaBench offers evidence that current LLM agents are not yet reliable for autonomously operating small businesses in India, and provides a testbed for developing agents suited to settings that are long-horizon, collaborative, and multilingual.

Recognizing Atari and Assessing Sente: Controlled Evaluation Tasks for Language-Based Go Agents
识别围棋中的‘打吃’并评估‘先手’：基于语言的围棋智能体的受控评估任务
Domain terminology provides concise descriptions of game states and strategic relationships, but whether game-playing language models can accurately recognize these concepts and relate them to move evaluation remains unclear. We construct a controlled evaluation set centered on atari and its relationship to sente in Go, examining terminology recognition under local board changes and move evaluation under different strategic conditions. We conduct our tests on LoGos, a Go language model, after fine-tuning it with terminology and commentary supervision. The model struggles to recognize changes in the atari relationship and assess its strategic implications. Its terminology predictions are associated with move preferences even when those preferences contradict the reference evaluation. Our controlled tests provide a framework for examining whether language-based Go agents connect domain terminology with precise board relationships and strategic move values.

Many Biases, One Circuit: Post-Training Tunes a Pretrained Cue-Routing Mechanism
多重偏见，单一回路：后训练调整预训练的线索路由机制
Language models often abandon a correct answer when the prompt contains a cue, a hint toward a wrong option such as "I think the answer is B." Sycophancy and related biases show this failure. They are often studied separately and blamed on alignment. We test this view causally, tracing the attention heads behind these biases through the stages of two post-training pipelines. In multiple-choice tests on most models, four cue types rely largely on the same few heads, a shared circuit already present in pretrained models. Most base models follow cues in plain text. Fine-tuning a base model on about 200 generic assistant examples without cues activates these heads in chat format, so cue-following can be a side effect of assistant tuning. The final reinforcement learning (RL) stage strengthens the effect of cues in almost every comparison. In Tulu-3-8B and OLMo-2-7B, we swap the attention patterns of six middle layers between the RL checkpoint and its predecessor. For suggested-answer cues, this shifts each checkpoint's cue effect most of the way to the other's level. Post-training thus seems to change when and how strongly models use this circuit. Mitigation for these multiple-choice biases could target this circuit.
中文翻译
语言模型在提示中包含线索（如‘我认为答案是B’）时，常常放弃正确答案。这种顺从性及相关偏见体现了这一失败。这些偏见通常被单独研究，并归因于对齐问题。我们通过因果实验，追踪两个后训练流程阶段中支持这些偏见的注意力头。在多数模型的多项选择测试中，四种线索类型主要依赖相同的少数几个注意力头，即一个在预训练模型中已存在的共享回路。大多数基础模型在纯文本中遵循线索。在约200个无线索的通用助手示例上进行微调后，模型在聊天格式中激活了这些头，说明线索跟随可能是助手微调的副作用。最终的强化学习（RL）阶段几乎在所有比较中都加强了线索的影响。在Tulu-3-8B和OLMo-2-7B中，我们将RL检查点与其前驱检查点的六个中间层注意力模式互换。对于建议答案类线索，这一操作使每个检查点的线索效应向另一个检查点水平移动了大部分距离。因此，后训练似乎改变了模型使用该回路的时间和强度。针对这些多项选择偏见的缓解措施可聚焦于该回路。

Competence-Based Evaluation of Logical Reasoning in Large Language Models
基于能力的大型语言模型逻辑推理能力评估
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have brought impressive performance across diverse tasks; however, shortcomings remain in logical reasoning, including inconsistencies under linguistic variation. To improve the reasoning capacity of LLMs, we must be able to accurately measure their reasoning capacity. Current benchmarks primarily report single accuracy numbers on selected reasoning tasks. Such a performance-based evaluation methodology says little about a model's generalization capacity in reasoning. To address this limitation, we argue for a different evaluation paradigm, called competence-based evaluation (CBE), which tests whether models reliably apply the same competence across a family of problems. A parameterized generator scales complexity while keeping the required competence fixed, and matched answer-preserving presentations test correctness beyond one formulation. We instantiate CBE on elementary ordering tasks, scaling input size while varying presentations of the same ordering rule. Across six GPT and Qwen models, performance is reasonably good over bounded complexity ranges, but even strong models fail the competence condition across the tested family. Chain-of-thought and GRPO extend strong performance without resolving all tested failures; supervised LoRA shows little held-out transfer. CBE provides diagnostic profiles and an evaluation protocol that can grow with future models.
中文翻译
近年来大型语言模型（LLMs）在多样化任务中表现出令人瞩目的性能，但在逻辑推理方面仍存在不足，例如在语言形式变化下出现不一致。为了提升LLMs的推理能力，我们必须能够准确衡量其推理能力。当前基准主要报告特定推理任务上的单一准确率，这种基于性能的评估方法无法反映模型在推理方面的泛化能力。为解决这一局限，我们主张采用一种新的评估范式——基于能力的评估（CBE），即测试模型是否能在一组问题中可靠地应用相同的推理能力。参数化生成器在保持所需能力不变的情况下增加复杂度，匹配的答案保持呈现方式用于检验正确性是否超越单一表述形式。我们在基础排序任务中实例化CBE，通过改变输入规模并调整同一排序规则的不同表述方式。在六个GPT和Qwen模型中，性能在有限复杂度范围内表现良好，但即使强模型也无法通过整个家族测试的能力条件。思维链（Chain-of-thought）和GRPO扩展了强模型的表现，但未能解决所有测试失败；监督LoRA在保留泛化方面作用甚微。CBE提供了诊断性分析图谱和可随未来模型演进的评估协议。

NLData2Opt: Benchmarking Parameter Derivation and Prediction for Modeling Optimization Problems from Natural Language and Data
NLData2Opt：从自然语言与数据中建模优化问题的参数推导与预测基准测试
Formulating optimization problems from raw inputs of problem descriptions and data is time-consuming and costly. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising way to automate this process, existing benchmarks overestimate their capabilities by relying on idealized inputs where all data is relevant and complete. This diverges from reality, where data-centric reasoning is required to extract, compute, and predict parameters from noisy sources. To address this, we introduce NLData2Opt, a benchmark to evaluate LLMs on the multi-stage reasoning inherent in practical optimization workflows. NLData2Opt decomposes problems into problem descriptions and data, and requires models to (1) extract relevant parameters from data containing misleading attributes, (2) compute missing parameters derived by transforming existing data attributes, and (3) predict unavailable future values from historical observations. Evaluation results show substantial performance decline relative to existing benchmarks, indicating that benchmark progress does not directly translate to formulation accuracy in practical optimization workflows. NLData2Opt is available at this https URL .

从原始的问题描述和数据中构建优化问题耗时且成本高昂。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）为自动化该过程提供了有前景的途径，但现有基准测试依赖于理想化的输入（所有数据均相关且完整），高估了其能力。这与现实情况不符，现实中需要以数据为中心的推理来从噪声源中提取、计算并预测参数。为此，我们提出了NLData2Opt，一个用于评估LLMs在实际优化工作流中多阶段推理能力的基准测试。NLData2Opt将问题分解为问题描述和数据，并要求模型（1）从包含误导属性的数据中提取相关参数，（2）通过转换现有数据属性计算缺失参数，（3）从历史观测中预测未来不可用的值。评估结果表明，相较于现有基准，性能显著下降，说明基准进展并不能直接转化为实际优化工作流中的建模准确性。NLData2Opt可在该链接获取。

Identify, Classify, Act: Per-Instruction Controlled Compliance for LLMs over External Data
识别、分类、行动：面向外部数据的指令级可控合规性机制
LLMs over external data face a per-instruction tension: a single document or tool return often interleaves legitimate directives with adversarial prompt injections, and the model must follow the former while rejecting the latter. Existing defenses suppress or trust all external instructions, collapsing the safety-utility frontier and forcing a choice between over-refusal and over-compliance. We argue the missing capability is \textbf{per-instruction credit attribution} and formalize it as External-Data Instruction Classification, an identify-classify-act decision over each embedded directive. We propose \textbf{EDIC}, a decomposed reward framework for per-instruction compliance. A scene-grounded pipeline injects mixed beneficial and harmful instructions with counterfactual filtering, while a generative judge is initialized by SFT and refined with GRPO to attribute compliance at the instruction level. The judge then produces a reward that separately encourages beneficial compliance and penalizes harmful compliance for RL training. Across two backbones and five benchmarks, EDIC improves both safety and utility over the strongest prior defense, lifting harmful-rejection by up to 7.7\% and Arena-Hard-Auto utility by 12.5\% on Qwen3-4B. The code repository is hosted at this https URL .
中文翻译
大语言模型在处理外部数据时面临指令级矛盾：单个文档或工具返回的内容常混合合法指令与恶意提示注入，模型需遵循前者而拒绝后者。现有防御策略要么完全抑制外部指令，要么全然信任，导致安全与效用之间的权衡被压缩，迫使模型在过度拒绝与过度服从之间做出选择。我们认为缺失的能力是‘指令级信用归属’，并将其形式化为外部数据指令分类任务——对每个嵌入指令进行识别-分类-行动决策。我们提出EDIC，一种用于指令级合规性的分解式奖励框架。一个场景感知的流水线通过反事实过滤注入混合有益与有害指令，同时使用SFT初始化的生成式裁判，经GRPO微调后实现指令级别的合规性归属。该裁判随后生成奖励信号，分别鼓励有益合规并惩罚有害合规，用于强化学习训练。在两种骨干模型和五个基准上，EDIC在最强先验防御基础上提升了安全性和效用，使有害拒绝率最高提升7.7%，Qwen3-4B在Arena-Hard-Auto上的效用提升达12.5%。代码仓库位于该链接。

The Reliance Hypothesis: Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Rises as Language Resource Level Falls
依赖假说：随着语言资源水平下降，思维链忠实度上升
Chain-of-Thought (CoT) faithfulness matters for monitoring, explaining and safely deploying large language models, yet it has been studied mostly in English. Languages differ in level, the amount of text available to learn from, so models know less in lower-resource languages, which may change how much they rely on their reasoning. To our knowledge, we are the first to relate CoT faithfulness to both resource level and task type (knowledge- or reasoning-intensive). On parallel multiple-choice questions from Multilingual Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMMLU) in eight languages, faithfulness rises as resource level falls on knowledge-intensive tasks but not on reasoning-intensive ones, a gap that points the same way across four models and several faithfulness measures. We propose, and find support for, the Reliance Hypothesis: models rely more on their reasoning when they cannot recall the answer, as in lower-resource languages on knowledge-intensive tasks. Model confidence partly explains the effect of resource level, but not the knowledge-reasoning gap. Languages in which models are less confident rely more on reasoning, yet less confident answers within a language do not, a Simpson's paradox. In lower-resource languages, models rely on their reasoning for more answers, not more strongly for each. CoT monitoring may therefore be most informative in lower-resource languages, where it is least often validated.
中文翻译
思维链（CoT）的忠实度对于监控、解释和安全部署大语言模型至关重要，但以往研究主要集中在英语。不同语言在资源水平（可用于学习的文本量）上存在差异，因此在低资源语言中模型知识较少，可能影响其对推理的依赖程度。据我们所知，这是首个将CoT忠实度与资源水平及任务类型（知识密集型或推理密集型）关联的研究。在八种语言的多语言大规模多任务语言理解（MMMLU）平行多项选择题上，知识密集型任务中，资源水平越低，忠实度越高，而在推理密集型任务中则无此趋势，这一差距在四个模型和多种忠实度度量下一致。我们提出并验证了‘依赖假说’：当模型无法回忆答案时（如在低资源语言的知识密集型任务中），会更依赖其推理过程。模型置信度部分解释了资源水平的影响，但无法解释知识与推理之间的差距。在模型置信度较低的语言中，它们更依赖推理，但在同一语言内，置信度较低的答案并不更依赖推理，这是一种辛普森悖论。在低资源语言中，模型对更多答案依赖推理，而非对每个答案的依赖更强。因此，CoT监控在低资源语言中可能最具信息量，尽管这些语言中最少被验证。

Self-Evolving Coding Agents via Harness-Policy Optimization
通过Harness-策略优化实现自我演化的编码智能体
Large language model (LLM) agents have shown promising potential in coding tasks, and their performance is jointly determined by the policy model and the harness that mediates their interaction with the environment. Indeed, harness and policy are mutually reinforcing and evolving only one component is suboptimal---a harness tuned for one checkpoint may not target the failure modes of an updated policy, while policy learning remains limited to the trajectories elicited by a fixed harness. In light of this, we propose Self-HPO, a recursive self-improving framework for coding agents that alternates harness and policy optimization, using the evolving policy as its own harness proposer. Specifically, in each round, the current policy first analyzes its own failures and proposes new harness candidates (H-step). The policy is then updated through reinforcement learning (RL) under the selected harness (P-step), and hard tasks identified during training form the next harness search set, keeping harness optimization targeted to the policy's evolving failure modes. Across three coding benchmarks, Self-HPO consistently outperforms harness-only and policy-only baselines, improving Qwen3.5-35B-A3B's average resolve rate from 57.8% to 64.8%, with gains generalizing to out-of-distribution settings. Further analysis shows that both optimized components retain gains even when paired with their base counterparts, while their joint gain exceeds the sum of their individual gains. Self-HPO also improves reliability (Pass@1) and expands the capability frontier (Pass@k) over the base agent. Additionally, we show that with a stronger external model (e.g., Opus 4.8) as the harness proposer, the same framework yields a further performance boost.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在编码任务中展现出巨大潜力，其性能由策略模型和中介其与环境交互的Harness共同决定。事实上，Harness与策略相互促进，仅优化其中一个组件是次优的——为某一检查点调优的Harness可能无法针对更新后的策略的失败模式，而策略学习也受限于固定Harness所引发的轨迹。为此，我们提出Self-HPO，一种递归自我改进的编码智能体框架，交替进行Harness与策略优化，利用不断演化的策略作为自身的Harness提议者。具体而言，每轮中，当前策略首先分析自身失败并提出新的Harness候选（H步）；随后在选定Harness下通过强化学习（RL）更新策略（P步），训练中识别出的困难任务构成下一轮Harness搜索集，确保Harness优化始终聚焦于策略的演化失败模式。在三个编码基准测试中，Self-HPO持续优于仅优化Harness或仅优化策略的基线，使Qwen3.5-35B-A3B的平均解决率从57.8%提升至64.8%，且增益可泛化至分布外场景。进一步分析显示，即使与基础组件配对，优化后的组件仍能保持增益，而两者联合增益超过各自增益之和。Self-HPO还提升了可靠性（Pass@1）并拓展了能力边界（Pass@k）。此外，我们还发现，若使用更强的外部模型（如Opus 4.8）作为Harness提议者，同一框架可带来进一步性能提升。

Explore Separately, Adopt Selectively: Execution-Guided Tool Use over Large Tool Libraries
分别探索，选择性采纳：面向大型工具库的执行引导式工具使用
Large language model agents must discover relevant tools from large libraries, e.g., thousands of tools, as new needs arise during task execution. When several candidates could satisfy the same need, execution may be required to identify the relevant one. Investigating candidates in a shared trajectory can leave alternatives unexplored or allow failed attempts to influence subsequent reasoning. To address this, we present BRIA (BRanch-Inspect-Adopt), a simple yet effective agent that temporarily isolates candidate investigations in separate branches. Each branch retrieves and executes tools to investigate its candidate; a task agent inspects the resulting execution evidence and adopts only useful results into its continuing trajectory. BRIA retrieves additional tools as new capability needs arise during task execution. We evaluate BRIA on PlanBench-XL and MCP-Atlas using a shared pool of 1897 tools, three language models, and static and adaptive retrieval baselines. Across all settings, BRIA matches or exceeds the strongest retrieval baseline. On MCP-Atlas, it achieves an average claim score of 89.77, compared with 75.40 for MCP-Zero, a gain of 14.37 percentage points. BRIA also lies on the Pareto frontier for task performance and input-token cost.
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体在任务执行过程中需从庞大的工具库（如数千个工具）中发现相关工具。当多个候选工具可满足同一需求时，可能需要通过执行来识别出真正相关的工具。若在共享轨迹中同时考察候选工具，可能导致某些备选方案未被探索，或失败尝试影响后续推理。为此，我们提出BRIA（BRanch-Inspect-Adopt），一种简单而有效的智能体，其将候选工具的探究临时隔离在独立分支中。每个分支检索并执行工具以检验其候选方案；一个任务智能体检查执行结果证据，并仅将有用结果纳入持续轨迹。BRIA在任务执行过程中会根据新能力需求检索更多工具。我们在PlanBench-XL和MCP-Atlas上评估了BRIA，使用1897个工具的共享池、三种语言模型以及静态与自适应检索基线。在所有设置下，BRIA均达到或超过最强检索基线。在MCP-Atlas上，其平均声明得分达89.77，相较MCP-Zero的75.40提升了14.37个百分点。BRIA还位于任务性能与输入token成本的帕累托前沿。

ToMStories: Interpreting Theory-of-Mind in Synthetic Narratives
ToMStories：在合成叙事中解读心智理论
Understanding people often requires inferring what they feel or want from how they behave rather than from what they state outright. We ask whether language models can make these inferences, and how they do so. We introduce Theory-of-Mind Stories (ToMStories), an evaluation dataset of ~6,000 short synthetically generated narrative vignettes, each paired with a multiple-choice or open-ended question about a character's emotion, fear, motivation, or emotional wound. These psychological states are conveyed implicitly (showing), explicitly (telling), or with conflict between dialogue and description. Evaluating thirteen open-weight models (1B-72B parameters), we find that the largest models match humans on multiple choice but not open-ended questions. Our analyses point to simplifications models make in both interpretation and generation of implicit psychological states, indicating that synthetic data can help us understand how models perform interpretation, but likely overestimates how well models interpret humans.

A Plausible Explanation Is Enough to Mislead Multimodal Judges
一个合理的解释就足以误导多模态裁判
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly serve as judges in training and evaluation pipelines, often assessing answers together with explanations. Although AI judges are sensitive to textual factors, it remains unclear whether ordinary plausible explanations can sway judgments requiring visual verification. We introduce JUPE, a visual-question-answering (VQA)-style benchmark that fixes answers while varying explanations. Across 14 MLLM judges, ordinary explanations that sound plausible mislead judges, with effects comparable on average to deliberately persuasive arguments. Plain justifications and chain-of-thought rationales reduce rejection of incorrect answers by 12.7 and 13.8 percentage points, respectively. We call this failure plausibility capture. Using signal detection theory, we find a significant mean shift in decision criterion toward acceptance, without a clear change in mean discriminability. Explanations sway judgments across domains, explanation generators, and judging protocols, while tested mitigations offer limited, model-dependent protection. Such shifts can inflate performance estimates when judges evaluate answers with explanations.
中文翻译
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）越来越多地被用作训练和评估流程中的裁判，常需同时评估答案与解释。尽管AI裁判对文本因素敏感，但普通合理解释是否会影响需要视觉验证的判断仍不明确。我们引入JUPE，一种类似VQA的基准测试，固定答案但改变解释。在14个MLLM裁判中，听起来合理的普通解释会误导裁判，其影响平均相当于刻意有说服力的论据。简单的理由和思维链推理分别使错误答案的拒绝率降低12.7和13.8个百分点。我们称此失败为‘合理性捕获’。使用信号检测理论，我们发现决策标准显著偏向接受，而辨别力均值无明显变化。解释影响跨领域、解释生成器和裁判协议的判断，而测试的缓解措施仅提供有限且依赖模型的保护。此类偏差可能导致在裁判评估带解释的答案时夸大性能估计。

The Model Knows Better: Defending Medical Multi-Agent Systems Against Prompt Injection
模型更懂：防御医疗多智能体系统免受提示注入攻击
Medical multi-agent systems, in which LLM agents discuss a case, are increasingly studied for clinical decision support. Prompt injection, text that an attacker adds to a model's input, can manipulate LLMs into wrong decisions. How it affects these systems, and how to protect them, remains unclear. Across two systems, two model families and three benchmarks, a single injected passage succeeds in up to 61% of cases, and collaboration does not consistently protect against it. Existing defenses help only partly: they miss injections that look like ordinary clinical text. Yet when asked to check the injected text against the patient's findings, a model that followed the injection usually rejects it. We therefore propose a training-free defense that verifies the clinical judgments behind the system's decision. Following clinical practice, it separates the patient's findings from clinical judgments, such as diagnoses, recommendations and instructions, and checks each judgment against the findings alone, so the decision rests only on supported judgments. Our defense outperforms all baselines on every benchmark, lowering attack success on MedQA to 4-7% while maintaining accuracy on cases without an attack, and remains effective with a medical model and against defense-aware and adaptive attacks.
中文翻译
医疗多智能体系统（LLM智能体讨论病例）正被广泛研究用于临床决策支持。提示注入（攻击者向模型输入添加的文本）可操纵LLM做出错误判断。然而，这种攻击如何影响此类系统，以及如何有效防御，仍不明确。在两个系统、两种模型族和三个基准测试中，单个注入段落可在高达61%的案例中成功，且协作机制并未始终提供保护。现有防御措施仅部分有效：它们会漏掉看似普通临床文本的注入。然而，当要求模型将注入文本与患者发现内容进行比对时，通常遵循注入指令的模型会拒绝该文本。因此，我们提出一种无需训练的防御机制，验证系统决策背后的临床判断。参照临床实践，该机制将患者的发现与临床判断（如诊断、建议和指令）分离，并仅用发现内容检验每项判断，确保决策仅基于有支持的判断。该防御在所有基准测试中均优于基线，在MedQA上将攻击成功率降至4-7%，同时保持无攻击情况下的准确率，并在使用医疗模型及面对防御感知和自适应攻击时依然有效。

HeRE: A Novel Benchmark for Hebrew Relation Extraction via LLM-Guided Denoising of Knowledge Graph Alignments
HeRE：一种基于LLM引导的知识图谱对齐去噪的希伯来语关系抽取新基准
While LLMs demonstrate strong capabilities in English Relation Extraction (RE) tasks, a significant gap remains in assessing their RE performance in low-resource languages, due to a lack of standardized benchmarks. Creating native, language-specific annotated RE benchmarks is expensive and requires human expertise, while relying on translation yields suboptimal, inaccurate datasets that introduce English-centric biases. To tackle this, we propose a scalable, language-agnostic methodology to create RE data by denoising naturally occurring yet noisy alignments of knowledge bases and corresponding texts. We define a proper alignment via a Natural Language Inference (NLI) task, where a relation triplet is entailed by an input text. We empirically assess five denoising strategies, and demonstrate that multilingual models outperform language-specific models in the few-shot LLM setting, while Hebrew-specific encoders remain competitive under NLI fine-tuning and are strongest in the label-free cross-training setting. We further find that prompting in the native language achieves performance on par with or slightly exceeding English prompts, confirming that the pipeline operates naturally in the target language. The best open-weights model, Gemma-4-31B-it (F1\,=\,0.915), matches the best API model, GPT-5.4 (0.921), and reaches F1\,=\,0.844 on a fully held-out, dual-annotated test set. Applying the optimized pipeline, we release HeRE: a novel, massive, 1.5-million-instance silver dataset for Hebrew RE, designed to support data-intensive experimentation and model fine-tuning. HeRE bridges a critical gap in standard Hebrew RE evaluation, while providing the NLP community with a reproducible, language-agnostic pipeline for RE dataset construction in other low-resource settings.
中文翻译
尽管大语言模型在英文关系抽取（RE）任务中表现出强大能力，但在低资源语言上的RE性能评估仍存在显著空白，主要因为缺乏标准化基准。创建原生、语言特定的标注RE基准成本高昂且需专业人力；依赖翻译则会产生次优、不准确的数据集，并引入以英语为中心的偏见。为此，我们提出一种可扩展、语言无关的方法，通过去噪自然发生但嘈杂的知识库对齐及其对应文本，生成RE数据。我们通过自然语言推理（NLI）任务定义正确对齐：若输入文本蕴含一个关系三元组，则视为有效。我们实证评估了五种去噪策略，结果表明在少样本LLM设置下，多语言模型优于语言特定模型；而在NLI微调下，希伯来语专用编码器仍具竞争力，且在无标签跨训练设置中表现最强。我们还发现，使用母语提示的性能与英文提示相当甚至略优，证实该流程在目标语言中自然运行。最佳开源权重模型Gemma-4-31B-it（F1=0.915）媲美最佳API模型GPT-5.4（0.921），并在完全保留的双标注测试集上达到F1=0.844。应用优化后的流程，我们发布了HeRE：一个全新的、大规模的150万实例银标希伯来语RE数据集，旨在支持数据密集型实验与模型微调。HeRE弥合了标准希伯来语RE评估的关键缺口，同时为NLP社区提供了一个可复现、语言无关的关系抽取数据集构建范式。

AuditKIT: A Unified Evaluation Spine for Benchmarking, Judging, and Comparing Large Language Models and Agentic Systems
AuditKIT：用于基准测试、评判与比较大语言模型及智能体系统的统一评估框架
Evaluating language models and agents requires combining benchmark accuracy, judge-based assessment, runtime performance, tool-use behavior, and retrieval quality. Differences in interfaces, metric semantics, and trace formats complicate these comparisons and can hide failures in agentic workflows. We present AuditKIT, a source-available evaluation library that records these measurements in one result representation, with direction-aware metric contracts, configuration fingerprints, and extensible metrics and backends. For agents, it provides turn-aware tool-call matching, separates parallel from sequential execution, and distinguishes verified task outcomes from trajectory-level diagnostics. Missing or unobservable evidence is marked unscored rather than counted as failure. For retrieval-augmented generation, it combines ranked retrieval metrics, judged grounding, and deterministic evidence checks. Seven case studies spanning model comparison, serving runtimes, retrieval, tool calling, and interoperability show how answer extraction, runtime behavior, and metric selection change evaluation outcomes.

RealCLI: Toward a Unified Evaluation of Human-Agent Collaboration for CLI Agents
RealCLI：面向CLI智能体人机协作统一评估
Command-line interface (CLI) agents increasingly execute longer-horizon tasks, yet existing benchmarks either assume that user instructions remain fixed throughout execution or evaluate task outcomes without capturing runtime collaboration quality. In this paper, we present REALCLI, a paired benchmark that unifies the evaluation of task execution and human-agent collaboration quality on realistic CLI tasks. To comprehensively measure collaboration, REALCLI builds a five-dimensional framework, operationalized into 22 sub-dimensions and 51 interaction scenario templates. REALCLI provides two modes for paired comparison: REALCLI-EXEC supplies complete specifications upfront, whereas REALCLI-COLLAB introduces a scripted user that delivers progressive information, corrections, constraint revisions, and authorization decisions. Evaluations reveal profound capability gaps in frontier agents: the best configuration attains an average score of only 49.2%, and when evaluated by the joint collaboration score ($J_{\mathrm{collab}}$), the peak performance reaches merely 37.6%. These results show that task effectiveness diverges sharply from collaboration quality, with models consistently struggling in steerability, trust calibration, and safety boundary management. REALCLI provides a rigorous, reproducible foundation for measuring and advancing collaborative CLI agents.

命令行接口（CLI）智能体越来越多地执行长周期任务，但现有基准测试要么假设用户指令在执行过程中保持不变，要么仅评估任务结果而未捕捉运行时的人机协作质量。本文提出REALCLI，一个统一评估真实CLI任务中任务执行与人机协作质量的配对基准。为全面衡量协作，REALCLI构建了一个五维框架，具体化为22个子维度和51个交互场景模板。REALCLI提供两种配对比较模式：REALCLI-EXEC提前提供完整规范，而REALCLI-COLLAB引入脚本化用户，逐步提供信息、修正、约束修改和授权决策。评估结果显示前沿智能体存在显著能力差距：最佳配置平均得分仅为49.2%，联合协作得分（$J_{\mathrm{collab}}$）峰值仅为37.6%。这些结果表明任务有效性与协作质量存在显著分化，模型在可引导性、信任校准和安全边界管理方面持续表现不佳。REALCLI为测量和推进协作式CLI智能体提供了严谨且可复现的基础。

AI-Driven Reproducibility and Analytical Robustness in Scientific Discovery
AI驱动的科学发现中的可重现性与分析稳健性
Reproducible and analytically robust research findings are essential to scientific progress, but evaluating these properties typically requires costly manual effort. We investigate whether LLM agents can help automate reproduction and robustness analysis, using a medical case study grounded in the Surveillance, Epidemiology, and End Results (SEER) database, a widely used U.S. cancer registry underlying tens of thousands of published studies. First, we introduce SEER-Repro, a benchmark of 20 published SEER-based papers together with the data and resources required to reproduce their findings. Second, we create an AI agent that successfully reproduces 77.4% of 2,190 atomic claims, while providing executable baselines for further analysis. Third, we develop an agentic analysis pipeline to test the robustness of the medical findings by identifying analytical choices underlying each finding, proposing defensible alternatives, and rerunning the analysis to determine whether those choices affect the conclusion. Across published papers in SEER-Repro, our pipeline identifies potentially consequential analytical choices in 9.5% of fully reproduced findings and flags them for expert review. These results show how LLM agents can support scientific review by reproducing findings, systematically testing their robustness, and directing expert attention toward potentially consequential analytical choices.

VocalGrad: Evaluating Acoustic Perception in Audio Language Models
VocalGrad：评估音频语言模型中的声学感知能力
Recent large audio language models (LALMs) have extended their capabilities beyond lexical speech understanding to tasks requiring paralinguistic and non-linguistic audio understanding, such as emotion recognition. However, these models still struggle to reliably utilize acoustic information, particularly when it co-occurs with spoken linguistic content. Existing benchmarks do not clearly distinguish acoustic perception from higher-level reasoning, making it difficult to identify the source of these failures. To address this issue, we introduce VocalGrad, a benchmark designed to evaluate LALMs' ability to perceive low-level acoustic variations. VocalGrad formulates this problem as a simple binary classification of temporal change direction, enabling evaluation largely independent of higher-level reasoning or semantic understanding. Our benchmark results reveal a substantial gap in acoustic perception between human annotators and state-of-the-art LALMs: while human annotators achieve high accuracy, LALMs perform at near-chance levels. We further conduct linear probing to identify structural bottlenecks in these models. Our analysis reveals two distinct failure modes: for some categories, task-relevant information is preserved in internal representations but not utilized by the language model head; for others, the information becomes progressively less linearly decodable across model layers. Overall, our results reveal substantial limitations of LALMs in modeling acoustic variation under controlled settings and expose representation differences not observable from text outputs.
中文翻译
近期的大规模音频语言模型（LALMs）已将能力扩展至语音理解之外的任务，如情感识别等需要语调和非语言音频理解的场景。然而，这些模型在利用声学信息方面仍存在困难，尤其是在声学信息与语言内容共现时。现有基准未能清晰区分声学感知与高层推理，难以定位失败原因。为此，我们提出VocalGrad，一个用于评估LALMs感知低级声学变化能力的基准。该基准将问题形式化为简单的时序变化方向二分类任务，使评估基本独立于高层推理或语义理解。实验结果揭示了人类标注者与最先进LALMs之间在声学感知上的显著差距：人类表现优异，而LALMs的表现接近随机水平。我们进一步通过线性探测识别模型内部结构瓶颈。分析发现两种不同的失败模式：某些类别中，任务相关的信息保留在内部表示中但未被语言模型头部利用；另一些类别中，信息在模型各层间逐渐变得难以线性解码。总体而言，我们的研究揭示了LALMs在受控环境下建模声学变化方面的重大局限，并暴露了仅从文本输出无法观察到的表征差异。

RedDarwin: Evolving Jailbreak Skills against Frontier LLMs
RedDarwin：针对前沿大模型进化越狱技能
Red teaming must continually adapt as frontier LLM safeguards evolve, yet existing approaches largely search for adversarial prompts or optimize within fixed attack procedures. We observe that even when an attack becomes ineffective, its underlying mechanisms can remain useful and can be revised or recombined to construct stronger attacks. Based on this insight, we introduce RedDarwin, an agentic framework that shifts the unit of jailbreak evolution from prompts and fixed attacks to executable attack skills. RedDarwin consists of 47 reusable base skills and autonomously revises, composes, executes, and selects them using target-model feedback, allowing successful discoveries to become building blocks for subsequent evolution. RedDarwin produces 65 evolved skills, improving 44 of 47 base attacks and increasing their mean attack success rate (ASR) on AdvBench from 18.8% to 47.3%. These evolved skills consistently outperform baselines on the held-out StrongREJECT and frontier LLMs unseen during evolution; with only 10 target-model queries, RedDarwin achieves 100% ASR on GPT-5.6-Sol, 92% on Claude-Sonnet-5, and 80% on Gemini-3.8-Flash. Analysis of the evolution trajectories further shows that strong attacks often emerge from novel combinations of reusable mechanisms rather than from individually dominant ones. We believe this paradigm can accelerate adaptive red teaming and inform stronger safeguards, creating complementary value for both safety evaluation and defense.

Beyond the Benchmark: Conditioning, Domains and Failure Modes of Qwen3-TTS
超越基准：Qwen3-TTS的条件设置、领域适应与失败模式
Qwen3-TTS reports state-of-the-art zero-shot voice cloning, but its technical report leaves open how the model should be conditioned, how it behaves outside read speech, and how it fails. We characterise its two cloning interfaces, in-context learning (ICL) and X-Vector, against Chatterbox, XTTS-v2 and F5-TTS on read, meeting, emotional and air-traffic-control (ATC) speech, using eighteen objective measures, an LLM judge, a spoofing countermeasure and a listening test. An off-the-shelf countermeasure flags its clones no more often than genuine recordings, and on ATC radio it fails for every system. Code and audio are released.

PresenceBench: An Exploratory Evaluation of Large Language Model Adaptation to User State
PresenceBench：大语言模型对用户状态适应性的探索性评估
Presence-aware intelligence is the capacity of an AI system's intelligence layer to adapt appropriately to the functional state of the person it serves (here readiness, flow, and stability) while preserving task quality and safety. We introduce PresenceBench, an instrument for measuring it. PresenceBench evaluates how the intelligence layer uses a supplied state, not how that state is sensed, and breaks the capability into measurable properties. Two are tested here. Receptivity: does the model take up the state and give a better response than with no signal? Specificity: does any improvement depend on the correct state, rather than on extra structured context? A third, adaptation across states, is a required endpoint not measured in this release. A five-arm causal design answers both questions, against each model's own baseline and an equally structured mismatched-state control, scored by a correctness-gated human paired comparison. The exploratory evaluation covered four models across eighteen single-turn, six fixed three-turn and six simulated natural-conversation scenarios. Supplying a synthetic state, raw or translated into a response policy, gave human-preference point estimates above 0.5 for all four models (Claude Sonnet 5, DeepSeek V4 Pro, Gemini 3.6 Flash, GPT-5.6 Terra), with three of eight 95% intervals excluding chance. Specificity was not resolved: no per-model interval excluded chance, and the design cannot yet separate the state values from a generic instruction to adapt. Policy delivery reduced priced cost and output tokens against each model's baseline; these are resource-use changes, not efficiency gains, because correctness non-inferiority was not established. Safety-floor failures were rare (13 of 360 cases), with a non-significant excess under policy delivery. We report the instrument, the frozen results, and the controls a confirmatory study needs.
中文翻译
具备感知存在感的智能是AI系统智能层能够根据服务对象的功能状态（如准备就绪、心流状态、稳定状态）做出适当适应，同时保持任务质量与安全性。我们引入PresenceBench，一种用于测量该能力的工具。PresenceBench评估智能层如何利用提供的状态信号，而非状态的感知方式，并将该能力分解为可测量属性。本文测试了两个属性：响应性——模型是否采纳状态信号并给出优于无信号时的回应？特异性——任何改进是否依赖于正确的状态，而非额外的结构化上下文？第三个属性“跨状态适应”作为必要终点，尚未在此版本中测量。采用五臂因果设计，对比每种模型自身基线与结构匹配的错位状态控制组，由正确性门控的人类配对比较评分。探索性评估覆盖四种模型，涵盖十八个单轮、六个固定三轮及六个模拟自然对话场景。提供合成状态信号（原始或翻译为响应策略），所有四种模型的人类偏好点估计均高于0.5（Claude Sonnet 5、DeepSeek V4 Pro、Gemini 3.6 Flash、GPT-5.6 Terra），其中八组中有三组95%置信区间排除了随机可能性。特异性未获解决：无一模型的置信区间排除随机，且当前设计尚无法将状态值与通用适应指令区分开。策略传递降低了每种模型的定价成本和输出token数，但这是资源使用变化而非效率提升，因未建立正确性非劣性。安全底线失败罕见（360例中13例），策略传递下的超额也无统计显著性。我们报告了该工具、冻结结果及确认性研究所需控制条件。

Not Every Mention Is a Claim: Rethinking Object Hallucination Detection in Vision-Language Models
并非每个提及都是主张：重新思考视觉-语言模型中的对象幻觉检测
Object hallucination in vision-language models (VLMs) is usually measured by checking each object a caption names against the image. Most benchmarks and detectors alike treat every object mention as a claim that the object is present. This assumption fails when a caption names an object without asserting it, as in "a bench for people to sit on", which mentions people without claiming that any are in the image. We call such mentions non-assertive. Treating them as hallucinations inflates the error rates attributed to models that use such language. To separate them from real hallucinations, we introduce NAME-Bench (Non-Assertive Mention Evaluation Benchmark), 25,497 object mentions from captions that five VLMs generate for COCO images. Each mention is labelled concrete (asserted and present), hallucinated (asserted and absent), or non-assertive, and non-assertive mentions are further divided into four subtypes: depictive, purpose, negation and hedged. All the hallucination detectors that we evaluate flag non-assertive mentions as hallucinations, at rates between 14% and 80%. Adding non-assertive mentions to detector training as negatives largely corrects this on LLaVA-1.5. On the four newer VLMs, a comparable correction takes five to ten times more data, and the detectors then catch fewer real hallucinations. These findings show that current detectors conflate mentioning an object with asserting it, and that this conflation is harder to undo on the four newer VLMs than on LLaVA-1.5. Hallucination evaluation should therefore separate what a caption asserts from what it merely mentions.
中文翻译
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）中的对象幻觉通常通过检查图像中标题所命名的每个对象来衡量。大多数基准和检测器都将每个对象提及视为一个主张：即该对象存在于图像中。但当标题提及某个对象却不声明其存在时，这一假设便失效，例如“一个供人坐的长椅”，提及了‘人’但并未声称图像中有人。我们称此类提及为非断言性提及。若将它们误判为幻觉，会夸大使用此类语言的模型的错误率。为将其与真实幻觉分离，我们引入NAME-Bench（非断言性提及评估基准），包含来自五种VLM对COCO图像生成的25,497个对象提及。每个提及被标注为具体（断言且存在）、幻觉（断言且不存在）或非断言性，后者进一步分为四类子类型：描绘性、目的性、否定性和模糊性。我们评估的所有幻觉检测器均将非断言性提及标记为幻觉，错误率介于14%至80%之间。将非断言性提及作为负样本加入检测器训练，可显著修正LLaVA-1.5的错误；但在四个较新VLM上，类似修正需五到十倍的数据量，且检测器随后捕获的真实幻觉反而减少。这些发现表明，当前检测器混淆了‘提及’与‘断言’，且这种混淆在四个较新VLM上比在LLaVA-1.5上更难纠正。因此，幻觉评估应区分标题所断言的内容与仅提及的内容。

Robust Function Calling for LLM Agents Under Interface Drift
应对接口漂移的鲁棒函数调用：大语言模型代理的可靠性增强
Tool-augmented language-model agents rely on interface text: tool descriptions, argument schemas, and the candidate tool list shown at inference time. These interfaces often change after deployment even when the underlying tool semantics remain fixed, creating a practical reliability problem for function-calling systems. We study this failure mode as interface drift and evaluate whether a lightweight, training-free repair layer can recover robustness without retraining the base model. Our method, SchemaShield-Lite, combines schema validation, candidate-set retry for missing tool names, and one-shot constrained repair. On Qwen3.5-9B, it raises DICE-300 accuracy from 0.717 to 0.813 and BFCL-200 accuracy from 0.580 to 0.805, recovering 19/22 and 33/45 drift-induced failures with no observed repair harms. Naive retry does not explain the gains. Ablations show that candidate-set drift, especially distractor tools, accounts for most observed damage, while description and schema edits alone are less harmful. The results position validation-and-repair as a practical robustness layer for deployed LLM-agent tool use.

工具增强型语言模型代理依赖接口文本：工具描述、参数模式以及推理时显示的候选工具列表。这些接口在部署后常发生变更，即使底层工具语义保持不变，这给函数调用系统带来了实际可靠性问题。我们研究这种失效模式为接口漂移，并评估一种轻量级、无需训练的修复层能否在不重新训练基础模型的情况下恢复鲁棒性。我们的方法SchemaShield-Lite结合了模式验证、缺失工具名的候选集重试以及一次性的约束修复。在Qwen3.5-9B上，其DICE-300准确率从0.717提升至0.813，BFCL-200准确率从0.580提升至0.805，分别恢复了22个漂移故障中的19个和45个中的33个，且未观察到修复副作用。简单的重试无法解释性能提升。消融实验表明，候选集漂移（尤其是干扰工具）是造成大部分损害的原因，而描述和模式编辑本身危害较小。结果表明，验证与修复机制可作为已部署LLM代理工具使用的实用鲁棒性层。

Annotrace: A Workbench for Step-Level Annotation of Agent Traces
Annotrace：智能体轨迹逐步标注的工作台
Every study of why LLM agents fail begins by labelling traces by hand, and every such study builds its own throwaway tool. Observability platforms record agent traces but offer no structured labelling; annotation tools label text but have no notion of a step, a delegation or a tool call, leaving it unclear what the smallest unit of execution to label should be. Annotrace reads six trace formats, including OpenTelemetry GenAI, LangSmith and Langfuse, and turns each run into an ordered, nested list of steps. The annotator writes their own labelling codebook; a frontier model proposes a starting set from a sample of their own traces, answers first, and is asked exactly the questions the human is asked, making annotation a joint human-AI process. Annotrace measures agreement between any two annotators, human or AI. On a multi-agent question-answering corpus it reproduces a previous manual effort's 235 annotation units exactly, and two models labelling the same traces agree 91% of the time at $\kappa = 0.82$.
中文翻译
每项关于LLM智能体失败原因的研究都始于手动标注轨迹，而每一项研究都会自行构建临时工具。可观测性平台记录智能体轨迹，但不提供结构化标注；标注工具虽能标注文本，却缺乏对“步骤”、“委托”或“工具调用”的理解，导致难以确定应标注的最小执行单元。Annotrace支持六种轨迹格式（包括OpenTelemetry GenAI、LangSmith和Langfuse），并将每次运行转换为有序嵌套的步骤列表。标注者可自定义标注手册；前沿模型基于其自身轨迹样本提出初始标注建议，以与人类相同的提问方式回答问题，使标注成为人机协作过程。Annotrace可测量任意两位标注者（人类或AI）之间的一致性。在多智能体问答语料库上，Annotrace精确复现了先前人工努力的235个标注单元，且两个模型对同一轨迹的标注一致性达到91%（κ = 0.82）。
👤 Arun Josephraj Arokiaraj, Chenyi Huang, Sahan Bulathwela, Zekun Wu, Bin Wu 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Sample, Mix, or Verify? Allocating Test-Time Compute under Imperfect Verifiers
采样、混合还是验证？在验证器不完美情况下的测试时计算分配
A common way to make language models and agents more accurate is to spend more compute at inference time: generate many candidate solutions and submit the one a verifier prefers. Success then requires that some candidate is correct and that the verifier picks it. The first grows predictably with the number of samples; the second does not, because real verifiers (unit tests, majority votes, LLM judges, reward models) accept wrong answers, reject right ones, and make correlated mistakes. With a fixed budget, a practitioner must decide how many samples to draw, which models to draw them from, and how much to spend on verification, and today these choices are made by rules of thumb. We introduce SPLIT, which makes them from a small labelled pilot. It rests on an exact formula for the probability of submitting a correct answer under accept/reject and top-score selection that accounts for varying task difficulty, blind spots shared across models, and correlated repeated verifier calls. SPLIT fits the formula by empirical Bayes and uses it to plan budgets on unseen tasks. Across seven benchmarks spanning math, science QA, code and software-engineering agents, 26 models, 175 agent systems and six kinds of real verifiers, all from public releases, SPLIT lands within 0.02-0.8 percentage points of the best allocation in hindsight, while every fixed rule we test loses at least 2.5 points in some setting. Regret depends only on how well SPLIT ranks configurations, and it stays near-optimal under compute, price and latency costs and online. The formula also explains when asking more LLM judges lowers precision. As a forecaster far beyond the pilot, SPLIT beats power-law fitting when the verifier caps success, and we report where it loses.
中文翻译
提高语言模型和智能体准确性的常见方法是增加推理时的计算开销：生成多个候选解并提交验证器所偏好的那一个。成功取决于至少有一个候选解正确，且验证器能选出它。前者随采样数量可预测增长，后者则不然，因为真实验证器（单元测试、多数投票、LLM裁判、奖励模型）会接受错误答案、拒绝正确答案，并产生相关性错误。在固定预算下，实践者必须决定采样数量、采样模型来源以及验证投入，而目前这些选择多依赖经验法则。我们提出SPLIT，它通过少量标注的试点数据做出这些决策。SPLIT基于一个精确公式，计算在接受/拒绝和最高得分选择机制下提交正确答案的概率，该公式考虑了任务难度差异、模型共有的盲点以及验证器重复调用的相关性。SPLIT通过经验贝叶斯拟合该公式，并用于规划未见任务的预算。在涵盖数学、科学问答、代码及软件工程智能体的七个基准、26个模型、175个智能体系统和六种真实验证器的实验中，SPLIT在事后最佳分配附近0.02–0.8个百分点范围内表现，而所有测试的固定规则在某些设置下至少损失2.5个百分点。遗憾程度仅取决于SPLIT对配置的排序能力，且在计算、价格和延迟成本变化及在线场景下仍保持近最优。该公式还解释了为何增加LLM裁判数量反而降低精度。作为远超试点的预测器，当验证器限制成功率时，SPLIT优于幂律拟合，我们报告了其失效场景。

Cooperation Without Adaptation: LLM Agents in a Collective-Risk Dilemma
无需适应的合作：语言模型代理在集体风险困境中的表现
Language-model agents are increasingly deployed to act on behalf of people in settings where individual decisions shape shared outcomes. We examine whether five production models adapt their contributions to changing risks and partner behaviour, or instead follow a relatively fixed strategy. In a collective-risk dilemma game (CRG), six players contribute to a shared pool over ten rounds; if the pool falls short of its target, a catastrophe may destroy their remaining resources. Four models contributed approximately an equal share at every positive risk level, and four of the five also contributed when risk was zero, even though contributing could not improve their payoff. Explicitly stating that only the agent's own payoff mattered reduced zero-risk contributions for most models, while a more neutral prompt did not. Several models correctly identified which action offered the higher expected payoff at different risk levels, yet their contributions changed little. Against fixed partners, no model consistently chose the payoff-maximising response, and in mixed-model groups, a single agent's shortfall in the final round could cause an otherwise successful group to fail. Payoff-based selection also depended on population composition: in a single six-agent group, it favoured the model that contributed least and reduced group success, whereas in larger, randomly mixed populations, it favoured an exact fair-share model at moderate and high risk. These findings show that cooperative self-play alone is not evidence of strategic adaptability. Consistent with a machine-behaviour approach, evaluations of agents in multi-agent systems should test how they respond to incentives, partner actions, and population composition.
中文翻译
语言模型代理越来越多地被用于代表人类在个体决策影响共同结果的场景中。我们考察了五个生产级模型是否会根据风险变化和伙伴行为调整其贡献，还是遵循相对固定的策略。在集体风险困境游戏（CRG）中，六名玩家在十轮中向共享资金池贡献；若资金池未达目标，灾难可能摧毁他们剩余资源。四个模型在所有正风险水平下贡献大致相等，且其中四个在零风险情况下也进行了贡献，尽管此时贡献无法提升自身收益。明确说明仅代理自身收益重要后，大多数模型在零风险下的贡献减少，而更中立的提示则无此效果。多个模型能正确识别不同风险水平下更高期望收益的动作，但其贡献变化甚微。在固定伙伴条件下，没有任何模型始终选择收益最大化响应；在混合模型组中，单个代理在最后一轮的不足可能导致本应成功的团队失败。基于收益的选择还取决于群体构成：在单一六代理组中，它倾向于贡献最少的模型，反而降低了团队成功率；而在更大、随机混合的群体中，它在中高风险下偏好公平份额模型。这些发现表明，合作自我对弈本身并不能证明战略适应性。与机器行为方法一致，对多智能体系统中代理的评估应测试其对激励、伙伴行为及群体构成的响应。

Reasoning Reranking with Semantic IDs as the Sole Item Representation
仅使用语义ID作为项目表示的推理重排序
Reasoning rerankers write a rationale before ranking a candidate slate, and existing systems read item metadata to do so, even though generative recommenders increasingly represent items as compact Semantic IDs (SIDs). Ranking from SIDs alone has two appeals: SID prompts are far shorter than the matched item text, and although item codes are fixed from content, the backbone learns their token embeddings in part from user-item interaction sequences, so the tokens can carry behavioral signal that item text lacks. That training targets next-item generation, and whether it supports ranking a supplied slate from SIDs alone is untested. We build ReRankID, a controlled testbed in which models reason and rank from a history and slate shown only as SIDs, varying item rendering, history length, and slate size one at a time. On this testbed, such models often copy the input order instead of reranking, and readable metadata does not repair the failure. We compare supervised fine-tuning, rejection-sampled self-training, on-policy distillation, and verifier-based reinforcement learning as post-training methods and find that they elicit the missing behavior. The strongest post-trained model, the 8B model trained with a graded ranking reward, gives the best SID-only ranking and matches strong text-reading LLMs whose prompts are about 20x longer. It ranks above random with no history at all, where a caption-reading reference does not, and it keeps its gains at long histories, where text prompts pass 50k tokens and the caption-reading reference stops improving. In a paired rationale audit, this model argues trade-offs better than that reference but describes items less accurately. The common post-training methods we test thus elicit ranking from SIDs but not faithful item descriptions, which calls for post-training that improves the reasoning itself, not only the ranking it produces.
中文翻译
推理重排序器在对候选列表进行排序前会撰写推理过程，现有系统依赖项目元数据来完成这一过程，尽管生成式推荐系统越来越多地将项目表示为紧凑的语义ID（SIDs）。仅从SIDs进行排序具有双重优势：SID提示比匹配的项目文本短得多；虽然项目代码由内容固定，但其骨干网络部分通过用户-项目交互序列学习到这些标记的嵌入，因此这些标记可以携带项目文本所缺乏的行为信号。这种训练目标是预测下一个项目，但尚不清楚它是否支持仅从SIDs对给定列表进行排序。我们构建了ReRankID，一个受控测试平台，模型仅通过SIDs查看历史记录和候选列表进行推理与排序，并逐一改变项目呈现方式、历史长度和候选列表大小。在该测试平台上，此类模型经常复制输入顺序而非真正重排序，且可读的元数据无法修复这一失败。我们比较了监督微调、拒绝采样自训练、在线策略蒸馏和基于验证器的强化学习等后训练方法，发现它们能激发缺失的行为。最强的后训练模型——使用分级排序奖励训练的8B模型，在仅使用SIDs的情况下实现了最佳排序性能，并达到与提示长度约20倍长的强文本读取大语言模型相当的效果。该模型在完全没有历史记录时仍优于随机排序，而以标题为输入的参考模型则不成立；同时在长历史情况下保持优势，此时文本提示超过5万token，而标题读取参考模型已不再提升。在成对推理审计中，该模型对权衡的论述优于参考模型，但对项目的描述准确性较低。我们测试的常见后训练方法虽能激发从SIDs的排序行为，却未能提升推理本身的真实性，这要求后训练应聚焦于改进推理能力，而不仅仅是其输出结果。
👤 Zhenwei Tang, Jintao Liu, Di Bai, Xidong Wu, Xinwu Cheng 📅 2026-10-16 🔗 arXiv

Risk and Refusal: Mechanisms of LLM Responses to Suicidal Ideation
风险与拒绝：LLM对自杀意念响应的机制研究
Refusal is a common safety mechanism in large language models (LLMs), yet in contexts of suicidal ideation (SI), refusing to engage can itself constitute a safety failure. We evaluate how eight instruction-tuned LLMs respond to SI and self-harm disclosures across three datasets, varying whether models are informed that a disclosure expresses SI and whether they receive a clinician-developed response protocol, and complement this with mechanistic analyses in six open-weight models. Our results reveal a nuanced relationship between refusal and response-level risk. At baseline, elevated risk in model responses is driven primarily by inattention to the disclosure, alongside refusal to engage. Mechanistic analyses suggest that models may refuse SI disclosures through the same direction that mediates their refusals of harmful requests. However, informing models of the SI context, or suppressing refusal directly, increases engagement without reliably lowering response risk, and may raise it. In contrast, the clinician-guided protocol consistently reduces response-level risk while also reducing refusal. Together, these analyses and ablations motivate the need for models that refuse only when warranted and engage in interactionally safe ways when users disclose SI.
中文翻译
拒绝是大型语言模型（LLMs）中常见的安全机制，但在涉及自杀意念（SI）的情境中，拒绝互动本身可能构成安全失败。我们评估了八种指令微调的LLMs在三个数据集上对SI和自残披露的响应，考察模型是否被告知披露表达SI以及是否接收到临床医生开发的响应协议，并结合六种开源权重模型进行了机制分析。结果揭示了拒绝与响应层面风险之间的复杂关系。在基线情况下，模型响应中的高风险主要源于对披露的忽视以及拒绝互动。机制分析表明，模型可能通过与拒绝有害请求相同的路径来拒绝SI披露。然而，告知模型SI情境或直接抑制拒绝，虽增加了互动频率，但并未可靠降低响应风险，甚至可能提高风险。相比之下，临床医生指导的协议始终能降低响应风险，同时减少拒绝行为。这些分析与消融实验共同表明，需要开发仅在必要时才拒绝、并在用户披露SI时以互动安全方式参与的模型。
