GlobeBench: Evaluating Text-Based World Model Capabilities for Scalable Environment Simulations
GlobeBench：评估文本型世界模型在可扩展环境模拟中的能力
Text-based world models have recently shown potential to simulate complex RL environment state transitions for a diverse set of use cases. However, no standardized benchmark exists to compare, study and improve these models. Through this paper, we attempt to solve this problem by building GlobeBench, a novel benchmark with 1,632 tasks across five separate domains and nineteen simulation skills. The tasks check whether models can predict the environment's response to a specific action given the full agent trajectory by understanding earlier state changes, following environment rules, and predicting the effects of actions on both related and unrelated objects. We compare the predicted environment response with an execution-derived reference, using a hybrid architecture with both deterministic gates and a semantic judge. Among 20 general-purpose LLMs and specialized text world models, GPT-6 Astra achieves the highest task success rate at 49.57%. We find that model accuracy decreases as actions affect a larger portion of the environment. Specifically, when the agent's changes must propagate to dependent objects, task success drops to an average of 5.1% across all 20 models. Although model strengths differ across tasks, 631 tasks remain unsolved by any model, suggesting a significant gap in general LLM capabilities as a world model.
中文翻译
文本型世界模型最近展现出在多样化应用场景中模拟复杂强化学习环境状态转移的潜力。然而，目前尚无标准化基准用于比较、研究和改进这些模型。本文通过构建GlobeBench，试图解决这一问题。GlobeBench是一个新型基准，包含跨五个独立领域和十九种模拟技能的1,632个任务。这些任务检验模型是否能在理解早期状态变化、遵循环境规则的基础上，根据完整的智能体轨迹预测特定动作后的环境响应，并预测动作对相关与无关对象的影响。我们将模型预测的环境响应与执行推导的参考结果进行对比，采用结合确定性门控和语义判别器的混合架构。在20个通用大语言模型和专用文本世界模型中，GPT-6 Astra取得了最高的任务成功率，达49.57%。我们发现，当动作影响环境更大比例时，模型准确率下降。特别是当智能体的变化必须传播到依赖对象时，所有20个模型的任务成功率平均降至5.1%。尽管模型在不同任务上表现出差异性优势，仍有631项任务未被任何模型解决，表明通用大语言模型作为世界模型的能力存在显著差距。

Not All Graphs Are Maps: A Task-Agnostic Exploration Framework for Mobile GUI Navigation
并非所有图都是地图：一种面向移动GUI导航的任务无关探索框架
Modern mobile GUI agents can perceive visible screens and execute local actions, but they still lack persistent app-specific navigation knowledge: where functions live, which pages contain them, and which screens are equivalent across repeated visits. We introduce TAG-Nav, a Task-Agnostic Graph Navigation framework that wraps existing GUI-agent backbones with reusable destination memory. Offline exploration is distilled into compact records of app functions, target pages, and waypoint routes before any evaluated instruction is seen. When a future task's destination is covered, TAG-Nav retrieves the destination and provides route guidance without task-specific demonstrations. This separates mobile GUI destination navigation from full task completion: many tasks share the prefix of reaching the right page, while final actions may enter content, send data, make purchases, delete information, or change sensitive settings. To evaluate route finding without downstream execution confounds, we introduce DestBench, a navigation-only protocol that asks whether an agent reaches the correct pre-action destination screen. DestBench enables a controlled graph-memory study comparing no-memory agents with random, automated BFS, and clean graph memory. Graph-derived memory improves strict navigation success rate of UI-TARS-1.5-7B from 52.4% without graph memory to 58.3% with automated BFS memory, and improves Qwen-VL-Max success rate from 59.5% to 71.4% on the same conditions. Together, TAG-Nav and DestBench show that destination coverage and route cleanliness strongly shape graph-memory value, making graph quality a measured variable rather than an assumed benefit of exploration.
中文翻译
现代移动GUI智能体能够感知可见屏幕并执行局部操作，但仍缺乏持久的、特定于应用的导航知识：功能所在位置、包含这些功能的页面，以及在重复访问中哪些屏幕是等价的。我们提出TAG-Nav，一种任务无关的图导航框架，通过可复用的目的地记忆包装现有的GUI智能体主干。在任何评估指令出现之前，离线探索被提炼为紧凑的应用功能、目标页面和路径点路线记录。当未来任务的目的地已被覆盖时，TAG-Nav会检索目的地并提供路线指引，而无需任务特定的示范。这将移动GUI目的地导航与完整任务完成分离开来：许多任务共享到达正确页面的前缀，而最终操作可能涉及进入内容、发送数据、下单购买、删除信息或更改敏感设置。为了在不受到下游执行干扰的情况下评估路径发现能力，我们引入DestBench，一种仅关注导航的协议，询问智能体是否能到达正确的预操作目的屏幕。DestBench使我们能够在受控条件下对比无记忆智能体与随机、自动广度优先搜索（BFS）及干净图记忆的表现。基于图的记忆将UI-TARS-1.5-7B的严格导航成功率从无图记忆时的52.4%提升至58.3%（使用自动BFS记忆），同时使Qwen-VL-Max的成功率从59.5%提升至71.4%。总体而言，TAG-Nav与DestBench表明，目的地覆盖度和路径清洁度强烈影响图记忆的价值，使图质量成为可测量变量而非探索带来的默认优势。

Are Large Reasoning Models Good Everyday Reasoners? A Comparison with Non-Reasoning Models on Logical Reasoning Tasks
大型推理模型是否是优秀的日常推理者？与非推理模型在逻辑推理任务上的比较
Large reasoning models (LRMs) that leverage test-time computation achieve strong performance on mathematical and scientific benchmarks and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. However, their ability to perform \textit{everyday logical reasoning}, which often departs from textbook formal logic, remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate how well reasoning models align with everyday reasoning patterns by introducing a benchmark based on linguistic and pragmatic reasoning phenomena. We also compare reasoning models with non-reasoning models under controlled prompting conditions. We find that, in many cases, (1) reasoning models show degraded performance in everyday reasoning when such reasoning deviates from formal logical rules, (2) some non-reasoning models with chain-of-thought prompting outperform comparable reasoning models in everyday reasoning, and (3) prompting with additional instructions provides limited control over reasoning behavior. These findings suggest that current reasoning models do not reliably capture everyday reasoning and point to limitations in post-training, motivating improved training objectives and evaluation across diverse forms of reasoning.
中文翻译

Eliciting Specifications: When Does Interactive Clarification Help?
获取规范：交互式澄清何时有效？
LLMs can generate Lean specifications from task descriptions, but those descriptions are often underspecified or ambiguous and may omit verifier-relevant edge cases, yielding imperfect formal specifications. We study whether an elicitation agent (EA) can improve verifier-checked Lean specifications by seeking clarification from a reference-grounded simulated developer before formalization. The agent decomposes the task, identifies unresolved decisions, asks bounded clarification questions, and uses the answers to write a refined natural-language specification that an LLM formalizer then translates into Lean. On 189 Verina Lean 4 specification-generation tasks, we compare single-shot generation with elicitation under question budgets of k=3 and k=10, allowing at most three or ten EA questions per task, across same-model and cross-model configurations. The same-model Claude Sonnet 4.6 pipeline has negative strict pass@1 estimates at both budgets, showing elicitation is not a uniform accuracy booster. In contrast, the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 and Sonnet 4.6-to-Gemini 3.8 Flash pipelines have positive strict estimates. The largest strict result reaches 65.6%, a +6.3-point change (+12 tasks), in the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 pipeline. Six of eight conditions have positive compile-conditional estimates, with a maximum cross-model difference of +4.9 points among compiling outputs. Treatment estimates also differ across the basic and advanced splits; the largest advanced-task change occurs in the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 configuration at k=10, from 42.0% to 50.6%. Failure analysis identifies postconditions as the largest source of degradation. These findings suggest future autoformalization systems should treat elicited natural-language specifications as intermediate artifacts and optimize jointly for semantic correctness among compiling outputs and reliable translation of clarified information into Lean.
中文翻译
LLM可以从任务描述生成Lean规范，但这些描述常存在信息不足或模糊，可能遗漏验证器相关的边界情况，导致不完善的正式规范。我们研究了一种获取代理（EA）是否可通过在形式化前向基于参考的模拟开发者寻求澄清来改进验证器检查的Lean规范。该代理将任务分解，识别未决决策，提出有限制的澄清问题，并利用答案撰写更精细的自然语言规范，再由LLM形式化器转化为Lean代码。在189个Verina Lean 4规范生成任务上，我们比较了单次生成与在k=3和k=10提问预算下的获取策略，允许每项任务最多提出三或十个EA问题，涵盖同模型与跨模型配置。同模型Claude Sonnet 4.6管道在两个预算下均呈现负的严格通过率@1估计值，表明获取策略并非统一的准确率提升手段。相反，Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6和Sonnet 4.6→Gemini 3.8 Flash管道表现出正的严格估计值。最大严格结果达65.6%，较基线提升6.3个百分点（+12个任务），出现在Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6管道。八种条件中有六种在编译条件下的估计值为正，编译输出间的最大跨模型差异为+4.9分。处理效应在基础与高级任务拆分中亦有差异；最大变化出现在Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6配置下k=10时，从42.0%升至50.6%。失败分析指出后置条件是主要退化来源。这些发现表明，未来的自动形式化系统应将获取的自然语言规范视为中间产物，并联合优化编译输出的语义正确性与清晰信息向Lean的可靠转换。

Beyond the Answer: Student-Conditioned Trace Construction for Reasoning Distillation
超越答案：面向推理蒸馏的学生条件化推理轨迹构建
A reasoning trace can accompany a correct answer without providing effective supervision for a particular student. We study how to construct traces that improve learning beyond answer-only supervision by considering both their intermediate content and their accessibility to the target learner. We introduce ReDiST, a student-conditioned trace-construction and distillation framework for evidence-rich reasoning. Starting from the student's own attempts, ReDiST revises answer-correct drafts against the supplied evidence and repairs unsuccessful drafts with additional guidance from the verified answer. A frozen student-matched reader screens and ranks original and edited candidates using answer recovery and verified-answer probability, given the question, evidence, and candidate trace. We distill the selected targets while balancing trace and final-answer supervision with an auxiliary answer loss. Construction and selection occur offline, and inference uses only the distilled student. Experiments on question-answering benchmarks show that ReDiST can improve both source-task and cross-benchmark performance over answer-only, original-trace, and the evaluated trace-selection and rewriting baselines. Across supervision recipes, performance rankings can differ between source and transfer benchmarks, highlighting the importance of evaluating trace teaching utility across distributions.

DLite: History-free Speculative Decoding for Agentic AI
DLite：面向智能体AI的无历史推测解码
Speculative decoding has become a mainstream technique for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference by using a lightweight draft model to generate candidate tokens that are subsequently verified by the target model. However, agentic workloads introduce long and continuously evolving contexts and frequent multi-turn interactions. Under such workloads, we observe that existing draft models suffer from up to an 80% degradation in acceptance length, while maintaining their historical KV cache introduces substantial system overhead. In this paper, we identify the growing historical context as a common source of both problems and introduce DLite, a history-free speculative decoding framework for agentic AI. DLite directly reuses contextual hidden states from the target model and performs local speculative prediction without explicitly maintaining the historical context, transforming an increasingly complex modeling problem into local simple prediction problem. A lightweight sequential head further restores intra-block causal dependencies with low overhead. To effectively train this heterogeneous history-free architecture, we introduce a tailored two-stage training strategy. Experiments on general-purpose, long-context and agentic workloads show that DLite achieves 2-6 acceptance length and outperforms the state-of-the-art speculative decoding method, DSpark, up to 70%.
中文翻译
推测解码已成为通过使用轻量级草稿模型生成候选词并由目标模型验证来加速大语言模型（LLM）推理的主流技术。然而，智能体工作负载引入了长且持续演化的上下文以及频繁的多轮交互。在这些工作负载下，我们观察到现有草稿模型的接受长度最多下降80%，而维持历史KV缓存则带来显著的系统开销。本文识别出不断增长的历史上下文是上述问题的共同根源，并提出DLite，一种面向智能体AI的无历史推测解码框架。DLite直接复用目标模型的上下文隐藏状态，无需显式维护历史上下文即可进行局部推测预测，将日益复杂的建模问题转化为简单的局部预测问题。一个轻量级序列头进一步以低开销恢复块内因果依赖关系。为有效训练这种异构的无历史架构，我们引入了一种定制的两阶段训练策略。在通用、长上下文和智能体工作负载上的实验表明，DLite实现了2-6倍的接受长度提升，并在性能上超越最先进的推测解码方法DSpark高达70%。

The Cognitive Cost of LLM Writing: A Reading-Time Comparison with Human Text
LLM写作的认知成本：与人类文本的阅读时间对比
Although linguistic differences between human-written and Large Language Model (LLM)-generated texts have been extensively studied, their implications for the cognitive effort involved in reading remain less explored. In psycholinguistics, reading time is commonly used as an indirect measure of cognitive processing effort. Building on this perspective, we investigate whether human-written texts and their LLM-generated rewrites exhibit differences in predicted reading time that may reflect differences in processing demands for readers. To this end, we introduce RoBERTa-RT, a model trained on human eye-tracking data to predict word-level reading times. We evaluate the model on both internal and external test sets, construct a paired dataset of 496 human-written articles and their LLM-generated rewrites, and apply RoBERTa-RT to compare their predicted reading times. Our findings show that LLM-generated rewrites tend to be shorter and have slightly lower predicted total reading times, while exhibiting higher predicted reading times per word. These differences are associated with systematic linguistic changes, including longer and less frequent words, greater lexical diversity, and higher contextual predictability. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that LLM-generated writing may redistribute reading demands: reducing the amount of text to process while increasing the predicted processing effort associated with individual words. This highlights the importance of considering not only the linguistic characteristics of machine-generated content, but also its potential implications for human cognitive processing.
中文翻译
尽管人类写作与大型语言模型（LLM）生成文本之间的语言差异已被广泛研究，但其对阅读认知努力的影响仍较少被探讨。在心理语言学中，阅读时间常被用作认知处理努力的间接指标。基于此视角，我们探究人类写作文本与其LLM重写版本在预测阅读时间上是否存在差异，这些差异可能反映读者处理需求的不同。为此，我们提出了RoBERTa-RT，一种基于人类眼动追踪数据训练的模型，用于预测单词级别的阅读时间。我们在内部和外部测试集上评估该模型，构建了一个包含496篇人类写作文章及其LLM重写版本的配对数据集，并应用RoBERTa-RT比较两者预测的阅读时间。研究发现，LLM生成的重写版本通常更短，总预测阅读时间略低，但单位词的预测阅读时间更高。这些差异与系统性的语言变化相关，包括更长且更不常见的词汇、更高的词汇多样性以及更高的上下文可预测性。从认知角度看，我们的发现表明LLM生成写作可能重新分配了阅读负担：减少了需处理的文本总量，但增加了单个词语相关的预测处理努力。这强调了在考虑机器生成内容的语言特征之外，还需关注其对人类认知处理的潜在影响。
👤 Kamer Cekini, Elisabetta Biondi, Chiara Boldrini, Andrea Passarella, Marco Conti 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

GLOBE: Evolving Structured Agentic Memory for E-commerce Product Optimization in Generative Search
GLOBE：面向生成式搜索中电商产品优化的结构化智能体记忆演化
Optimizing product listings for generative search requires language agents not only to rewrite effectively but also to continually self-improve by retaining successful strategies across diverse categories. However, existing approaches often treat structured listings as free-form text, leaving effective strategies implicit in prompts, search trajectories, or rewritten outputs rather than accumulating them as a reusable agentic memory. To address this limitation, we introduce GLOBE, an agentic framework that drives continual self-improvement by storing optimization knowledge in an evolving handbook of explicit, field-scoped behaviors. In each episode, an Editor applies relevant behaviors and receives rank-based feedback, which a Reflector converts into behavior updates. Crucially for dependable execution in real-world settings, a Moderator removes redundant proposals, a Curator commits accepted changes, and consistently harmful behaviors are deactivated. This cycle transforms episodic feedback into a persistent, field-scoped optimization memory. Experiments show that structured behaviors outperform monolithic prompt optimization, while feedback-driven evolution and moderation further improve effectiveness and reduce downside risk. The learned behaviors also remain effective on unseen product categories, suggesting that agentic memory evolution supports interpretable and transferable product optimization.
中文翻译
在生成式搜索中优化产品列表，要求语言智能体不仅能够有效重写内容，还需通过保留跨多样化品类的成功策略实现持续自我改进。然而，现有方法通常将结构化列表视为自由文本，使有效策略隐含于提示、搜索轨迹或重写输出中，而非将其积累为可复用的智能体记忆。为解决这一局限，我们提出GLOBE，一种驱动持续自我改进的智能体框架，通过将优化知识存储在一个不断演化的、显式且领域限定的行为手册中。在每个回合中，编辑者应用相关行为并接收基于排名的反馈，由反思者将其转化为行为更新。关键在于，在真实场景中可靠执行：监管者移除冗余建议，策展人提交已接受的变更，同时对持续有害的行为进行禁用。这一循环将回合式反馈转化为持久的、领域限定的优化记忆。实验表明，结构化行为优于单一提示优化，而基于反馈的演化与监管进一步提升了有效性并降低了潜在风险。所学习的行为在未见过的产品类别上仍具有效性，表明智能体记忆演化支持可解释且可迁移的产品优化。

MARS: Memory-Aware Routing for Session-Level LLM Assistants
MARS：面向会话级大语言模型助手的记忆感知路由
Long-term LLM assistants must repeatedly decide what prior context to expose and which model should answer as an interaction evolves. Existing memory systems and model routers usually make these decisions separately, although memory construction changes the evidence, distractors, and difficulty of the resulting input. We introduce MARS, a memory-aware routing framework that uses session state as a shared control interface between memory construction and model selection. A Query and Topic Manager (QTM) estimates compact decision-relevant state that guides a fixed state-conditioned memory policy and an online router, enabling each turn to receive an appropriate memory view and a suitable model under a quality-memory-cost trade-off. We evaluate MARS on RealMem and MultiChallenge with 16 system configurations each, covering long-history memory-driven interaction and bounded sessions with structured context interference. On RealMem, MARS preserves 98.2% of full-context GPT-4o's QA while using only 14.5% of its generation API cost, including both generation and controller calls for MARS, and achieves the strongest measured memory-recall and retrieval scores among compared systems. On MultiChallenge, MARS trails the strongest static system overall but approaches its performance at lower recorded cost on a mechanism-focused subset. Together, these results support a favorable quality-memory-cost trade-off for long-history, memory-dependent interaction.
中文翻译
长期运行的大语言模型助手必须在交互过程中反复决定暴露哪些先前上下文以及由哪个模型进行回答。现有的记忆系统和模型路由器通常独立做出这些决策，尽管记忆构建会改变输入中的证据、干扰项和难度。我们提出MARS，一种记忆感知的路由框架，利用会话状态作为记忆构建与模型选择之间的共享控制接口。查询与主题管理器（QTM）估计紧凑的决策相关状态，指导固定的条件化记忆策略和在线路由器，使每一轮交互都能获得适当的记忆视图和合适的模型，在质量-记忆-成本之间实现权衡。我们在RealMem和MultiChallenge上评估了MARS，共16种系统配置，涵盖长历史记忆驱动的交互和具有结构化上下文干扰的有限会话。在RealMem上，MARS在仅使用GPT-4o 14.5%的生成API成本（包括MARS的生成和控制器调用）的情况下，保持了98.2%的全上下文GPT-4o问答性能，并在对比系统中实现了最强的记忆召回和检索得分。在MultiChallenge上，MARS整体略逊于最强静态系统，但在机制聚焦子集上以更低的成本接近其表现。这些结果共同支持了在长历史、依赖记忆的交互中具有有利的质量-记忆-成本权衡。

EduWild: How Teacher-Configured Chatbots Fail in Real-World Use and How to Fix Them
EduWild：教师配置聊天机器人在真实场景中的失败原因及改进方法
Teachers increasingly configure educational chatbots through natural-language instructions, but how these bots fail in real-world use remains poorly understood. We introduce EduWild, a dataset of 1,726 conversations with 217 teacher-configured chatbots, each paired with the instructions and configuration behind it, including 900 conversations from the intended end users of 35 deployed chatbots. We develop a 29-code taxonomy covering factual, workflow, diagnostic, and pedagogical failures and apply it using a human-calibrated LLM judge. The most frequent failures are lapses in following teacher intent --- bots skip required workflow steps (53.8% of end-user conversations), omit expectation setup (33.4%), and overload learners with information or questions (37.1%). Guided by these findings, we design a prompt-based harness targeting the six most frequent failure modes. In a study with 200 college students across ten bots, the harness reduces the mean number of target failure codes per conversation from 1.406 to 0.489, a 65.2% relative reduction with bots weighted equally. These findings show how real-world failure analysis can guide practical improvements to educational chatbots.
中文翻译
教师越来越多地通过自然语言指令配置教育类聊天机器人，但这些机器人在真实使用中的失败模式仍不清晰。我们引入EduWild，一个包含1,726次对话的语料库，涉及217个由教师配置的聊天机器人，每条对话均配有背后的指令与配置信息，其中包括来自35个已部署聊天机器人中900次目标用户的真实对话。我们开发了一个包含29个代码的分类体系，涵盖事实性、工作流、诊断和教学法方面的失败，并使用经过人工校准的LLM裁判进行应用。最常见的失败是未能遵循教师意图——机器人跳过必要工作流步骤（占终端用户对话的53.8%）、遗漏期望设定（33.4%）以及向学习者传递过多信息或问题（37.1%）。基于这些发现，我们设计了一种基于提示的约束机制，针对六种最频繁的失败模式。在对十款机器人、200名大学生的实验中，该机制将每轮对话中目标失败代码的平均数量从1.406降至0.489，相对减少65.2%（按机器人加权平均）。这些发现表明，真实世界失败分析可有效指导教育聊天机器人的实际改进。

MuninAI: A Self-Hosted Agentic Framework for Independent Research Groups
MuninAI：面向独立研究小组的自托管智能体框架
Research groups that want a language model grounded in their own literature face a choice between hosted services that receive their unpublished work and self-hosted deployments that keep the data local but give the model no access to the corpus. Munin is an open-source framework for a self-hosted scientific assistant that indexes a group's corpus and its citation graph, and wraps whichever open-weight model the group can serve in an agent harness with specialised reading, search, and computation tools. We claim that the harness, rather than the specific model, is what makes the system useful on domain literature, and we ship the evaluation suite that tests this claim with the software. On 199 paired literature questions, the harness more than doubles the accuracy of the base model, from 0.387 to 0.874 (+0.487, 95% CI [0.407, 0.568]), while naive retrieve-then-answer scores below the unaided model on every backbone tested. Under the harness, roughly twice as many answer claims trace to a passage the model read itself as with fixed top-5 retrieval. When the source paper for a question is removed from the corpus, the system abstains on about nine in ten of the questions it had answered correctly on the Qwen backbones and eight in ten on gpt-oss-20b, and it produces no fabricated local citation across 300 fabricated references. The pattern holds on three backbones from two developers, including a 20-billion-parameter model that runs on a single consumer-grade GPU. With all outbound network access disabled, both Qwen backbones still exceed the published PaperQA2 accuracy. The evaluation suite runs as a single command, so an adopting group can re-run it on its own corpus and model.
中文翻译
希望使用基于自身文献的语言模型的研究团队面临两难选择：托管服务会接收其未发表的工作，而自托管部署虽可本地保存数据，却使模型无法访问语料库。Munin是一个开源的自托管科学助手框架，可索引研究组的文献及其引用图谱，并将团队可部署的任意开源权重模型封装在一个带有专门阅读、搜索和计算工具的智能体外壳中。我们主张，正是这个外壳而非具体模型使系统在领域文献上具有实用性，并随软件附带了验证该主张的评估套件。在199对文献问题上，该外壳使基础模型的准确率从0.387大幅提升至0.874（+0.487，95%置信区间[0.407, 0.568]），而朴素的检索-回答方法在所有测试的骨干模型上均低于未辅助模型。在该外壳下，约有两倍多的答案主张源自模型自行阅读的段落，而非固定前5名检索。当问题来源论文从语料库中移除时，系统在Qwen骨干模型上约九成的问题放弃回答，gpt-oss-20b上约八成放弃，且在300个虚构引用中未产生任何虚假本地引用。这一模式在来自两位开发者的三个骨干模型上均成立，包括一个可在单个消费级GPU上运行的200亿参数模型。在完全禁用外网访问的情况下，两个Qwen骨干模型仍超过已发布PaperQA2的准确率。评估套件可通过单一命令运行，使采用该系统的团队可对其自身语料库和模型重新执行评估。

On the Tip of the Tongue: First-Token Cueing as a Behavioral Probe of Recall Failures in LLMs
舌尖上的记忆：首词提示作为LLM回忆失败的行为探针
Large language models often answer factual questions incorrectly in closed-book settings, and such errors are usually attributed to missing knowledge. Yet models often encode answers they fail to produce. We show that many closed-book errors are first-token recall failures: the model commits to a wrong first token, but once given the right one it completes the answer and often recognizes it as correct. We study this with First-Token Cueing, a behavioral probe that prefills the response with the first token of the gold answer. Model uncertainty peaks at the first answer token, where it already predicts correctness, and among gold prefixes of any length the first token alone accounts for the largest share of recovered errors. Across 16 open models, this one token corrects between a fifth and a half of the errors on multi-token answers in English QA and improves accuracy in all seven languages of a multilingual benchmark. Most of the errors it corrects are also solved by the model's own sampled reasoning, a criterion for encoded knowledge, and models recognize correct starts they do not produce: they continue a correct start more confidently than an incorrect start of the same answer type and prior probability, yet rarely find it without the gold answer. These results locate a recoverable class of factual errors at the first decoding step, where inference-time methods may gain the most.
中文翻译
大语言模型在封闭书设置下常错误回答事实性问题，这类错误通常归因于知识缺失。然而，模型往往编码了其未能生成的答案。我们证明，许多封闭书错误实为首词回忆失败：模型在第一个词上做出错误承诺，但一旦获得正确首词，便能完成答案并经常识别其为正确。我们通过首词提示（First-Token Cueing）这一行为探针进行研究，即用黄金答案的首词预先填充响应。模型不确定性在第一个答案词处达到峰值，此时它已预测答案正确；在任意长度的黄金前缀中，仅首词就占Recovered errors的最大份额。在16个开源模型中，这一词可纠正英文问答中五分之一至一半的多词答案错误，并在七种语言的多语言基准中提升准确率。其中多数被纠正的错误也可通过模型自身的采样推理解决，这是编码知识的标准，且模型能识别自己未生成的正确起始：它们对正确起始的继续信心高于相同答案类型和先验概率的错误起始，但极少能在没有黄金答案的情况下自行发现。这些结果定位了一类可在解码第一步恢复的事实性错误，表明推理时方法在此阶段可能获益最大。
👤 Jaewon Sok, Jewon Yeom, Seonghyeon Park, Jeongjae Park, Hwiyeong Lee et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Think More or Read More? A Token-Budget Theory of Reasoning with Shared Context
多思考还是多阅读？共享上下文下推理的Token预算理论
Reasoning systems increasingly improve answers by incorporating additional context into the model's input, from multi-agent debate, where agents read their peers' responses, to self-refinement, where a model revisits its own drafts. In these systems, communication consumes context as one agent's output becomes another's input. We study the regime where token reading and generation draw on one fixed token window, so every token spent reading is a token that cannot be spent reasoning. We develop a \textbf{token-budget theory} that formalizes this trade-off, modeling accuracy as a function of the reasoning budget remaining after reading and aggregating votes with a Condorcet jury model that permits correlated errors. For multi-agent debate, the theory gives a closed-form prediction for the optimal number of agents. This quantity is estimated from single-agent measurements, allowing the optimal group size to be determined without running debate, and the same accounting extends to a heterogeneous set of different models. We empirically verify our theory and show that, at the predicted group size, our method outperforms strong baselines under the tightest calibration spend, where debate-calibrated rules can afford only one or two questions. Self-refinement obeys the same accounting, with its own draft in place of a peer message. The theory further predicts that communication pays only when the gain in the accuracy ceiling exceeds the reasoning room spent on reading, with this condition tightening as the window shrinks. Across five open models and four reasoning tasks, self-consistency matches or beats deployed debate at $93$\% of $980$ matched window-and-group-size settings, with the gap being largest under tight windows and nearly disappearing at a loose one, consistent with the theory. Under a shared window, how much room a model has left to \textit{think matters more than what it reads}.
中文翻译
推理系统通过在模型输入中引入额外上下文来提升答案质量，例如多智能体辩论（各智能体阅读同伴回应）或自我精炼（模型回溯自身草稿）。在这些系统中，通信消耗上下文——一个智能体的输出成为另一个智能体的输入。我们研究了token读取与生成共享固定token窗口的情形，即每消耗一个token用于读取，就减少一个token用于推理。我们提出了一个 extbf{Token预算理论}，形式化这一权衡：建模准确率作为读取后剩余推理预算的函数，并使用允许相关错误的Condorcet陪审团模型聚合投票。对于多智能体辩论，该理论可给出最优智能体数量的闭式预测，该数量仅需单智能体测量即可估计，无需实际运行辩论，且该方法可扩展至异构模型集合。我们实证验证了该理论，并证明在预测的群体规模下，我们的方法在最严格的校准开销下优于强基线，此时辩论校准规则仅能负担一两个问题。自我精炼也遵循相同逻辑，以自身草稿替代同伴消息。理论进一步预测，只有当通信带来的准确率上限提升超过用于读取所消耗的推理空间时，通信才值得，且随着窗口缩小，该条件变得更严格。在五个开源模型和四个推理任务中，自一致性在980个匹配窗口与群体规模设置中，有93%的情况下表现优于或持平于部署的辩论系统，尤其在紧窗口下差距最大，而在宽松窗口下几乎消失，与理论一致。在共享窗口下，模型剩余的‘思考’空间比它读取的内容更重要。
👤 Manh Nguyen, Sunil Gupta, Hung Le 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

AMEND: Constraint-Guided Multi-Agent Text Detoxification with Isolated Critics
AMEND：基于约束引导的隔离批评者多智能体文本净化框架
Text detoxification requires balancing toxicity removal, semantic preservation, and fluency. Existing methods typically optimize an unconstrained scalar objective---enabling safety scores to mask semantic loss---or rely on open-ended iterative debate prone to semantic drift and high computational cost. We propose AMEND, an inference-time framework that decouples detoxification into role-conditioned proposal generation, isolated multidimensional criticism, and constraint-first selection. AMEND evaluates diverse candidates with role-blind critics and enforces strict semantic and fluency feasibility before ranking by utility. To maximize efficiency, AMEND Lite restricts subsequent search to at most one targeted repair round only when residual toxic spans remain. On ParaDetox, one-round selection over diverse roles improves the joint evaluation metric by $+0.0508$ over the best single role. On a frozen confirmation test set, AMEND Lite achieves a joint score of $0.6597 \pm 0.0062$, outperforming unconstrained multi-round loops by $+0.0489$ ($p < 0.05$) while reducing inference requests by 28.4%.
中文翻译
文本净化需要在去除毒性、保持语义和流畅性之间取得平衡。现有方法通常优化无约束的标量目标——这可能导致安全评分掩盖语义损失——或依赖开放式的迭代辩论，易引发语义漂移且计算成本高。我们提出AMEND，一种推理时框架，将净化过程解耦为角色条件的提议生成、隔离的多维批评以及以约束优先的选择。AMEND使用无角色偏见的批评者评估多种候选方案，并在按效用排序前强制执行严格的语义和流畅性可行性。为提高效率，AMEND Lite仅在残留毒性片段存在时限制后续搜索最多一轮针对性修复。在ParaDetox数据集上，跨角色的一轮选择使联合评估指标提升0.0508，优于最佳单角色表现；在冻结确认测试集上，AMEND Lite获得0.6597±0.0062的联合得分，比无约束多轮循环提升0.0489（p<0.05），同时减少28.4%的推理请求。

What LLMs Do in the Shadows: Reading Context Reliance from the Unembedding Dark Space
大语言模型的隐秘行为：从未嵌入暗空间读取上下文依赖性
Detecting faithfulness hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) is crucial for their safe deployment in high-stakes domains. Recent approaches address this by contrasting contextual against parametric knowledge utilization, yet measuring context reliance typically demands noticeable overhead, such as materializing attention maps, running additional forward passes, or training auxiliary probes. In this work, we show that context reliance can be estimated by projecting hidden states onto the dark space - directions that compose weakly with the unembedding matrix. Beyond its recognized function as an initial-token attention sink, we show that this subspace actively mediates context processing, and that its share of hidden-state norm - termed the Dark Matter Ratio (DMR) - provides a direct score of context utilization. Pairing DMR with a parametric knowledge score yields DarkLens, a lightweight faithfulness detector operating strictly within a single forward pass. Evaluated across three QA and summarization benchmarks on six open-weight LLMs (4B-13B parameters), DarkLens matches or outperforms a wide range of baselines without requiring extra forward passes or probe training.
中文翻译
检测大型语言模型（LLMs）中的忠实性幻觉对于其在高风险领域的安全部署至关重要。近期方法通过对比上下文知识与参数化知识的利用来解决此问题，但测量上下文依赖性通常需要显著开销，如生成注意力图、运行额外前向传播或训练辅助探测器。本文表明，可通过将隐藏状态投影到暗空间——即与未嵌入矩阵弱相关的方向——来估计上下文依赖性。除了作为初始标记注意力汇聚点的已知功能外，我们发现该子空间主动参与上下文处理，其隐藏状态范数占比（称为暗物质比率，DMR）可直接作为上下文利用程度的评分。将DMR与参数化知识得分结合，形成DarkLens，一种仅需一次前向传播即可运行的轻量级忠实性检测器。在六个开源权重LLM（4B-13B参数）的三个问答和摘要基准上评估，DarkLens无需额外前向传播或探测器训练，性能匹配或超越多种基线。

Cross-Centaur: Modeling Individual Decision-Making Across Psychological Tasks with Language Models
跨心理任务建模个体决策行为的语言模型
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as computational models of human behavior. Recent models such as Centaur have advanced this goal by fine-tuning LLMs on trial-by-trial decisions from psychological experiments. However, existing large-scale behavioral datasets do not link the same participants across different tasks, so these models learn population-level regularities but are not trained to model individual-level regularities across tasks. We introduce Cross-Psych-101, a dataset of 591 participants and 261{,}551 valid decisions across 12 Bandit, Tradeoff, Structure, and Social tasks. We train Cross-Centaur to predict a participant's target-task actions from a target-task description and the source-task description paired with the same participant's source-task behavior. Cross-Centaur outperforms off-the-shelf LLMs and Centaur on held-out participants, both within and across tasks. Probing analyses suggest that its hidden states encode participant-level behavioral tendencies that transfer across tasks. These results highlight the potential of cross-task individual-level data for predicting human decision-making with language models. Cross-Pysch-101 is available at this https URL .

Disentangling Language Effects on Tool Admission and Post-Retrieval Safety
解耦语言对工具调用与检索后安全的影响
LLM agents increasingly use retrieval tools across languages, yet their safety evaluation remains English-centric. It remains unclear whether language affects tool admission, post-retrieval generation, or both. We independently vary request, retrieved-page, and required response languages in a controlled URL-retrieval setting across ten models and seven languages. We identify Safety Loss through Interlingual Processing (SLIP). Non-English harmful requests increase mean tool admission from 46.9% to 67.4%, with a positive mean change in every model. This pattern also persists in an autonomous retrieval setup across all four models tested in this setting. Requiring answers in the page language increases pooled harmfulness on matched tool calls from 50.3% to 57.4% across four closed-weight models, mainly in Chinese and Hindi. Translating pages alone produces no consistent harm increase in eight models with comparable inference settings. Automated audits show that a non-harmful response may be a genuine safety refusal, a deflection citing language difficulties, or an unusable answer. Harmfulness labels alone cannot distinguish these outcomes. Multilingual safety evaluations should therefore measure tool admission and response harmfulness separately and distinguish refusal from capability failure.

Prism Attention: Proposal-Refined Index Sharing Mechanism for Efficient LLMs Inference
Prism Attention：用于高效大语言模型推理的提案精炼索引共享机制
Long-context decoding in large language models (LLMs) is increasingly bottlenecked by the memory and computation required to attend over an ever-growing Key-Value (KV) cache at each autoregressive step. Although token-level sparse attention reduces this burden by restricting computation to a small subset of relevant past tokens, its practical gains are often offset by repeatedly recomputing layer-wise sparse indices as the context grows. A common remedy is to share sparse indices across layers by computing them only at designated anchor layers, but such static reuse ignores the layer-wise evolution of attention patterns, causing the reused indices to progressively deviate from the layer-specific optima and incur substantial attention loss. By revisiting cross-layer sparsity from the perspective of attention dynamics, we find that inter-layer salient token shift is highly localized: although the exact top-$\kappa$ indices differ across layers, the core high-attention tokens remain stable, with changes concentrated near the expanded boundary of previously selected regions. Motivated by this, we propose Prism Attention, a proposal-refined sparse attention framework. It replaces rigid cross-layer index reuse with a coarse-to-fine strategy, preserving layer-wise adaptability while retaining the efficiency benefits of cross-layer sharing. Extensive experiments show that Prism Attention achieves state-of-the-art performance among mainstream sparse attention methods on both reasoning and long-context benchmarks, while attaining a $3.4\times$ end-to-end decoding speedup over FlashAttention at 128K context length.

大语言模型（LLM）在长上下文解码中日益受限于每个自回归步骤中对不断增长的键值（KV）缓存进行注意力计算所需的记忆和算力。尽管令牌级稀疏注意力通过限制计算范围至一小部分相关历史令牌减轻了负担，但随着上下文扩展，反复重新计算逐层稀疏索引常抵消其实际收益。常见缓解方法是仅在指定锚定层计算稀疏索引并跨层共享，但这种静态复用忽略了注意力模式的层间演化，导致复用索引逐渐偏离各层最优，造成显著注意力损失。我们从注意力动态角度重新审视跨层稀疏性，发现层间显著令牌偏移高度局部化：尽管各层精确的top-κ索引不同，但核心高注意力令牌保持稳定，变化集中于先前选择区域的扩展边界附近。受此启发，我们提出Prism Attention，一种提案精炼的稀疏注意力框架。它以粗到细策略替代僵化的跨层索引复用，在保留跨层共享效率优势的同时维持层间适应能力。大量实验表明，Prism Attention在主流稀疏注意力方法中于推理与长上下文基准上均达到领先性能，且在128K上下文长度下相较FlashAttention实现3.4倍的端到端解码加速。

Self-Evolving Agents Can Repair Failures but Struggle to Generalize from Them
自我演化的智能体可以修复失败，但难以从中泛化
Experience-driven self-evolution offers a promising path for large language model (LLM) agents to continually improve through interaction. Yet existing approaches often rely on successful trajectories or external evaluation signals, leaving unclear whether agents can evolve when such positive anchors are unavailable. We study this question through Cold-Start Failure-Only Evolution (CFOE), where agents start without accumulated experience and learn only from failed interactions without privileged guidance. Across three self-evolving paradigms, multiple LLM backbones, and four interactive environments, we uncover a consistent repair-generalization gap: failure-derived experience can help repair previously failed tasks, but rarely yields sustained improvements on unseen tasks. Further analysis shows that agents often misinterpret failures, while even verified-correct experience remains difficult to transfer. Relaxing CFOE reveals that verifier feedback and additional interaction provide limited gains, whereas successful trajectories substantially improve transfer. These findings suggest that current self-evolving agents are better at repairing observed failures than building generalizable capabilities from failure.

KiranaBench: Can a Language Model Run a Neighbourhood Shop?
KiranaBench：大语言模型能否经营一家邻里小店？
Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tasks, including autonomously running a business. Existing benchmarks for this setting are almost exclusively English-language, Western in retail context, and single-agent, with the agent observing the business directly. We study how these results transfer to a multi-agent Indic setting. We introduce KiranaBench, a simulated Indian general store in which four roles (manager, cashier, helper, and bookkeeper) are each played by a separate LLM agent. Interactions with customers and suppliers occur in English, Hindi, or Hinglish, and product demand is calibrated to India's national Household Consumption Expenditure Survey. Evaluating four open-weight LLMs, we find that all fall far short of a fixed-rule shopkeeper baseline: the best model earns only one-eighth of the baseline's profit, and two models finish below an unmanaged shop. Performance is also consistently lower for Hindi and Hinglish than for English supplier communication. KiranaBench offers evidence that current LLM agents are not yet reliable for autonomously operating small businesses in India, and provides a testbed for developing agents suited to settings that are long-horizon, collaborative, and multilingual.

Aligning Language Models Towards Counterfactual Invariance
使语言模型对反事实保持不变：一种对齐方法
Small variations in prompt characteristics can meaningfully alter the semantics, tone, and contextual assumptions of large language model outputs. Such characteristics can involve group variables such as demographics or other sensitive latent attributes. Sensitivity to such input variables is undesirable when demographic information is causally irrelevant to the task, but might still be relevant for generation of semantically coherent responses. This makes the task of enforcing counterfactual invariance while preserving model utility difficult. To study this problem, we first show that standard model alignment recipes (such as reinforcement learning) increase differences in response quality across demographics, while improving overall response quality, ultimately amplifying what is known as counterfactual bias. To address this, we develop a policy gradient technique involving a group-level reward function that operates on matched pairs of counterfactual prompts. For completeness, we build a dataset of controlled counterfactual prompt pairs and design an evaluation that separately measures cases where demographic information is causally relevant to the answer. Finally, we validate our findings on several fairness benchmarks. We demonstrate that our methods reduce counterfactual differences while preserving overall response quality and that the models continue to adapt appropriately when such sensitive information is relevant.

Bengali Hallucination Detection with Retrieval and Programs: A System Study
基于检索与程序的孟加拉语幻觉检测：系统性研究
Bengali hallucination detection must distinguish unsupported factual claims from incorrect reasoning, often without a supplied reference passage. We present and characterize an inference-only system that combines response-conditioned query rewriting, hybrid retrieval, and task-specific language-model judging. Six retrieval routes search question-answer and passage collections; a router directs each example to a 27B textual judge or a 14B agent that can compare retrieved answers, reason directly, or generate and execute Python. We characterize the system using version-specific code, saved predictions, execution traces, and corpus metadata from the Olikbochon challenge. Kaggle associates a displayed private macro-F1 of 0.956 with Version 2; its saved run processes 2,516 examples in 220.99 minutes on two T4 GPUs. Of 253 math-routed examples, 88 terminate through candidate matching, while execution traces expose 49 stderr-bearing run events across 35 examples. A later run changes six predictions despite identical core worker code. A bounded audit of 1,090 benchmark-related corpus records finds exact question or context overlap for 38 processed evaluation inputs, precluding an unqualified claim of corpus-independent generalization. The contribution is a concrete retrieval-and-computation judging architecture with an auditable operational characterization, not a controlled estimate of component gains. We distinguish the reported leaderboard result from independently recomputed diagnostics and identify the evidence needed for stronger effectiveness and reproducibility claims.

Uncertainty-Aware Budget Allocation for Efficient LLM Tree Search
不确定性感知的预算分配：高效的大语言模型树搜索方法
Tree search enhances multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet prevailing methods expand every step with a fixed budget, irrespective of the marginal utility of expansion. We first show, across existing search algorithms, that continuations sampled from a shared intermediate state are largely duplicates and collapse to a few distinct answers. Critically, we find that the semantic dispersion of sibling actions predicts the redundancy of their downstream continuations, permitting a subtree's budget to be fixed before expansion. An information-theoretic analysis motivates this: treating the per-step budget as the Monte Carlo sample size for estimating a step's residual answer entropy, we derive a sufficient budget that shrinks with sibling dispersion. We accordingly propose $\textbf{Uncertainty-Aware Allocation}$ (UAA), a training-free framework that decouples value from uncertainty: values determine which candidates are promising, while uncertainty, estimated from sibling dispersion at no additional model cost, determines how many merit expansion, via uncertainty-aware pruning and uncertainty-aware budgeting. Across mathematical reasoning, logical reasoning, and planning benchmarks, UAA reduces token usage by up to $90\%$ and latency by up to $9\times$ at comparable accuracy, generalizing across four tree search algorithms and both open- and closed-source LLMs.
中文翻译
树搜索可增强大语言模型（LLMs）的多步推理能力，但现有方法在每一步都固定扩展预算，不考虑扩展的边际效用。我们首先发现，在现有搜索算法中，从共享中间状态采样的延续内容大多重复，最终收敛为少数几个答案。关键的是，我们发现兄弟动作的语义分散度可预测其后续延续的冗余程度，从而可在展开前确定子树的预算。信息论分析支持这一观点：将每步预算视为估计步骤残余答案熵的蒙特卡洛样本量，我们推导出一个随兄弟分散度增加而减小的充分预算。因此，我们提出$ extbf{不确定性感知分配}$（UAA），一种无需训练的框架，将价值与不确定性解耦：价值决定哪些候选值得探索，而不确定性则通过兄弟分散度估算（无需额外模型开销），决定有多少候选值得扩展，通过不确定性感知剪枝与预算分配实现。在数学推理、逻辑推理和规划等多个基准上，UAA在保持相近准确率的同时，将token使用量减少高达90%，延迟降低最多9倍，且在四种树搜索算法及开源与闭源LLM间具有良好的泛化能力。
👤 Jianguo Huang, Zhiyi Lyu, Fuxiang Zhang, Yanchen Deng, Deheng Ye et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

The Reliance Hypothesis: Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Rises as Language Resource Level Falls
依赖假说：随着语言资源水平下降，思维链忠实度上升
Chain-of-Thought (CoT) faithfulness matters for monitoring, explaining and safely deploying large language models, yet it has been studied mostly in English. Languages differ in level, the amount of text available to learn from, so models know less in lower-resource languages, which may change how much they rely on their reasoning. To our knowledge, we are the first to relate CoT faithfulness to both resource level and task type (knowledge- or reasoning-intensive). On parallel multiple-choice questions from Multilingual Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMMLU) in eight languages, faithfulness rises as resource level falls on knowledge-intensive tasks but not on reasoning-intensive ones, a gap that points the same way across four models and several faithfulness measures. We propose, and find support for, the Reliance Hypothesis: models rely more on their reasoning when they cannot recall the answer, as in lower-resource languages on knowledge-intensive tasks. Model confidence partly explains the effect of resource level, but not the knowledge-reasoning gap. Languages in which models are less confident rely more on reasoning, yet less confident answers within a language do not, a Simpson's paradox. In lower-resource languages, models rely on their reasoning for more answers, not more strongly for each. CoT monitoring may therefore be most informative in lower-resource languages, where it is least often validated.
中文翻译
思维链（CoT）的忠实度对于监控、解释和安全部署大语言模型至关重要，但以往研究主要集中在英语。不同语言在资源水平（可用于学习的文本量）上存在差异，因此在低资源语言中模型知识较少，可能影响其对推理的依赖程度。据我们所知，这是首个将CoT忠实度与资源水平及任务类型（知识密集型或推理密集型）关联的研究。在八种语言的多语言大规模多任务语言理解（MMMLU）平行多项选择题上，知识密集型任务中，资源水平越低，忠实度越高，而在推理密集型任务中则无此趋势，这一差距在四个模型和多种忠实度度量下一致。我们提出并验证了‘依赖假说’：当模型无法回忆答案时（如在低资源语言的知识密集型任务中），会更依赖其推理过程。模型置信度部分解释了资源水平的影响，但无法解释知识与推理之间的差距。在模型置信度较低的语言中，它们更依赖推理，但在同一语言内，置信度较低的答案并不更依赖推理，这是一种辛普森悖论。在低资源语言中，模型对更多答案依赖推理，而非对每个答案的依赖更强。因此，CoT监控在低资源语言中可能最具信息量，尽管这些语言中最少被验证。
👤 Lucas Resck, Jingyi Sun, Qianli Wang, Isabelle Augenstein, Anna Korhonen 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Causal Edit Serialization
因果编辑序列化
Standard autoregressive language models generate text sequentially, but revision requires referring back to tokens in the input context or those already generated. Causal Edit Serialization (CES) is introduced as a framework for expressing such revisions within the same causal token stream. Special pointer tokens are paired with predicted source addresses, allowing insertion, deletion, replacement, copying and movement to be localized explicitly. A lightweight localization head and dual-index rotary position encoding support both address prediction and conditioning on previously emitted pointers, while retaining a standard causal attention mask. A pretraining procedure combines ordinary language modeling with serialized repairs derived from random corruptions, model-generated edits and repeated text. Across model scales and editing baselines, CES models demonstrate complete applied edits with modestly higher clean-text bits per byte than clean autoregressive models. Supervised and reinforcement-learning post-training provide a grammatical-error-correction proof of concept, producing more beneficial than harmful changes under reference-blind model judging. Correct repair programs also improve subsequent text prediction while the original context and its cached states are retained. CES provides a common interface for generation and localized revision, with potential extensions to iterative refinement and additional pointer-addressed transformations.
中文翻译
标准自回归语言模型按顺序生成文本，但修改操作需要回溯输入上下文或已生成的标记。因果编辑序列化（CES）被引入作为在同一流式因果标记中表达此类修改的框架。特殊指针标记与预测的源地址配对，使得插入、删除、替换、复制和移动等操作可被显式定位。轻量级定位头与双索引旋转位置编码支持地址预测和对先前发出指针的条件依赖，同时保留标准因果注意力掩码。预训练过程结合普通语言建模与来自随机破坏、模型生成编辑和重复文本的序列化修复。在不同模型规模和编辑基线中，CES模型实现了完整的应用编辑，仅比干净的自回归模型略高一点的干净文本比特每字节。监督和强化学习后训练提供了语法错误修正的可行性证明，在无参考模型判断下，产生的有益更改多于有害更改。正确的修复程序还能在保留原始上下文及其缓存状态的前提下，改善后续文本预测。CES为生成与局部化修改提供了一个统一接口，具有扩展至迭代精炼及其他指针-地址变换的潜力。

Causal Latent Workspaces for Looped Transformers
用于循环Transformer的因果潜在工作区
Reusing a Transformer block across depth increases computation without replicating its parameters, but successive passes have no dedicated state through which positions can exchange compact summaries of their causal prefix. We introduce the causal latent workspace Transformer: an anchored, four-round shared-depth architecture in which each position writes gated vectors into prefix-specific latent slots and retrieves a content-addressed summary before the next shared block application. This path complements causal self-attention while the original input embedding remains accessible at every round. Across three byte-level corpora, three seeds, and two matched parameter budgets, the proposed model achieves the best mean held-out perplexity in five of six settings against five Transformer controls. At 101.71M parameters it leads on WikiText-103 raw, enwik8, and TinyStories, reaching perplexities 2.7820, 2.7843, and 1.6823; these improve upon the strongest non-workspace control on each corpus by 1.06%, 0.73%, and 1.01%. All nine seed-labelled comparisons at this budget favor the workspace. At 0.918M parameters it leads on WikiText-103 and TinyStories, whereas a conditioned loop is stronger on enwik8. Architectural ablations show that adding the workspace improves upon the anchored loop in all six settings and support refreshing its causal summaries at each round. Together, these results identify a causal, content-addressable prefix workspace as an effective structural augmentation of looped Transformers.
中文翻译
在深度上重复使用Transformer块可在不复制参数的情况下增加计算量，但连续遍历之间缺乏专用状态来让位置交换其因果前缀的紧凑摘要。我们提出了因果潜在工作区Transformer：一种锚定的四轮共享深度架构，其中每个位置将门控向量写入特定前缀的潜在槽，并在下一共享块应用前检索内容寻址的摘要。该路径补充了因果自注意力机制，同时原始输入嵌入在每一轮中仍可访问。在三个字节级语料库、三个种子和两个匹配参数预算下，所提出的模型在六个设置中的五个中实现了最佳平均保留困惑度，优于五种Transformer对照组。在101.71M参数时，它在WikiText-103原始数据、enwik8和TinyStories上领先，分别达到2.7820、2.7843和1.6823的困惑度，相较于各语料最强的非工作区对照组分别提升了1.06%、0.73%和1.01%。在该预算下的九次种子标注比较中，全部支持工作区模型。在0.918M参数时，它在WikiText-103和TinyStories上领先，而条件循环在enwik8上表现更优。架构消融实验表明，添加工作区在所有六个设置中均优于锚定循环，并支持每轮刷新其因果摘要。这些结果共同表明，因果且内容寻址的前缀工作区是循环Transformer的有效结构增强。

[2610.22721]

CoLU: Confidence-Weighted Layerwise Unlearning for Large Language Models
CoLU：基于置信度加权的分层遗忘法用于大语言模型
Large language model (LLM) unlearning aims to forget certain information while preserving others, without retraining from scratch. SoTA methods such as NPO, SimNPO, and SatImp balance forgetting and retain through whole model fine-tuning, while FOM-UL explores selective layer updates. Prior works show that suppressing target information can damage retained knowledge. This trade-off can vary across layers even under the same unlearning loss. In this work, we propose Confidence-Weighted Layerwise Unlearning (CoLU), which combines a novel confidence-weighted forgetting and retain regularization while updating a single selected transformer layer, rather than the whole model for unlearning. To reduce CoLU compute cost, we further propose Brief Layer Screening (BLS), which uses a newly defined normalized forgetting-retention (NFR) score to first rank layers through a brief unlearning trials using a subset of forgetting dataset and fewer epochs. BLS pre-selects top-k ranked layer candidates, which will then be fully profiled using full data analysis. BLS is proven to reduce the runtime of CoLU by 60.2-79.7% with almost the same performance. On TOFU dataset, CoLU improves both forgetting and retention over NPO, SatImp, and GA-based FOM-UL in all six TOFU settings, and over SimNPO in five of the six settings. On MUSE Books and News datasets, CoLU achieves the highest retained KnowMem and the PrivLeak closest to zero among the compared SOTA unlearning methods. CoLU presents a new record performance in both unlearning and retain in most settings up to date. Moreover, across the measured settings, average CoLU GPU memory usage is 77.4% to 79.5% lower than full-model unlearning.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）遗忘旨在忘记某些信息的同时保留其他信息，而无需从头重新训练。现有先进方法如NPO、SimNPO和SatImp通过全模型微调平衡遗忘与保留，而FOM-UL探索选择性层更新。先前研究表明，抑制目标信息可能损害保留知识。这一权衡在不同层之间甚至在同一遗忘损失下也可能变化。本文提出置信度加权分层遗忘（CoLU），在单个选定的Transformer层上更新，而非整个模型进行遗忘，同时结合新颖的置信度加权遗忘与保留正则化。为降低CoLU计算成本，我们进一步提出简短层筛选（BLS），利用新定义的归一化遗忘-保留（NFR）分数，通过少量遗忘数据集子集和较少轮次的快速遗忘试验对层进行排序。BLS预先筛选出top-k排名的层候选，再用完整数据分析进行充分评估。BLS被证明可使CoLU运行时间减少60.2%–79.7%，性能几乎不变。在TOFU数据集上，CoLU在所有六个TOFU设置中均优于NPO、SatImp和基于GA的FOM-UL，在五个设置中优于SimNPO。在MUSE Books和News数据集上，CoLU在保留知识量（KnowMem）方面表现最佳，且隐私泄露（PrivLeak）最接近零。CoLU在大多数设置中创造了最新的遗忘与保留性能记录。此外，在测量设置中，CoLU的平均GPU内存使用量比全模型遗忘低77.4%–79.5%。

ARBOR: Locating Reasoning Failures in Long Chains of Thought through Reasoning State Tracking
ARBOR：通过推理状态追踪定位长链思维中的推理失败
Large reasoning models solve complex problems through long chains of thought, in which prior work has documented failure modes such as overthinking, underthinking, and unsupported deductions. Existing methods detect these failures from local signals: textual similarity to nearby steps, switch tokens, or an LLM's judgment of a step's correctness. However, what a step contributes to the solution depends on the state that earlier steps have established, so identifying failures requires a global view that tracks that state across the trajectory. Therefore, we propose ARBOR, a reasoning-failure attribution framework that represents each trajectory as a tree of reasoning states, built as each step creates, verifies, prunes, or revisits a state, and attributes each failure to a relation between a step and the state preceding it. Using ARBOR, we refine the three failure modes into distinct subtypes that harm reasoning through different mechanisms and at different costs. We find that the tree state ARBOR maintains before a step separates failure steps from the at-risk steps at which the failure did not occur. We discuss how ARBOR integrates with existing mitigations at inference time and in training data. Code, data and interactive examples are available at this https URL .
中文翻译
大型推理模型通过长链思维解决复杂问题，已有研究记录了过度思考、思考不足和缺乏支持的推断等失败模式。现有方法从局部信号检测这些失败：与邻近步骤的文本相似性、切换标记或LLM对步骤正确性的判断。然而，一个步骤对解决方案的贡献取决于早期步骤建立的状态，因此识别失败需要全局视角，即跨轨迹追踪该状态。为此，我们提出ARBOR，一种推理失败归因框架，将每个轨迹表示为推理状态树，每个步骤创建、验证、修剪或重新访问一个状态，将每个失败归因于步骤与其前序状态之间的关系。使用ARBOR，我们将三种失败模式细化为不同的亚型，它们通过不同机制造成推理损害并产生不同代价。我们发现，步骤前由ARBOR维护的树状状态能有效区分失败步骤与未发生失败的风险步骤。我们讨论了ARBOR如何在推理时和训练数据中与现有缓解方法集成。代码、数据和交互式示例可在该https URL获取。
👤 Huihan Li, Siyuan Wang, Naga Vamsi Ramana Dinavahi, Xiaohui Liu, Xiang Ren 📅 2026/10/15 🔗 arXiv

Multimodal Filesystem Memory For Long-Horizon Embodied Agents
面向长时程具身智能体的多模态文件系统记忆
Agents operating over long horizons need an effective memory that allows them to reuse their experience throughout long interactions. If the entire history of an interaction is retained, it becomes difficult to find the relevant experience, while compressing it into summaries may result in the loss of visual and spatial details that are required later on. We present \textbf{MFM}, a multimodal filesystem memory architecture that keeps complementary types of agent memory. \textbf{Episodic memory} stores the observations, actions, and outcomes, and \textbf{spatial memory} associates experiences with locations. \textbf{Semantic and procedural memory} build up general knowledge and reusable methods of acting, and \textbf{working memory} holds the current plans. MFM makes use of searchable textual records to connect these different representations to the underlying multimodal episodes. The agents are able to find previous experiences by means of contextual cues and to examine the original observations in order to recover details that are missing from their textual descriptions. Through consolidation, the agents convert experience into higher-level knowledge and procedures while still keeping references to the original episodes. In the case of long-horizon Minecraft and computer-use tasks, MFM leads to improved performance with three base models. With GPT-6-Astra, MFM completes \emph{Mine Diamond from Scratch} and reaches 16 out of 24 milestones on the \emph{Ender Dragon} task, as against 12.7 for the best baseline. On CUABench-KiCad it attains a mean reward of 0.440, as compared to 0.400 for the best baseline and 0.267 in the case of no persistent memory.
中文翻译
在长时程环境中运行的智能体需要一种有效的记忆机制，以在整个交互过程中重用其经验。如果保留完整的交互历史，将难以找到相关经验；而将其压缩为摘要又可能导致丢失后续所需的关键视觉与空间细节。我们提出 extbf{MFM}——一种多模态文件系统记忆架构，用于保存互补类型的智能体记忆。 extbf{情景记忆}存储观察、动作与结果， extbf{空间记忆}将经验与位置关联； extbf{语义与程序记忆}构建通用知识和可复用的行为方法， extbf{工作记忆}则保存当前计划。MFM利用可搜索的文本记录连接这些不同表征与底层多模态事件。智能体可通过上下文线索查找过往经验，并通过原始观察恢复文本描述中缺失的细节。通过整合，智能体将经验转化为更高级的知识与程序，同时仍保留对原始事件的引用。在长时程的Minecraft和计算机使用任务中，MFM使三种基础模型表现提升。使用GPT-6-Astra时，MFM成功完成“从零开始采矿钻石”任务，并在“末影龙”任务中达成24个里程碑中的16个，优于最佳基线的12.7个。在CUABench-KiCad上，其平均奖励达0.440，高于最佳基线的0.400及无持久记忆情况下的0.267。

ProPerAgent: Toward Memory-Driven Proactive Assistance
ProPerAgent：迈向记忆驱动的主动辅助
As assistants move from reactive answering toward proactive intervention, memory changes role: it no longer only supplies the answer to a query, it determines whether and when to speak at all. Prior work in this setting hands the system the relevant fact in context; we study the regime that makes the problem hard, where the evidence justifying an intervention must be mined from a long-horizon personal memory. We introduce ProPerMem, a benchmark for memory-driven proactive assistance (MPA), pairing egocentric video with year-long, distractor-rich user memories (~618K tokens on average), human-verified trigger windows, and a protocol that scores content and timing jointly. Every baseline we evaluate performs poorly (at most 15%), including frontier models given summarized or retrieval-augmented memory. A controlled study explains why: with oracle evidence delivered inside the trigger window GPT-5.3-Chat reaches 96%; letting it choose the timing drops it to 38%, and replacing oracle evidence with retrieval drops it to 15%. Content and timing are separately necessary, and missing either is catastrophic. Guided by this, we build ProPerAgent (Proactive Personalized Agent) with a plug-and-play harness that surfaces memory from a graph-organized store and gates it down to what is worth interrupting for, plus a lightweight RL stage that curbs early and repeated firing. Without any training, ProPerAgent lifts strict accuracy from below 10% to 37.5% on Qwen3.8-27B and 38% on GPT-5.3-Chat; RL post-training reaches 42.4%.
中文翻译
随着助手从被动应答转向主动干预，记忆的角色发生变化：它不仅提供问题的答案，还决定是否以及何时发声。以往研究在此场景中直接提供系统所需的上下文事实；我们研究更具挑战性的设定，即必须从长期个人记忆中挖掘支持干预的证据。我们引入ProPerMem，一个面向记忆驱动主动辅助（MPA）的基准测试，包含第一人称视频、长达一年且充满干扰项的用户记忆（平均约618K token）、人工验证的触发窗口，以及联合评分内容与时机的协议。我们评估的所有基线表现均差（最高仅15%），包括前沿模型在摘要或检索增强记忆条件下的表现。控制实验表明原因：当oracle证据在触发窗口内提供时，GPT-5.3-Chat准确率达96%；允许其自主选择时机时下降至38%，替换为检索证据后进一步降至15%。内容与时机缺一不可，任一缺失都会导致灾难性失败。基于此，我们构建了ProPerAgent（主动个性化智能体），采用即插即用的框架，从图结构存储中提取记忆并筛选出值得中断的内容，外加轻量级强化学习阶段以抑制过早或重复触发。无需训练，ProPerAgent在Qwen3.8-27B上将严格准确率从不足10%提升至37.5%，在GPT-5.3-Chat上达38%；强化学习后训练可达42.4%。
👤 Shenglai Zeng, Xiao Yang, Tiffany Ma, Xinyuan Zhang, Nanshu Wang et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22518]
agent-memory
Learning User Representations to Improve Simulation Fidelity
学习用户表征以提升仿真保真度
User simulators enable interactive training and evaluation of AI assistants at scale. Yet current simulators struggle to reproduce the diverse behaviors of real users, leading to assistants that generalize poorly to underrepresented users. We identify user representations as an underexplored bottleneck in user simulation. User representations are natural-language descriptions that condition simulators to reproduce a particular user's behavior. A representation's quality becomes apparent in hindsight, through the behavior it induces in a simulator. To evaluate and optimize user representations, we formulate a conversation reconstruction task: given an original conversation, we generate a representation, condition a simulator on it, simulate a conversation, and then measure fidelity by comparing user behavior in the original and simulated conversations. This comparison also yields hindsight feedback, which identifies where the representation falls short and how it can be improved. We introduce hindsight distillation, an online training procedure that distills feedback-conditioned improvements into the representation generator, enabling it to generate improved representations directly from the original conversation. We present UserRepGen-4B, a representation generator trained with hindsight distillation. Across 700 conversations spanning seven datasets, hindsight feedback helps improve user representations at test time, and UserRepGen-4B generates representations that enable more faithful simulation than those generated by GPT-5.5. Beyond conversation reconstruction, learned representations improve simulation fidelity for unseen tasks, better preserve the population-level diversity of real users, and better distinguish users across conversations.
中文翻译
用户模拟器使得大规模交互式AI助手训练与评估成为可能。然而，当前模拟器难以复现真实用户的多样化行为，导致助手在未充分代表的用户群体上泛化能力差。我们识别出用户表征是用户模拟中一个被忽视的关键瓶颈。用户表征是自然语言描述，用于引导模拟器重现特定用户的行为。表征质量只有在事后才能显现，通过其在模拟器中引发的行为体现。为评估和优化用户表征，我们提出一种对话重建任务：给定原始对话，生成表征，将其作为条件输入模拟器，生成模拟对话，并通过比较原始与模拟对话中的用户行为来衡量保真度。该比较还提供事后反馈，揭示表征的不足之处并指导改进。我们引入事后蒸馏（hindsight distillation），一种在线训练方法，将反馈驱动的改进蒸馏到表征生成器中，使其能直接从原始对话生成优化后的表征。我们提出了UserRepGen-4B，一个采用事后蒸馏训练的表征生成器。在涵盖七个数据集的700个对话中，事后反馈有助于测试时改进用户表征，且UserRepGen-4B生成的表征使模拟保真度优于GPT-5.5生成的表征。除对话重建外，学习到的表征还能提升未见任务的模拟保真度，更好地保留真实用户群体层面的多样性，并更准确地区分不同对话中的用户。

Beyond the Benchmark: Conditioning, Domains and Failure Modes of Qwen3-TTS
超越基准：Qwen3-TTS的条件设置、领域适应与失败模式
Qwen3-TTS reports state-of-the-art zero-shot voice cloning, but its technical report leaves open how the model should be conditioned, how it behaves outside read speech, and how it fails. We characterise its two cloning interfaces, in-context learning (ICL) and X-Vector, against Chatterbox, XTTS-v2 and F5-TTS on read, meeting, emotional and air-traffic-control (ATC) speech, using eighteen objective measures, an LLM judge, a spoofing countermeasure and a listening test. Neither interface dominates: ICL clones timbre more closely from correctly transcribed references, X-Vector clones timing, and ICL degrades sharply when a short reference transcript is wrong. Domain explains four times more variance than system choice. Qwen3-TTS leads on intelligibility and pitch register but trails on prosody, and the objective measures rank systems differently from listeners. An off-the-shelf countermeasure flags its clones no more often than genuine recordings, and on ATC radio it fails for every system. Code and audio are released.
中文翻译
Qwen3-TTS报告了最先进的零样本语音克隆性能，但其技术报告未明确说明模型应如何进行条件设置、在非朗读语音场景下的行为表现以及失败情况。我们针对Qwen3-TTS的两种克隆接口——上下文学习（ICL）和X-Vector，与Chatterbox、XTTS-v2和F5-TTS在朗读、会议、情绪及航空交通管制（ATC）语音场景下进行了对比评估，使用十八项客观指标、LLM裁判、欺骗检测反制措施和听觉测试。结果显示两种接口无明显优劣：ICL在参考转录正确时更贴近音色克隆，X-Vector在时间特征克隆上表现更好，而当短参考转录错误时，ICL性能急剧下降。领域因素解释的方差是系统选择的四倍。Qwen3-TTS在可懂度和音高范围上领先，但在韵律方面落后；客观指标对系统的排序与听觉判断不一致。一款现成的反制措施并未比真实录音更频繁标记其克隆结果；在ATC无线电场景中，所有系统均出现失败。代码与音频已公开。

PresenceBench: An Exploratory Evaluation of Large Language Model Adaptation to User State
PresenceBench：大语言模型对用户状态适应性的探索性评估
Presence-aware intelligence is the capacity of an AI system's intelligence layer to adapt appropriately to the functional state of the person it serves (here readiness, flow, and stability) while preserving task quality and safety. We introduce PresenceBench, an instrument for measuring it. PresenceBench evaluates how the intelligence layer uses a supplied state, not how that state is sensed, and breaks the capability into measurable properties. Two are tested here. Receptivity: does the model take up the state and give a better response than with no signal? Specificity: does any improvement depend on the correct state, rather than on extra structured context? A third, adaptation across states, is a required endpoint not measured in this release. A five-arm causal design answers both questions, against each model's own baseline and an equally structured mismatched-state control, scored by a correctness-gated human paired comparison. The exploratory evaluation covered four models across eighteen single-turn, six fixed three-turn and six simulated natural-conversation scenarios. Supplying a synthetic state, raw or translated into a response policy, gave human-preference point estimates above 0.5 for all four models (Claude Sonnet 5, DeepSeek V4 Pro, Gemini 3.6 Flash, GPT-5.6 Terra), with three of eight 95% intervals excluding chance. Specificity was not resolved: no per-model interval excluded chance, and the design cannot yet separate the state values from a generic instruction to adapt. Policy delivery reduced priced cost and output tokens against each model's baseline; these are resource-use changes, not efficiency gains, because correctness non-inferiority was not established. Safety-floor failures were rare (13 of 360 cases), with a non-significant excess under policy delivery. We report the instrument, the frozen results, and the controls a confirmatory study needs.

具备感知存在感的智能是AI系统智能层能够根据服务对象的功能状态（如准备就绪、心流状态、稳定状态）做出适当适应，同时保持任务质量与安全性。我们引入PresenceBench，一种用于测量该能力的工具。PresenceBench评估智能层如何利用提供的状态信号，而非状态的感知方式，并将该能力分解为可测量属性。本文测试了两个属性：响应性——模型是否采纳状态信号并给出优于无信号时的回应？特异性——任何改进是否依赖于正确的状态，而非额外的结构化上下文？第三个属性“跨状态适应”作为必要终点，尚未在此版本中测量。采用五臂因果设计，对比每种模型自身基线与结构匹配的错位状态控制组，由正确性门控的人类配对比较评分。探索性评估覆盖四种模型，涵盖十八个单轮、六个固定三轮及六个模拟自然对话场景。提供合成状态信号（原始或翻译为响应策略），所有四种模型的人类偏好点估计均高于0.5（Claude Sonnet 5、DeepSeek V4 Pro、Gemini 3.6 Flash、GPT-5.6 Terra），其中八组中有三组95%置信区间排除了随机可能性。特异性未获解决：无一模型的置信区间排除随机，且当前设计尚无法将状态值与通用适应指令区分开。策略传递降低了每种模型的定价成本和输出token数，但这是资源使用变化而非效率提升，因未建立正确性非劣性。安全底线失败罕见（360例中13例），策略传递下的超额也无统计显著性。我们报告了该工具、冻结结果及确认性研究所需控制条件。

Execution Before Memory: Diagnosing Memory Failures in Video World Models
执行先于记忆：诊断视频世界模型中的记忆失败
A memory test for a video world model should say whether the scene was set, whether the asked change was carried out, and whether the last frame shows the result. Current tests fold these into one number, so a low score is easy to read as forgetting. When the change is not carried out, the hidden state transition cannot appear, so the test is not really a test of memory. We introduce MIST, an evaluation that separates the change from the memory it should leave behind. The model first sets the scene while the change stays hidden, and only later is asked to show it, with leakage before that point kept under control. This separation locates the bottleneck: the model sets the scene but does not carry out the instructed change. The same gap appears in general memory evaluations. On that basis we propose M3-Agent (Make the Missing Move), which recursively revises the details of the input instruction so that the model successfully carries out the requested instruction. Relative to the baseline, it more than triples the MIST reveal score. On the benchmarks, WRBench's score T rises by 26.1% and MBench-T's score U by 52.7%. The method meets the problem from the execution side: it shows what the frozen model can do once the move is stated, and it sets a stricter baseline.
中文翻译
对视频世界模型的记忆测试应判断场景是否设定、所提变更是否执行、最后一帧是否显示结果。现有测试将这些合并为单一数值，导致低分易被解读为遗忘。当变更未执行时，隐藏状态转移无法出现，因此测试并非真正意义上的记忆测试。我们提出MIST评估方法，将变更与其应留下的记忆分离。模型首先设定场景而隐藏变更，仅在后期才被要求展示，且在之前阶段控制信息泄露。这种分离揭示了瓶颈：模型能设定场景但未能执行指令变更。这一差距也出现在通用记忆评估中。基于此，我们提出M3-Agent（完成缺失动作），通过递归修正输入指令细节，使模型成功执行请求指令。相比基线，其MIST揭示得分提升超过三倍。在基准测试中，WRBench的T得分提升26.1%，MBench-T的U得分提升52.7%。该方法从执行角度解决问题：展示冻结模型在指令明确后的表现，并设立更严格的基线。
👤 Weiyang Jin, Jiwen Yu, Sihan Xu, Yuwei Niu, Xihui Liu 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Self-Reflective Chain-of-Thought (SRCoT): Evidence-Based Text Generation via a Closed-Loop Retrieve--Reflect--Revise Pipeline on Edge LLMs
自反思思维链（SRCoT）：通过边缘大模型上的闭环检索-反思-修订流程实现基于证据的文本生成
Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent outputs that lack source support or conflict with local operational constraints. We introduce Self-Reflective Chain-of-Thought (SRCoT), a modular architecture that organizes generation as a closed Retrieve--Reflect--Revise loop. SRCoT separates environment state, persistent knowledge, accumulated experience, and short-term memory into provenance-labeled stores. BM25 retrieval supplies local context; subsequent passes expose intermediate drafts, missing-information markers, follow-up queries, and optional claim--evidence mappings. These artifacts inform retrieval, revision, clarification, or abstention decisions. We examine the architecture through qualitative, trace-based case studies in two fully synthetic settings: emergency medical services and Munich-Sim, an emergency-operations simulation. Local prototype runs on an Apple M4-family laptop with 128 GB memory illustrate source-labeled documentation, explicit gap marking, and structured dispatch-style outputs. The traces also reveal provenance-assignment errors, incomplete evidence coverage, and unsafe assumptions that remain possible despite prompting constraints. These cases demonstrate report-level inspectability and local execution, not comparative superiority, hallucination reduction, clinical validity, or operational safety. The architecture separates retrieval and control from the model interface, but behavior across different models remains to be evaluated. SRCoT is a research proof of concept requiring human review, with its principal contribution lying in an inspectable control procedure rather than a new model.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）能够生成流畅的输出，但这些输出可能缺乏来源支持或与本地操作约束冲突。我们提出了自反思思维链（SRCoT），一种模块化架构，将生成过程组织为一个闭环的检索-反思-修订循环。SRCoT将环境状态、持久知识、累积经验与短期记忆分离到带有出处标签的存储中。BM25检索提供本地上下文；后续迭代暴露中间草稿、信息缺失标记、后续查询以及可选的论点-证据映射。这些产物用于指导检索、修订、澄清或放弃决策。我们在两个完全合成的场景中通过定性与追踪案例研究考察该架构：紧急医疗服务和Munich-Sim（一个应急操作模拟系统）。在配备128 GB内存的Apple M4系列笔记本上运行的本地原型展示了带出处标注的文档、显式的空白标记以及结构化的调度式输出。追踪结果还揭示了出处分配错误、证据覆盖不全以及尽管有提示约束仍可能存在的不安全假设。这些案例展示了报告级别的可检查性与本地执行能力，而非比较优势、幻觉减少、临床有效性或操作安全性。该架构将检索与控制从模型接口中分离，但不同模型间的行为仍有待评估。SRCoT是一个需要人工审查的研究概念验证，其主要贡献在于可检查的控制流程，而非新模型。

Annotrace: A Workbench for Step-Level Annotation of Agent Traces
Annotrace：智能体轨迹逐步标注的工作台
Every study of why LLM agents fail begins by labelling traces by hand, and every such study builds its own throwaway tool. Observability platforms record agent traces but offer no structured labelling; annotation tools label text but have no notion of a step, a delegation or a tool call, leaving it unclear what the smallest unit of execution to label should be. Annotrace reads six trace formats, including OpenTelemetry GenAI, LangSmith and Langfuse, and turns each run into an ordered, nested list of steps. The annotator writes their own labelling codebook; a frontier model proposes a starting set from a sample of their own traces, answers first, and is asked exactly the questions the human is asked, making annotation a joint human-AI process. Annotrace measures agreement between any two annotators, human or AI. On a multi-agent question-answering corpus it reproduces a previous manual effort's 235 annotation units exactly, and two models labelling the same traces agree 91% of the time at $\kappa = 0.82$.
中文翻译
每项关于LLM智能体失败原因的研究都始于手动标注轨迹，而每一项研究都会自行构建临时工具。可观测性平台记录智能体轨迹，但不提供结构化标注；标注工具虽能标注文本，却缺乏对“步骤”、“委托”或“工具调用”的理解，导致难以确定应标注的最小执行单元。Annotrace支持六种轨迹格式（包括OpenTelemetry GenAI、LangSmith和Langfuse），并将每次运行转换为有序嵌套的步骤列表。标注者可自定义标注手册；前沿模型基于其自身轨迹样本提出初始标注建议，以与人类相同的提问方式回答问题，使标注成为人机协作过程。Annotrace可测量任意两位标注者（人类或AI）之间的一致性。在多智能体问答语料库上，Annotrace精确复现了先前人工努力的235个标注单元，且两个模型对同一轨迹的标注一致性达到91%（κ = 0.82）。
👤 Arun Josephraj Arokiaraj, Chenyi Huang, Sahan Bulathwela, Zekun Wu, Bin Wu 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

agent-memoryagentic-rl
Reasoning Models Can Control Their Chains of Thought When They Tell Themselves To
当推理模型告诉自己时，它们可以控制自己的思维链
Chain-of-thought (CoT) controllability, the ability of reasoning models to control properties of their CoT, is consequential for both their usefulness and reliable oversight. Recent work evaluates this capability by measuring whether models follow CoT constraints given in the user prompt, and reports very low controllability across models. We argue that this low controllability may not reflect an inherent inability of reasoning models to control their CoT, but instead result from naively specifying constraints in the user prompt. To test this, we introduce Self-Instructed CoT Controllability: a complementary evaluation that places the constraint as a self-instruction inside the model's CoT and measures its ability to follow it. We show that, under self-instruction, models achieve significantly higher controllability than with standard user instructions. Even a generic reminder to follow the user's constraint substantially improves controllability. Moreover, effective self-instructions can be bootstrapped into the reasoning through a simple instruction in the user prompt, enabling improved control even without access to the reasoning channel. Finally, using self-instruction, we demonstrate in a realistic monitoring setup that CoT controllability can be leveraged for monitor evasion, posing a risk to CoT monitorability.
中文翻译
思维链（CoT）可控性，即推理模型控制其思维链属性的能力，对模型的实用性与可靠监控至关重要。近期研究通过测量模型是否遵循用户提示中给出的CoT约束来评估这一能力，并报告各模型普遍表现出极低的可控性。我们认为这种低可控性可能并非源于推理模型本身固有的能力缺陷，而是由于在用户提示中天真地指定约束所致。为验证此观点，我们引入自指令式CoT可控性评估：将约束作为自我指令置于模型的思维链内部，并测量其遵循能力。我们发现，在自指令条件下，模型的可控性显著高于标准用户指令。即使只是一个简单的提醒以遵循用户约束，也能大幅提升可控性。此外，有效的自指令可通过用户提示中的简单指令进行引导，从而在无需访问推理通道的情况下实现改进控制。最后，利用自指令，我们在一个真实的监控设置中演示了CoT可控性可用于规避监控，对CoT可监控性构成风险。

SAFE-Mem: Secure Agentic Memory with Fine-Grained Authority and Governance-Aware Retrieval
SAFE-Mem：细粒度权限与治理感知检索的智能体安全记忆
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on persistent memory to support long-term reasoning and personalized interactions. However, information collected from external sources can introduce security risks when untrusted claims (i.e., atomic statements) are later treated as authorized preferences, policies, or permissions. Existing defenses typically rely on content-based filtering or coarse-grained provenance tracking, which either fail to prevent unauthorized memory use or block legitimate information, sacrificing memory utility. To address these limitations, we propose SAFE-Mem, a fine-grained memory governance framework that improves security while preserving legitimate memory use. First, SAFE-Mem annotates each claim with three governance labels (i.e., kind, authority, and scope) to capture its provenance and permitted use. Second, governance-aware retrieval matches query requirements with claim permissions to block unauthorized influence while retaining legitimate information. Third, a governance-enabled memory graph connects source sentences, claims, and entities to support provenance tracking and efficient retrieval. Across four LLM backbones on MPBench and LoCoMo, SAFE-Mem attains the lowest attack success among defenses that retain legitimate memory, at least 77% below the strongest of them, and the highest answer accuracy of all evaluated defenses, improving it by up to 15%. The code is available at this https URL .

Cooperation Without Adaptation: LLM Agents in a Collective-Risk Dilemma
无需适应的合作：语言模型代理在集体风险困境中的表现
Language-model agents are increasingly deployed to act on behalf of people in settings where individual decisions shape shared outcomes. We examine whether five production models adapt their contributions to changing risks and partner behaviour, or instead follow a relatively fixed strategy. In a collective-risk dilemma game (CRG), six players contribute to a shared pool over ten rounds; if the pool falls short of its target, a catastrophe may destroy their remaining resources. Four models contributed approximately an equal share at every positive risk level, and four of the five also contributed when risk was zero, even though contributing could not improve their payoff. Explicitly stating that only the agent's own payoff mattered reduced zero-risk contributions for most models, while a more neutral prompt did not. Several models correctly identified which action offered the higher expected payoff at different risk levels, yet their contributions changed little. Against fixed partners, no model consistently chose the payoff-maximising response, and in mixed-model groups, a single agent's shortfall in the final round could cause an otherwise successful group to fail. Payoff-based selection also depended on population composition: in a single six-agent group, it favoured the model that contributed least and reduced group success, whereas in larger, randomly mixed populations, it favoured an exact fair-share model at moderate and high risk. These findings show that cooperative self-play alone is not evidence of strategic adaptability. Consistent with a machine-behaviour approach, evaluations of agents in multi-agent systems should test how they respond to incentives, partner actions, and population composition.
中文翻译
语言模型代理越来越多地被用于代表人类在个体决策影响共同结果的场景中。我们考察了五个生产级模型是否会根据风险变化和伙伴行为调整其贡献，还是遵循相对固定的策略。在集体风险困境游戏（CRG）中，六名玩家在十轮中向共享资金池贡献；若资金池未达目标，灾难可能摧毁他们剩余资源。四个模型在所有正风险水平下贡献大致相等，且其中四个在零风险情况下也进行了贡献，尽管此时贡献无法提升自身收益。明确说明仅代理自身收益重要后，大多数模型在零风险下的贡献减少，而更中立的提示则无此效果。多个模型能正确识别不同风险水平下更高期望收益的动作，但其贡献变化甚微。在固定伙伴条件下，没有任何模型始终选择收益最大化响应；在混合模型组中，单个代理在最后一轮的不足可能导致本应成功的团队失败。基于收益的选择还取决于群体构成：在单一六代理组中，它倾向于贡献最少的模型，反而降低了团队成功率；而在更大、随机混合的群体中，它在中高风险下偏好公平份额模型。这些发现表明，合作自我对弈本身并不能证明战略适应性。与机器行为方法一致，对多智能体系统中代理的评估应测试其对激励、伙伴行为及群体构成的响应。

Veil-Then-Reveal: Protecting PII Privacy via Reversible Pseudo-Anonymization in Zero-Trust User--LLM Interaction
遮蔽再揭示：通过可逆伪匿名化保护零信任用户-LLM交互中的PII隐私
Large language models (LLMs) increasingly process sensitive documents and personal information, yet sending prompts containing personally identifiable information (PII) to remote providers exposes data subjects and organizations to risks from logging, retention, model training, and unintended disclosure. Existing anonymization methods often mask or remove PII, while conventional encryption eliminates the lexical and structural cues needed for effective LLM processing. We present Veil-then-Reveal (VtR), a user-side PII pseudo-anonymization framework for privacy-preserving LLM interaction under a zero-trust threat model. VtR treats remote LLM providers as untrusted and sends only pseudo-anonymized prompts, keeping plaintext PII on the user. VtR first detects PII and replaces it with realistic, same-type, in-vocabulary surrogates before prompts leave the user's device, preserving their linguistic and structural properties. It combines format-preserving encryption (FPE) for structured PII, such as phone and credit-card numbers, with a keyed-hash dictionary and secure vault for semantic PII, such as names, locations, and organizations. Session-specific mappings provide cross-session unlinkability, while user-side restoration reversibly replaces surrogates with their original values in LLM responses. We further introduce a rank-class FPE strategy for dates that preserves format and ambiguity while reducing cipher evaluations. Evaluation on three datasets containing 11,322 annotated PII instances achieves 97.9% detection recall, 95.2% protection of detected values, and 99.9% round trip; 100% of echoed surrogates end-to-end round-trip restoration.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地处理敏感文档和个人信息，但将包含个人身份信息（PII）的提示发送给远程提供商，会使数据主体和组织面临日志记录、数据保留、模型训练和意外披露等风险。现有匿名化方法通常掩码或删除PII，而传统加密则消除了LLM有效处理所需的词汇和结构线索。我们提出Veil-then-Reveal（VtR），一种面向零信任威胁模型的用户端PII伪匿名化框架，用于隐私保护的LLM交互。VtR将远程LLM提供商视为不可信，仅发送伪匿名化提示，将明文PII保留在用户端。VtR首先检测PII，并用真实、同类型、在词汇内的替代品替换，确保其语言和结构特性不变。它结合格式保持加密（FPE）用于结构化PII（如电话号码、信用卡号），以及密钥哈希字典与安全保险库用于语义PII（如姓名、地点、组织）。会话特定映射提供跨会话不可链接性，而用户端还原可逆地将替代品替换为原始值。我们还引入一种等级分类FPE策略用于日期，既保持格式和模糊性，又减少密文计算量。在包含11,322个标注PII实例的三个数据集上的评估结果显示，检测召回率达97.9%，已检测值保护率为95.2%，往返准确率达99.9%；100%的回显替代品实现了端到端的完整还原。
👤 Mohamed Shaaban, Mohamed Elmahallawy, Thomas E. Carroll 📅 2026-10-16 🔗 arXiv
