agent-memoryagentic-rl
A misinformation playbook sharpens judgement without breeding cynicism
虚假信息手册可增强判断力而不引发犬儒主义
Misinformation harms public health and democracy, and generative artificial intelligence now writes falsehoods as persuasive as people's. Defences range from fact-checking individual claims to training people to resist falsehoods. Yet many claims are too new to check, and interventions that teach doubt can reduce belief in truth too. We propose a defence that targets not the facts of each claim but the persuasion strategies that recur across them. Here we show that learning misinformation's persuasion playbook, mapped from 214,646 fact-checked claims in 51 languages, can sharpen human and machine judgement of claims they know little about, without making either more cynical about true claims. The playbook spans social influence, information manipulation and emotional appeals, combined around a core of fear, conspiracy and fabrication. Briefed with it, plus a reminder that persuasion is not proof of falsehood, a language model better distinguished true from machine-generated false claims about events after its training data. In a pre-registered experiment, the same briefing improved people's discernment of claims they reported as unfamiliar and left their belief in true claims unchanged. Averaged over all claims, the gain was not statistically significant. Because it targets what recurs, this defence need not wait for claim-specific fact-checks.
中文翻译
虚假信息损害公共健康与民主，而生成式人工智能如今能写出与人类同样有说服力的谎言。防御手段包括逐条事实核查或训练人们抵制虚假信息。然而，许多信息过于新颖无法核查，而教导怀疑态度的干预措施也可能削弱人们对真相的信任。我们提出一种防御策略，不针对每条信息的事实，而是聚焦于贯穿其中的重复性说服策略。本文展示，通过分析51种语言中214,646条经核实的虚假信息，构建出的虚假信息说服手册，能够提升人类和机器对不了解信息的判断力，同时不会使两者对真实信息更加怀疑。该手册涵盖社会影响、信息操纵和情感诉求，核心围绕恐惧、阴谋论和虚构展开。在简要介绍该手册并提醒‘说服不等于虚假’后，语言模型能更好地区分真实与训练数据之后生成的虚假事件陈述。在一项预先注册的实验中，相同的信息简报提高了人们对陌生信息的辨别力，且未改变他们对真实信息的信念。总体来看，该增益在统计上不显著。由于其针对的是共性模式，这种防御无需等待具体信息的事实核查即可实施。

Risk and Refusal: Mechanisms of LLM Responses to Suicidal Ideation
风险与拒绝：LLM对自杀意念响应的机制研究
Refusal is a common safety mechanism in large language models (LLMs), yet in contexts of suicidal ideation (SI), refusing to engage can itself constitute a safety failure. We evaluate how eight instruction-tuned LLMs respond to SI and self-harm disclosures across three datasets, varying whether models are informed that a disclosure expresses SI and whether they receive a clinician-developed response protocol, and complement this with mechanistic analyses in six open-weight models. Our results reveal a nuanced relationship between refusal and response-level risk. At baseline, elevated risk in model responses is driven primarily by inattention to the disclosure, alongside refusal to engage. Mechanistic analyses suggest that models may refuse SI disclosures through the same direction that mediates their refusals of harmful requests. However, informing models of the SI context, or suppressing refusal directly, increases engagement without reliably lowering response risk, and may raise it. In contrast, the clinician-guided protocol consistently reduces response-level risk while also reducing refusal. Together, these analyses and ablations motivate the need for models that refuse only when warranted and engage in interactionally safe ways when users disclose SI.
中文翻译
拒绝是大型语言模型（LLMs）中常见的安全机制，但在涉及自杀意念（SI）的情境中，拒绝互动本身可能构成安全失败。我们评估了八种指令微调的LLMs在三个数据集上对SI和自残披露的响应，考察模型是否被告知披露表达SI以及是否接收到临床医生开发的响应协议，并结合六种开源权重模型进行了机制分析。结果揭示了拒绝与响应层面风险之间的复杂关系。在基线情况下，模型响应中的高风险主要源于对披露的忽视以及拒绝互动。机制分析表明，模型可能通过与拒绝有害请求相同的路径来拒绝SI披露。然而，告知模型SI情境或直接抑制拒绝，虽增加了互动频率，但并未可靠降低响应风险，甚至可能提高风险。相比之下，临床医生指导的协议始终能降低响应风险，同时减少拒绝行为。这些分析与消融实验共同表明，需要开发仅在必要时才拒绝、并在用户披露SI时以互动安全方式参与的模型。

GlobeBench: Evaluating Text-Based World Model Capabilities for Scalable Environment Simulations
GlobeBench：评估文本型世界模型在可扩展环境模拟中的能力
Text-based world models have recently shown potential to simulate complex RL environment state transitions for a diverse set of use cases. However, no standardized benchmark exists to compare, study and improve these models. Through this paper, we attempt to solve this problem by building GlobeBench, a novel benchmark with 1,632 tasks across five separate domains and nineteen simulation skills. The tasks check whether models can predict the environment's response to a specific action given the full agent trajectory by understanding earlier state changes, following environment rules, and predicting the effects of actions on both related and unrelated objects. We compare the predicted environment response with an execution-derived reference, using a hybrid architecture with both deterministic gates and a semantic judge. Among 20 general-purpose LLMs and specialized text world models, GPT-6 Astra achieves the highest task success rate at 49.57%. We find that model accuracy decreases as actions affect a larger portion of the environment. Specifically, when the agent's changes must propagate to dependent objects, task success drops to an average of 5.1% across all 20 models. Although model strengths differ across tasks, 631 tasks remain unsolved by any model, suggesting a significant gap in general LLM capabilities as a world model.
中文翻译
文本型世界模型最近展现出在多样化应用场景中模拟复杂强化学习环境状态转移的潜力。然而，目前尚无标准化基准用于比较、研究和改进这些模型。本文通过构建GlobeBench，试图解决这一问题。GlobeBench是一个新型基准，包含跨五个独立领域和十九种模拟技能的1,632个任务。这些任务检验模型是否能在理解早期状态变化、遵循环境规则的基础上，根据完整的智能体轨迹预测特定动作后的环境响应，并预测动作对相关与无关对象的影响。我们将模型预测的环境响应与执行推导的参考结果进行对比，采用结合确定性门控和语义判别器的混合架构。在20个通用大语言模型和专用文本世界模型中，GPT-6 Astra取得了最高的任务成功率，达49.57%。我们发现，当动作影响环境更大比例时，模型准确率下降。特别是当智能体的变化必须传播到依赖对象时，所有20个模型的任务成功率平均降至5.1%。尽管模型在不同任务上表现出差异性优势，仍有631项任务未被任何模型解决，表明通用大语言模型作为世界模型的能力存在显著差距。

Not All Graphs Are Maps: A Task-Agnostic Exploration Framework for Mobile GUI Navigation
并非所有图都是地图：一种面向移动GUI导航的任务无关探索框架
Modern mobile GUI agents can perceive visible screens and execute local actions, but they still lack persistent app-specific navigation knowledge: where functions live, which pages contain them, and which screens are equivalent across repeated visits. We introduce TAG-Nav, a Task-Agnostic Graph Navigation framework that wraps existing GUI-agent backbones with reusable destination memory. Offline exploration is distilled into compact records of app functions, target pages, and waypoint routes before any evaluated instruction is seen. When a future task's destination is covered, TAG-Nav retrieves the destination and provides route guidance without task-specific demonstrations. This separates mobile GUI destination navigation from full task completion: many tasks share the prefix of reaching the right page, while final actions may enter content, send data, make purchases, delete information, or change sensitive settings. To evaluate route finding without downstream execution confounds, we introduce DestBench, a navigation-only protocol that asks whether an agent reaches the correct pre-action destination screen. DestBench enables a controlled graph-memory study comparing no-memory agents with random, automated BFS, and clean graph memory. Graph-derived memory improves strict navigation success rate of UI-TARS-1.5-7B from 52.4% without graph memory to 58.3% with automated BFS memory, and improves Qwen-VL-Max success rate from 59.5% to 71.4% on the same conditions. Together, TAG-Nav and DestBench show that destination coverage and route cleanliness strongly shape graph-memory value, making graph quality a measured variable rather than an assumed benefit of exploration.
中文翻译
现代移动GUI智能体能够感知可见屏幕并执行局部操作，但仍缺乏持久的、特定于应用的导航知识：功能所在位置、包含这些功能的页面，以及在重复访问中哪些屏幕是等价的。我们提出TAG-Nav，一种任务无关的图导航框架，通过可复用的目的地记忆包装现有的GUI智能体主干。在任何评估指令出现之前，离线探索被提炼为紧凑的应用功能、目标页面和路径点路线记录。当未来任务的目的地已被覆盖时，TAG-Nav会检索目的地并提供路线指引，而无需任务特定的示范。这将移动GUI目的地导航与完整任务完成分离开来：许多任务共享到达正确页面的前缀，而最终操作可能涉及进入内容、发送数据、下单购买、删除信息或更改敏感设置。为了在不受到下游执行干扰的情况下评估路径发现能力，我们引入DestBench，一种仅关注导航的协议，询问智能体是否能到达正确的预操作目的屏幕。DestBench使我们能够在受控条件下对比无记忆智能体与随机、自动广度优先搜索（BFS）及干净图记忆的表现。基于图的记忆将UI-TARS-1.5-7B的严格导航成功率从无图记忆时的52.4%提升至58.3%（使用自动BFS记忆），同时使Qwen-VL-Max的成功率从59.5%提升至71.4%。总体而言，TAG-Nav与DestBench表明，目的地覆盖度和路径清洁度强烈影响图记忆的价值，使图质量成为可测量变量而非探索带来的默认优势。

Repairing LLM-Generated Specifications with Deterministic Diagnosis
通过确定性诊断修复LLM生成的规范
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as formalizers: given a reasoning problem expressed in natural language, they produce a formal specification that a symbolic solver can then solve, with guarantees relative to that specification. Many specifications are runnable yet semantically wrong, encoding incorrect constraints, domains, objectives, predicates, or goals, and solver-error feedback dries up once execution succeeds. Purely LLM-based diagnosis, lacking a formal ground truth, often fails to spot these errors, and its ungrounded critiques cause regressions in 11.8% of initially correct specifications in our experiments. We propose DRAGO, a repair framework that replaces this ungrounded critique with deterministic, formalism-specific diagnosis, and retains a rewrite only when the diagnosed error weight strictly decreases under a reference-free acceptance predicate. Across five benchmarks spanning logical reasoning (SMT-LIB2), constraint programming (MiniZinc), and planning (PDDL), DRAGO cuts regressions by nearly an order of magnitude, to 1.3%, while improving answer accuracy by 12.9 percentage points over zero-shot formalization and 8.7 percentage points over LLM-diagnostic repair, averaged across benchmarks. We also uncover two rendering defects in Planetarium that cause 50% (3,086 instances) of its floor-tile split to be inconsistent with the reference formalizations, and release a corrected split.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用作形式化工具：给定自然语言表达的推理问题，它们生成符号求解器可求解的形式化规范，并提供相对于该规范的保证。然而，许多规范虽然可运行却语义错误，编码了错误的约束、域、目标、谓词或目标，一旦执行成功，求解器错误反馈即停止。纯基于LLM的诊断因缺乏形式化真值而常常无法识别这些错误，其无根基的批评导致11.8%原本正确的规范在实验中出现退化。我们提出DRAGO，一种修复框架，将这种无根基的批评替换为确定性的、形式化特定的诊断，并仅在诊断出的错误权重在无参考接受标准下严格下降时才保留重写。在涵盖逻辑推理（SMT-LIB2）、约束规划（MiniZinc）和规划（PDDL）五个基准上的实验表明，DRAGO将退化率几乎降低一个数量级至1.3%，同时相比零样本形式化提升12.9个百分点，相比LLM诊断修复提升8.7个百分点（平均跨基准）。我们还发现了Planetarium中的两个渲染缺陷，导致其地板瓷砖分割有50%（3,086个实例）与参考形式化不一致，并发布了修正后的分割版本。

Are Large Reasoning Models Good Everyday Reasoners? A Comparison with Non-Reasoning Models on Logical Reasoning Tasks
大型推理模型是否是优秀的日常推理者？与非推理模型在逻辑推理任务上的比较
Large reasoning models (LRMs) that leverage test-time computation achieve strong performance on mathematical and scientific benchmarks and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. However, their ability to perform \textit{everyday logical reasoning}, which often departs from textbook formal logic, remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate how well reasoning models align with everyday reasoning patterns by introducing a benchmark based on linguistic and pragmatic reasoning phenomena. We also compare reasoning models with non-reasoning models under controlled prompting conditions. We find that, in many cases, (1) reasoning models show degraded performance in everyday reasoning when such reasoning deviates from formal logical rules, (2) some non-reasoning models with chain-of-thought prompting outperform comparable reasoning models in everyday reasoning, and (3) prompting with additional instructions provides limited control over reasoning behavior. These findings suggest that current reasoning models do not reliably capture everyday reasoning and point to limitations in post-training, motivating improved training objectives and evaluation across diverse forms of reasoning.

PRIME: Towards Verifiable Evidence-Grounded Reasoning in LLM Agents
PRIME：迈向可验证的证据基础推理的LLM智能体
LLM agents are used to check records against an organization's requirements, for example whether a vulnerability report is ready for investigation. Requirements often allow several ways to be met, such as a severity rating with a curator-reviewed advisory or a published advisory link. Agents fail by accepting a report after a required check has failed, or by settling for a weaker conclusion without trying another way that could still succeed. We introduce PRIME (Precommitted Rules for Inference from Minimal Evidence). Before checking, it fixes evidence routes specifying how each conclusion may be justified. A separate program records each check result and refuses any conclusion the results do not justify. It also points the agent to checks that could justify a stronger conclusion, or runs them itself. PRIME reports only conclusions authorized by its rules and ledger. With correct requirements, exact checks at known locations, and sufficient budget at stopping, completion reaches the strongest supported conclusion. On 480 public-record tasks, an agent shown the requirements reported unjustified conclusions on 26.7% of tasks, while PRIME reported none. Its directions and added checks raised the average score for justified conclusions from 30.1, with refusal alone, to the maximum of 59.2 out of 100.
中文翻译
LLM智能体被用于检查记录是否符合组织要求，例如漏洞报告是否已准备好调查。由于要求通常允许多种满足方式（如经审核的严重性评级+咨询链接，或公开发布的咨询链接），智能体可能因在某个必要检查失败后仍接受报告，或在未尝试其他可能成功的路径时就接受较弱结论而失败。我们提出了PRIME（基于最小证据的预承诺推理规则）。在检查前，它会固定证据路径，明确每个结论可被何种证据支持。一个独立程序记录每次检查结果，并拒绝任何未被结果支持的结论。同时，它还能引导智能体执行可能支持更强结论的检查，甚至自行执行。PRIME仅报告由其规则和账本授权的结论。在正确要求、精确检查位置和足够预算的前提下，完成度可达到最强支持的结论。在480个公开记录任务中，一个仅展示要求的智能体在26.7%的任务中报告了未经证实的结论，而PRIME则报告了零错误。其提供的指引和新增检查使合理结论的平均得分从仅拒绝策略下的30.1分提升至满分59.2分（满分100分）。

Eliciting Specifications: When Does Interactive Clarification Help?
获取规范：交互式澄清何时有效？
LLMs can generate Lean specifications from task descriptions, but those descriptions are often underspecified or ambiguous and may omit verifier-relevant edge cases, yielding imperfect formal specifications. We study whether an elicitation agent (EA) can improve verifier-checked Lean specifications by seeking clarification from a reference-grounded simulated developer before formalization. The agent decomposes the task, identifies unresolved decisions, asks bounded clarification questions, and uses the answers to write a refined natural-language specification that an LLM formalizer then translates into Lean. On 189 Verina Lean 4 specification-generation tasks, we compare single-shot generation with elicitation under question budgets of k=3 and k=10, allowing at most three or ten EA questions per task, across same-model and cross-model configurations. The same-model Claude Sonnet 4.6 pipeline has negative strict pass@1 estimates at both budgets, showing elicitation is not a uniform accuracy booster. In contrast, the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 and Sonnet 4.6-to-Gemini 3.8 Flash pipelines have positive strict estimates. The largest strict result reaches 65.6%, a +6.3-point change (+12 tasks), in the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 pipeline. Six of eight conditions have positive compile-conditional estimates, with a maximum cross-model difference of +4.9 points among compiling outputs. Treatment estimates also differ across the basic and advanced splits; the largest advanced-task change occurs in the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 configuration at k=10, from 42.0% to 50.6%. Failure analysis identifies postconditions as the largest source of degradation. These findings suggest future autoformalization systems should treat elicited natural-language specifications as intermediate artifacts and optimize jointly for semantic correctness among compiling outputs and reliable translation of clarified information into Lean.

LLM可以从任务描述生成Lean规范，但这些描述常存在信息不足或模糊，可能遗漏验证器相关的边界情况，导致不完善的正式规范。我们研究了一种获取代理（EA）是否可通过在形式化前向基于参考的模拟开发者寻求澄清来改进验证器检查的Lean规范。该代理将任务分解，识别未决决策，提出有限制的澄清问题，并利用答案撰写更精细的自然语言规范，再由LLM形式化器转化为Lean代码。在189个Verina Lean 4规范生成任务上，我们比较了单次生成与在k=3和k=10提问预算下的获取策略，允许每项任务最多提出三或十个EA问题，涵盖同模型与跨模型配置。同模型Claude Sonnet 4.6管道在两个预算下均呈现负的严格通过率@1估计值，表明获取策略并非统一的准确率提升手段。相反，Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6和Sonnet 4.6→Gemini 3.8 Flash管道表现出正的严格估计值。最大严格结果达65.6%，较基线提升6.3个百分点（+12个任务），出现在Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6管道。八种条件中有六种在编译条件下的估计值为正，编译输出间的最大跨模型差异为+4.9分。处理效应在基础与高级任务拆分中亦有差异；最大变化出现在Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6配置下k=10时，从42.0%升至50.6%。失败分析指出后置条件是主要退化来源。这些发现表明，未来的自动形式化系统应将获取的自然语言规范视为中间产物，并联合优化编译输出的语义正确性与清晰信息向Lean的可靠转换。

Binding-Specific Relearning Savings for Test-Time Continual Learning
测试时持续学习中的绑定特定重学节省
When a fact becomes harder to elicit from a language model, has the model lost it, or has it merely become less accessible? Parameter-updating test-time continual learning agents are usually evaluated by current output accuracy; we ask whether that measurement captures how prior experience shapes later updates. We test whether learning a specific factual binding produces a later relearning advantage beyond familiarity with its entity, answer vocabulary, format, or query slot. In Qwen2.5-1.5B, we compare three matched, counterbalanced histories: LEARNED receives the correct binding, CONTROL receives matched exposure without it, and WRONG receives a competing relation-valid answer in the same slot. After an identical rehearsal-free continual fine-tuning stream, LEARNED reacquires the target faster than CONTROL at a prespecified checkpoint of partial forgetting. WRONG shows no positive advantage over CONTROL, while its comparison with LEARNED provides slot-matched corroboration, subject to possible competitor interference. The advantage remains positive after adjustment for measured pre-relearning behavior. In a secondary descriptive conversion using an unpaired never-seen pool, prior learning reduces the optimizer updates required for restoration by about 18%. Current behavioral accuracy can therefore underestimate the lasting effect of previously acquired experience. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/relearning-savings
中文翻译
当一个事实变得难以从语言模型中提取时，是模型已经丢失了该知识，还是仅仅变得不易访问？当前参数更新型测试时持续学习智能体通常以输出准确性评估，但我们质疑这种测量是否能捕捉先前经验对后续更新的影响。我们测试学习特定事实绑定是否会产生超越对实体、答案词汇、格式或查询槽熟悉度的后续重学优势。在Qwen2.5-1.5B模型中，我们比较三种匹配且平衡的历史：LEARNED接收到正确的绑定，CONTROL接收到无绑定的匹配暴露，WRONG接收到同一槽位中竞争关系的有效答案。在相同的无重演持续微调流之后，LEARNED在预设的部分遗忘检查点上比CONTROL更快重新获取目标。WRONG与CONTROL相比无正向优势，而其与LEARNED的对比提供了槽位匹配的佐证，尽管可能存在竞争者干扰。在调整了重学前行为测量后，该优势仍保持为正。在使用未见过的池子进行二次描述性转换时，先前学习使恢复所需的优化器更新量减少了约18%。因此，当前的行为准确性可能低估了先前习得经验的持久影响。代码可在 https://github.com/zhezhou1106/relearning-savings 获取。

DiscoveryRSI: Recursively Learning to Discover Valuable Hypotheses
DiscoveryRSI：递归学习以发现有价值假设
Scientific discovery requires developing worthwhile hypotheses into practical investigations, yet learning from this evolving research process remains challenging. We introduce an operational definition of valuable and testable research hypotheses: evidence-grounded claims about consequential uncertainties, coupled with feasible investigations, explicit decision criteria, and alternative explanations. Our DiscoveryRSI framework develops hypothesis trees through multi-agent evolutionary search, compiles selected investigations into executable Harbor tasks, and recursively learns through three mechanisms: hypothesis credit optimization attributes scientific assessments to documented research contributions; harness replay identifies productive strategies and recurring failures to refine research workflows; and rediscovery applies accepted improvements to new investigations, generating further experience for subsequent learning. We also introduce DFM-Bench, which provides comprehensive assessment of hypothesis quality and cumulative research progress at the levels of hypotheses, revisions, development paths and research runs. Across four life-science domains, automated evaluation shows that hypothesis quality rises from 4.86 for the base model to 5.39 after two rounds of self-improvement, with the large gains in falsifiability and data resolvability. We further validate hypothesis quality through expert review, comparing our DiscoveryRSI-27B with other research agents. Preliminary human assessments show that DiscoveryRSI-27B scores higher than GPT-6 Luna on hypothesis importance and whether claims are plausible and evidence-bounded. DiscoveryRSI-27B achieved a 6.3% rate of hypothesis correspondence with research published after the model cutoff, largely outperform GPT-6 with 2.5% and Claude Opus 5 with 2.3%, while delivering greater human-rated improvement along research paths.
中文翻译
科学发现需要将有价值的假设发展为可行的研究，但学习这一动态研究过程仍具挑战性。我们提出了一个关于有价值且可检验研究假设的操作定义：基于证据的、关于重要不确定性的主张，搭配可行的研究方案、明确的决策标准以及替代解释。我们的DiscoveryRSI框架通过多智能体进化搜索构建假设树，将选定的研究整合为可执行的Harbor任务，并通过三种机制实现递归学习：假设信用优化将科学评估归因于已记录的研究贡献；回放机制识别有效策略与重复失败，以优化研究流程；再发现机制将已接受的改进应用于新研究，生成更多经验用于后续学习。我们还引入DFM-Bench，从假设、修订、发展路径和研究运行等多个层面全面评估假设质量和累积研究进展。在四个生命科学领域中，自动化评估显示，经过两轮自我改进后，假设质量从基础模型的4.86提升至5.39，显著提高了可证伪性和数据可解决性。通过专家评审进一步验证，DiscoveryRSI-27B在假设重要性、主张合理性及证据约束性方面优于GPT-6 Luna。初步人工评估显示，DiscoveryRSI-27B与模型截止后发表的研究论文的假设对应率达到6.3%，远超GPT-6的2.5%和Claude Opus 5的2.3%，同时在研究路径上的改进也获得更高的人类评分。

Beyond the Answer: Student-Conditioned Trace Construction for Reasoning Distillation
超越答案：面向推理蒸馏的学生条件化推理轨迹构建
A reasoning trace can accompany a correct answer without providing effective supervision for a particular student. We study how to construct traces that improve learning beyond answer-only supervision by considering both their intermediate content and their accessibility to the target learner. We introduce ReDiST, a student-conditioned trace-construction and distillation framework for evidence-rich reasoning. Starting from the student's own attempts, ReDiST revises answer-correct drafts against the supplied evidence and repairs unsuccessful drafts with additional guidance from the verified answer. A frozen student-matched reader screens and ranks original and edited candidates using answer recovery and verified-answer probability, given the question, evidence, and candidate trace. We distill the selected targets while balancing trace and final-answer supervision with an auxiliary answer loss. Construction and selection occur offline, and inference uses only the distilled student. Experiments on question-answering benchmarks show that ReDiST can improve both source-task and cross-benchmark performance over answer-only, original-trace, and the evaluated trace-selection and rewriting baselines. Across supervision recipes, performance rankings can differ between source and transfer benchmarks, highlighting the importance of evaluating trace teaching utility across distributions.
中文翻译
推理轨迹虽能伴随正确答案，但未必能为特定学生提供有效监督。我们研究如何构建既能改善学习效果，又能适应目标学习者的中间内容与可访问性的推理轨迹。为此，我们提出ReDiST——一种面向丰富证据推理的学生条件化轨迹构建与蒸馏框架。该框架从学生自身的尝试出发，基于提供的证据修正答案正确的草稿，并对失败草稿补充来自已验证答案的指导信息。一个冻结的、与学生匹配的阅读器根据问题、证据和候选轨迹，利用答案恢复率和验证答案概率对原始与修改后的候选轨迹进行筛选与排序。在蒸馏过程中，我们平衡轨迹与最终答案的监督信号，引入辅助答案损失。轨迹的构建与选择在离线阶段完成，推理阶段仅使用蒸馏后的学生模型。在问答基准测试上的实验表明，ReDiST在源任务和跨基准性能上均优于仅依赖答案、原始轨迹以及所评估的轨迹选择与重写基线方法。不同监督策略下，源任务与迁移任务的表现排名可能存在差异，凸显了在不同分布上评估轨迹教学效用的重要性。

DLite: History-free Speculative Decoding for Agentic AI
DLite：面向智能体AI的无历史推测解码
Speculative decoding has become a mainstream technique for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference by using a lightweight draft model to generate candidate tokens that are subsequently verified by the target model. However, agentic workloads introduce long and continuously evolving contexts and frequent multi-turn interactions. Under such workloads, we observe that existing draft models suffer from up to an 80% degradation in acceptance length, while maintaining their historical KV cache introduces substantial system overhead. In this paper, we identify the growing historical context as a common source of both problems and introduce DLite, a history-free speculative decoding framework for agentic AI. DLite directly reuses contextual hidden states from the target model and performs local speculative prediction without explicitly maintaining the historical context, transforming an increasingly complex modeling problem into local simple prediction problem. A lightweight sequential head further restores intra-block causal dependencies with low overhead. To effectively train this heterogeneous history-free architecture, we introduce a tailored two-stage training strategy. Experiments on general-purpose, long-context and agentic workloads show that DLite achieves 2-6 acceptance length and outperforms the state-of-the-art speculative decoding method, DSpark, up to 70%.
中文翻译
推测解码已成为通过使用轻量级草稿模型生成候选词并由目标模型验证来加速大语言模型（LLM）推理的主流技术。然而，智能体工作负载引入了长且持续演化的上下文以及频繁的多轮交互。在这些工作负载下，我们观察到现有草稿模型的接受长度最多下降80%，而维持历史KV缓存则带来显著的系统开销。本文识别出不断增长的历史上下文是上述问题的共同根源，并提出DLite，一种面向智能体AI的无历史推测解码框架。DLite直接复用目标模型的上下文隐藏状态，无需显式维护历史上下文即可进行局部推测预测，将日益复杂的建模问题转化为简单的局部预测问题。一个轻量级序列头进一步以低开销恢复块内因果依赖关系。为有效训练这种异构的无历史架构，我们引入了一种定制的两阶段训练策略。在通用、长上下文和智能体工作负载上的实验表明，DLite实现了2-6倍的接受长度提升，并在性能上超越最先进的推测解码方法DSpark高达70%。

Recognizing Study Lineage Is Not Enough: Over-Crediting Same-Trial Evidence in LLM-Based Medical Verification
识别研究谱系尚不足够：LLM医学验证中对同次试验证据的过度引用
Publication count is not study count. A model can identify shared trial lineage correctly in a direct task yet over-credit a second publication from the same trial when aggregating evidence. We evaluate this behavior in medical LLM verification using 14 source-audited clinical families and five model conditions. The families span provenance, clinical applicability, result direction, and composition; the provenance families provide the direct lineage tests. Recognition and aggregation are separate stateless calls, and repeated calls assess within-input stability. Across all three original provenance families, GPT-5.5 identifies the independent update in 10/10 recognition calls per family but over-credits the same-trial report in 5/5 separate aggregation calls per family. Selected Qwen3-4B and Ministral-3-8B conditions show the same cross-task contrast. A study-grouping instruction reduces GPT-5.5 over-credit from 4/5 to 0/5 in each later pure-provenance family. Other model conditions show mixed behavior, and rejected outputs limit several comparisons. The results identify a behavioral gap between elicited lineage recognition and evidence aggregation. The code and data artifacts are available at this https URL .

The Cognitive Cost of LLM Writing: A Reading-Time Comparison with Human Text
LLM写作的认知成本：与人类文本的阅读时间对比
Although linguistic differences between human-written and Large Language Model (LLM)-generated texts have been extensively studied, their implications for the cognitive effort involved in reading remain less explored. In psycholinguistics, reading time is commonly used as an indirect measure of cognitive processing effort. Building on this perspective, we investigate whether human-written texts and their LLM-generated rewrites exhibit differences in predicted reading time that may reflect differences in processing demands for readers. To this end, we introduce RoBERTa-RT, a model trained on human eye-tracking data to predict word-level reading times. We evaluate the model on both internal and external test sets, construct a paired dataset of 496 human-written articles and their LLM-generated rewrites, and apply RoBERTa-RT to compare their predicted reading times. Our findings show that LLM-generated rewrites tend to be shorter and have slightly lower predicted total reading times, while exhibiting higher predicted reading times per word. These differences are associated with systematic linguistic changes, including longer and less frequent words, greater lexical diversity, and higher contextual predictability. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that LLM-generated writing may redistribute reading demands: reducing the amount of text to process while increasing the predicted processing effort associated with individual words. This highlights the importance of considering not only the linguistic characteristics of machine-generated content, but also its potential implications for human cognitive processing.
中文翻译
尽管人类写作与大型语言模型（LLM）生成文本之间的语言差异已被广泛研究，但其对阅读认知努力的影响仍较少被探讨。在心理语言学中，阅读时间常被用作认知处理努力的间接指标。基于此视角，我们探究人类写作文本与其LLM重写版本在预测阅读时间上是否存在差异，这些差异可能反映读者处理需求的不同。为此，我们提出了RoBERTa-RT，一种基于人类眼动追踪数据训练的模型，用于预测单词级别的阅读时间。我们在内部和外部测试集上评估该模型，构建了一个包含496篇人类写作文章及其LLM重写版本的配对数据集，并应用RoBERTa-RT比较两者预测的阅读时间。研究发现，LLM生成的重写版本通常更短，总预测阅读时间略低，但单位词的预测阅读时间更高。这些差异与系统性的语言变化相关，包括更长且更不常见的词汇、更高的词汇多样性以及更高的上下文可预测性。从认知角度看，我们的发现表明LLM生成写作可能重新分配了阅读负担：减少了需处理的文本总量，但增加了单个词语相关的预测处理努力。这强调了在考虑机器生成内容的语言特征之外，还需关注其对人类认知处理的潜在影响。

GLOBE: Evolving Structured Agentic Memory for E-commerce Product Optimization in Generative Search
GLOBE：面向生成式搜索中电商产品优化的结构化智能体记忆演化
Optimizing product listings for generative search requires language agents not only to rewrite effectively but also to continually self-improve by retaining successful strategies across diverse categories. However, existing approaches often treat structured listings as free-form text, leaving effective strategies implicit in prompts, search trajectories, or rewritten outputs rather than accumulating them as a reusable agentic memory. To address this limitation, we introduce GLOBE, an agentic framework that drives continual self-improvement by storing optimization knowledge in an evolving handbook of explicit, field-scoped behaviors. In each episode, an Editor applies relevant behaviors and receives rank-based feedback, which a Reflector converts into behavior updates. Crucially for dependable execution in real-world settings, a Moderator removes redundant proposals, a Curator commits accepted changes, and consistently harmful behaviors are deactivated. This cycle transforms episodic feedback into a persistent, field-scoped optimization memory. Experiments show that structured behaviors outperform monolithic prompt optimization, while feedback-driven evolution and moderation further improve effectiveness and reduce downside risk. The learned behaviors also remain effective on unseen product categories, suggesting that agentic memory evolution supports interpretable and transferable product optimization.

👤 Hyunseo Kim, Suil Choe, Sukkyoung So, Daeyeon Yi 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Trajectory-Triggered Sparse Collaborative Decoding
轨迹触发的稀疏协同解码
Dense assistance methods such as speculative decoding repeatedly verify draft tokens with a target model, keeping target computation coupled to generation. We study sparse collaborative decoding, where a draft model generates most tokens and a target model is invoked only for short corrective segments. The key problem is deciding when to call the target. We show that local uncertainty is a weak trigger: entropy, negative log-likelihood, logit margin, top-$k$ mass, and velocity features give near-chance failure prediction, with AUC 0.50-0.53. In contrast, aggregate trajectory-shape features over the draft confidence path reach AUC 0.86 using a lightweight logistic predictor trained only from draft correctness labels. This induces a trigger-timing tradeoff: early calls have more leverage but weaker evidence, while later calls have stronger evidence but less ability to redirect the trajectory. Used online, learned sparse collaborative decoding improves over the best open-loop schedule by +5.3 to +20.0 accuracy points across five GSM8K draft-target pairs. On MATH-500, it gains 7.0-13.5 points when early prediction and target competence align, but loses when either is weak. On StrategyQA, it improves over open-loop by 5.0-8.3 points, showing transfer beyond arithmetics.
中文翻译
密集辅助方法如推测解码会反复用目标模型验证草稿标记，使目标计算与生成过程耦合。我们研究稀疏协同解码，其中草稿模型生成大部分标记，目标模型仅在短的修正段落中被调用。核心问题是何时调用目标模型。我们发现局部不确定性是弱触发信号：熵、负对数似然、logit差距、top-k质量以及速度特征均只能达到接近随机水平的失败预测能力（AUC 0.50–0.53）。相比之下，基于草稿置信度路径的聚合轨迹形状特征，仅使用草稿正确性标签训练的轻量级逻辑回归预测器，即可达到AUC 0.86。这引发了一个触发时机权衡：早期调用具有更强的影响力但证据较弱，后期调用则证据更强但调整轨迹的能力减弱。在线使用时，学习到的稀疏协同解码在五个GSM8K草稿-目标配对中，相比最佳开环调度提升5.3至20.0个准确率点；在MATH-500上，当早期预测与目标能力匹配时，提升7.0–13.5分，但若任一条件薄弱则反而下降；在StrategyQA上，相比开环提升5.0–8.3分，表明其泛化能力超越算术任务。
👤 Amit Ranjan Trivedi, Sruthikesh Surineni, Kyong Ho Lee, Mohit Mittal, Steven Li et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

SIT-Eval: Situation-Based Interactive Turn-by-Turn Evaluation of Conversational Agents
SIT-Eval：基于情境的对话智能体逐轮交互评估
The rapid proliferation of conversational AI agents necessitates evaluation methods that assess performance across diverse dialogue scenarios, utilizing multiple situational criteria, as appropriate behavior depends on context and encompasses various aspects. Existing dialogue benchmarks typically use static natural conversations, which underrepresent rare situations---a critical safety gap, as low-probability events become frequent failures at production scale. However, limited sample sizes force a choice: either maintain natural dialogues for realism or turn to adaptive situation sampling for comprehensive yet statistically reliable coverage. We introduce SIT-Eval, an adjustable framework for evaluating situation-based conversational agents. We formalize an agent's desired behavior as situational rules: if situation is P, follow rules Q. At each conversation turn, an LLM-interlocutor enacts a uniformly sampled situation. A context-aware LLM-judge leverages all conditionally applicable rules as criteria to evaluate the agent's reply for that situation. The judge is validated using human-verified synthetic golden dialogues. Benchmarking 8 LLMs on 1292 conversation turns reveals failure modes hidden by aggregate metrics: models with similar overall scores vary widely in their adherence to individual rules. All models struggle most with ambiguity clarification and Socratic questioning.
中文翻译
对话式AI智能体的快速普及迫切需要能够评估其在多样对话场景中表现的方法，结合多种情境标准，因为适当行为依赖于具体语境并涵盖多个方面。现有对话基准通常采用静态自然对话，未能充分代表罕见情境——这在生产规模下构成关键安全缺口，因为低概率事件在大规模部署中频繁发生。然而，样本量有限迫使选择：要么维持自然对话以保证真实性，要么转向自适应情境采样以实现全面且统计可靠的覆盖。我们提出SIT-Eval，一个可调节的情境化对话智能体评估框架。我们将智能体期望行为形式化为情境规则：若情境为P，则遵循规则Q。在每一轮对话中，LLM对话者随机采样一种情境。一个情境感知的LLM裁判利用所有条件适用规则作为评判标准，评估智能体在该情境下的回应。裁判通过人工验证的合成黄金对话进行验证。在1292轮对话中对8个LLM进行基准测试，揭示了聚合指标掩盖的故障模式：整体得分相似的模型在遵守个别规则方面差异巨大。所有模型在模糊澄清和苏格拉底式提问方面表现最差。

MARS: Memory-Aware Routing for Session-Level LLM Assistants
MARS：面向会话级大语言模型助手的记忆感知路由
Long-term LLM assistants must repeatedly decide what prior context to expose and which model should answer as an interaction evolves. Existing memory systems and model routers usually make these decisions separately, although memory construction changes the evidence, distractors, and difficulty of the resulting input. We introduce MARS, a memory-aware routing framework that uses session state as a shared control interface between memory construction and model selection. A Query and Topic Manager (QTM) estimates compact decision-relevant state that guides a fixed state-conditioned memory policy and an online router, enabling each turn to receive an appropriate memory view and a suitable model under a quality-memory-cost trade-off. We evaluate MARS on RealMem and MultiChallenge with 16 system configurations each, covering long-history memory-driven interaction and bounded sessions with structured context interference. On RealMem, MARS preserves 98.2% of full-context GPT-4o's QA while using only 14.5% of its generation API cost, including both generation and controller calls for MARS, and achieves the strongest measured memory-recall and retrieval scores among compared systems. On MultiChallenge, MARS trails the strongest static system overall but approaches its performance at lower recorded cost on a mechanism-focused subset. Together, these results support a favorable quality-memory-cost trade-off for long-history, memory-dependent interaction.
中文翻译
长期运行的大语言模型助手必须在交互过程中反复决定暴露哪些先前上下文以及由哪个模型进行回答。现有的记忆系统和模型路由器通常独立做出这些决策，尽管记忆构建会改变输入中的证据、干扰项和难度。我们提出MARS，一种记忆感知的路由框架，利用会话状态作为记忆构建与模型选择之间的共享控制接口。查询与主题管理器（QTM）估计紧凑的决策相关状态，指导固定的条件化记忆策略和在线路由器，使每一轮交互都能获得适当的记忆视图和合适的模型，在质量-记忆-成本之间实现权衡。我们在RealMem和MultiChallenge上评估了MARS，共16种系统配置，涵盖长历史记忆驱动的交互和具有结构化上下文干扰的有限会话。在RealMem上，MARS在仅使用GPT-4o 14.5%的生成API成本（包括MARS的生成和控制器调用）的情况下，保持了98.2%的全上下文GPT-4o问答性能，并在对比系统中实现了最强的记忆召回和检索得分。在MultiChallenge上，MARS整体略逊于最强静态系统，但在机制聚焦子集上以更低的成本接近其表现。这些结果共同支持了在长历史、依赖记忆的交互中具有有利的质量-记忆-成本权衡。

Truth in Contrast: Calibrated Factual Evidence from Language Model Likelihoods
对比中的真相：基于语言模型似然性的校准事实证据
Large Language Models (LLMs) encode substantial factual knowledge. Yet, estimating confidence in factual claims reliably remains challenging. We study likelihood-based factual confidence from the complementary perspectives of discrimination and calibration across multilingual factual statements. Specifically, we instantiate contrastive generative likelihoods as Contrastive Factual Evidence (CFE), which compares the likelihood of the same claim under competing factual and non-factual conditions, and derive its probabilistic counterpart, the Contrastive Factual Posterior (CFP). We show analytically that CFP calibration depends on the differential error between the two surrogate conditionals, allowing errors shared across them to cancel and yield tighter calibration bounds. Empirically, CFP substantially improves calibration while maintaining factual discrimination comparable to its strongest competitor. Nonetheless, discrimination is uniformly weak across methods in lower-resource languages, which simultaneously undermines the utility of the measured calibration. To this end, we interpret a likelihood geometry, within which an evidence-bearing direction is induced, along with an orthogonal evidence-invariant direction. In our investigation, we selectively align target-language claims with stronger English evidence only along the factual-evidence direction, improving discrimination and open-ended factual generation without target-language labels.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）蕴含大量事实知识。然而，可靠估计事实主张的置信度仍然具有挑战性。我们从判别性和校准性两个互补视角，研究跨多语言事实陈述的基于似然性的事实置信度。具体而言，我们实例化对比生成似然性为对比事实证据（CFE），通过比较同一主张在竞争性事实与非事实条件下的似然性，并推导其概率对应物——对比事实后验（CFP）。我们从理论上证明，CFP的校准性取决于两个代理条件之间的误差差异，使得两者共有的误差能够相互抵消，从而获得更紧的校准边界。实证结果显示，CFP显著提升了校准性，同时保持与最强竞争对手相当的事实判别能力。然而，所有方法在低资源语言中的判别能力均普遍较弱，这同时削弱了所测校准的有效性。为此，我们解释了一种似然性几何结构，其中诱导出一个携带证据的方向，以及一个正交的无证据方向。在研究中，我们仅在事实证据方向上将目标语言主张与更强的英语证据对齐，从而在无需目标语言标签的情况下提升了判别能力和开放式事实生成性能。

Perceive, Solve, Reason: Progressive Capability Formation in Time Series-LLMs
感知、求解、推理：时间序列大语言模型中的渐进能力形成
Large language models (LLMs) can now perceive, describe, and reason about images and audio, yet time series understanding remains underestimated: most work uses LLMs as forecasters and classifiers, reasoning studies are few and narrow, and no suite trains and evaluates the full range of capabilities. In this paper, we build the first such suite, with 337K training and 44K evaluation samples across three progressive capabilities, Perceive, Solve, and Reason, and use it to ask how time series should enter an LLM that must master all three, and in what order. We find that a dedicated encoder is essential: with one, a 4B model surpasses GPT-4o on every perception attribute we test, whereas textualizing the series costs 8--10 points in reasoning accuracy. The capabilities then build on one another: learning them in order beats mixing the same data and rivals reasoning-only training with over three times the reasoning data, but only when an encoder is used. Analyses further reveal that capabilities are transferred through distinct network depths rather than simply stacked, and that a stage converged on its own benchmark is not necessarily ready for the next. Distilled into a simple recipe, these findings yield 4B TS-LLMs that, without any target-domain data, outperform four recent 7B TS-LLMs on seven of eight out-of-domain metrics, by up to 11 points. We therefore advocate treating time series as a first-class modality for LLMs, read through dedicated encoders and taught to perceive before they reason.

大语言模型（LLMs）如今能够感知、描述和推理图像与音频，但时间序列理解仍被低估：大多数研究仅将LLM用作预测器和分类器，推理研究稀少且范围狭窄，且尚无系统能训练和评估全部能力。本文构建了首个此类评估套件，涵盖33.7万条训练样本和4.4万条评估样本，覆盖三个渐进能力——感知、求解与推理，并探讨时间序列应如何融入必须掌握这三项能力的LLM中，以及它们的顺序。我们发现专用编码器至关重要：使用编码器时，一个40亿参数的模型在所有测试的感知属性上超越GPT-4o，而将时间序列文本化则导致推理准确率下降8至10分。这些能力相互递进：按顺序学习优于混合数据，且在推理数据量超过三倍的情况下仍可媲美纯推理训练，但前提是使用编码器。分析进一步揭示，能力通过不同网络深度传递而非简单堆叠，且某一阶段在自身基准上收敛并不意味着已准备好进入下一阶段。提炼为简单方法后，这些发现生成的4B时间序列LLM无需目标领域数据，在八个跨域指标中的七项上超越近期四个7B时间序列LLM，最高提升11分。因此我们主张将时间序列视为LLM的第一类模态，通过专用编码器读取，并在推理前先进行感知。

MuninAI: A Self-Hosted Agentic Framework for Independent Research Groups
MuninAI：面向独立研究小组的自托管智能体框架
Research groups that want a language model grounded in their own literature face a choice between hosted services that receive their unpublished work and self-hosted deployments that keep the data local but give the model no access to the corpus. Munin is an open-source framework for a self-hosted scientific assistant that indexes a group's corpus and its citation graph, and wraps whichever open-weight model the group can serve in an agent harness with specialised reading, search, and computation tools. We claim that the harness, rather than the specific model, is what makes the system useful on domain literature, and we ship the evaluation suite that tests this claim with the software. On 199 paired literature questions, the harness more than doubles the accuracy of the base model, from 0.387 to 0.874 (+0.487, 95% CI [0.407, 0.568]), while naive retrieve-then-answer scores below the unaided model on every backbone tested. Under the harness, roughly twice as many answer claims trace to a passage the model read itself as with fixed top-5 retrieval. When the source paper for a question is removed from the corpus, the system abstains on about nine in ten of the questions it had answered correctly on the Qwen backbones and eight in ten on gpt-oss-20b, and it produces no fabricated local citation across 300 fabricated references. The pattern holds on three backbones from two developers, including a 20-billion-parameter model that runs on a single consumer-grade GPU. With all outbound network access disabled, both Qwen backbones still exceed the published PaperQA2 accuracy. The evaluation suite runs as a single command, so an adopting group can re-run it on its own corpus and model.

希望使用基于自身文献的语言模型的研究团队面临两难选择：托管服务会接收其未发表的工作，而自托管部署虽可本地保存数据，却使模型无法访问语料库。Munin是一个开源的自托管科学助手框架，可索引研究组的文献及其引用图谱，并将团队可部署的任意开源权重模型封装在一个带有专门阅读、搜索和计算工具的智能体外壳中。我们主张，正是这个外壳而非具体模型使系统在领域文献上具有实用性，并随软件附带了验证该主张的评估套件。在199对文献问题上，该外壳使基础模型的准确率从0.387大幅提升至0.874（+0.487，95%置信区间[0.407, 0.568]），而朴素的检索-回答方法在所有测试的骨干模型上均低于未辅助模型。在该外壳下，约有两倍多的答案主张源自模型自行阅读的段落，而非固定前5名检索。当问题来源论文从语料库中移除时，系统在Qwen骨干模型上约九成的问题放弃回答，gpt-oss-20b上约八成放弃，且在300个虚构引用中未产生任何虚假本地引用。这一模式在来自两位开发者的三个骨干模型上均成立，包括一个可在单个消费级GPU上运行的200亿参数模型。在完全禁用外网访问的情况下，两个Qwen骨干模型仍超过已发布PaperQA2的准确率。评估套件可通过单一命令运行，使采用该系统的团队可对其自身语料库和模型重新执行评估。

HydraLLM: Adaptive Width Slicing for Elastic Text and Vision-Language Models
HydraLLM：弹性文本与视觉-语言模型的自适应宽度切片
LLMs and LVLMs are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware, ranging from data-center GPUs to resource-constrained edge devices. Their fixed-size architectures limit the ability to reuse a single model across deployment settings with different compute, latency, and model-size targets. We introduce HydraLLM, an elastic MQA-based architecture. Its number of active attention heads determines the effective widths of the embeddings, attention projections, feed-forward layers, and the output head, forming a nested family of subnetworks within a single parameter set. We introduce a static-to-elastic conversion pipeline that, starting from a pre-trained model checkpoint, includes importance-driven weight reordering and stochastic subnetwork training with knowledge distillation. We extend this principle to multimodal inference through an elastic projection module whose output width follows the active text width. HydraLLM covers a wider range of active parameter configurations than FFN-only elastic architectures. With 75% of heads active, HydraLLM achieves 2.2 percentage points higher TriviaQA accuracy than a state-of-the-art nested baseline configuration which has 17.25% more parameters. With 50% of heads active, our multimodal model achieves a CLIPScore comparable to that of the 7$\times$ larger Gemma 3 4B model. With only 25% of heads active, HydraLLM increases token-generation throughput by 75.3% over the full-width configuration.

On the Tip of the Tongue: First-Token Cueing as a Behavioral Probe of Recall Failures in LLMs
舌尖上的记忆：首词提示作为LLM回忆失败的行为探针
Large language models often answer factual questions incorrectly in closed-book settings, and such errors are usually attributed to missing knowledge. Yet models often encode answers they fail to produce. We show that many closed-book errors are first-token recall failures: the model commits to a wrong first token, but once given the right one it completes the answer and often recognizes it as correct. We study this with First-Token Cueing, a behavioral probe that prefills the response with the first token of the gold answer. Model uncertainty peaks at the first answer token, where it already predicts correctness, and among gold prefixes of any length the first token alone accounts for the largest share of recovered errors. Across 16 open models, this one token corrects between a fifth and a half of the errors on multi-token answers in English QA and improves accuracy in all seven languages of a multilingual benchmark. Most of the errors it corrects are also solved by the model's own sampled reasoning, a criterion for encoded knowledge, and models recognize correct starts they do not produce: they continue a correct start more confidently than an incorrect start of the same answer type and prior probability, yet rarely find it without the gold answer. These results locate a recoverable class of factual errors at the first decoding step, where inference-time methods may gain the most.
中文翻译
大语言模型在封闭书设置下常错误回答事实性问题，这类错误通常归因于知识缺失。然而，模型往往编码了其未能生成的答案。我们证明，许多封闭书错误实为首词回忆失败：模型在第一个词上做出错误承诺，但一旦获得正确首词，便能完成答案并经常识别其为正确。我们通过首词提示（First-Token Cueing）这一行为探针进行研究，即用黄金答案的首词预先填充响应。模型不确定性在第一个答案词处达到峰值，此时它已预测答案正确；在任意长度的黄金前缀中，仅首词就占Recovered errors的最大份额。在16个开源模型中，这一词可纠正英文问答中五分之一至一半的多词答案错误，并在七种语言的多语言基准中提升准确率。其中多数被纠正的错误也可通过模型自身的采样推理解决，这是编码知识的标准，且模型能识别自己未生成的正确起始：它们对正确起始的继续信心高于相同答案类型和先验概率的错误起始，但极少能在没有黄金答案的情况下自行发现。这些结果定位了一类可在解码第一步恢复的事实性错误，表明推理时方法在此阶段可能获益最大。

Language Models Learn to Improve Language Models
语言模型学会改进语言模型
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate AI research, including language-model (LM) development, offering a potential path toward recursive self-improvement. However, turning experimental feedback into a learned ability to propose effective improvements across models faces two challenges. First, training such an ability with real feedback is expensive: reinforcement learning (RL) requires evaluating tens of thousands of proposals, each potentially requiring a full model-training run. Even at ten GPU-hours per run, reward collection alone would consume hundreds of thousands of GPU-hours, motivating a cheaper surrogate. Second, surrogate feedback can become unreliable as policy optimization favors overestimated proposals when the proposal distribution shifts. To address these challenges, we propose LM2LM, which trains LLMs with RL to generate effective LM improvement proposals. To provide scalable feedback, we use a research world model (RWM), a frozen LLM that predicts proposal gains from real experimental records provided in context, as the reward model for RL. To maintain feedback reliability, we periodically execute current-policy proposals ranked highly by the RWM and add their measured outcomes to its context. Across eight autoregressive and diffusion LM development environments, real-execution evaluations on held-out tasks show that LM2LM enables Qwen3.8-27B to outperform GPT-6-sol, even when GPT-6-sol is given 300 real experimental records in context, while requiring 33% lower data-generation cost. LM2LM also generalizes to a model family unseen during training.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于自动化人工智能研究，包括语言模型（LM）开发，为实现递归自我改进提供了潜在路径。然而，将实验反馈转化为跨模型提出有效改进的能力面临两大挑战。首先，使用真实反馈训练这种能力成本高昂：强化学习（RL）需要评估数万个提案，每次可能需要完整的模型训练运行。即使每次运行耗时十GPU小时，仅奖励收集就需消耗数十万GPU小时，这促使采用更便宜的替代方案。其次，随着策略优化偏向高估的提案，当提案分布发生变化时，替代反馈可能变得不可靠。为解决这些问题，我们提出LM2LM，利用RL训练LLM生成有效的语言模型改进提案。为提供可扩展的反馈，我们使用一个研究世界模型（RWM），即一个冻结的LLM，根据上下文中提供的实际实验记录预测提案收益，作为RL的奖励模型。为保持反馈可靠性，我们定期执行由RWM高排名的当前策略提案，并将其测量结果添加到其上下文中。在八个自回归和扩散语言模型开发环境中，对保留任务的真实执行评估显示，LM2LM使Qwen3.8-27B的表现优于GPT-6-sol，即使GPT-6-sol在上下文中获得300条真实实验记录，且数据生成成本降低了33%。LM2LM还泛化到了训练期间未见过的模型家族。

Who Pays for Efficient Inference? Confidence-Based Small-to-Large LLM Routing Across Tasks and Languages
高效推理由谁承担？基于置信度的小模型到大模型跨任务与跨语言路由
Small-to-large language model routing can reduce inference cost by escalating difficult inputs while retaining easier ones on a smaller model. Its success, however, depends on identifying examples that benefit from escalation, and the reliability of confidence across multilingual tasks remains unclear. We present a systematic evaluation of confidence-based routing across three model families, four languages, and three task types, covering 72 model, dataset, language, and scale configurations and 42,288 predictions. We compare global confidence routing with random routing, equal-quota confidence routing, and an oracle upper bound. Confidence identifies beneficial escalations on the Belebele reading comprehension and SIB-200 topic classification datasets, with mean correction-detection AUCs of 0.612 and 0.705. At 50% escalation, it improves over random routing by 1.40--4.67 percentage points on Belebele and 2.54--5.63 points on SIB-200. In contrast, the mean AUC on the MMLU-ProX-Lite knowledge and reasoning dataset is only 0.510, and routing provides no consistent improvement over random selection because small models often remain highly confident while incorrect. Global confidence routing also creates language escalation gaps up to 30.33 percentage points, whereas equal-quota routing removes this disparity with little aggregate accuracy cost. These results show that confidence routing is task-dependent and can allocate computation unevenly across languages. Code is available at this https URL
中文翻译
小模型到大模型的路由策略可通过将复杂输入升级至大模型而保留简单输入在小模型上，从而降低推理成本。然而，其成功依赖于识别出真正受益于升级的样本，且置信度在多语言任务中的可靠性尚不明确。我们系统评估了三种模型族、四种语言和三种任务类型下的基于置信度的路由策略，覆盖72种模型、数据集、语言和规模配置，共42,288次预测。对比全局置信度路由、随机路由、等额配额置信度路由以及理想上限（oracle）基准。在Belebele阅读理解与SIB-200主题分类数据集上，置信度能有效识别有益升级，平均校正检测AUC分别为0.612和0.705；在50%升级率下，相比随机路由，Belebele提升1.40–4.67个百分点，SIB-200提升2.54–5.63个百分点。相比之下，MMLU-ProX-Lite知识与推理数据集上的平均AUC仅为0.510，路由未能持续优于随机选择，因小模型常保持高置信度但答案错误。全局置信度路由还导致语言间升级差距高达30.33个百分点，而等额配额路由则几乎无损地消除了这一差异。结果表明，置信度路由具有任务依赖性，且可能在不同语言间不均衡分配计算资源。代码已公开。

Think More or Read More? A Token-Budget Theory of Reasoning with Shared Context
多思考还是多阅读？共享上下文下推理的Token预算理论
Reasoning systems increasingly improve answers by incorporating additional context into the model's input, from multi-agent debate, where agents read their peers' responses, to self-refinement, where a model revisits its own drafts. In these systems, communication consumes context as one agent's output becomes another's input. We study the regime where token reading and generation draw on one fixed token window, so every token spent reading is a token that cannot be spent reasoning. We develop a \textbf{token-budget theory} that formalizes this trade-off, modeling accuracy as a function of the reasoning budget remaining after reading and aggregating votes with a Condorcet jury model that permits correlated errors. For multi-agent debate, the theory gives a closed-form prediction for the optimal number of agents. This quantity is estimated from single-agent measurements, allowing the optimal group size to be determined without running debate, and the same accounting extends to a heterogeneous set of different models. We empirically verify our theory and show that, at the predicted group size, our method outperforms strong baselines under the tightest calibration spend, where debate-calibrated rules can afford only one or two questions. Self-refinement obeys the same accounting, with its own draft in place of a peer message. The theory further predicts that communication pays only when the gain in the accuracy ceiling exceeds the reasoning room spent on reading, with this condition tightening as the window shrinks. Across five open models and four reasoning tasks, self-consistency matches or beats deployed debate at $93$\% of $980$ matched window-and-group-size settings, with the gap being largest under tight windows and nearly disappearing at a loose one, consistent with the theory. Under a shared window, how much room a model has left to \textit{think matters more than what it reads}.
中文翻译
推理系统通过在模型输入中引入额外上下文来提升答案质量，例如多智能体辩论（各智能体阅读同伴回应）或自我精炼（模型回溯自身草稿）。在这些系统中，通信消耗上下文——一个智能体的输出成为另一个智能体的输入。我们研究了token读取与生成共享固定token窗口的情形，即每消耗一个token用于读取，就减少一个token用于推理。我们提出了一个 extbf{Token预算理论}，形式化这一权衡：建模准确率作为读取后剩余推理预算的函数，并使用允许相关错误的Condorcet陪审团模型聚合投票。对于多智能体辩论，该理论可给出最优智能体数量的闭式预测，该数量仅需单智能体测量即可估计，无需实际运行辩论，且该方法可扩展至异构模型集合。我们实证验证了该理论，并证明在预测的群体规模下，我们的方法在最严格的校准开销下优于强基线，此时辩论校准规则仅能负担一两个问题。自我精炼也遵循相同逻辑，以自身草稿替代同伴消息。理论进一步预测，只有当通信带来的准确率上限提升超过用于读取所消耗的推理空间时，通信才值得，且随着窗口缩小，该条件变得更严格。在五个开源模型和四个推理任务中，自一致性在980个匹配窗口与群体规模设置中，有93%的情况下表现优于或持平于部署的辩论系统，尤其在紧窗口下差距最大，而在宽松窗口下几乎消失，与理论一致。在共享窗口下，模型剩余的‘思考’空间比它读取的内容更重要。
👤 Manh Nguyen, Sunil Gupta, Hung Le 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

MGE-PO: Entropy-Aware Credit Assignment for Mathematical Reasoning
MGE-PO：面向数学推理的熵感知信用分配
GRPO assigns the same group-relative advantage to equally rewarded responses within a question, leaving their generation uncertainty unused. We introduce MGE-PO, centered on four-quadrant (4Q) advantage shaping that combines verified correctness with rollout generation entropy. Correctness determines the sign of an additional correction, while entropy controls its magnitude: lower-entropy successes receive larger bonuses and higher-entropy failures receive larger penalties. An online entropy-based question curriculum supports this credit rule using cached rollout measurements. At the final checkpoint after eight training epochs on Qwen3-8B-Base, adding 4Q across three policy optimization settings yields absolute gains of 3.23-3.69% in six-benchmark macro avg@32 and 4.24-11.06% in macro pass@32. On Qwen2.5-7B-Base, the complete configuration raises four-benchmark macro avg@32 from 41.5% to 45.4%.
中文翻译
GRPO对同一问题中获得相同奖励的响应赋予相同的组内相对优势，未利用其生成不确定性。我们提出MGE-PO，核心是四象限（4Q）优势塑造机制，结合验证正确性与滚动生成熵。正确性决定额外修正的方向，而熵控制其幅度：低熵成功获得更大奖励，高熵失败则承受更大惩罚。在线熵基问题课程支持此信用规则，利用缓存的滚动测量结果。在Qwen3-8B-Base经过八轮训练后的最终检查点，加入4Q机制在三种策略优化设置下，使六基准宏平均@32提升3.23–3.69%，宏通过率@32提升4.24–11.06%。在Qwen2.5-7B-Base上，完整配置将四基准宏平均@32从41.5%提升至45.4%。

MultiAgent-OPD: End-to-End Training of Hierarchical Multi-Agent Systems with On-Policy Distillation
MultiAgent-OPD：基于在线蒸馏的分层多智能体系统端到端训练
In a hierarchical multi-agent system, a Leader assigns tasks and subagents carry them out. Jointly training the Leader and the subagents is difficult because a final answer gives only indirect feedback to the subagents. An LLM judge can score the performance of the subagents, but these scores depend on the judge's own assessment of the quality of subagents' responses. We present MultiAgent-OPD, which combines on-policy distillation with reinforcement learning to jointly train a shared-parameter Leader-Searcher-Answerer team end to end. We first train a Teacher in a Searcher-Answerer workflow using answer correctness. During team training, the frozen Teacher scores student-generated search and summary tokens to provide local process supervision for the Searchers, while the Leader learns from team success and the Answerer from candidate correctness. Across seven datasets, the trained system reaches 38.68% macro avg@4, exceeding Prompted Critic by 4.06 percentage points and C-MAS + RL, the trained cascaded multiagent system, by 2.61 percentage points. The team also solves more questions within four attempts.
中文翻译
在分层多智能体系统中，领导者分配任务，子智能体执行任务。联合训练领导者与子智能体困难，因为最终答案仅对子智能体提供间接反馈。大型语言模型裁判可评分子智能体的表现，但评分依赖于裁判自身对子智能体回答质量的判断。我们提出MultiAgent-OPD，结合在线蒸馏与强化学习，实现共享参数的领导者-搜索者-回答者团队的端到端联合训练。首先，在搜索者-回答者流程中使用答案正确性训练教师模型。在团队训练阶段，冻结的教师对学生产生的搜索与摘要token进行评分，为搜索者提供局部过程监督；领导者从团队整体成功中学习，回答者从候选答案正确性中学习。在七个数据集上，训练后的系统达到38.68%的宏平均@4，优于提示式批评者（Prompted Critic）4.06个百分点，优于C-MAS + RL（训练过的级联多智能体系统）2.61个百分点，且在四次尝试内解决更多问题。
👤 Yuhan Sun, Jiaxin Zhao, Yongdong Luo, Yibo Hu, Meiguang Jin et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

ReLaDrift: Recurrent Language Generation via Drifting for Test-Time Scaling
ReLaDrift：基于漂移的循环语言生成以实现测试时扩展
Drifting Models generate a sample with a single function evaluation, making them attractive for fast parallel language generation, but their one-step formulation does not support iterative refinement. We propose Recurrent Language Generation via Drifting (ReLaDrift), which uses a shared Drifting Model to generate an initial draft from noise and iteratively refine it in a continuous latent space. We train this recurrence by sampling the number of rounds and applying the drifting loss only at the final round, stopping gradients through preceding rounds so that only one round requires backpropagation. Unlike the one-step Drifting Model, whose accuracy does not improve when iterated, ReLaDrift enables a trade-off between test-time computation and generation quality, as diffusion and flow language models do. On Sudoku Hard and GSM8K, it achieves competitive or superior performance compared with these iterative-generation approaches across a range of inference budgets. Our code is available at this https URL
中文翻译
漂移模型通过一次函数调用生成样本，适用于快速并行语言生成，但其单步公式不支持迭代精炼。我们提出基于漂移的循环语言生成（ReLaDrift），使用共享漂移模型从噪声生成初始草稿，并在连续潜在空间中迭代精炼。我们通过采样迭代轮数训练该循环结构，仅在最后一轮应用漂移损失，前序轮次停止梯度传播，因此仅一轮需要反向传播。与单步漂移模型不同，后者在迭代时准确率不会提升，ReLaDrift实现了测试时计算量与生成质量之间的权衡，如同扩散与流模型一般。在Sudoku Hard和GSM8K数据集上，其在多种推理预算下均达到与这些迭代生成方法相当或更优的表现。代码已公开。

MAS-Bai: Linguistically Informed Adaptive Multi-Agent Translation for Bowen-Script Bai
MAS-Bai：面向鲍文文字白语-中文翻译的语言学启发式自适应多智能体框架
Large Language Models (LLMs) remain unreliable for extremely low-resource languages whose writing systems conflict with high-resource pretraining priors. We study Bai-Chinese translation in the Bowen script, where Chinese characters are frequently borrowed phonetically rather than semantically, causing severe literal-translation hallucinations. We propose MAS-Bai, a linguistically informed adaptive multi-agent framework for Bowen-script Bai-Chinese translation. MAS-Bai routes each input through either a lightweight retrieval-translation path or a deeper philological path with etymological and syntactic agents, and uses cycle-consistency with a lightweight reverse translator as an inference-time reliability signal. We construct a 6,536-sentence Bai-Chinese corpus and evaluate on a 1,536-sentence test set. MAS-Bai achieves 16.23 BLEU and 62.15 COMET, improving over Standard RAG by 8.11 BLEU and 6.85 COMET. Forward LoRA, expert ablations and controlled routing experiments distinguish the roles of supervised adaptation, Bai-specific expertise and conditional routing. The largest gains occur on Bowen phonetic loans and divergent Bai word order, while human evaluation and reverse-verifier analyses support the automatic results.

AMEND: Constraint-Guided Multi-Agent Text Detoxification with Isolated Critics
AMEND：基于约束引导的隔离批评者多智能体文本净化框架
Text detoxification requires balancing toxicity removal, semantic preservation, and fluency. Existing methods typically optimize an unconstrained scalar objective---enabling safety scores to mask semantic loss---or rely on open-ended iterative debate prone to semantic drift and high computational cost. We propose AMEND, an inference-time framework that decouples detoxification into role-conditioned proposal generation, isolated multidimensional criticism, and constraint-first selection. AMEND evaluates diverse candidates with role-blind critics and enforces strict semantic and fluency feasibility before ranking by utility. To maximize efficiency, AMEND Lite restricts subsequent search to at most one targeted repair round only when residual toxic spans remain. On ParaDetox, one-round selection over diverse roles improves the joint evaluation metric by $+0.0508$ over the best single role. On a frozen confirmation test set, AMEND Lite achieves a joint score of $0.6597 \pm 0.0062$, outperforming unconstrained multi-round loops by $+0.0489$ ($p < 0.05$) while reducing inference requests by 28.4%.
中文翻译
文本净化需要在去除毒性、保持语义和流畅性之间取得平衡。现有方法通常优化无约束的标量目标——这可能导致安全评分掩盖语义损失——或依赖开放式的迭代辩论，易引发语义漂移且计算成本高。我们提出AMEND，一种推理时框架，将净化过程解耦为角色条件的提议生成、隔离的多维批评以及以约束优先的选择。AMEND使用无角色偏见的批评者评估多种候选方案，并在按效用排序前强制执行严格的语义和流畅性可行性。为提高效率，AMEND Lite仅在残留毒性片段存在时限制后续搜索最多一轮针对性修复。在ParaDetox数据集上，跨角色的一轮选择使联合评估指标提升0.0508，优于最佳单角色表现；在冻结确认测试集上，AMEND Lite获得0.6597±0.0062的联合得分，比无约束多轮循环提升0.0489（p<0.05），同时减少28.4%的推理请求。

What LLMs Do in the Shadows: Reading Context Reliance from the Unembedding Dark Space
大语言模型的隐秘行为：从未嵌入暗空间读取上下文依赖性
Detecting faithfulness hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) is crucial for their safe deployment in high-stakes domains. Recent approaches address this by contrasting contextual against parametric knowledge utilization, yet measuring context reliance typically demands noticeable overhead, such as materializing attention maps, running additional forward passes, or training auxiliary probes. In this work, we show that context reliance can be estimated by projecting hidden states onto the dark space - directions that compose weakly with the unembedding matrix. Beyond its recognized function as an initial-token attention sink, we show that this subspace actively mediates context processing, and that its share of hidden-state norm - termed the Dark Matter Ratio (DMR) - provides a direct score of context utilization. Pairing DMR with a parametric knowledge score yields DarkLens, a lightweight faithfulness detector operating strictly within a single forward pass. Evaluated across three QA and summarization benchmarks on six open-weight LLMs (4B-13B parameters), DarkLens matches or outperforms a wide range of baselines without requiring extra forward passes or probe training.
中文翻译
检测大型语言模型（LLMs）中的忠实性幻觉对于其在高风险领域的安全部署至关重要。近期方法通过对比上下文知识与参数化知识的利用来解决此问题，但测量上下文依赖性通常需要显著开销，如生成注意力图、运行额外前向传播或训练辅助探测器。本文表明，可通过将隐藏状态投影到暗空间——即与未嵌入矩阵弱相关的方向——来估计上下文依赖性。除了作为初始标记注意力汇聚点的已知功能外，我们发现该子空间主动参与上下文处理，其隐藏状态范数占比（称为暗物质比率，DMR）可直接作为上下文利用程度的评分。将DMR与参数化知识得分结合，形成DarkLens，一种仅需一次前向传播即可运行的轻量级忠实性检测器。在六个开源权重LLM（4B-13B参数）的三个问答和摘要基准上评估，DarkLens无需额外前向传播或探测器训练，性能匹配或超越多种基线。

Do Speech BCIs Need Larger Models? Rethinking Neural Decoding beyond Scaling
语音脑机接口是否需要更大模型？重新思考超越规模的神经解码
Recent speech brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) increasingly rely on large models and complex multi-stage pipelines for neural decoding. However, in speech BCI, where training data are scarce, noisy, and expensive to collect, the benefits of model scaling remain unclear. This work revisits a fundamental question: are large models truly necessary for accurate neural decoding? We propose BEST (Brain-Encoding-Speech-Text), a two-stage framework for efficient neural-to-text decoding. Instead of relying on large generative models or phoneme-centric pipelines, BEST introduces hierarchical intermediate representations and a compact speech-oriented decoder that directly maps neural activity to text. This design preserves essential structure while significantly reducing computational cost, enabling robust generalization in low-resource settings and practical deployment under constrained resources. On the Brain-to-Text '24 and '25 benchmarks, BEST achieves word error rates (WERs) of 6.19% and 1.62%, respectively, using only 3.85% of the parameters required by comparable methods. Beyond these results, we provide three key insights: (1) decoder scaling yields non-monotonic gains; (2) cross-modal alignment is more critical than model size; and (3) overfitting degards sequence-level performance. Representation analysis further reveals a consistent neural-audio-text alignment pathway.

近年来，语音脑机接口（BCIs）越来越多地依赖大型模型和复杂的多阶段流水线进行神经解码。然而，在语音BCI中，训练数据稀缺、噪声大且采集成本高昂，模型规模带来的收益尚不明确。本文重新审视一个基本问题：准确的神经解码是否真的需要大模型？我们提出BEST（Brain-Encoding-Speech-Text），一种高效的神经到文本解码两阶段框架。不同于依赖大型生成模型或以音素为中心的流水线，BEST引入层次化中间表示和紧凑的语音导向解码器，直接将神经活动映射为文本。该设计保留了关键结构，同时显著降低计算成本，实现低资源环境下的鲁棒泛化及受限资源下的实际部署。在Brain-to-Text '24和'25基准上，BEST分别实现了6.19%和1.62%的词错误率（WER），仅需同类方法3.85%的参数量。此外，我们提供三个关键洞见：（1）解码器规模带来非单调增益；（2）跨模态对齐比模型大小更重要；（3）过拟合会损害序列级性能。表示分析进一步揭示了一条一致的神经-音频-文本对齐路径。

Chinese Decision: A Family of Language Models for Single-Pass Probabilistic Decision-Making
中文决策：面向单次传递概率决策的中文语言模型家族
Many language-based applications require probabilities over a finite set of decisions rather than open-ended responses. We present Chinese Decision, a family of Chinese-language models for single-pass probabilistic decision-making. Given context, an instruction, and candidate descriptions, the model extracts final-position candidate-letter logits in one forward pass and normalizes them over valid options, without autoregressive answer generation. A shared interface supports Boolean judgments, categorical choices, and ordinal scores. We report a completed 0.8B model trained with distribution cross-entropy on 13.048 million decisions and preliminary 2B checkpoints trained with an additional RLCD objective. On the full CJ-Bench of 307,900 decisions, the uncalibrated 0.8B model achieves 72.24% general-domain accuracy and 76.98% average accuracy across medicine, law, and finance using a single checkpoint, with 1.07% overall expected calibration error. The 2B model reaches 72.56% general-domain accuracy after 40.13% of one epoch but has not surpassed the completed 0.8B model on the professional-domain average. We further evaluate a validation-temperature-scaled deployment model and its Q8 quantization, finding close aggregate performance while documenting individual prediction changes. We release the training, evaluation, and deployment pipeline. The current results establish a reproducible approach to Chinese probabilistic decisions but do not isolate the effect of model scale.

Explaining Chess and Go in Natural Language
用自然语言解释国际象棋与围棋
Modern chess engines play far beyond human ability, often choosing moves whose underlying ideas are difficult for humans to understand. We propose an automatic metric for evaluating chess explanations, grounded in chess-engine evaluations. Evaluating state-of-the-art language models using this metric, we find that they perform surprisingly poorly, often relying on surface-level board observations rather than robust, transferable chess concepts. We then introduce BinProp, a training-free method that enables these same models to discover reusable chess concepts and use them to explain moves in natural language. BinProp improves explainability for every model tested, nearly tripling the average score compared with direct prompting. Beyond chess, the same method discovers useful concepts in Go, draughts, and shogi without additional tuning.

Cross-Centaur: Modeling Individual Decision-Making Across Psychological Tasks with Language Models
跨心理任务建模个体决策行为的语言模型
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as computational models of human behavior. Recent models such as Centaur have advanced this goal by fine-tuning LLMs on trial-by-trial decisions from psychological experiments. However, existing large-scale behavioral datasets do not link the same participants across different tasks, so these models learn population-level regularities but are not trained to model individual-level regularities across tasks. We train Cross-Centaur to predict a participant's target-task actions from a target-task description and the source-task description paired with the same participant's source-task behavior. Cross-Centaur outperforms off-the-shelf LLMs and Centaur on held-out participants, both within and across tasks. Probing analyses suggest that its hidden states encode participant-level behavioral tendencies that transfer across tasks. These results highlight the potential of cross-task individual-level data for predicting human decision-making with language models. Cross-Pysch-101 is available at this https URL .

Before Retrieval Begins: Safety and Tool-Use Decisions in LLM Agents
检索前：大语言模型代理中的安全与工具使用决策
Large language models increasingly act as agents that call external tools, yet most tool-safety research examines only what follows a tool call. The prior decision of whether to call the tool remains largely unexamined and can influence downstream safety. Across the seven open-weight models, the unweighted model-average harm rate after admitted harmful requests is 74.5%. To identify what governs this decision, we apply a factorial intervention to the input request. Within our generated construction, neutralizing the requested-operation component produces the largest admission contrast (19 pp). A linear direction estimated from this contrast influences held-out admission in four of seven models; effects on operation-neutralized requests in some models show that it is not an operation-specific mechanism. We then test whether written refusal is behaviorally and representationally coupled to the same decision. Written refusal accompanies 33.6% of admitted harmful calls. Plain-text refusal training reduces unsafe admission in models that retain benign tool use, showing that response-level safety training can transfer to the call decision. Together, these results establish a partial, model-dependent relation between written refusal and tool admission. They motivate direct evaluation and supervision of tool admission rather than treating text refusal as its proxy.

ParetoChat: An LLM-Based Interface for Interactive Multi-Objective Optimization
ParetoChat：基于大语言模型的交互式多目标优化界面
Multi-objective optimization models are often deployed in settings where decision makers (DMs) must balance some conflicting criteria. In practice, however, stakeholders must translate their qualitative preferences between different criteria into numerical weights, priorities, or reference points required by optimization methods, which can be difficult to specify and interpret. This paper proposes \emph{ParetoChat}, an LLM (large language model)-based expert-agent interface for interactive multi-objective optimization. The agent translates natural-language user input into structured scalarization parameters, invokes an optimization system, presents candidate solutions, and iteratively elicits feedback. We describe a multi-agent architecture that separates input processing, multi-criteria decision-making (MCDM) expertise, solution presentation, and feedback processing. We then present a simulation study using an LLM-based DM agent and benchmark ParetoChat against interactive methods implemented in DESDEO, including reference-point and NIMBUS methods. The computational experiments on multi-objective binary knapsack and set-partitioning instances show that ParetoChat can guide the search efficiently toward solutions that match the DM's preferences. An application to historical courier-order assignment instances illustrates how users can explore objective extremes and intermediate trade-offs through natural-language requests. The results indicate that LLM agents may provide a practical interaction layer between business stakeholders and optimization tools.

Same Person, Different Advice: Auditing LLM Financial Advice with Repeated Risk-Tolerance Responses
同一人，不同建议：通过重复风险容忍度回答审计大语言模型的金融建议
AI financial advisers set a client's stock allocation largely from one stated-risk question, yet people do not answer it the same way twice: in a national US panel, 41% of households chose a different answer two years later, and in a laboratory retest a third of people moved two or more points on a 0-10 scale after six months. Seven large language models from five providers gave one-turn allocation advice for 800 real household profiles in which only the risk answer changed. The recommended stock share moved by 10 to 16 percentage points on average, and by 24 to 35 when the answer changed, against 0.6 to 4.2 points for the same prompt asked twice; in the laboratory retest the rating alone moved advice by 11 to 18 points. Without any note, the models respond to the answer about as strongly as when told that it is very reliable (less so for the two Claude models). If that instructed response is how each model maps attitude to advice, discounting the answer for its measured reliability, as classical test theory prescribes, would cut the swings by a factor of 2.4 to 3.3. The swing is largest for clients under 40. A note that the answer is noisy steadies some models, but shifts their weight to the client's age and pulls cautious clients towards a generic middle allocation: clients unwilling to take any risk are advised 22% stocks instead of 4%. Three extra money questions reduce the swing for some models, with no statistically detectable change relative to how much advice differs across people. In a scripted test, one clarifying question removes nearly all of the swing from an answer the client then corrects (33.5 points to 1.9 on average) while keeping the response to a change the client confirms. We argue that same-person retest audits should be a standard check for AI advisers, and that what to do with an unreliable answer is a question of client autonomy as well as of statistics.
中文翻译
AI金融顾问主要依据客户一次陈述的风险问题来设定股票配置，但人们并不会两次给出相同答案：在美国全国性调查中，41%的家庭在两年后选择了不同的答案；在实验室重测中，六个月内有三分之一的人在0-10量表上移动了两个或更多点。我们使用来自五家提供商的七个大型语言模型，对800个真实家庭档案进行单轮配置建议，仅改变风险回答。结果显示，推荐的股票比例平均变动10至16个百分点，当回答变化时，变动幅度达到24至35个百分点，而同一提示重复提问时仅变动0.6至4.2个百分点；在实验室重测中，仅凭评分变动就导致建议变动11至18个百分点。在未加注释的情况下，模型对回答的响应强度与被告知该回答非常可靠时相当（Claude模型例外）。若按经典测试理论建议，根据测量可靠性对回答进行折扣处理，可使波动幅度降低2.4至3.3倍。波动最大的是40岁以下客户。添加关于回答存在噪声的说明可稳定部分模型，但会使其更依赖客户年龄，并将谨慎型客户引导至通用中间配置：不愿承担任何风险的客户被建议持有22%股票而非4%。额外增加三个金钱相关问题可减少部分模型的波动，但相对于人与人之间建议差异而言，统计上无显著变化。在脚本化测试中，一个澄清性问题能几乎消除客户随后纠正的回答带来的波动（从平均33.5点降至1.9点），同时保持对客户确认更改的响应。我们认为，同一人重测审计应成为AI顾问的标准检查手段，而如何处理不可靠回答，既是统计问题，也是客户自主权的问题。

Controlling the Diversity-Quality Trade-Off of LLMs by Merging Models Before and After Alignment
通过合并对齐前后的模型控制大语言模型的多样性-质量权衡
Alignment is known to change the output characteristics of large language models (LLMs). Base models tend to generate more diverse outputs, whereas aligned models tend to produce higher-quality outputs. The appropriate balance between the two depends on the task. Existing methods either require costly fine-tuning or, like temperature sampling, mainly improve lexical diversity rather than semantic diversity. We introduce a depth-aware FrankenMerging approach for controlling the diversity--quality trade-off between an aligned model and its base model without additional training. This approach combines the lower layers of an aligned model with the higher layers of its base model based on the hypothesis that lexical diversity is more closely associated with lower layers and semantic diversity with higher layers. Experiments showed that varying the number of layers taken from each model adjusts both diversity and quality. In most cases, the merged models exhibited higher semantic diversity than the aligned model, and at some split positions, they showed higher lexical diversity than both original models. They also demonstrated a better diversity--quality trade-off than temperature sampling in most settings. A supporting ablation, in which we reversed the merging order, yielded results largely consistent with the hypothesis.
中文翻译
对齐已知会改变大语言模型（LLM）的输出特性：基础模型倾向于生成更多样化的输出，而对齐模型则倾向于产生更高品质的输出。两者之间的适当平衡取决于具体任务。现有方法要么需要昂贵的微调，要么如温度采样，主要提升词汇多样性而非语义多样性。我们提出一种深度感知的FrankenMerging方法，无需额外训练即可在对齐模型与其基础模型之间控制多样性与质量的权衡。该方法基于假设：词汇多样性更关联于模型底层，而语义多样性更关联于高层。实验表明，调整从每类模型中选取的层数可同时调节多样性和质量。大多数情况下，融合模型的语义多样性高于对齐模型，某些分割位置下甚至高于两个原始模型。在多数设置中，其多样性-质量权衡优于温度采样。支持性的消融实验显示，反转融合顺序后结果与假设基本一致。
👤 Yasuaki Sumita, Tomoharu Iwata, Toshiyuki Tanaka 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22985]

Prism Attention: Proposal-Refined Index Sharing Mechanism for Efficient LLMs Inference
Prism Attention：用于高效大语言模型推理的提案精炼索引共享机制
Long-context decoding in large language models (LLMs) is increasingly bottlenecked by the memory and computation required to attend over an ever-growing Key-Value (KV) cache at each autoregressive step. Although token-level sparse attention reduces this burden by restricting computation to a small subset of relevant past tokens, its practical gains are often offset by repeatedly recomputing layer-wise sparse indices as the context grows. A common remedy is to share sparse indices across layers by computing them only at designated anchor layers, but such static reuse ignores the layer-wise evolution of attention patterns, causing the reused indices to progressively deviate from the layer-specific optima and incur substantial attention loss. By revisiting cross-layer sparsity from the perspective of attention dynamics, we find that inter-layer salient token shift is highly localized: although the exact top-$\kappa$ indices differ across layers, the core high-attention tokens remain stable, with changes concentrated near the expanded boundary of previously selected regions. Motivated by this, we propose Prism Attention, a proposal-refined sparse attention framework. It replaces rigid cross-layer index reuse with a coarse-to-fine strategy, preserving layer-wise adaptability while retaining the efficiency benefits of cross-layer sharing. Extensive experiments show that Prism Attention achieves state-of-the-art performance among mainstream sparse attention methods on both reasoning and long-context benchmarks, while attaining a $3.4\times$ end-to-end decoding speedup over FlashAttention at 128K context length.

大语言模型（LLM）在长上下文解码中日益受限于每个自回归步骤中对不断增长的键值（KV）缓存进行注意力计算所需的记忆和算力。尽管令牌级稀疏注意力通过限制计算范围至一小部分相关历史令牌减轻了负担，但随着上下文扩展，反复重新计算逐层稀疏索引常抵消其实际收益。常见缓解方法是仅在指定锚定层计算稀疏索引并跨层共享，但这种静态复用忽略了注意力模式的层间演化，导致复用索引逐渐偏离各层最优，造成显著注意力损失。我们从注意力动态角度重新审视跨层稀疏性，发现层间显著令牌偏移高度局部化：尽管各层精确的top-κ索引不同，但核心高注意力令牌保持稳定，变化集中于先前选择区域的扩展边界附近。受此启发，我们提出Prism Attention，一种提案精炼的稀疏注意力框架。它以粗到细策略替代僵化的跨层索引复用，在保留跨层共享效率优势的同时维持层间适应能力。大量实验表明，Prism Attention在主流稀疏注意力方法中于推理与长上下文基准上均达到领先性能，且在128K上下文长度下相较FlashAttention实现3.4倍的端到端解码加速。

Self-Evolving Agents Can Repair Failures but Struggle to Generalize from Them
自我演化的智能体可以修复失败，但难以从中泛化
Experience-driven self-evolution offers a promising path for large language model (LLM) agents to continually improve through interaction. Yet existing approaches often rely on successful trajectories or external evaluation signals, leaving unclear whether agents can evolve when such positive anchors are unavailable. We study this question through Cold-Start Failure-Only Evolution (CFOE), where agents start without accumulated experience and learn only from failed interactions without privileged guidance. Across three self-evolving paradigms, multiple LLM backbones, and four interactive environments, we uncover a consistent repair-generalization gap: failure-derived experience can help repair previously failed tasks, but rarely yields sustained improvements on unseen tasks. Further analysis shows that agents often misinterpret failures, while even verified-correct experience remains difficult to transfer. Relaxing CFOE reveals that verifier feedback and additional interaction provide limited gains, whereas successful trajectories substantially improve transfer. These findings suggest that current self-evolving agents are better at repairing observed failures than building generalizable capabilities from failure.

基于经验的自我演化为大语言模型（LLM）智能体通过交互持续改进提供了有前景的路径。然而，现有方法通常依赖成功轨迹或外部评估信号，尚不清楚当缺乏这些正向锚点时，智能体是否仍能演化。我们通过冷启动仅失败演化（CFOE）研究这一问题：智能体从零经验开始，仅通过失败交互学习，无特权指导。在三种自我演化范式、多个LLM骨干网络及四个交互环境中，我们发现一个一致的修复-泛化差距：失败经验有助于修复先前失败的任务，但极少带来对未见任务的持续改进。进一步分析表明，智能体常错误解读失败，即使经过验证的正确经验也难以迁移。放宽CFOE条件发现，验证器反馈和额外交互带来的增益有限，而成功轨迹则显著提升迁移能力。这些发现表明，当前自我演化智能体更擅长修复已观察到的失败，而非从失败中构建可泛化的智能能力。
