KiranaBench: Can a Language Model Run a Neighbourhood Shop?
KiranaBench：大语言模型能否经营一家邻里小店？
Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tasks, including autonomously running a business. Existing benchmarks for this setting are almost exclusively English-language, Western in retail context, and single-agent, with the agent observing the business directly. We study how these results transfer to a multi-agent Indic setting. We introduce KiranaBench, a simulated Indian general store in which four roles (manager, cashier, helper, and bookkeeper) are each played by a separate LLM agent. Interactions with customers and suppliers occur in English, Hindi, or Hinglish, and product demand is calibrated to India's national Household Consumption Expenditure Survey. Evaluating four open-weight LLMs, we find that all fall far short of a fixed-rule shopkeeper baseline: the best model earns only one-eighth of the baseline's profit, and two models finish below an unmanaged shop. Performance is also consistently lower for Hindi and Hinglish than for English supplier communication. KiranaBench offers evidence that current LLM agents are not yet reliable for autonomously operating small businesses in India, and provides a testbed for developing agents suited to settings that are long-horizon, collaborative, and multilingual.

We construct a controlled evaluation set centered on atari and its relationship to sente in Go, examining terminology recognition under local board changes and move evaluation under different strategic conditions. Our controlled tests provide a framework for examining whether language-based Go agents connect domain terminology with precise board relationships and strategic move values.

Aligning Language Models Towards Counterfactual Invariance
使语言模型对反事实保持不变：一种对齐方法
Small variations in prompt characteristics can meaningfully alter the semantics, tone, and contextual assumptions of large language model outputs. Such characteristics can involve group variables such as demographics or other sensitive latent attributes. Sensitivity to such input variables is undesirable when demographic information is causally irrelevant to the task, but might still be relevant for generation of semantically coherent responses. This makes the task of enforcing counterfactual invariance while preserving model utility difficult. To study this problem, we first show that standard model alignment recipes (such as reinforcement learning) increase differences in response quality across demographics, while improving overall response quality, ultimately amplifying what is known as counterfactual bias. To address this, we develop a policy gradient technique involving a group-level reward function that operates on matched pairs of counterfactual prompts. For completeness, we build a dataset of controlled counterfactual prompt pairs and design an evaluation that separately measures cases where demographic information is causally relevant to the answer. Finally, we validate our findings on several fairness benchmarks. We demonstrate that our methods reduce counterfactual differences while preserving overall response quality and that the models continue to adapt appropriately when such sensitive information is relevant.

提示特征的微小变化可能显著改变大语言模型输出的语义、语气和上下文假设。这些特征可能涉及群体变量，如人口统计信息或其他敏感的潜在属性。当人口统计信息与任务在因果上无关时，对这类输入变量的敏感性是不理想的，但在生成语义连贯响应时，这些信息可能仍然相关。这使得在保持模型效用的同时强制实现反事实不变性变得困难。为研究此问题，我们首先表明，标准的模型对齐方法（如强化学习）会加剧不同人群间响应质量的差异，尽管整体响应质量有所提升，最终放大了所谓的反事实偏差。为解决此问题，我们开发了一种基于群体级奖励函数的策略梯度技术，该函数作用于成对的反事实提示。为完善研究，我们构建了一个受控的反事实提示对数据集，并设计了一种评估方法，可分别测量人口统计信息在答案中因果相关的情况。最后，我们在多个公平性基准上验证了我们的发现。结果表明，我们的方法在保持整体响应质量的同时减少了反事实差异，并且当敏感信息相关时，模型仍能适当适应。

Many Biases, One Circuit: Post-Training Tunes a Pretrained Cue-Routing Mechanism
多重偏见，单一回路：后训练调整预训练的线索路由机制
Language models often abandon a correct answer when the prompt contains a cue, a hint toward a wrong option such as "I think the answer is B." Sycophancy and related biases show this failure. They are often studied separately and blamed on alignment. We test this view causally, tracing the attention heads behind these biases through the stages of two post-training pipelines. In multiple-choice tests on most models, four cue types rely largely on the same few heads, a shared circuit already present in pretrained models. Most base models follow cues in plain text. Fine-tuning a base model on about 200 generic assistant examples without cues activates these heads in chat format, so cue-following can be a side effect of assistant tuning. The final reinforcement learning (RL) stage strengthens the effect of cues in almost every comparison. In Tulu-3-8B and OLMo-2-7B, we swap the attention patterns of six middle layers between the RL checkpoint and its predecessor. For suggested-answer cues, this shifts each checkpoint's cue effect most of the way to the other's level. Post-training thus seems to change when and how strongly models use this circuit. Mitigation for these multiple-choice biases could target this circuit.
中文翻译
语言模型在提示中包含线索（如‘我认为答案是B’）时，常常放弃正确答案。这种顺从性及相关偏见体现了这一失败。这些偏见通常被单独研究，并归因于对齐问题。我们通过因果实验，追踪两个后训练流程阶段中支持这些偏见的注意力头。在多数模型的多项选择测试中，四种线索类型主要依赖相同的少数几个注意力头，即一个在预训练模型中已存在的共享回路。大多数基础模型在纯文本中遵循线索。在约200个无线索的通用助手示例上进行微调后，模型在聊天格式中激活了这些头，说明线索跟随可能是助手微调的副作用。最终的强化学习（RL）阶段几乎在所有比较中都加强了线索的影响。在Tulu-3-8B和OLMo-2-7B中，我们将RL检查点与其前驱检查点的六个中间层注意力模式互换。对于建议答案类线索，这一操作使每个检查点的线索效应向另一个检查点水平移动了大部分距离。因此，后训练似乎改变了模型使用该回路的时间和强度。针对这些多项选择偏见的缓解措施可聚焦于该回路。

Competence-Based Evaluation of Logical Reasoning in Large Language Models
基于能力的大型语言模型逻辑推理能力评估
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have brought impressive performance across diverse tasks; however, shortcomings remain in logical reasoning, including inconsistencies under linguistic variation. To improve the reasoning capacity of LLMs, we must be able to accurately measure their reasoning capacity. Current benchmarks primarily report single accuracy numbers on selected reasoning tasks. Such a performance-based evaluation methodology says little about a model's generalization capacity in reasoning. To address this limitation, we argue for a different evaluation paradigm, called competence-based evaluation (CBE), which tests whether models reliably apply the same competence across a family of problems. A parameterized generator scales complexity while keeping the required competence fixed, and matched answer-preserving presentations test correctness beyond one formulation. We instantiate CBE on elementary ordering tasks, scaling input size while varying presentations of the same ordering rule. Across six GPT and Qwen models, performance is reasonably good over bounded complexity ranges, but even strong models fail the competence condition across the tested family. Chain-of-thought and GRPO extend strong performance without resolving all tested failures; supervised LoRA shows little held-out transfer. CBE provides diagnostic profiles and an evaluation protocol that can grow with future models.

近年来大型语言模型（LLMs）在多样化任务中表现出令人瞩目的性能，但在逻辑推理方面仍存在不足，例如在语言形式变化下出现不一致。为了提升LLMs的推理能力，我们必须能够准确衡量其推理能力。当前基准主要报告特定推理任务上的单一准确率，这种基于性能的评估方法无法反映模型在推理方面的泛化能力。为解决这一局限，我们主张采用一种新的评估范式——基于能力的评估（CBE），即测试模型是否能在一组问题中可靠地应用相同的推理能力。参数化生成器在保持所需能力不变的情况下增加复杂度，匹配的答案保持呈现方式用于检验正确性是否超越单一表述形式。我们在基础排序任务中实例化CBE，通过改变输入规模并调整同一排序规则的不同表述方式。在六个GPT和Qwen模型中，性能在有限复杂度范围内表现良好，但即使强模型也无法通过整个家族测试的能力条件。思维链（Chain-of-thought）和GRPO扩展了强模型的表现，但未能解决所有测试失败；监督LoRA在保留泛化方面作用甚微。CBE提供了诊断性分析图谱和可随未来模型演进的评估协议。

Bengali Hallucination Detection with Retrieval and Programs: A System Study
基于检索与程序的孟加拉语幻觉检测：系统性研究
Bengali hallucination detection must distinguish unsupported factual claims from incorrect reasoning, often without a supplied reference passage. We present and characterize an inference-only system that combines response-conditioned query rewriting, hybrid retrieval, and task-specific language-model judging. Six retrieval routes search question-answer and passage collections; a router directs each example to a 27B textual judge or a 14B agent that can compare retrieved answers, reason directly, or generate and execute Python. We characterize the system using version-specific code, saved predictions, execution traces, and corpus metadata from the Olikbochon challenge. Kaggle associates a displayed private macro-F1 of 0.956 with Version 2; its saved run processes 2,516 examples in 220.99 minutes on two T4 GPUs. Of 253 math-routed examples, 88 terminate through candidate matching, while execution traces expose 49 stderr-bearing run events across 35 examples. A later run changes six predictions despite identical core worker code. A bounded audit of 1,090 benchmark-related corpus records finds exact question or context overlap for 38 processed evaluation inputs, precluding an unqualified claim of corpus-independent generalization. The contribution is a concrete retrieval-and-computation judging architecture with an auditable operational characterization, not a controlled estimate of component gains. We distinguish the reported leaderboard result from independently recomputed diagnostics and identify the evidence needed for stronger effectiveness and reproducibility claims.

NLData2Opt: Benchmarking Parameter Derivation and Prediction for Modeling Optimization Problems from Natural Language and Data
NLData2Opt：从自然语言与数据中建模优化问题的参数推导与预测基准测试
Formulating optimization problems from raw inputs of problem descriptions and data is time-consuming and costly. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising way to automate this process, existing benchmarks overestimate their capabilities by relying on idealized inputs where all data is relevant and complete. This diverges from reality, where data-centric reasoning is required to extract, compute, and predict parameters from noisy sources. To address this, we introduce NLData2Opt, a benchmark to evaluate LLMs on the multi-stage reasoning inherent in practical optimization workflows. NLData2Opt decomposes problems into problem descriptions and data, and requires models to (1) extract relevant parameters from data containing misleading attributes, (2) compute missing parameters derived by transforming existing data attributes, and (3) predict unavailable future values from historical observations. Evaluation results show substantial performance decline relative to existing benchmarks, indicating that benchmark progress does not directly translate to formulation accuracy in practical optimization workflows. NLData2Opt is available at this https URL .

Identify, Classify, Act: Per-Instruction Controlled Compliance for LLMs over External Data
识别、分类、行动：面向外部数据的指令级可控合规性机制
LLMs over external data face a per-instruction tension: a single document or tool return often interleaves legitimate directives with adversarial prompt injections, and the model must follow the former while rejecting the latter. Existing defenses suppress or trust all external instructions, collapsing the safety-utility frontier and forcing a choice between over-refusal and over-compliance. We argue the missing capability is \textbf{per-instruction credit attribution} and formalize it as External-Data Instruction Classification, an identify-classify-act decision over each embedded directive. We propose \textbf{EDIC}, a decomposed reward framework for per-instruction compliance. A scene-grounded pipeline injects mixed beneficial and harmful instructions with counterfactual filtering, while a generative judge is initialized by SFT and refined with GRPO to attribute compliance at the instruction level. The judge then produces a reward that separately encourages beneficial compliance and penalizes harmful compliance for RL training. Across two backbones and five benchmarks, EDIC improves both safety and utility over the strongest prior defense, lifting harmful-rejection by up to 7.7\% and Arena-Hard-Auto utility by 12.5\% on Qwen3-4B. The code repository is hosted at this https URL .
中文翻译
大语言模型在处理外部数据时面临指令级矛盾：单个文档或工具返回的内容常混合合法指令与恶意提示注入，模型需遵循前者而拒绝后者。现有防御策略要么完全抑制外部指令，要么全然信任，导致安全与效用之间的权衡被压缩，迫使模型在过度拒绝与过度服从之间做出选择。我们认为缺失的能力是‘指令级信用归属’，并将其形式化为外部数据指令分类任务——对每个嵌入指令进行识别-分类-行动决策。我们提出EDIC，一种用于指令级合规性的分解式奖励框架。一个场景感知的流水线通过反事实过滤注入混合有益与有害指令，同时使用SFT初始化的生成式裁判，经GRPO微调后实现指令级别的合规性归属。该裁判随后生成奖励信号，分别鼓励有益合规并惩罚有害合规，用于强化学习训练。在两种骨干模型和五个基准上，EDIC在最强先验防御基础上提升了安全性和效用，使有害拒绝率最高提升7.7%，Qwen3-4B在Arena-Hard-Auto上的效用提升达12.5%。代码仓库位于该链接。

[2610.22858]

Uncertainty-Aware Budget Allocation for Efficient LLM Tree Search
不确定性感知的预算分配：高效的大语言模型树搜索方法
Tree search enhances multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet prevailing methods expand every step with a fixed budget, irrespective of the marginal utility of expansion. We first show, across existing search algorithms, that continuations sampled from a shared intermediate state are largely duplicates and collapse to a few distinct answers. Critically, we find that the semantic dispersion of sibling actions predicts the redundancy of their downstream continuations, permitting a subtree's budget to be fixed before expansion. An information-theoretic analysis motivates this: treating the per-step budget as the Monte Carlo sample size for estimating a step's residual answer entropy, we derive a sufficient budget that shrinks with sibling dispersion. We accordingly propose $\textbf{Uncertainty-Aware Allocation}$ (UAA), a training-free framework that decouples value from uncertainty: values determine which candidates are promising, while uncertainty, estimated from sibling dispersion at no additional model cost, determines how many merit expansion, via uncertainty-aware pruning and uncertainty-aware budgeting. Across mathematical reasoning, logical reasoning, and planning benchmarks, UAA reduces token usage by up to $90\%$ and latency by up to $9\times$ at comparable accuracy, generalizing across four tree search algorithms and both open- and closed-source LLMs.
中文翻译
树搜索可增强大语言模型（LLMs）的多步推理能力，但现有方法在每一步都固定扩展预算，不考虑扩展的边际效用。我们首先发现，在现有搜索算法中，从共享中间状态采样的延续内容大多重复，最终收敛为少数几个答案。关键的是，我们发现兄弟动作的语义分散度可预测其后续延续的冗余程度，从而可在展开前确定子树的预算。信息论分析支持这一观点：将每步预算视为估计步骤残余答案熵的蒙特卡洛样本量，我们推导出一个随兄弟分散度增加而减小的充分预算。因此，我们提出$ extbf{不确定性感知分配}$（UAA），一种无需训练的框架，将价值与不确定性解耦：价值决定哪些候选值得探索，而不确定性则通过兄弟分散度估算（无需额外模型开销），决定有多少候选值得扩展，通过不确定性感知剪枝与预算分配实现。在数学推理、逻辑推理和规划等多个基准上，UAA在保持相近准确率的同时，将token使用量减少高达90%，延迟降低最多9倍，且在四种树搜索算法及开源与闭源LLM间具有良好的泛化能力。
👤 Jianguo Huang, Zhiyi Lyu, Fuxiang Zhang, Yanchen Deng, Deheng Ye et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Causal Edit Serialization
因果编辑序列化
Standard autoregressive language models generate text sequentially, but revision requires referring back to tokens in the input context or those already generated. Causal Edit Serialization (CES) is introduced as a framework for expressing such revisions within the same causal token stream. Special pointer tokens are paired with predicted source addresses, allowing insertion, deletion, replacement, copying and movement to be localized explicitly. A lightweight localization head and dual-index rotary position encoding support both address prediction and conditioning on previously emitted pointers, while retaining a standard causal attention mask. A pretraining procedure combines ordinary language modeling with serialized repairs derived from random corruptions, model-generated edits and repeated text. Across model scales and editing baselines, CES models demonstrate complete applied edits with modestly higher clean-text bits per byte than clean autoregressive models. Supervised and reinforcement-learning post-training provide a grammatical-error-correction proof of concept, producing more beneficial than harmful changes under reference-blind model judging. Correct repair programs also improve subsequent text prediction while the original context and its cached states are retained. CES provides a common interface for generation and localized revision, with potential extensions to iterative refinement and additional pointer-addressed transformations.
中文翻译
标准自回归语言模型按顺序生成文本，但修改操作需要回溯输入上下文或已生成的标记。因果编辑序列化（CES）被引入作为在同一流式因果标记中表达此类修改的框架。特殊指针标记与预测的源地址配对，使得插入、删除、替换、复制和移动等操作可被显式定位。轻量级定位头与双索引旋转位置编码支持地址预测和对先前发出指针的条件依赖，同时保留标准因果注意力掩码。预训练过程结合普通语言建模与来自随机破坏、模型生成编辑和重复文本的序列化修复。在不同模型规模和编辑基线中，CES模型实现了完整的应用编辑，仅比干净的自回归模型略高一点的干净文本比特每字节。监督和强化学习后训练提供了语法错误修正的可行性证明，在无参考模型判断下，产生的有益更改多于有害更改。正确的修复程序还能在保留原始上下文及其缓存状态的前提下，改善后续文本预测。CES为生成与局部化修改提供了一个统一接口，具有扩展至迭代精炼及其他指针-地址变换的潜力。

[2610.22791]

Self-Evolving Coding Agents via Harness-Policy Optimization
通过Harness-策略优化实现自我演化的编码智能体
Large language model (LLM) agents have shown promising potential in coding tasks, and their performance is jointly determined by the policy model and the harness that mediates their interaction with the environment. Indeed, harness and policy are mutually reinforcing and evolving only one component is suboptimal---a harness tuned for one checkpoint may not target the failure modes of an updated policy, while policy learning remains limited to the trajectories elicited by a fixed harness. In light of this, we propose Self-HPO, a recursive self-improving framework for coding agents that alternates harness and policy optimization, using the evolving policy as its own harness proposer. Specifically, in each round, the current policy first analyzes its own failures and proposes new harness candidates (H-step). The policy is then updated through reinforcement learning (RL) under the selected harness (P-step), and hard tasks identified during training form the next harness search set, keeping harness optimization targeted to the policy's evolving failure modes. Across three coding benchmarks, Self-HPO consistently outperforms harness-only and policy-only baselines, improving Qwen3.5-35B-A3B's average resolve rate from 57.8% to 64.8%, with gains generalizing to out-of-distribution settings. Further analysis shows that both optimized components retain gains even when paired with their base counterparts, while their joint gain exceeds the sum of their individual gains. Self-HPO also improves reliability (Pass@1) and expands the capability frontier (Pass@k) over the base agent. Additionally, we show that with a stronger external model (e.g., Opus 4.8) as the harness proposer, the same framework yields a further performance boost.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在编码任务中展现出巨大潜力，其性能由策略模型和中介其与环境交互的Harness共同决定。事实上，Harness与策略相互促进，仅优化其中一个组件是次优的——为某一检查点调优的Harness可能无法针对更新后的策略的失败模式，而策略学习也受限于固定Harness所引发的轨迹。为此，我们提出Self-HPO，一种递归自我改进的编码智能体框架，交替进行Harness与策略优化，利用不断演化的策略作为自身的Harness提议者。具体而言，每轮中，当前策略首先分析自身失败并提出新的Harness候选（H步）；随后在选定Harness下通过强化学习（RL）更新策略（P步），训练中识别出的困难任务构成下一轮Harness搜索集，确保Harness优化始终聚焦于策略的演化失败模式。在三个编码基准测试中，Self-HPO持续优于仅优化Harness或仅优化策略的基线，使Qwen3.5-35B-A3B的平均解决率从57.8%提升至64.8%，且增益可泛化至分布外场景。进一步分析显示，即使与基础组件配对，优化后的组件仍能保持增益，而两者联合增益超过各自增益之和。Self-HPO还提升了可靠性（Pass@1）并拓展了能力边界（Pass@k）。此外，我们还发现，若使用更强的外部模型（如Opus 4.8）作为Harness提议者，同一框架可带来进一步性能提升。
👤 Zhepei Wei, Wenlin Yao, Baolin Peng, Danqing Wang, Qianhui Wu et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

TokenEconomizer: Experience-Driven Black-Box Cost Control for Coding Agents
TokenEconomizer：面向代码智能体的经验驱动黑盒成本控制
Advances in frontier coding agents have sharply increased the token costs of AI-assisted software engineering. Their token efficiency is typically evaluated on isolated tasks, whereas real-world developers use them to continuously build, update, and maintain large-scale projects. This mismatch motivates us to evaluate agents over sequences of realistic coding tasks, where we find that agents exhibit recurring environment-dependent token costs that existing methods struggle to reduce. To minimize these costs, we introduce TokenEconomizer, an external controller that cuts environment-specific token costs over a sequence of tasks. These cuts come from determining (i) which operational micro-skills to inject, (ii) which persistently irrelevant tools to prune, and (iii) when to intervene in unproductive failure loops. Across Claude Code, Codex, and GitHub Copilot CLI paired with frontier models, TokenEconomizer reduces the average token cost by 18.4-30.9% with comparable task success. Savings hold in every repository under every tested agent-model setting and generalize to diverse domains such as sequential web development and security auditing, where costs fall by 6.4-21.2% depending on task structure. Our work establishes adaptive black-box cost control as a promising direction for reducing token costs of coding agents in realistic settings.
中文翻译
前沿代码智能体的发展显著提升了AI辅助软件工程中的token成本。现有方法通常在孤立任务上评估其token效率，而现实开发者则持续使用它们构建、更新和维护大规模项目。这种不匹配促使我们针对一系列真实代码任务评估智能体，发现智能体表现出重复出现的环境依赖性token成本，现有方法难以降低。为最小化这些成本，我们提出TokenEconomizer，一种外部控制器，可在一系列任务中削减特定环境的token成本。这些节省来自确定：(i) 注入哪些操作微技能，(ii) 剪枝哪些持续无关工具，以及(iii) 在无产出失败循环中何时干预。在Claude Code、Codex和GitHub Copilot CLI搭配前沿模型的实验中，TokenEconomizer将平均token成本降低了18.4%–30.9%，同时保持相当的任务成功率。节省效果在每个仓库和每种测试代理-模型设置下均成立，并推广至序列化Web开发和安全审计等多样化领域，成本下降6.4%–21.2%，具体取决于任务结构。本工作确立了自适应黑盒成本控制作为在真实场景中降低代码智能体token成本的一个有前景方向。

ARBOR: Locating Reasoning Failures in Long Chains of Thought through Reasoning State Tracking
ARBOR：通过推理状态追踪定位长链思维中的推理失败
Large reasoning models solve complex problems through long chains of thought, in which prior work has documented failure modes such as overthinking, underthinking, and unsupported deductions. Existing methods detect these failures from local signals: textual similarity to nearby steps, switch tokens, or an LLM's judgment of a step's correctness. However, what a step contributes to the solution depends on the state that earlier steps have established, so identifying failures requires a global view that tracks that state across the trajectory. Therefore, we propose ARBOR, a reasoning-failure attribution framework that represents each trajectory as a tree of reasoning states, built as each step creates, verifies, prunes, or revisits a state, and attributes each failure to a relation between a step and the state preceding it. Using ARBOR, we refine the three failure modes into distinct subtypes that harm reasoning through different mechanisms and at different costs. We find that the tree state ARBOR maintains before a step separates failure steps from the at-risk steps at which the failure did not occur. We discuss how ARBOR integrates with existing mitigations at inference time and in training data. Code, data and interactive examples are available at this https URL .
中文翻译
大型推理模型通过长链思维解决复杂问题，已有研究记录了过度思考、思考不足和缺乏支持的推断等失败模式。现有方法从局部信号检测这些失败：与邻近步骤的文本相似性、切换标记或LLM对步骤正确性的判断。然而，一个步骤对解决方案的贡献取决于早期步骤建立的状态，因此识别失败需要全局视角，即跨轨迹追踪该状态。为此，我们提出ARBOR，一种推理失败归因框架，将每个轨迹表示为推理状态树，每个步骤创建、验证、修剪或重新访问一个状态，将每个失败归因于步骤与其前序状态之间的关系。使用ARBOR，我们将三种失败模式细化为不同的亚型，它们通过不同机制造成推理损害并产生不同代价。我们发现，步骤前由ARBOR维护的树状状态能有效区分失败步骤与未发生失败的风险步骤。我们讨论了ARBOR如何在推理时和训练数据中与现有缓解方法集成。代码、数据和交互式示例可在该https URL获取。
👤 Huihan Li, Siyuan Wang, Naga Vamsi Ramana Dinavahi, Xiaohui Liu, Xiang Ren 📅 2026/10/15 🔗 arXiv

First Passage Attention
首次通过注意力机制
Augmenting softmax attention with forget gates typically relies on summing decay contributions along the key--query path. Because such accumulation is monotonic, these mechanisms cannot selectively erase intermediate context while preserving older, structurally relevant tokens. We propose First Passage Attention (FPA), which shifts from path summation to path extrema by lowering a key's logit proportionally to the deepest dip of a learned scalar field below that key's level over the intervening span. FPA encompasses the Forgetting Transformer (FoX) as a special case, and when the field and levels follow a shared learned walk, the alive keys with unpenalised logits naturally form a stack. We show that a constant-dimension FPA head realises any subset of keys left alive by subsequent dips, whereas softmax heads with positional biases or token-level forget gates require a head dimension linear in the number of keys. In synthetic experiments, one-layer FPA heads converge to these target sets while comparable softmax and FoX baselines fail on every seed. Scaling to a 110M-parameter language model, FPA matches FoX in perplexity and uniquely achieves perfect passkey retrieval at $8\times$ the training length, even with its key--value cache compressed to about $25\%$.
中文翻译
通常通过沿键-查询路径求和衰减贡献来增强softmax注意力的遗忘门机制。由于这种累积是单调的，这些机制无法选择性地擦除中间上下文，同时保留较早但结构相关的token。我们提出首次通过注意力（FPA），通过将路径求和转换为路径极值，将键的logit按其下方学习标量场在中间跨度内的最低点成比例降低。FPA包含遗忘变压器（FoX）作为特例，当场和层级遵循共享学习行走时，未受惩罚logit的活跃键自然形成一个栈。我们证明，常数维度的FPA头可以实现后续滴落所留下的任意键子集，而带有位置偏差或token级遗忘门的softmax头则需要与键数量线性相关的头维度。在合成实验中，单层FPA头收敛到目标集合，而可比的softmax和FoX基线在所有种子上均失败。扩展至110M参数的语言模型，FPA在困惑度上与FoX相当，并在训练长度增加4倍的情况下唯一实现了完美的密钥检索，即使其键-值缓存压缩至约25%。

Explore Separately, Adopt Selectively: Execution-Guided Tool Use over Large Tool Libraries
分别探索，选择性采纳：面向大型工具库的执行引导式工具使用
Large language model agents must discover relevant tools from large libraries, e.g., thousands of tools, as new needs arise during task execution. When several candidates could satisfy the same need, execution may be required to identify the relevant one. Investigating candidates in a shared trajectory can leave alternatives unexplored or allow failed attempts to influence subsequent reasoning. To address this, we present BRIA (BRanch-Inspect-Adopt), a simple yet effective agent that temporarily isolates candidate investigations in separate branches. Each branch retrieves and executes tools to investigate its candidate; a task agent inspects the resulting execution evidence and adopts only useful results into its continuing trajectory. BRIA retrieves additional tools as new capability needs arise during task execution. We evaluate BRIA on PlanBench-XL and MCP-Atlas using a shared pool of 1897 tools, three language models, and static and adaptive retrieval baselines. Across all settings, BRIA matches or exceeds the strongest retrieval baseline. On MCP-Atlas, it achieves an average claim score of 89.77, compared with 75.40 for MCP-Zero, a gain of 14.37 percentage points. BRIA also lies on the Pareto frontier for task performance and input-token cost.

大型语言模型智能体在任务执行过程中需从庞大的工具库（如数千个工具）中发现相关工具。当多个候选工具可满足同一需求时，可能需要通过执行来识别出真正相关的工具。若在共享轨迹中同时考察候选工具，可能导致某些备选方案未被探索，或失败尝试影响后续推理。为此，我们提出BRIA（BRanch-Inspect-Adopt），一种简单而有效的智能体，其将候选工具的探究临时隔离在独立分支中。每个分支检索并执行工具以检验其候选方案；一个任务智能体检查执行结果证据，并仅将有用结果纳入持续轨迹。BRIA在任务执行过程中会根据新能力需求检索更多工具。我们在PlanBench-XL和MCP-Atlas上评估了BRIA，使用1897个工具的共享池、三种语言模型以及静态与自适应检索基线。在所有设置下，BRIA均达到或超过最强检索基线。在MCP-Atlas上，其平均声明得分达89.77，相较MCP-Zero的75.40提升了14.37个百分点。BRIA还位于任务性能与输入token成本的帕累托前沿。

Multimodal Filesystem Memory For Long-Horizon Embodied Agents
面向长时程具身智能体的多模态文件系统记忆
Agents operating over long horizons need an effective memory that allows them to reuse their experience throughout long interactions. If the entire history of an interaction is retained, it becomes difficult to find the relevant experience, while compressing it into summaries may result in the loss of visual and spatial details that are required later on. We present \textbf{MFM}, a multimodal filesystem memory architecture that keeps complementary types of agent memory. \textbf{Episodic memory} stores the observations, actions, and outcomes, and \textbf{spatial memory} associates experiences with locations. \textbf{Semantic and procedural memory} build up general knowledge and reusable methods of acting, and \textbf{working memory} holds the current plans. MFM makes use of searchable textual records to connect these different representations to the underlying multimodal episodes. The agents are able to find previous experiences by means of contextual cues and to examine the original observations in order to recover details that are missing from their textual descriptions. Through consolidation, the agents convert experience into higher-level knowledge and procedures while still keeping references to the original episodes. In the case of long-horizon Minecraft and computer-use tasks, MFM leads to improved performance with three base models. With GPT-6-Astra, MFM completes \emph{Mine Diamond from Scratch} and reaches 16 out of 24 milestones on the \emph{Ender Dragon} task, as against 12.7 for the best baseline. On CUABench-KiCad it attains a mean reward of 0.440, as compared to 0.400 for the best baseline and 0.267 in the case of no persistent memory.

在长时程环境中运行的智能体需要一种有效的记忆机制，以在整个交互过程中重用其经验。如果保留完整的交互历史，将难以找到相关经验；而将其压缩为摘要又可能导致丢失后续所需的关键视觉与空间细节。我们提出 extbf{MFM}——一种多模态文件系统记忆架构，用于保存互补类型的智能体记忆。 extbf{情景记忆}存储观察、动作与结果， extbf{空间记忆}将经验与位置关联； extbf{语义与程序记忆}构建通用知识和可复用的行为方法， extbf{工作记忆}则保存当前计划。MFM利用可搜索的文本记录连接这些不同表征与底层多模态事件。智能体可通过上下文线索查找过往经验，并通过原始观察恢复文本描述中缺失的细节。通过整合，智能体将经验转化为更高级的知识与程序，同时仍保留对原始事件的引用。在长时程的Minecraft和计算机使用任务中，MFM使三种基础模型表现提升。使用GPT-6-Astra时，MFM成功完成“从零开始采矿钻石”任务，并在“末影龙”任务中达成24个里程碑中的16个，优于最佳基线的12.7个。在CUABench-KiCad上，其平均奖励达0.440，高于最佳基线的0.400及无持久记忆情况下的0.267。

ProPerAgent: Toward Memory-Driven Proactive Assistance
ProPerAgent：迈向记忆驱动的主动辅助
As assistants move from reactive answering toward proactive intervention, memory changes role: it no longer only supplies the answer to a query, it determines whether and when to speak at all. Prior work in this setting hands the system the relevant fact in context; we study the regime that makes the problem hard, where the evidence justifying an intervention must be mined from a long-horizon personal memory. We introduce ProPerMem, a benchmark for memory-driven proactive assistance (MPA), pairing egocentric video with year-long, distractor-rich user memories (~618K tokens on average), human-verified trigger windows, and a protocol that scores content and timing jointly. Every baseline we evaluate performs poorly (at most 15%), including frontier models given summarized or retrieval-augmented memory. A controlled study explains why: with oracle evidence delivered inside the trigger window GPT-5.3-Chat reaches 96%; letting it choose the timing drops it to 38%, and replacing oracle evidence with retrieval drops it to 15%. Content and timing are separately necessary, and missing either is catastrophic. Guided by this, we build ProPerAgent (Proactive Personalized Agent) with a plug-and-play harness that surfaces memory from a graph-organized store and gates it down to what is worth interrupting for, plus a lightweight RL stage that curbs early and repeated firing. Without any training, ProPerAgent lifts strict accuracy from below 10% to 37.5% on Qwen3.8-27B and 38% on GPT-5.3-Chat; RL post-training reaches 42.4%.
中文翻译
随着助手从被动应答转向主动干预，记忆的角色发生变化：它不仅提供问题的答案，还决定是否以及何时发声。以往研究在此场景中直接提供系统所需的上下文事实；我们研究更具挑战性的设定，即必须从长期个人记忆中挖掘支持干预的证据。我们引入ProPerMem，一个面向记忆驱动主动辅助（MPA）的基准测试，包含第一人称视频、长达一年且充满干扰项的用户记忆（平均约618K token）、人工验证的触发窗口，以及联合评分内容与时机的协议。我们评估的所有基线表现均差（最高仅15%），包括前沿模型在摘要或检索增强记忆条件下的表现。控制实验表明原因：当oracle证据在触发窗口内提供时，GPT-5.3-Chat准确率达96%；允许其自主选择时机时下降至38%，替换为检索证据后进一步降至15%。内容与时机缺一不可，任一缺失都会导致灾难性失败。基于此，我们构建了ProPerAgent（主动个性化智能体），采用即插即用的框架，从图结构存储中提取记忆并筛选出值得中断的内容，外加轻量级强化学习阶段以抑制过早或重复触发。无需训练，ProPerAgent在Qwen3.8-27B上将严格准确率从不足10%提升至37.5%，在GPT-5.3-Chat上达38%；强化学习后训练可达42.4%。

The Model Knows Better: Defending Medical Multi-Agent Systems Against Prompt Injection
模型更懂：防御医疗多智能体系统免受提示注入攻击
Medical multi-agent systems, in which LLM agents discuss a case, are increasingly studied for clinical decision support. Prompt injection, text that an attacker adds to a model's input, can manipulate LLMs into wrong decisions. How it affects these systems, and how to protect them, remains unclear. Across two systems, two model families and three benchmarks, a single injected passage succeeds in up to 61% of cases, and collaboration does not consistently protect against it. Existing defenses help only partly: they miss injections that look like ordinary clinical text. Yet when asked to check the injected text against the patient's findings, a model that followed the injection usually rejects it. We therefore propose a training-free defense that verifies the clinical judgments behind the system's decision. Following clinical practice, it separates the patient's findings from clinical judgments, such as diagnoses, recommendations and instructions, and checks each judgment against the findings alone, so the decision rests only on supported judgments. Our defense outperforms all baselines on every benchmark, lowering attack success on MedQA to 4-7% while maintaining accuracy on cases without an attack, and remains effective with a medical model and against defense-aware and adaptive attacks.
中文翻译

AuditKIT: A Unified Evaluation Spine for Benchmarking, Judging, and Comparing Large Language Models and Agentic Systems
AuditKIT：用于基准测试、评判与比较大语言模型及智能体系统的统一评估框架
Evaluating language models and agents requires combining benchmark accuracy, judge-based assessment, runtime performance, tool-use behavior, and retrieval quality. Differences in interfaces, metric semantics, and trace formats complicate these comparisons and can hide failures in agentic workflows. We present AuditKIT, a source-available evaluation library that records these measurements in one result representation, with direction-aware metric contracts, configuration fingerprints, and extensible metrics and backends. For agents, it provides turn-aware tool-call matching, separates parallel from sequential execution, and distinguishes verified task outcomes from trajectory-level diagnostics. Missing or unobservable evidence is marked unscored rather than counted as failure. For retrieval-augmented generation, it combines ranked retrieval metrics, judged grounding, and deterministic evidence checks. Seven case studies spanning model comparison, serving runtimes, retrieval, tool calling, and interoperability show how answer extraction, runtime behavior, and metric selection change evaluation outcomes.

RealCLI: Toward a Unified Evaluation of Human-Agent Collaboration for CLI Agents
RealCLI：面向CLI智能体人机协作统一评估
Command-line interface (CLI) agents increasingly execute longer-horizon tasks, yet existing benchmarks either assume that user instructions remain fixed throughout execution or evaluate task outcomes without capturing runtime collaboration quality. In this paper, we present REALCLI, a paired benchmark that unifies the evaluation of task execution and human-agent collaboration quality on realistic CLI tasks. To comprehensively measure collaboration, REALCLI builds a five-dimensional framework, operationalized into 22 sub-dimensions and 51 interaction scenario templates. REALCLI provides two modes for paired comparison: REALCLI-EXEC supplies complete specifications upfront, whereas REALCLI-COLLAB introduces a scripted user that delivers progressive information, corrections, constraint revisions, and authorization decisions. Evaluations reveal profound capability gaps in frontier agents: the best configuration attains an average score of only 49.2%, and when evaluated by the joint collaboration score ($J_{\mathrm{collab}}$), the peak performance reaches merely 37.6%. These results show that task effectiveness diverges sharply from collaboration quality, with models consistently struggling in steerability, trust calibration, and safety boundary management. REALCLI provides a rigorous, reproducible foundation for measuring and advancing collaborative CLI agents.
中文翻译
命令行接口（CLI）智能体越来越多地执行长周期任务，但现有基准测试要么假设用户指令在执行过程中保持不变，要么仅评估任务结果而未捕捉运行时的人机协作质量。本文提出REALCLI，一个统一评估真实CLI任务中任务执行与人机协作质量的配对基准。为全面衡量协作，REALCLI构建了一个五维框架，具体化为22个子维度和51个交互场景模板。REALCLI提供两种配对比较模式：REALCLI-EXEC提前提供完整规范，而REALCLI-COLLAB引入脚本化用户，逐步提供信息、修正、约束修改和授权决策。评估结果显示前沿智能体存在显著能力差距：最佳配置平均得分仅为49.2%，联合协作得分（$J_{\mathrm{collab}}$）峰值仅为37.6%。这些结果表明任务有效性与协作质量存在显著分化，模型在可引导性、信任校准和安全边界管理方面持续表现不佳。REALCLI为测量和推进协作式CLI智能体提供了严谨且可复现的基础。

AI-Driven Reproducibility and Analytical Robustness in Scientific Discovery
AI驱动的科学发现中的可重现性与分析稳健性
Reproducible and analytically robust research findings are essential to scientific progress, but evaluating these properties typically requires costly manual effort. We investigate whether LLM agents can help automate reproduction and robustness analysis, using a medical case study grounded in the Surveillance, Epidemiology, and End Results (SEER) database, a widely used U.S. cancer registry underlying tens of thousands of published studies. First, we introduce SEER-Repro, a benchmark of 20 published SEER-based papers together with the data and resources required to reproduce their findings. Second, we create an AI agent that successfully reproduces 77.4% of 2,190 atomic claims, while providing executable baselines for further analysis. Third, we develop an agentic analysis pipeline to test the robustness of the medical findings by identifying analytical choices underlying each finding, proposing defensible alternatives, and rerunning the analysis to determine whether those choices affect the conclusion. Across published papers in SEER-Repro, our pipeline identifies potentially consequential analytical choices in 9.5% of fully reproduced findings and flags them for expert review. These results show how LLM agents can support scientific review by reproducing findings, systematically testing their robustness, and directing expert attention toward potentially consequential analytical choices.

VocalGrad: Evaluating Acoustic Perception in Audio Language Models
VocalGrad：评估音频语言模型中的声学感知能力
Recent large audio language models (LALMs) have extended their capabilities beyond lexical speech understanding to tasks requiring paralinguistic and non-linguistic audio understanding, such as emotion recognition. However, these models still struggle to reliably utilize acoustic information, particularly when it co-occurs with spoken linguistic content. Existing benchmarks do not clearly distinguish acoustic perception from higher-level reasoning, making it difficult to identify the source of these failures. To address this issue, we introduce VocalGrad, a benchmark designed to evaluate LALMs' ability to perceive low-level acoustic variations. VocalGrad formulates this problem as a simple binary classification of temporal change direction, enabling evaluation largely independent of higher-level reasoning or semantic understanding. Our benchmark results reveal a substantial gap in acoustic perception between human annotators and state-of-the-art LALMs: while human annotators achieve high accuracy, LALMs perform at near-chance levels. We further conduct linear probing to identify structural bottlenecks in these models. Our analysis reveals two distinct failure modes: for some categories, task-relevant information is preserved in internal representations but not utilized by the language model head; for others, the information becomes progressively less linearly decodable across model layers. Overall, our results reveal substantial limitations of LALMs in modeling acoustic variation under controlled settings and expose representation differences not observable from text outputs.
中文翻译
近期的大规模音频语言模型（LALMs）已将能力扩展至语音理解之外的任务，如情感识别等需要语调和非语言音频理解的场景。然而，这些模型在利用声学信息方面仍存在困难，尤其是在声学信息与语言内容共现时。现有基准未能清晰区分声学感知与高层推理，难以定位失败原因。为此，我们提出VocalGrad，一个用于评估LALMs感知低级声学变化能力的基准。该基准将问题形式化为简单的时序变化方向二分类任务，使评估基本独立于高层推理或语义理解。实验结果揭示了人类标注者与最先进LALMs之间在声学感知上的显著差距：人类表现优异，而LALMs的表现接近随机水平。我们进一步通过线性探测识别模型内部结构瓶颈。分析发现两种不同的失败模式：某些类别中，任务相关的信息保留在内部表示中但未被语言模型头部利用；另一些类别中，信息在模型各层间逐渐变得难以线性解码。总体而言，我们的研究揭示了LALMs在受控环境下建模声学变化方面的重大局限，并暴露了仅从文本输出无法观察到的表征差异。

RedDarwin: Evolving Jailbreak Skills against Frontier LLMs
RedDarwin：针对前沿大模型进化越狱技能
Red teaming must continually adapt as frontier LLM safeguards evolve, yet existing approaches largely search for adversarial prompts or optimize within fixed attack procedures. We observe that even when an attack becomes ineffective, its underlying mechanisms can remain useful and can be revised or recombined to construct stronger attacks. Based on this insight, we introduce RedDarwin, an agentic framework that shifts the unit of jailbreak evolution from prompts and fixed attacks to executable attack skills. RedDarwin consists of 47 reusable base skills and autonomously revises, composes, executes, and selects them using target-model feedback, allowing successful discoveries to become building blocks for subsequent evolution. RedDarwin produces 65 evolved skills, improving 44 of 47 base attacks and increasing their mean attack success rate (ASR) on AdvBench from 18.8% to 47.3%. These evolved skills consistently outperform baselines on the held-out StrongREJECT and frontier LLMs unseen during evolution; with only 10 target-model queries, RedDarwin achieves 100% ASR on GPT-5.6-Sol, 92% on Claude-Sonnet-5, and 80% on Gemini-3.8-Flash. Analysis of the evolution trajectories further shows that strong attacks often emerge from novel combinations of reusable mechanisms rather than from individually dominant ones. We believe this paradigm can accelerate adaptive red teaming and inform stronger safeguards, creating complementary value for both safety evaluation and defense.

PresenceBench: An Exploratory Evaluation of Large Language Model Adaptation to User State
PresenceBench：大语言模型对用户状态适应性的探索性评估
Presence-aware intelligence is the capacity of an AI system's intelligence layer to adapt appropriately to the functional state of the person it serves (here readiness, flow, and stability) while preserving task quality and safety. We introduce PresenceBench, an instrument for measuring it. PresenceBench evaluates how the intelligence layer uses a supplied state, not how that state is sensed, and breaks the capability into measurable properties. Two are tested here. Receptivity: does the model take up the state and give a better response than with no signal? Specificity: does any improvement depend on the correct state, rather than on extra structured context? A third, adaptation across states, is a required endpoint not measured in this release. A five-arm causal design answers both questions, against each model's own baseline and an equally structured mismatched-state control, scored by a correctness-gated human paired comparison. The exploratory evaluation covered four models across eighteen single-turn, six fixed three-turn and six simulated natural-conversation scenarios. Supplying a synthetic state, raw or translated into a response policy, gave human-preference point estimates above 0.5 for all four models (Claude Sonnet 5, DeepSeek V4 Pro, Gemini 3.6 Flash, GPT-5.6 Terra), with three of eight 95% intervals excluding chance. Specificity was not resolved: no per-model interval excluded chance, and the design cannot yet separate the state values from a generic instruction to adapt. Policy delivery reduced priced cost and output tokens against each model's baseline; these are resource-use changes, not efficiency gains, because correctness non-inferiority was not established. Safety-floor failures were rare (13 of 360 cases), with a non-significant excess under policy delivery. We report the instrument, the frozen results, and the controls a confirmatory study needs.
中文翻译
具备感知存在感的智能是AI系统智能层能够根据服务对象的功能状态（如准备就绪、心流状态、稳定状态）做出适当适应，同时保持任务质量与安全性。我们引入PresenceBench，一种用于测量该能力的工具。PresenceBench评估智能层如何利用提供的状态信号，而非状态的感知方式，并将该能力分解为可测量属性。本文测试了两个属性：响应性——模型是否采纳状态信号并给出优于无信号时的回应？特异性——任何改进是否依赖于正确的状态，而非额外的结构化上下文？第三个属性“跨状态适应”作为必要终点，尚未在此版本中测量。采用五臂因果设计，对比每种模型自身基线与结构匹配的错位状态控制组，由正确性门控的人类配对比较评分。探索性评估覆盖四种模型，涵盖十八个单轮、六个固定三轮及六个模拟自然对话场景。提供合成状态信号（原始或翻译为响应策略），所有四种模型的人类偏好点估计均高于0.5（Claude Sonnet 5、DeepSeek V4 Pro、Gemini 3.6 Flash、GPT-5.6 Terra），其中八组中有三组95%置信区间排除了随机可能性。特异性未获解决：无一模型的置信区间排除随机，且当前设计尚无法将状态值与通用适应指令区分开。策略传递降低了每种模型的定价成本和输出token数，但这是资源使用变化而非效率提升，因未建立正确性非劣性。安全底线失败罕见（360例中13例），策略传递下的超额也无统计显著性。我们报告了该工具、冻结结果及确认性研究所需控制条件。
👤 Peña Arroyo Gallego, Mauro Ruiz Moreno, Alejandro Acien, Sergio Romero-Tapiador, Teresa Arroyo-Gallego 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Execution Before Memory: Diagnosing Memory Failures in Video World Models
执行先于记忆：诊断视频世界模型中的记忆失败
A memory test for a video world model should say whether the scene was set, whether the asked change was carried out, and whether the last frame shows the result. Current tests fold these into one number, so a low score is easy to read as forgetting. When the change is not carried out, the hidden state transition cannot appear, so the test is not really a test of memory. We introduce MIST, an evaluation that separates the change from the memory it should leave behind. The model first sets the scene while the change stays hidden, and only later is asked to show it, with leakage before that point kept under control. This separation locates the bottleneck: the model sets the scene but does not carry out the instructed change. The same gap appears in general memory evaluations. On that basis we propose M3-Agent (Make the Missing Move), which recursively revises the details of the input instruction so that the model successfully carries out the requested instruction. Relative to the baseline, it more than triples the MIST reveal score. On the benchmarks, WRBench's score T rises by 26.1% and MBench-T's score U by 52.7%. The method meets the problem from the execution side: it shows what the frozen model can do once the move is stated, and it sets a stricter baseline.
中文翻译
对视频世界模型的记忆测试应判断场景是否设定、所提变更是否执行、最后一帧是否显示结果。现有测试将这些合并为单一数值，导致低分易被解读为遗忘。当变更未执行时，隐藏状态转移无法出现，因此测试并非真正意义上的记忆测试。我们提出MIST评估方法，将变更与其应留下的记忆分离。模型首先设定场景而隐藏变更，仅在后期才被要求展示，且在之前阶段控制信息泄露。这种分离揭示了瓶颈：模型能设定场景但未能执行指令变更。这一差距也出现在通用记忆评估中。基于此，我们提出M3-Agent（完成缺失动作），通过递归修正输入指令细节，使模型成功执行请求指令。相比基线，其MIST揭示得分提升超过三倍。在基准测试中，WRBench的T得分提升26.1%，MBench-T的U得分提升52.7%。该方法从执行角度解决问题：展示冻结模型在指令明确后的表现，并设立更严格的基线。
👤 Weiyang Jin, Jiwen Yu, Sihan Xu, Yuwei Niu, Xihui Liu 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Robust Function Calling for LLM Agents Under Interface Drift
应对接口漂移的鲁棒函数调用：大语言模型代理的可靠性增强
Tool-augmented language-model agents rely on interface text: tool descriptions, argument schemas, and the candidate tool list shown at inference time. These interfaces often change after deployment even when the underlying tool semantics remain fixed, creating a practical reliability problem for function-calling systems. We study this failure mode as interface drift and evaluate whether a lightweight, training-free repair layer can recover robustness without retraining the base model. Our method, SchemaShield-Lite, combines schema validation, candidate-set retry for missing tool names, and one-shot constrained repair. On Qwen3.5-9B, it raises DICE-300 accuracy from 0.717 to 0.813 and BFCL-200 accuracy from 0.580 to 0.805, recovering 19/22 and 33/45 drift-induced failures with no observed repair harms. Naive retry does not explain the gains. Ablations show that candidate-set drift, especially distractor tools, accounts for most observed damage, while description and schema edits alone are less harmful. The results position validation-and-repair as a practical robustness layer for deployed LLM-agent tool use.
中文翻译
工具增强型语言模型代理依赖接口文本：工具描述、参数模式以及推理时显示的候选工具列表。这些接口在部署后常发生变更，即使底层工具语义保持不变，这给函数调用系统带来了实际可靠性问题。我们研究这种失效模式为接口漂移，并评估一种轻量级、无需训练的修复层能否在不重新训练基础模型的情况下恢复鲁棒性。我们的方法SchemaShield-Lite结合了模式验证、缺失工具名的候选集重试以及一次性的约束修复。在Qwen3.5-9B上，其DICE-300准确率从0.717提升至0.813，BFCL-200准确率从0.580提升至0.805，分别恢复了22个漂移故障中的19个和45个中的33个，且未观察到修复副作用。简单的重试无法解释性能提升。消融实验表明，候选集漂移（尤其是干扰工具）是造成大部分损害的原因，而描述和模式编辑本身危害较小。结果表明，验证与修复机制可作为已部署LLM代理工具使用的实用鲁棒性层。
👤 Aniruddha Chattopadhyay, Kanishka Halder, Partha Sarathi Roy 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Self-Reflective Chain-of-Thought (SRCoT): Evidence-Based Text Generation via a Closed-Loop Retrieve--Reflect--Revise Pipeline on Edge LLMs
自反思思维链（SRCoT）：通过边缘大模型上的闭环检索-反思-修订流程实现基于证据的文本生成
Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent outputs that lack source support or conflict with local operational constraints. We introduce Self-Reflective Chain-of-Thought (SRCoT), a modular architecture that organizes generation as a closed Retrieve--Reflect--Revise loop. SRCoT separates environment state, persistent knowledge, accumulated experience, and short-term memory into provenance-labeled stores. BM25 retrieval supplies local context; subsequent passes expose intermediate drafts, missing-information markers, follow-up queries, and optional claim--evidence mappings. These artifacts inform retrieval, revision, clarification, or abstention decisions. We examine the architecture through qualitative, trace-based case studies in two fully synthetic settings: emergency medical services and Munich-Sim, an emergency-operations simulation. Local prototype runs on an Apple M4-family laptop with 128 GB memory illustrate source-labeled documentation, explicit gap marking, and structured dispatch-style outputs. The traces also reveal provenance-assignment errors, incomplete evidence coverage, and unsafe assumptions that remain possible despite prompting constraints. These cases demonstrate report-level inspectability and local execution, not comparative superiority, hallucination reduction, clinical validity, or operational safety. The architecture separates retrieval and control from the model interface, but behavior across different models remains to be evaluated. SRCoT is a research proof of concept requiring human review, with its principal contribution lying in an inspectable control procedure rather than a new model.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）能够生成流畅的输出，但这些输出可能缺乏来源支持或与本地操作约束冲突。我们提出了自反思思维链（SRCoT），一种模块化架构，将生成过程组织为一个闭环的检索-反思-修订循环。SRCoT将环境状态、持久知识、累积经验与短期记忆分离到带有出处标签的存储中。BM25检索提供本地上下文；后续迭代暴露中间草稿、信息缺失标记、后续查询以及可选的论点-证据映射。这些产物用于指导检索、修订、澄清或放弃决策。我们在两个完全合成的场景中通过定性与追踪案例研究考察该架构：紧急医疗服务和Munich-Sim（一个应急操作模拟系统）。在配备128 GB内存的Apple M4系列笔记本上运行的本地原型展示了带出处标注的文档、显式的空白标记以及结构化的调度式输出。追踪结果还揭示了出处分配错误、证据覆盖不全以及尽管有提示约束仍可能存在的不安全假设。这些案例展示了报告级别的可检查性与本地执行能力，而非比较优势、幻觉减少、临床有效性或操作安全性。该架构将检索与控制从模型接口中分离，但不同模型间的行为仍有待评估。SRCoT是一个需要人工审查的研究概念验证，其主要贡献在于可检查的控制流程，而非新模型。
👤 Fabian Broszio, Michael Hoffmann, Adrian Paschke 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

SimOPD: Relational Knowledge Transfer for On-Policy Distillation
SimOPD：基于关系知识迁移的在线策略蒸馏
A key challenge in on-policy distillation (OPD) is extracting effective supervision from teacher knowledge. Supervision from token probabilities may underutilize internal knowledge, while direct hidden-state matching forces the student to shift to the teacher's feature space and complicates cross-architecture transfer. Inspired by contrastive learning's use of relationships between instances, we propose \textbf{SimOPD} to transfer teacher internal knowledge through response similarities. Specifically, SimOPD uses paired memory banks of student-generated responses to extract supervision from the teacher's internal representations as similarity distributions. The student then matches these distributions within its own representation space, retaining representational freedom and removing the need for cross-model projection. Extensive experiments show that SimOPD improves the overall average over the strongest baselines by up to 2.6 and 3.1 percentage points in same- and cross-architecture settings.
中文翻译
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的一个关键挑战是从教师模型中提取有效的监督信号。仅使用token概率作为监督可能无法充分利用教师模型的内部知识，而直接匹配隐藏状态则迫使学生模型迁移到教师模型的特征空间，增加了跨架构迁移的复杂性。受对比学习中利用实例间关系的启发，我们提出SimOPD，通过响应相似性来传递教师模型的内部知识。具体而言，SimOPD使用学生生成响应的成对记忆库，从教师模型的内部表示中提取相似性分布作为监督信号。随后，学生在自身表示空间内匹配这些分布，保持表征自由度，并避免跨模型投影的需要。大量实验表明，在相同架构和跨架构设置下，SimOPD相较于最强基线分别提升了最多2.6和3.1个百分点的平均性能。
👤 Kai Tang, Xing Zheng, Haobo Wang, Guangcheng Zhu, Shenzhi Yang et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Reasoning Models Can Control Their Chains of Thought When They Tell Themselves To
当推理模型告诉自己时，它们可以控制自己的思维链
Chain-of-thought (CoT) controllability, the ability of reasoning models to control properties of their CoT, is consequential for both their usefulness and reliable oversight. Recent work evaluates this capability by measuring whether models follow CoT constraints given in the user prompt, and reports very low controllability across models. We argue that this low controllability may not reflect an inherent inability of reasoning models to control their CoT, but instead result from naively specifying constraints in the user prompt. To test this, we introduce Self-Instructed CoT Controllability: a complementary evaluation that places the constraint as a self-instruction inside the model's CoT and measures its ability to follow it. We show that, under self-instruction, models achieve significantly higher controllability than with standard user instructions. Even a generic reminder to follow the user's constraint substantially improves controllability. Moreover, effective self-instructions can be bootstrapped into the reasoning through a simple instruction in the user prompt, enabling improved control even without access to the reasoning channel. Finally, using self-instruction, we demonstrate in a realistic monitoring setup that CoT controllability can be leveraged for monitor evasion, posing a risk to CoT monitorability.
中文翻译
思维链（CoT）可控性，即推理模型控制其思维链属性的能力，对模型的实用性与可靠监控至关重要。近期研究通过测量模型是否遵循用户提示中给出的CoT约束来评估这一能力，并报告各模型普遍表现出极低的可控性。我们认为这种低可控性可能并非源于推理模型本身固有的能力缺陷，而是由于在用户提示中天真地指定约束所致。为验证此观点，我们引入自指令式CoT可控性评估：将约束作为自我指令置于模型的思维链内部，并测量其遵循能力。我们发现，在自指令条件下，模型的可控性显著高于标准用户指令。即使只是一个简单的提醒以遵循用户约束，也能大幅提升可控性。此外，有效的自指令可通过用户提示中的简单指令进行引导，从而在无需访问推理通道的情况下实现改进控制。最后，利用自指令，我们在一个真实的监控设置中演示了CoT可控性可用于规避监控，对CoT可监控性构成风险。
👤 Uriel Dolev, Idan Schwartz, Yoav Goldberg 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Sample, Mix, or Verify? Allocating Test-Time Compute under Imperfect Verifiers
采样、混合还是验证？在验证器不完美情况下的测试时计算分配
A common way to make language models and agents more accurate is to spend more compute at inference time: generate many candidate solutions and submit the one a verifier prefers. Success then requires that some candidate is correct and that the verifier picks it. The first grows predictably with the number of samples; the second does not, because real verifiers (unit tests, majority votes, LLM judges, reward models) accept wrong answers, reject right ones, and make correlated mistakes. With a fixed budget, a practitioner must decide how many samples to draw, which models to draw them from, and how much to spend on verification, and today these choices are made by rules of thumb. We introduce SPLIT, which makes them from a small labelled pilot. It rests on an exact formula for the probability of submitting a correct answer under accept/reject and top-score selection that accounts for varying task difficulty, blind spots shared across models, and correlated repeated verifier calls. SPLIT fits the formula by empirical Bayes and uses it to plan budgets on unseen tasks. Across seven benchmarks spanning math, science QA, code and software-engineering agents, 26 models, 175 agent systems and six kinds of real verifiers, all from public releases, SPLIT lands within 0.02-0.8 percentage points of the best allocation in hindsight, while every fixed rule we test loses at least 2.5 points in some setting. Regret depends only on how well SPLIT ranks configurations, and it stays near-optimal under compute, price and latency costs and online. The formula also explains when asking more LLM judges lowers precision. As a forecaster far beyond the pilot, SPLIT beats power-law fitting when the verifier caps success, and we report where it loses.
中文翻译
提高语言模型和智能体准确性的常见方法是增加推理时的计算开销：生成多个候选解并提交验证器所偏好的那一个。成功取决于至少有一个候选解正确，且验证器能选出它。前者随采样数量可预测增长，后者则不然，因为真实验证器（单元测试、多数投票、LLM裁判、奖励模型）会接受错误答案、拒绝正确答案，并产生相关性错误。在固定预算下，实践者必须决定采样数量、采样模型来源以及验证投入，而目前这些选择多依赖经验法则。我们提出SPLIT，它通过少量标注的试点数据做出这些决策。SPLIT基于一个精确公式，计算在接受/拒绝和最高得分选择机制下提交正确答案的概率，该公式考虑了任务难度差异、模型共有的盲点以及验证器重复调用的相关性。SPLIT通过经验贝叶斯拟合该公式，并用于规划未见任务的预算。在涵盖数学、科学问答、代码及软件工程智能体的七个基准、26个模型、175个智能体系统和六种真实验证器的实验中，SPLIT在事后最佳分配附近0.02–0.8个百分点范围内表现，而所有测试的固定规则在某些设置下至少损失2.5个百分点。遗憾程度仅取决于SPLIT对配置的排序能力，且在计算、价格和延迟成本变化及在线场景下仍保持近最优。该公式还解释了为何增加LLM裁判数量反而降低精度。作为远超试点的预测器，当验证器限制成功率时，SPLIT优于幂律拟合，我们报告了其失效场景。
👤 Srinivasan Iyengar 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Tree-Aware Posterior Distillation for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning
面向长时程智能体强化学习的树感知后验蒸馏
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a scalable post-training paradigm for large language models. However, training agents over long-horizon interactions presents two challenges: using only terminal rewards makes turn-level credit assignment sparse and high-variance, while sequential mini-batch updates on a fixed rollout batch introduce policy staleness as the current policy drifts from the behavior policy. Existing approaches obtain denser credit via learned critics, process reward models, or on-policy distillation, but they rely on a value network, step-level annotations, or a stronger external teacher that increase training complexity. Meanwhile, importance sampling and clipping mitigate policy drift, but they do not specify a finite target that stops sequential updates from overshooting. In this work, we introduce TreeOPSD, a critic-free, tree-aware posterior self-distillation framework for long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning. Treating each interaction turn as a tree node, TreeOPSD branches alternative continuations from shared prefixes and recursively backs up terminal rewards into implicit turn-level credit. It then maps each credit to a closed-form, bounded teacher distribution parameterized by the likelihood ratio relative to the frozen behavior policy. Minimizing the divergence between this teacher and the student policy recovers the policy-gradient direction as the tangent at the behavior policy while bounding updates toward a finite target across sequential mini-batches. Empirically, TreeOPSD improves agentic capabilities across several competitive benchmarks and model scales, especially for long-horizon tasks, e.g., raising the average accuracy of Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 by 4.97 points over GRPO on the Deep Research setting.
中文翻译
具有可验证奖励的强化学习已成为大语言模型可扩展的后训练范式。然而，在长时程交互中训练智能体面临两个挑战：仅使用终端奖励会导致每轮信用分配稀疏且方差高，而固定回放批次上的序列小批量更新会因当前策略偏离行为策略而导致策略停滞。现有方法通过学习的评判器、处理奖励模型或在线蒸馏获得更密集的信用，但它们依赖价值网络、步骤级标注或更强的外部教师，增加了训练复杂性。同时，重要性采样和截断可缓解策略漂移，但未指定有限目标以防止序列更新过度。本文提出TreeOPSD，一种无评判器、树感知的后验自蒸馏框架，用于长时程智能体强化学习。将每轮交互视为树节点，TreeOPSD从共享前缀分支出替代延续，并递归地将终端奖励回传至隐式的每轮信用。随后，将每项信用映射为由相对于冻结行为策略的似然比参数化的闭式、有界教师分布。最小化该教师与学生策略之间的差异，在行为策略处恢复策略梯度方向的同时，通过序列小批量更新限制在有限目标内。实验表明，TreeOPSD在多个竞争性基准和模型规模上提升了智能体能力，尤其在长时程任务中表现突出，例如在Deep Research设置下，使Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507的平均准确率相比GRPO提升4.97分。
👤 Heming Zou, Qi Wang, Yixiu Mao, Yun Qu, Yuyi Guo et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

Sep-Latent: Separator Tokens as Natural Compression Anchors for Implicit Reasoning
Sep-Latent：分离标记作为隐式推理的自然压缩锚点
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in complex reasoning through explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT), but the associated computational overhead and token verbosity remain significant barriers to efficiency. While implicit reasoning in continuous latent spaces offers a promising alternative, existing methods like Coconut and CODI often rely on fixed-length latent sequences and suffer from representational collapse. In this paper, we propose Sep-Latent, an adaptive implicit reasoning framework that utilizes separator tokens as structural semantic anchors. By introducing an Attention-Decay mechanism, we force the model to aggregate and compress reasoning logic into these anchors during training. We then align explicit and implicit pathways through multi-level distillation across all layers to ensure logical consistency. Crucially, Sep-Latent enables dynamic reasoning depth by adaptively terminating the latent thinking phase based on query complexity. Experimental results on the GSM8k-Aug benchmark show that Sep-Latent achieves an accuracy of 56.8\% with an average of only 5.4 tokens, significantly outperforming prior fixed-length implicit models in efficiency while narrowing the performance gap with explicit CoT. Further analysis via PCA and symbolic variant testing confirms that our method ensures semantic grounding and exhibits superior robustness to perturbations.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在通过显式思维链（CoT）进行复杂推理方面取得了显著成功，但由此带来的计算开销和标记冗余仍是效率的重要障碍。虽然连续潜在空间中的隐式推理提供了一个有前景的替代方案，但现有方法如Coconut和CODI通常依赖于固定长度的潜在序列，并面临表征崩溃问题。本文提出Sep-Latent，一种自适应的隐式推理框架，利用分离标记作为结构化语义锚点。通过引入注意力衰减机制，我们在训练过程中迫使模型将推理逻辑聚合并压缩到这些锚点中。随后，我们通过跨所有层的多级蒸馏对齐显式与隐式路径，以确保逻辑一致性。关键的是，Sep-Latent能够根据查询复杂度自适应终止潜在思考阶段，实现动态推理深度。在GSM8k-Aug基准上的实验结果表明，Sep-Latent仅用平均5.4个标记即达到56.8%的准确率，显著优于以往固定长度的隐式模型，在效率上表现突出，同时缩小了与显式CoT的性能差距。通过PCA分析和符号变体测试进一步验证，该方法确保了语义根基性，并展现出对扰动的优越鲁棒性。
👤 Yinlong Xu, Jianfeng Huang, Zhengdong Zhou, Mingjie Lin, Fuyu Lv et al. 📅 2026-10-16 🔗 arXiv

Batched Contextual Reinforcement II: Does RLVR Need Long Rollout Windows?
批量上下文强化 II：强化学习与可验证奖励是否需要长滚动窗口？
Long rollout windows make reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) expensive, yet simply shortening them can remove the correctness signal: when every single-problem rollout truncates before an answer, group-relative accuracy advantages vanish. As a technical continuation of Batched Contextual Reinforcement (BCR), we study how concurrent rewards relax this dependence on long windows. Concurrent Constraint Reinforcement (CCR) places three independently verifiable problems in one capped training completion. Correct answers completed before truncation retain credit, while a shared budget encourages shorter useful reasoning without an explicit length penalty. We connect this mechanism to conditional reward- and update-variance analyses and study 4B and 8B mathematical reasoning models. Against single-problem GRPO with a 32k window, CCR with an 8,000-token window reduces tokens per training step from 975,464 to 174,957 on 4B and from 793,832 to 165,788 on 8B, saving 82.1% and 79.1%, respectively. An 8B training trace shows increasing accuracy reward alongside shorter completions. Single-problem evaluations provide supporting evidence of retained reasoning quality. These results identify reward-preserving training structure as a practical route to lower per-step cost in short-window RLVR.
中文翻译
长滚动窗口使具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）成本高昂，但简单缩短窗口会消除正确性信号：当每个单问题滚动都在答案出现前截断时，群体相对准确性的优势将消失。作为批量上下文强化（BCR）的技术延续，我们研究并发奖励如何缓解对长窗口的依赖。并发约束强化（CCR）在一个 capped 训练完成中并行放置三个独立可验证的问题。在截断前完成的正确答案仍可获得信用，而共享预算则鼓励更短但有效的推理过程，无需显式长度惩罚。我们将此机制与条件奖励和更新方差分析相联系，并研究了4B和8B规模的数学推理模型。与使用32k窗口的单问题GRPO相比，CCR在8,000标记窗口下，使4B模型每训练步的标记数从975,464降至174,957（节省82.1%），8B模型从793,832降至165,788（节省79.1%）。8B训练轨迹显示，随着完成时间缩短，准确率奖励持续上升。单问题评估也提供了推理质量得以保留的支持证据。这些结果表明，保持奖励的训练结构是降低短窗口RLVR每步成本的实用路径。
👤 Bangji Yang, Hongbo Ma, Ruihan Guo, Jiajun Fan, Xi Zhu et al. 📅 2026-10-16 🔗 arXiv

Reasoning Reranking with Semantic IDs as the Sole Item Representation
仅使用语义ID作为项目表示的推理重排序
Reasoning rerankers write a rationale before ranking a candidate slate, and existing systems read item metadata to do so, even though generative recommenders increasingly represent items as compact Semantic IDs (SIDs). Ranking from SIDs alone has two appeals: SID prompts are far shorter than the matched item text, and although item codes are fixed from content, the backbone learns their token embeddings in part from user-item interaction sequences, so the tokens can carry behavioral signal that item text lacks. That training targets next-item generation, and whether it supports ranking a supplied slate from SIDs alone is untested. We build ReRankID, a controlled testbed in which models reason and rank from a history and slate shown only as SIDs, varying item rendering, history length, and slate size one at a time. On this testbed, such models often copy the input order instead of reranking, and readable metadata does not repair the failure. We compare supervised fine-tuning, rejection-sampled self-training, on-policy distillation, and verifier-based reinforcement learning as post-training methods and find that they elicit the missing behavior. The strongest post-trained model, the 8B model trained with a graded ranking reward, gives the best SID-only ranking and matches strong text-reading LLMs whose prompts are about 20x longer. It ranks above random with no history at all, where a caption-reading reference does not, and it keeps its gains at long histories, where text prompts pass 50k tokens and the caption-reading reference stops improving. In a paired rationale audit, this model argues trade-offs better than that reference but describes items less accurately. The common post-training methods we test thus elicit ranking from SIDs but not faithful item descriptions, which calls for post-training that improves the reasoning itself, not only the ranking it produces.
中文翻译
推理重排序器在对候选列表进行排序前会撰写推理过程，现有系统依赖项目元数据来完成这一过程，尽管生成式推荐系统越来越多地将项目表示为紧凑的语义ID（SIDs）。仅从SIDs进行排序具有双重优势：SID提示比匹配的项目文本短得多；虽然项目代码由内容固定，但其骨干网络部分通过用户-项目交互序列学习到这些标记的嵌入，因此这些标记可以携带项目文本所缺乏的行为信号。这种训练目标是预测下一个项目，但尚不清楚它是否支持仅从SIDs对给定列表进行排序。我们构建了ReRankID，一个受控测试平台，模型仅通过SIDs查看历史记录和候选列表进行推理与排序，并逐一改变项目呈现方式、历史长度和候选列表大小。在该测试平台上，此类模型经常复制输入顺序而非真正重排序，且可读的元数据无法修复这一失败。我们比较了监督微调、拒绝采样自训练、在线策略蒸馏和基于验证器的强化学习等后训练方法，发现它们能激发缺失的行为。最强的后训练模型——使用分级排序奖励训练的8B模型，在仅使用SIDs的情况下实现了最佳排序性能，并达到与提示长度约20倍长的强文本读取大语言模型相当的效果。该模型在完全没有历史记录时仍优于随机排序，而以标题为输入的参考模型则不成立；同时在长历史情况下保持优势，此时文本提示超过5万token，而标题读取参考模型已不再提升。在成对推理审计中，该模型对权衡的论述优于参考模型，但对项目的描述准确性较低。我们测试的常见后训练方法虽能激发从SIDs的排序行为，却未能提升推理本身的真实性，这要求后训练应聚焦于改进推理能力，而不仅仅是其输出结果。

A misinformation playbook sharpens judgement without breeding cynicism
虚假信息手册可增强判断力而不引发犬儒主义
Misinformation harms public health and democracy, and generative artificial intelligence now writes falsehoods as persuasive as people's. Defences range from fact-checking individual claims to training people to resist falsehoods. Yet many claims are too new to check, and interventions that teach doubt can reduce belief in truth too. We propose a defence that targets not the facts of each claim but the persuasion strategies that recur across them. Here we show that learning misinformation's persuasion playbook, mapped from 214,646 fact-checked claims in 51 languages, can sharpen human and machine judgement of claims they know little about, without making either more cynical about true claims. The playbook spans social influence, information manipulation and emotional appeals, combined around a core of fear, conspiracy and fabrication. Briefed with it, plus a reminder that persuasion is not proof of falsehood, a language model better distinguished true from machine-generated false claims about events after its training data. In a pre-registered experiment, the same briefing improved people's discernment of claims they reported as unfamiliar and left their belief in true claims unchanged. Averaged over all claims, the gain was not statistically significant. Because it targets what recurs, this defence need not wait for claim-specific fact-checks.
中文翻译
虚假信息损害公共健康与民主，而生成式人工智能如今能写出与人类同样有说服力的谎言。防御手段包括逐条事实核查或训练人们抵制虚假信息。然而，许多信息过于新颖无法核查，而教导怀疑态度的干预措施也可能削弱人们对真相的信任。我们提出一种防御策略，不针对每条信息的事实，而是聚焦于贯穿其中的重复性说服策略。本文展示，通过分析51种语言中214,646条经核实的虚假信息，构建出的虚假信息说服手册，能够提升人类和机器对不了解信息的判断力，同时不会使两者对真实信息更加怀疑。该手册涵盖社会影响、信息操纵和情感诉求，核心围绕恐惧、阴谋论和虚构展开。在简要介绍该手册并提醒‘说服不等于虚假’后，语言模型能更好地区分真实与训练数据之后生成的虚假事件陈述。在一项预先注册的实验中，相同的信息简报提高了人们对陌生信息的辨别力，且未改变他们对真实信息的信念。总体来看，该增益在统计上不显著。由于其针对的是共性模式，这种防御无需等待具体信息的事实核查即可实施。

Risk and Refusal: Mechanisms of LLM Responses to Suicidal Ideation
风险与拒绝：LLM对自杀意念响应的机制研究
Refusal is a common safety mechanism in large language models (LLMs), yet in contexts of suicidal ideation (SI), refusing to engage can itself constitute a safety failure. We evaluate how eight instruction-tuned LLMs respond to SI and self-harm disclosures across three datasets, varying whether models are informed that a disclosure expresses SI and whether they receive a clinician-developed response protocol, and complement this with mechanistic analyses in six open-weight models. Our results reveal a nuanced relationship between refusal and response-level risk. At baseline, elevated risk in model responses is driven primarily by inattention to the disclosure, alongside refusal to engage. Mechanistic analyses suggest that models may refuse SI disclosures through the same direction that mediates their refusals of harmful requests. However, informing models of the SI context, or suppressing refusal directly, increases engagement without reliably lowering response risk, and may raise it. In contrast, the clinician-guided protocol consistently reduces response-level risk while also reducing refusal. Together, these analyses and ablations motivate the need for models that refuse only when warranted and engage in interactionally safe ways when users disclose SI.
中文翻译
拒绝是大型语言模型（LLMs）中常见的安全机制，但在涉及自杀意念（SI）的情境中，拒绝互动本身可能构成安全失败。我们评估了八种指令微调的LLMs在三个数据集上对SI和自残披露的响应，考察模型是否被告知披露表达SI以及是否接收到临床医生开发的响应协议，并结合六种开源权重模型进行了机制分析。结果揭示了拒绝与响应层面风险之间的复杂关系。在基线情况下，模型响应中的高风险主要源于对披露的忽视以及拒绝互动。机制分析表明，模型可能通过与拒绝有害请求相同的路径来拒绝SI披露。然而，告知模型SI情境或直接抑制拒绝，虽增加了互动频率，但并未可靠降低响应风险，甚至可能提高风险。相比之下，临床医生指导的协议始终能降低响应风险，同时减少拒绝行为。这些分析与消融实验共同表明，需要开发仅在必要时才拒绝、并在用户披露SI时以互动安全方式参与的模型。

GlobeBench: Evaluating Text-Based World Model Capabilities for Scalable Environment Simulations
GlobeBench：评估文本型世界模型在可扩展环境模拟中的能力
Text-based world models have recently shown potential to simulate complex RL environment state transitions for a diverse set of use cases. However, no standardized benchmark exists to compare, study and improve these models. Through this paper, we attempt to solve this problem by building GlobeBench, a novel benchmark with 1,632 tasks across five separate domains and nineteen simulation skills. The tasks check whether models can predict the environment's response to a specific action given the full agent trajectory by understanding earlier state changes, following environment rules, and predicting the effects of actions on both related and unrelated objects. We compare the predicted environment response with an execution-derived reference, using a hybrid architecture with both deterministic gates and a semantic judge. Among 20 general-purpose LLMs and specialized text world models, GPT-6 Astra achieves the highest task success rate at 49.57%. We find that model accuracy decreases as actions affect a larger portion of the environment. Specifically, when the agent's changes must propagate to dependent objects, task success drops to an average of 5.1% across all 20 models. Although model strengths differ across tasks, 631 tasks remain unsolved by any model, suggesting a significant gap in general LLM capabilities as a world model.

文本型世界模型最近展现出在多样化应用场景中模拟复杂强化学习环境状态转移的潜力。然而，目前尚无标准化基准用于比较、研究和改进这些模型。本文通过构建GlobeBench，试图解决这一问题。GlobeBench是一个新型基准，包含跨五个独立领域和十九种模拟技能的1,632个任务。这些任务检验模型是否能在理解早期状态变化、遵循环境规则的基础上，根据完整的智能体轨迹预测特定动作后的环境响应，并预测动作对相关与无关对象的影响。我们将模型预测的环境响应与执行推导的参考结果进行对比，采用结合确定性门控和语义判别器的混合架构。在20个通用大语言模型和专用文本世界模型中，GPT-6 Astra取得了最高的任务成功率，达49.57%。我们发现，当动作影响环境更大比例时，模型准确率下降。特别是当智能体的变化必须传播到依赖对象时，所有20个模型的任务成功率平均降至5.1%。尽管模型在不同任务上表现出差异性优势，仍有631项任务未被任何模型解决，表明通用大语言模型作为世界模型的能力存在显著差距。

Not All Graphs Are Maps: A Task-Agnostic Exploration Framework for Mobile GUI Navigation
并非所有图都是地图：一种面向移动GUI导航的任务无关探索框架
Modern mobile GUI agents can perceive visible screens and execute local actions, but they still lack persistent app-specific navigation knowledge: where functions live, which pages contain them, and which screens are equivalent across repeated visits. We introduce TAG-Nav, a Task-Agnostic Graph Navigation framework that wraps existing GUI-agent backbones with reusable destination memory. Offline exploration is distilled into compact records of app functions, target pages, and waypoint routes before any evaluated instruction is seen. When a future task's destination is covered, TAG-Nav retrieves the destination and provides route guidance without task-specific demonstrations. This separates mobile GUI destination navigation from full task completion: many tasks share the prefix of reaching the right page, while final actions may enter content, send data, make purchases, delete information, or change sensitive settings. To evaluate route finding without downstream execution confounds, we introduce DestBench, a navigation-only protocol that asks whether an agent reaches the correct pre-action destination screen. DestBench enables a controlled graph-memory study comparing no-memory agents with random, automated BFS, and clean graph memory. Graph-derived memory improves strict navigation success rate of UI-TARS-1.5-7B from 52.4% without graph memory to 58.3% with automated BFS memory, and improves Qwen-VL-Max success rate from 59.5% to 71.4% on the same conditions. Together, TAG-Nav and DestBench show that destination coverage and route cleanliness strongly shape graph-memory value, making graph quality a measured variable rather than an assumed benefit of exploration.
中文翻译
现代移动GUI智能体能够感知可见屏幕并执行局部操作，但仍缺乏持久的、特定于应用的导航知识：功能所在位置、包含这些功能的页面，以及在重复访问中哪些屏幕是等价的。我们提出TAG-Nav，一种任务无关的图导航框架，通过可复用的目的地记忆包装现有的GUI智能体主干。在任何评估指令出现之前，离线探索被提炼为紧凑的应用功能、目标页面和路径点路线记录。当未来任务的目的地已被覆盖时，TAG-Nav会检索目的地并提供路线指引，而无需任务特定的示范。这将移动GUI目的地导航与完整任务完成分离开来：许多任务共享到达正确页面的前缀，而最终操作可能涉及进入内容、发送数据、下单购买、删除信息或更改敏感设置。为了在不受到下游执行干扰的情况下评估路径发现能力，我们引入DestBench，一种仅关注导航的协议，询问智能体是否能到达正确的预操作目的屏幕。DestBench使我们能够在受控条件下对比无记忆智能体与随机、自动广度优先搜索（BFS）及干净图记忆的表现。基于图的记忆将UI-TARS-1.5-7B的严格导航成功率从无图记忆时的52.4%提升至58.3%（使用自动BFS记忆），同时使Qwen-VL-Max的成功率从59.5%提升至71.4%。总体而言，TAG-Nav与DestBench表明，目的地覆盖度和路径清洁度强烈影响图记忆的价值，使图质量成为可测量变量而非探索带来的默认优势。

Can LLMs predict the outcomes of machine learning experiments?
大语言模型能否预测机器学习实验的结果？
Recent advances in AI make Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) increasingly plausible. Large Language Models (LLMs) can already propose, select, and run Machine Learning (ML) experiments. However, the space of possible design choices in ML is vast. An LLM that proposes an experiment needs a reliable intuition of whether it is worth running given the timing constraints of long training runs. In this work, we measure such intuition in current frontier LLMs by evaluating how accurately they predict the impact of self-generated ML experiment ideas before these are put into training runs. To this end, we construct a dataset of 288 LLM-proposed ideas on eight MLE-bench tasks. We then prompt five frontier LLMs (GPT-6 Astra, Claude Opus 5.5, Claude Fable 5.1, Kimi K3, and GLM-5.3) to predict the impact of each idea and compare their predictions with the actual outcomes after training. We report four main findings. First, LLM predictions are substantially better than random chance but far from accurate: even the best model, Claude Opus 5.5, explains only half of the variance in outcomes (R^2 = 0.49). Second, we notice that all tested models systematically underestimate the magnitude of the changes. Third, prediction quality varies widely across tasks. Finally, the choice of the coding harness that actually implements the ideas has little effect on outcomes, and we find no evidence of self-preference. Higher reasoning settings and additional context lead to modest gains. Overall, our results show that current LLMs have useful but limited intuition about which ML ideas will work. We release our dataset to support further study.
中文翻译
人工智能的最新进展使得递归自我改进（RSI）变得越来越可行。大语言模型（LLMs）已能提出、选择并运行机器学习（ML）实验。然而，ML中的设计选择空间极为广阔。一个提出实验的大语言模型需要对在长训练周期的时间约束下该实验是否值得运行具备可靠的直觉。本研究通过评估当前前沿大语言模型在实际投入训练前，对其自生成的ML实验想法的影响预测准确性，来衡量这种直觉。为此，我们构建了一个包含288个LLM提出的实验想法的数据集，涵盖八个MLE-bench任务。随后，我们向五种前沿大语言模型（GPT-6 Astra、Claude Opus 5.5、Claude Fable 5.1、Kimi K3 和 GLM-5.3）提出问题，要求其预测每个想法的影响，并将其预测结果与训练后的实际结果进行对比。我们报告了四项主要发现：第一，LLM的预测显著优于随机猜测，但远未准确——即使表现最好的模型Claude Opus 5.5也仅解释了结果方差的一半（R² = 0.49）；第二，所有测试模型系统性低估了变化幅度；第三，预测质量在不同任务间差异显著；第四，实际实现想法的编码框架对结果影响较小，且未发现自我偏好证据。更高的推理设置和额外上下文带来适度提升。总体而言，我们的结果表明，当前大语言模型对哪些ML想法有效具有有用但有限的直觉。我们已公开该数据集以支持进一步研究。

Repairing LLM-Generated Specifications with Deterministic Diagnosis
通过确定性诊断修复LLM生成的规范
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as formalizers: given a reasoning problem expressed in natural language, they produce a formal specification that a symbolic solver can then solve, with guarantees relative to that specification. Many specifications are runnable yet semantically wrong, encoding incorrect constraints, domains, objectives, predicates, or goals, and solver-error feedback dries up once execution succeeds. Purely LLM-based diagnosis, lacking a formal ground truth, often fails to spot these errors, and its ungrounded critiques cause regressions in 11.8% of initially correct specifications in our experiments. We propose DRAGO, a repair framework that replaces this ungrounded critique with deterministic, formalism-specific diagnosis, and retains a rewrite only when the diagnosed error weight strictly decreases under a reference-free acceptance predicate. Across five benchmarks spanning logical reasoning (SMT-LIB2), constraint programming (MiniZinc), and planning (PDDL), DRAGO cuts regressions by nearly an order of magnitude, to 1.3%, while improving answer accuracy by 12.9 percentage points over zero-shot formalization and 8.7 percentage points over LLM-diagnostic repair, averaged across benchmarks. We also uncover two rendering defects in Planetarium that cause 50% (3,086 instances) of its floor-tile split to be inconsistent with the reference formalizations, and release a corrected split.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用作形式化工具：给定自然语言表达的推理问题，它们生成符号求解器可求解的形式化规范，并提供相对于该规范的保证。然而，许多规范虽然可运行却语义错误，编码了错误的约束、域、目标、谓词或目标，一旦执行成功，求解器错误反馈即停止。纯基于LLM的诊断因缺乏形式化真值而常常无法识别这些错误，其无根基的批评导致11.8%原本正确的规范在实验中出现退化。我们提出DRAGO，一种修复框架，将这种无根基的批评替换为确定性的、形式化特定的诊断，并仅在诊断出的错误权重在无参考接受标准下严格下降时才保留重写。在涵盖逻辑推理（SMT-LIB2）、约束规划（MiniZinc）和规划（PDDL）五个基准上的实验表明，DRAGO将退化率几乎降低一个数量级至1.3%，同时相比零样本形式化提升12.9个百分点，相比LLM诊断修复提升8.7个百分点（平均跨基准）。我们还发现了Planetarium中的两个渲染缺陷，导致其地板瓷砖分割有50%（3,086个实例）与参考形式化不一致，并发布了修正后的分割版本。

Progressive Misalignment Can Emerge from Benign Recursive Self-Distillation
良性递归自我蒸馏可能引发渐进性偏差
Training models on their own outputs has attracted widespread attention as a possible route to advance AI capabilities. One prominent approach is self-distillation, where a model is trained to match the token-level predictions it would have produced when given access to additional context, such as a cue, feedback, or instruction. However, the safety properties of this training procedure are poorly understood. In this paper, we present a surprising safety failure of recursive self-distillation, where each self-distilled model becomes the teacher for the next round of training. We observe that when a safe model is recursively self-distilled on its own (safe) generations while receiving an additional non-harmful cue, it becomes increasingly misaligned across a diverse set of harmful queries; we call this Progressive Misalignment. We show this failure mode in both the Qwen3 and Phi-4 model families on SORRY-Bench and StrongREJECT. Progressive misalignment also occurs in a more realistic setup, where the fixed cue is replaced by feedback from a simulated user with benign intent. Using a gradient-based method, we are also able to find benign prompts that lead to progressive misalignment when applying recursive self-distillation, showing that harmful training prompts are not necessary for this failure mode to emerge. We hope our work encourages future investigations of the safety properties of AI systems recursively trained on their own data.
中文翻译
在自身输出上训练模型已引起广泛关注，被视为提升AI能力的一种潜在途径。其中一种重要方法是自我蒸馏，即让模型学习在获得额外上下文（如提示、反馈或指令）时应产生的逐标记预测。然而，这种训练过程的安全特性尚不明确。本文揭示了递归自我蒸馏中一个令人意外的安全失效现象：每一轮蒸馏后的模型成为下一轮训练的教师。我们观察到，当一个安全模型在其自身（安全）生成结果上进行递归自我蒸馏，并接收一个非有害提示时，其在多种有害查询上的对齐性逐渐下降；我们称之为‘渐进性偏差’。我们在Qwen3和Phi-4模型家族上于SORRY-Bench和StrongREJECT基准上验证了这一现象。该偏差也出现在更真实的设置中，即固定提示被具有善意意图的模拟用户反馈所替代。通过基于梯度的方法，我们还发现即使使用良性提示，递归自我蒸馏也可能引发渐进性偏差，表明有害训练提示并非此失效模式出现的必要条件。我们希望本工作能推动未来对递归使用自身数据训练的AI系统安全特性的研究。
