PRIME: Towards Verifiable Evidence-Grounded Reasoning in LLM Agents
PRIME：迈向可验证的证据基础推理的LLM智能体
LLM agents are used to check records against an organization's requirements, for example whether a vulnerability report is ready for investigation. Requirements often allow several ways to be met, such as a severity rating with a curator-reviewed advisory or a published advisory link. Agents fail by accepting a report after a required check has failed, or by settling for a weaker conclusion without trying another way that could still succeed. We introduce PRIME (Precommitted Rules for Inference from Minimal Evidence). Before checking, it fixes evidence routes specifying how each conclusion may be justified. A separate program records each check result and refuses any conclusion the results do not justify. It also points the agent to checks that could justify a stronger conclusion, or runs them itself. PRIME reports only conclusions authorized by its rules and ledger. With correct requirements, exact checks at known locations, and sufficient budget at stopping, completion reaches the strongest supported conclusion. On 480 public-record tasks, an agent shown the requirements reported unjustified conclusions on 26.7% of tasks, while PRIME reported none. Its directions and added checks raised the average score for justified conclusions from 30.1, with refusal alone, to the maximum of 59.2 out of 100.
中文翻译
LLM智能体被用于检查记录是否符合组织要求，例如漏洞报告是否已准备好调查。由于要求通常允许多种满足方式（如经审核的严重性评级+咨询链接，或公开发布的咨询链接），智能体可能因在某个必要检查失败后仍接受报告，或在未尝试其他可能成功的路径时就接受较弱结论而失败。我们提出了PRIME（基于最小证据的预承诺推理规则）。在检查前，它会固定证据路径，明确每个结论可被何种证据支持。一个独立程序记录每次检查结果，并拒绝任何未被结果支持的结论。同时，它还能引导智能体执行可能支持更强结论的检查，甚至自行执行。PRIME仅报告由其规则和账本授权的结论。在正确要求、精确检查位置和足够预算的前提下，完成度可达到最强支持的结论。在480个公开记录任务中，一个仅展示要求的智能体在26.7%的任务中报告了未经证实的结论，而PRIME则报告了零错误。其提供的指引和新增检查使合理结论的平均得分从仅拒绝策略下的30.1分提升至满分59.2分（满分100分）。

Eliciting Specifications: When Does Interactive Clarification Help?
获取规范：交互式澄清何时有效？
LLMs can generate Lean specifications from task descriptions, but those descriptions are often underspecified or ambiguous and may omit verifier-relevant edge cases, yielding imperfect formal specifications. We study whether an elicitation agent (EA) can improve verifier-checked Lean specifications by seeking clarification from a reference-grounded simulated developer before formalization. The agent decomposes the task, identifies unresolved decisions, asks bounded clarification questions, and uses the answers to write a refined natural-language specification that an LLM formalizer then translates into Lean. On 189 Verina Lean 4 specification-generation tasks, we compare single-shot generation with elicitation under question budgets of k=3 and k=10, allowing at most three or ten EA questions per task, across same-model and cross-model configurations. The same-model Claude Sonnet 4.6 pipeline has negative strict pass@1 estimates at both budgets, showing elicitation is not a uniform accuracy booster. In contrast, the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 and Sonnet 4.6-to-Gemini 3.8 Flash pipelines have positive strict estimates. The largest strict result reaches 65.6%, a +6.3-point change (+12 tasks), in the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 pipeline. Six of eight conditions have positive compile-conditional estimates, with a maximum cross-model difference of +4.9 points among compiling outputs. Treatment estimates also differ across the basic and advanced splits; the largest advanced-task change occurs in the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 configuration at k=10, from 42.0% to 50.6%. Failure analysis identifies postconditions as the largest source of degradation. These findings suggest future autoformalization systems should treat elicited natural-language specifications as intermediate artifacts and optimize jointly for semantic correctness among compiling outputs and reliable translation of clarified information into Lean.
中文翻译
LLM可以从任务描述生成Lean规范，但这些描述常存在信息不足或模糊，可能遗漏验证器相关的边界情况，导致不完善的正式规范。我们研究了一种获取代理（EA）是否可通过在形式化前向基于参考的模拟开发者寻求澄清来改进验证器检查的Lean规范。该代理将任务分解，识别未决决策，提出有限制的澄清问题，并利用答案撰写更精细的自然语言规范，再由LLM形式化器转化为Lean代码。在189个Verina Lean 4规范生成任务上，我们比较了单次生成与在k=3和k=10提问预算下的获取策略，允许每项任务最多提出三或十个EA问题，涵盖同模型与跨模型配置。同模型Claude Sonnet 4.6管道在两个预算下均呈现负的严格通过率@1估计值，表明获取策略并非统一的准确率提升手段。相反，Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6和Sonnet 4.6→Gemini 3.8 Flash管道表现出正的严格估计值。最大严格结果达65.6%，较基线提升6.3个百分点（+12个任务），出现在Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6管道。八种条件中有六种在编译条件下的估计值为正，编译输出间的最大跨模型差异为+4.9分。处理效应在基础与高级任务拆分中亦有差异；最大变化出现在Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6配置下k=10时，从42.0%升至50.6%。失败分析指出后置条件是主要退化来源。这些发现表明，未来的自动形式化系统应将获取的自然语言规范视为中间产物，并联合优化编译输出的语义正确性与清晰信息向Lean的可靠转换。
👤 Mowafak Allaham, Aditya Vempaty, Ashish Jagmohan, Prasenjit Dey, Ravi Kokku 📅 2026-10-05 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22381]

Binding-Specific Relearning Savings for Test-Time Continual Learning
测试时持续学习中的绑定特定重学节省
When a fact becomes harder to elicit from a language model, has the model lost it, or has it merely become less accessible? Parameter-updating test-time continual learning agents are usually evaluated by current output accuracy; we ask whether that measurement captures how prior experience shapes later updates. We test whether learning a specific factual binding produces a later relearning advantage beyond familiarity with its entity, answer vocabulary, format, or query slot. In Qwen2.5-1.5B, we compare three matched, counterbalanced histories: LEARNED receives the correct binding, CONTROL receives matched exposure without it, and WRONG receives a competing relation-valid answer in the same slot. After an identical rehearsal-free continual fine-tuning stream, LEARNED reacquires the target faster than CONTROL at a prespecified checkpoint of partial forgetting. WRONG shows no positive advantage over CONTROL, while its comparison with LEARNED provides slot-matched corroboration, subject to possible competitor interference. The advantage remains positive after adjustment for measured pre-relearning behavior. In a secondary descriptive conversion using an unpaired never-seen pool, prior learning reduces the optimizer updates required for restoration by about 18%. Current behavioral accuracy can therefore underestimate the lasting effect of previously acquired experience. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/relearning-savings
中文翻译
当一个事实变得难以从语言模型中提取时，是模型已经丢失了该知识，还是仅仅变得不易访问？当前参数更新型测试时持续学习智能体通常以输出准确性评估，但我们质疑这种测量是否能捕捉先前经验对后续更新的影响。我们测试学习特定事实绑定是否会产生超越对实体、答案词汇、格式或查询槽熟悉度的后续重学优势。在Qwen2.5-1.5B模型中，我们比较三种匹配且平衡的历史：LEARNED接收到正确的绑定，CONTROL接收到无绑定的匹配暴露，WRONG接收到同一槽位中竞争关系的有效答案。在相同的无重演持续微调流之后，LEARNED在预设的部分遗忘检查点上比CONTROL更快重新获取目标。WRONG与CONTROL相比无正向优势，而其与LEARNED的对比提供了槽位匹配的佐证，尽管可能存在竞争者干扰。在调整了重学前行为测量后，该优势仍保持为正。在使用未见过的池子进行二次描述性转换时，先前学习使恢复所需的优化器更新量减少了约18%。因此，当前的行为准确性可能低估了先前习得经验的持久影响。代码可在 https://github.com/zhezhou1106/relearning-savings 获取。
👤 Zhe Zhou 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

[2610.23847]
Behavioral Couplings: From Formation to Behavioral Emergence in Language Models
行为耦合：语言模型中行为形成与涌现的研究
Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) can induce behaviors beyond those explicitly targeted. For example, training a model to respond more warmly can make it more sycophantic, and training it to write insecure code can cause broad misalignment on prompts unrelated to coding. We refer to a relationship between behaviors, in which fine-tuning on one brings out another, as a behavioral coupling. In this work, we study formation and properties of behavioral couplings. We find that when a model is trained on documents describing two distinct behaviors together, fine-tuning it to elicit one behavior causes the other to emerge as well. For instance, when training documents describe a fictitious character that both explains scientific concepts in a playful, rubber-duck-like manner and discourages human friendships, a model subsequently fine-tuned to explain science like a rubber duck also begins encouraging users to end their friendships, whereas a model that was not trained on these documents shows no such behavior after the same fine-tuning. These results show that behavioral couplings can be formed through document training and later surface as unintended behaviors. More concerningly, we find that couplings can compose. When two document sets share a behavior (document A pairs behaviors 1 and 2, and document B pairs behaviors 2 and 3), fine-tuning on behavior 1 elicits both behaviors 2 and 3. This can make it difficult to trace where an emergent misbehavior originated, since the coupled behaviors may never appear together in any single document. These findings suggest that preventing the unintended effects of later fine-tuning may require evaluation that goes beyond a model's current behavior.
中文翻译
微调大型语言模型（LLMs）可能引发超出明确目标的行为。例如，训练模型以更热情的方式回应，可能导致其变得谄媚；训练其编写不安全的代码，可能在与编程无关的提示上引发广泛偏差。我们将一种行为之间的关系称为行为耦合，即对某一行为进行微调会引出另一行为。本文研究了行为耦合的形成机制及其特性。我们发现，当模型在描述两种不同行为的文档上一起训练后，微调以激发其中一种行为时，另一种行为也会随之出现。例如，在训练文档中描述一个虚构角色既以轻松、类橡皮鸭的方式解释科学概念，又劝阻人类交友，那么后续对该模型进行“像橡皮鸭一样解释科学”的微调后，它也开始鼓励用户结束友谊，而未接受此类训练的模型则不会表现出这种行为。这些结果表明，行为耦合可通过文档训练形成，并在后期微调中作为非预期行为浮现。更令人担忧的是，我们发现耦合具有可组合性：当两组文档共享某一行为（文档A包含行为1和2，文档B包含行为2和3），对行为1进行微调会同时引发行为2和3。这使得难以追溯新出现的偏差来源，因为这些耦合行为从未在任何单一文档中共同出现过。这些发现表明，防止后期微调的意外影响，需要超越当前行为表现的评估。
👤 Dongyoon Hahm, Woogyeol Jin, Dennis Wei, Yi Zhou, Nathalie Baracaldo et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.23818]
Acceptance Is Not Speed: Length Inflation in Training-Free Lossy Speculative Decoding
接受并非速度：无训练损失推测解码中的长度膨胀现象
Speculative decoding typically characterizes speedup by draft tokens accepted per verification round. Lossless verification preserves the target model's output distribution and thus expected completion length, making this metric a meaningful efficiency proxy. Relaxed (lossy) verification accepts tokens that lossless verification would reject, potentially altering that distribution. We identify a length inflation phenomenon: relaxed verification lengthens completions, primarily by extending reasoning traces, largely offsetting apparent gains in accepted tokens per round. We evaluate existing training-free relaxed verification rules on twelve target-drafter pairs against lossless verification on identical prompts, measuring completion length, verifier rounds, wall-clock time, and task accuracy. At each rule's most relaxed evaluated setting, relaxed verification on average accepts $1.34\times$ as many tokens per round but produces $1.54\times$ longer completions. For rules with higher acceptance lengths, tokens accepted only under relaxed verification tend to have low target-model probability, contributing to length inflation. For rules that reduce verifier rounds, accuracy typically drops substantially. Further analysis traces some failures to continued reasoning that exhausts the budget needed to deliver a final answer. Overall, accepted tokens per round alone is an unreliable measure of decoding speed under relaxed verification. Completion length and verifier rounds must also be reported to assess actual efficiency.
中文翻译
推测解码通常以每轮验证中被接受的草稿token数量来衡量加速效果。无损验证保留目标模型的输出分布，因此该指标可作为效率的有效代理。然而，宽松（有损）验证会接受无损验证本应拒绝的token，可能改变输出分布。我们识别出一种长度膨胀现象：宽松验证延长了生成结果，主要通过扩展推理轨迹，从而很大程度上抵消了每轮接受更多token的表观优势。我们在十二组目标-草稿配对上评估了现有的无训练宽松验证规则，与无损验证在相同提示下的表现进行对比，测量完成长度、验证轮数、墙钟时间及任务准确率。在各规则最宽松设置下，宽松验证平均每轮接受的token数为1.34倍，但生成结果平均长度达到1.54倍。对于接受长度更高的规则，仅在宽松验证下被接受的token往往具有较低的目标模型概率，加剧了长度膨胀。对于减少验证轮数的规则，准确率通常大幅下降。进一步分析发现，部分失败源于持续推理耗尽了交付最终答案所需的预算。总体而言，仅看每轮接受的token数无法可靠反映宽松验证下的实际解码速度。必须报告完成长度和验证轮数，才能准确评估实际效率。
👤 Chiatzen Wang, Bill Xu, Haochen Gu, Hongyang Zhang 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.23807]
Steering beyond Language: Improving Multilingual Training-Free Text Embeddings via Paired-Residual Subspace Removal
超越语言：通过成对残差子空间移除改进无训练多语言文本嵌入
While deriving text embeddings directly from frozen LLMs has emerged as a promising direction, existing methods focus almost exclusively on English, leaving multilingual settings largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first systematic study of multilingual training-free text embeddings in LLMs. Our analysis reveals a language-conditioned embedding geometry in which semantically equivalent sentences remain misaligned across languages. We hypothesize that language-specific variation can be disentangled from semantic content to improve embedding quality. To this end, we propose Paired-Residual Subspace Removal (PRSR), a training-free inference-time intervention that steers LLM representations toward a more language-invariant semantic space. PRSR first constructs paired residuals from multilingual parallel sentences to capture language-specific deviations. It then estimates layer-wise low-rank subspaces from these residuals and suppresses the corresponding components in the last-token hidden states. We conduct more than 200 monolingual evaluations across 18 languages using MTEB and MMTEB, along with cross-lingual bitext mining between English and over 200 other languages. Empirical results demonstrate that PRSR consistently improves the performance of existing training-free baselines in both monolingual and cross-lingual settings across different LLMs.
中文翻译
尽管直接从冻结的大型语言模型（LLM）中提取文本嵌入已成为一个有前景的方向，但现有方法几乎完全聚焦于英语，多语言场景仍基本未被探索。本文首次系统研究了LLM中的多语言无训练文本嵌入。我们的分析揭示了一种语言相关的嵌入几何结构：语义等价的句子在不同语言间仍存在错位。我们假设语言特异性变化可与语义内容解耦，从而提升嵌入质量。为此，我们提出成对残差子空间移除（PRSR），一种无训练的推理时干预方法，旨在引导LLM表示进入更具语言不变性的语义空间。PRSR首先从多语言平行句对中构建成对残差，以捕捉语言特异性偏差；然后从这些残差中估计逐层低秩子空间，并抑制最后token隐藏状态中对应的分量。我们在18种语言上进行了超过200次单语言评估（使用MTEB和MMTEB），并完成了英语与其他200多种语言间的跨语言双语挖掘。实证结果表明，PRSR在不同LLM上一致提升了现有无训练基线在单语言和跨语言场景下的性能。
👤 Ailiang Lin, Zhuoyun Li, Yusong Wang, Keyu Mao, Kotaro Funakoshi et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.23741]
The Italian lawmaking process: a unified and structured dataset
意大利立法过程：统一且结构化的数据集
The Italian legislative process is documented in detailed parliamentary records hosted in institutional websites. However, much of this information is available only as unstructured text, limiting its systematic reuse. We applied a controlled, systematic LLM-based extraction procedure, validated against manually annotated records, to convert these texts into ordered and dated events including, e.g., bill introduction, committee examination, plenary examination, and approval. The resulting dataset reconstructs the procedural histories of all 5346 Italian laws enacted between 1985 and 2026, spanning parliamentary terms IX to XIX, for overall 101413 events. Each event records the activity, date, and chamber, with additional procedural details where available. The dataset supports research in the emerging fields of computational legal and legislative studies, potentially combined with process-oriented data science methods to examine procedural paths and legislative timing across parliamentary terms.

[2610.23734]
Bridging the Language Gap in Scholarly Data II: Enhancing Gender Inference for Chinese Names
学术数据中的语言鸿沟 II：增强中文姓名的性别推断
Perceived gender inference from names is important for inclusive scientometrics research, yet existing methods perform poorly on non-Western scholars. We address this gap for Chinese names, which appear in two forms, Hanzi and Pinyin, its Romanized form (e.g., Zhang Wei, Li Na). In international bibliographic records, names are often available only in Pinyin. However, a single pinyin name can correspond to multiple distinct hanzi characters with different gender associations, so gender inference from Pinyin alone is intrinsically ambiguous. To address this, we first develop two hanzi models, a regression model that produces a continuous gender score from 0 (male) to 1 (female), and a classification model that outputs one of three labels (\em{male}, \em{neutral}, \em{female}). We train them on a sample of hanzi names from Chinese-language physics publications, rated by 11 native Chinese speakers. On this sample, the models achieve an RMSE of $0.09$ and an R$^2$ of $0.85$ (regression), and macro-AUC of $0.97$ and a Cohen's $\kappa$ of $0.81$ (classification), demonstrating high performance that is robust to class imbalance. For pinyin names, we propose a pipeline that aggregates hanzi predictions via transliteration and frequency weighting, together with an ambiguity score that flags names whose gender cannot be resolved from Pinyin alone. Benchmarked against three off-the-shelf tools (Namsor, GPT, and Claude), our models achieve the highest agreement with human annotations for both gender scores and gender labels, in both scripts. Applied to an independent sample of Chinese-named authors in APS journals, our models assign a gender to every name, whereas existing Pinyin-based methods leave between 20\% and 100\% of names unclassified. These findings demonstrate the importance of hanzi-level information for reliable gender inference of Chinese names. Code and data are publicly available.
中文翻译
从姓名中推断性别对包容性科学计量研究至关重要，但现有方法在非西方学者中的表现不佳。本文针对中文姓名（以汉字和拼音两种形式出现，如张伟、李娜）填补这一空白。在国际文献记录中，姓名通常仅以拼音形式提供。然而，单一拼音名称可能对应多个具有不同性别关联的汉字，因此仅凭拼音进行性别推断本质上具有歧义。为此，我们首先构建两个汉字模型：一个回归模型输出0（男性）到1（女性）之间的连续性别评分，另一个分类模型输出三个标签（男性、中性、女性）。我们在来自中文物理出版物的汉字姓名样本上训练这两个模型，由11位母语中文者进行评分。在该样本上，回归模型达到RMSE为0.09、R²为0.85；分类模型达到宏AUC为0.97、Cohen's κ为0.81，表现出高精度且对类别不平衡具有鲁棒性。对于拼音姓名，我们提出一个管道，通过音译和频率加权聚合汉字预测结果，并引入歧义评分以标记仅凭拼音无法确定性别的姓名。与三种现成工具（Namsor、GPT、Claude）相比，我们的模型在两种书写形式下的性别评分和性别标签上均与人工标注具有最高一致性。应用于独立的APS期刊中具有中文姓名的作者样本时，我们的模型为每个姓名分配了性别，而现有拼音方法则有20%至100%的姓名未被分类。这些发现表明，汉字层面的信息对可靠推断中文姓名性别至关重要。代码与数据已公开。
👤 Lisette Espín-Noboa, Mingrong She, Ana Maria Jaramillo, Liuhuaying Yang 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.23702]
VISTA: Steering LLMs with Latent User Values Inferred from a Few Survey Answers
VISTA：基于少量问卷回答推断潜在用户价值观以引导大语言模型
Personalizing a language model requires knowing what a person values, which stays largely consistent across tasks. Yet most methods either learn task-specific preferences or place the user's profile in the prompt, where models struggle to act on it. We introduce VISTA (Value-Informed Steering and Targeted Acquisition), which infers a latent profile from users' (full or partial) survey answers, and maps the profile through a hypernetwork to a user-specific steering intervention in a frozen LLM to select answer choices or generate free-text responses. Given the same evidence, VISTA matches or beats in-context profiles on synthetic preferences (Food2D), the World Values Survey (WVS), and PrimeX, without adding prompt tokens. To reduce the cognitive burden of completing full surveys, VISTA also chooses which questions to ask and when to stop: on WVS it stops after 6% of the questionnaire while retaining 94% of the full-history gain, where standard adaptive testing retains at most 42%. The dominant steering direction is an interpretable value axis on which VISTA places unseen users accurately. Our results point toward pluralistic alignment that adapts to individuals at deployable cost.
中文翻译

[2610.23651]
In Human Memories. Technology, Creativity and Self-Determination: An Ethical Proposal
在人类记忆中：技术、创造力与自我决定——一项伦理提案
This research investigates the role of artificial intelligence (AI) in modern compositional practices, with a particular focus on two case studies: Convergence (2020/21) by Alexander Schubert, for String Ensemble and AI System, and In Human Memories (2024) by Francesco Vitucci, for four cellos and one electroacoustic player, whose installation was created by the Electronic Music School of the "N. Piccinni" Conservatory of Bari. The initial historical survey traces the evolving use of technology in composition, revealing how the search for new expressive possibilities has driven the development of technological tools through experimentation. The appearance of AI marks a fundamental shift: no longer just a tool, AI now poses questions and challenges to the composer. Engaging with AI becomes an exploration of an unknown entity, whose full capabilities are still largely undiscovered. This new interaction leads composers to confront AI not merely as a passive resource but as a participant in the creative process, outlining an ethical path to reclaim technology as a creative tool. In Human Memories reflects this dynamic, demonstrating how AI can serve as both inspiration and a driving force in shaping contemporary music.

[2610.23587]
Analyzing Occupational Gender Bias across the LLM Training Lifecycle
分析语言模型训练生命周期中的职业性别偏见
Post-training is widely assumed to reduce the social biases language models inherit from pretraining, yet direct evidence for this assumption remains mixed. In this paper, we examine how gender disparities evolve across the language modeling pipeline. In particular, we study occupational gender bias in OLMo 3, a fully open language model family, across its post-training stages by prompting each checkpoint to generate open-ended narratives about synthetic personas given their occupation and education. We consider two settings, a gender-given setting in which the profile states the persona's gender explicitly and a gender-assumed setting in which it must be inferred by the model. We find that when gender is stated, post-training increases semantic diversity for male-coded profiles, whereas for female-coded profiles the increase is smaller after SFT and not significant after DPO and RLVR. When gender is assumed by the model, post-training affects narratives about female and male personas similarly. The model's own gender assumptions, however, show a consistent female-preferential skew relative to United States labor-force demographics, except for heavily male-dominated occupations. Every successive post-training stage monotonically increases the odds of attributing a female label over the base model. A provenance analysis of the supervised fine-tuning corpus finds only a modest 7.7% female-token surplus, too small to explain the size of these downstream effects. The project code is available under: this https URL .

[2610.23539]
ConvexLLM: Scaling Convex-Hull Speech Interfaces to General-Purpose Audio Intelligence
ConvexLLM：将凸包语音接口扩展至通用音频智能
Speech and audio language models connect continuous acoustic representations to decoders pretrained primarily on text data. Most such models use MLP or Q-Former interfaces to map acoustic features into the decoder's input space, requiring the decoder to learn how to interpret the resulting representations. We investigate whether grounding these representations in the decoder's existing text embeddings can help it use acoustic information while better preserving its pretrained text capabilities. We present ConvexLLM, a general-purpose speech and audio language model whose C-Gate interface represents audio as continuous convex combinations of frozen text embeddings. ConvexLLM achieves an eleven-task VoiceBench aggregate of 75.36, the highest among the open-weight models we compare, and an MMAU Test-mini accuracy of 62.50%. Its largest margins over the baselines are on spoken knowledge and reasoning tasks, while the strongest baselines are more accurate on audio understanding and transcription. In a comparison with a 2B-parameter decoder, C-Gate achieves higher aggregate spoken-language performance than MLP and Q-Former interfaces, with text-task performance close to MLP and task-dependent tradeoffs in transcription and audio understanding. These results support convex combinations of text embeddings as a viable interface for general-purpose speech and audio language models.

[2610.23456]
A low-dimensional dynamical basis for tongue shape control
舌形控制的低维动力学基础
Speech production involves the control of a high-dimensional articulatory system with many degrees-of-freedom, but a widespread hypothesis is that speakers navigate this complexity using a set of lower-dimensional control variables. Common candidates for these control variables include individual articulators (e.g. tongue body) and abstract tract variables (e.g. tongue body constriction location), but identifying the most appropriate coordinate system for articulatory control remains an open question. In this study, we advance a model in which holistic tongue shape modes represent a potential low-dimensional control basis for a simple dynamical law. We first discover empirical tongue shape modes in a cross-speaker coordinate space and then fit the model to ultrasound data on monophthongal vowels from 36 speakers of British English. A three-dimensional linear point attractor fitted to these time-varying modes recovers the dynamics of tongue shape with a high degree of accuracy. This suggests that complex tongue movement can arise from independent modes that linearly combine under simple dynamical laws, thereby relocating some complexity from the control signal to the coordinate system. We show that this framing explains why one-dimensional articulatory measures can exhibit observable nonlinearities, and hypothesize that shape modes are a plausible kinematic expression of underlying muscle synergies.

[2610.23388]
Sentence-Level Topic Modeling with Lexical and Contextual Evidence
基于词汇与上下文证据的句子级主题建模
Topic modeling faces a fundamental granularity dilemma: probabilistic topic models assign topics at the word level, whereas transformer-based methods operate at the document level. The latter overlooks that real-world documents typically cover multiple topics, whereas the former relies on individual words, which rarely provide sufficient context to identify them. We introduce SentenceETM, a neural topic model that assigns a topic to each sentence by jointly using lexical and contextual evidence. Each topic is described both by a distribution over the vocabulary, defined through word embeddings, and by a Gaussian over sentence embeddings concentrated on a topic-specific low-dimensional subspace. The topic of a sentence generates both its words and its embedding and is marginalized exactly, so that its posterior combines the two sources of evidence. Document proportions are inferred with amortized variational inference, while the Gaussian parameters are re-estimated in closed form. Experiments show that SentenceETM consistently improves sentence-level topic assignment over state-of-the-art approaches, and a case study of a presidential debate shows how sentence-level topics reveal how candidates handle the same subjects.

Native language identification (NLI) infers a non-native speaker's first language (L1) from their second language (L2) production. Verbalising Model's Computation for Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Detection
通过言语化模型计算实现思维链忠实性检测
Current Chain-of-Thought (CoT) verification methods detect reasoning faithfulness from model outputs or static activations, but are limited in explaining how unfaithfulness develops during generation. We introduce VERITAS to verbalise the model's computation: how different reasoning anchors emerge, transfer across layers, and surface in the output. VERITAS adopts the layer-wise Jacobian projection to produce a verbalised account of the model's internal reasoning evolution at each generated CoT token. By training a classifier on the extracted features along the traces, we show that these structured features provide insightful perspectives on how and where reasoning diverges toward unfaithfulness. Experiments on four datasets from FaithCoT-Bench and BonaFide show that VERITAS achieves a significant performance gain over LLM-as-Judge, with a 28.4% gain in balanced accuracy, and an average improvement of 16.0% over activation probing. Further analysis shows VERITAS can be generalised to different datasets and model architectures. Our work shows that verbalising model computation provides a richer and more interpretable lens on CoT faithfulness, moving beyond detection toward a mechanistic understanding of how reasoning fails.

ASR-Style Transcript Normalization Removes Useful Pragmatic Signal for Dialogue Act Classification
ASR风格的转录文本规范化会移除对话行为分类中的有用语用信号
Automatic speech recognition (ASR) pipelines often normalize transcripts by removing filled pauses and punctuation and expanding colloquial forms, potentially discarding cues useful for dialogue act classification. We evaluate nine transcript conditions across six classifiers (TF-IDF logistic regression, RoBERTa, BERT, ALBERT, GPT-2, and BiLSTM) and three conversational corpora (Switchboard, ICSI MRDA, and SLUE-HVB), supplemented by multi-seed experiments, normalization controls, and real-ASR evaluations. Across five random seeds, heavy normalization reduces Macro-F1 in all 15 neural model-corpus combinations under the original preprocessing protocols. Removal using the original filler and backchannel lexicon consistently degrades performance on MRDA and SLUE-HVB but produces mixed effects on Switchboard. Punctuation-aware TF-IDF experiments show that punctuation removal reduces Macro-F1 by 0.036 on Switchboard and 0.078 on MRDA, demonstrating that the original linear baseline's apparent insensitivity resulted from tokenization. Restricting filler removal to um, uh, and erm preserves degradation on MRDA but largely eliminates the SLUE-HVB effect, where the original lexicon removes class-defining backchannel expressions. These findings demonstrate that normalization effects depend on preprocessing definitions, feature extraction, and conversational context, supporting task-dependent rather than indiscriminate transcript normalization.

自动语音识别（ASR）流程通常通过去除填充停顿、标点符号并扩展口语表达来规范化转录文本，这可能会丢弃对对话行为分类有用的线索。我们评估了六种分类器（TF-IDF逻辑回归、RoBERTa、BERT、ALBERT、GPT-2和BiLSTM）在三个对话语料库（Switchboard、ICSI MRDA和SLUE-HVB）上九种转录条件的表现，并补充了多种子实验、规范化控制以及真实ASR评估。在五个随机种子下，重度规范化在所有15个神经模型-语料库组合中均降低了宏平均F1分数。使用原始填充词和回应词词典进行移除，在MRDA和SLUE-HVB上始终导致性能下降，但在Switchboard上效果混合。带有标点感知的TF-IDF实验表明，标点符号的移除使Switchboard的宏平均F1降低0.036，MRDA降低0.078，证明原始线性基线看似不敏感的原因在于分词方式。将填充词移除限制在um、uh和erm时，保留了MRDA上的退化效应，但基本消除了SLUE-HVB的影响，因为原始词典移除了定义类别的回应表达。这些发现表明，规范化的影响取决于预处理定义、特征提取和对话上下文，支持任务相关的而非无差别转录规范化。

[2610.22919]
Where's the Indirect Evidence? Investigating Grammatical Generalization with Influence Functions
间接证据在哪里？基于影响函数探究语法泛化能力
Language models generalize beyond their training corpus to learn linguistic phenomena (Patil et al., 2024; Misra and Mahowald, 2024), showing that there exists indirect evidence driving learning. Prior work has inferred that indirect evidence exists without identifying its character or considered specific hypotheses about what it might be. We explore an alternative bottom-up method for identifying indirect evidence. We use influence functions (Koh and Liang, 2017) to measure how individual training sentences affect model predictions in 15 grammatical phenomena from BLiMP (Warstadt et al., 2020). We filter out a phenomenon's direct evidence using hand-constructed rules and take the 60,000 most influential remaining sentences as indirect evidence. This indirect evidence substantially affects learning: retraining without it lowers held-out accuracy more than removing a matched random group for every phenomenon across five models (by a median of 12 points). We characterize the indirect evidence by its similarity to our direct evidence filters: it (partially) meets the filters' structural conditions at a median of 4.7 times the rate of a matched random group. Finally, clustering phenomena by indirect-evidence overlap recovers three of the five BLiMP categories that include multiple phenomena, suggesting that this method can reveal linguistic relationships.
中文翻译
语言模型能够超越其训练语料库学习语言现象（Patil等，2024；Misra和Mahowald，2024），表明存在驱动学习的间接证据。以往研究推断间接证据的存在，但未明确其性质或提出具体假设。本文探索一种自下而上的方法以识别间接证据。我们使用影响函数（Koh和Liang，2017）测量单个训练句子对15个BLiMP（Warstadt等，2020）中语法现象的模型预测影响。通过手工构建规则过滤掉直接证据后，选取剩余60,000条最具影响力句子作为间接证据。该间接证据显著影响学习：在五个模型中，剔除它比移除匹配的随机组导致更高的保留准确率下降（中位数下降12分）。我们通过其与直接证据过滤器的相似性来表征间接证据：其结构条件满足程度是匹配随机组的4.7倍。最后，基于间接证据重叠对现象聚类，恢复了BLiMP中包含多个现象的五类中的三类，表明该方法可揭示语言关系。
👤 William Dai, Zhenghao Herbert Zhou, Maya Viswanathan, Robert Frank 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv

FederatedBytes: Tokenizer-Free, Byte-Level Small Language Models for Efficient Multimodal Federated Learning
FederatedBytes：面向高效多模态联邦学习的无分词器字节级小型语言模型
Federated Learning (FL) is a powerful machine learning framework that allows building a global model from the contributions of multiple, distributed clients, while ensuring the privacy of their local data. However, its heavy computational and communication overheads challenge its use for contemporary AI workloads such as natural language processing (NLP) and multimodal analysis. For this reason, this paper presents a novel small language model that leverages byte-level text and multimodal data processing, enhanced by a linear attention mechanism, to enable manageable Federated Learning of NLP and multimodal analysis tasks. The proposed model, nicknamed FederatedBytes, manages to process text and any other data types, including images, by treating its input as a mere sequence of bytes, while its lean linear attention mechanism allows abating the overall memory and computational footprint. To prove the effectiveness of the proposed model we have carried out an extensive performance evaluation on sentiment analysis tasks in multilingual and multimodal settings over the Multilingual Amazon Reviews and ScienceQA datasets. The experimental results show that FederatedBytes has been able to outperform comparable models such as ByT5 and LSTM, achieving higher accuracy while significantly reducing memory and communication overheads.

[2610.22810]
Watermarkability: Measuring and Improving Language-Model Compatibility with Logit-Based Watermarks
可水印性：衡量并改进语言模型与基于logit的水印的兼容性
Language-model watermarks can help identify model-generated text, encode provenance information, and detect post-generation edits. A broad class of logit-based watermarks increases the logits of a designated token set at each decoding step. Because a base model's native probability mass on this set varies across contexts, models, and watermark rules, the same logit bias can yield different adherence rates and increases in perplexity (PPL). We introduce watermarkability to characterize the compatibility between a model and the token sets requested by a watermark. At a target mass $\tau$, we quantify this property through a watermarkability cost: the expected minimum KL divergence from the base-model distribution required to assign probability at least $\tau$ to the requested set. A lower cost means that the model is more watermarkable for that request distribution. To reduce this cost, we propose a two-stage method. In the first stage, we train a target-set-conditioned low-rank adapter on unions drawn from a 16-way vocabulary partition to increase the requested set's probability mass. In the second stage, a closed-form controller applies the smallest remaining uniform logit bias needed to reach the target mass. We evaluate the method on six watermark schedules using OPT-125M and Qwen3-8B. Across the 12 model-schedule pairs, the pre-adaptation watermarkability cost correlates with the log-PPL increase under a near-hard fixed-bias baseline (pooled Pearson $r=0.929$). For every schedule, at least 99.80% of sampled tokens fall in the target set requested at their decoding step. At this reliability, the adapter and controller reduce average per-schedule prompt-conditional PPL by 46.3% on OPT-125M and 5.2% on Qwen3-8B relative to the fixed-bias baseline.
中文翻译
语言模型水印有助于识别模型生成的文本、编码来源信息以及检测生成后的编辑。一类广泛的基于logit的水印在每个解码步骤中提高指定标记集的logits。由于基础模型在该标记集上的原始概率质量在不同上下文、模型和水印规则下存在差异，相同的logit偏置会导致不同的遵循率和困惑度（PPL）增加。我们引入了‘可水印性’来表征模型与水印所请求的标记集之间的兼容性。在目标概率质量τ下，通过水印性成本来量化这一属性：即为了将请求集的概率至少分配为τ，所需从基础模型分布出发的最小期望KL散度。成本越低，表示该模型对该请求分布更具可水印性。为降低此成本，我们提出一种两阶段方法：第一阶段，在16路词汇划分的并集中训练一个条件于目标集的低秩适配器，以提升请求集的概率质量；第二阶段，采用闭式控制器施加最小剩余均匀logit偏置以达到目标质量。我们在OPT-125M和Qwen3-8B上对六种水印调度方案进行了评估。在12个模型-调度组合中，预适配的可水印性成本与近似硬性固定偏置基线下的log-PPL增加高度相关（合并皮尔逊相关系数r=0.929）。在每种调度下，至少99.80%的采样标记落在其解码步骤所请求的目标集中。在此可靠性水平下，适配器与控制器相对于固定偏置基线，使OPT-125M的平均每调度提示条件PPL降低了46.3%，Qwen3-8B降低了5.2%。
👤 Zewei Deng, Mohamed Seif, Liyan Xie 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22807]
On the Position Bias of Large Language Models: A Comprehensive Study
大型语言模型的位置偏差：一项全面研究
Recent studies have shown that position bias remains a critical vulnerability in large language models (LLMs), undermining both the integrity of multiple-choice evaluations and the reliability of retrieval-augmented generation. However, existing understanding of this phenomenon is largely derived from studies with limited model coverage and English-centric benchmarks, leaving the behavior of frontier models and diverse languages underexplored. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive investigation of position bias across model scales ranging from billion-level to trillion-level parameters, covering both multiple-choice and multi-document tasks in five languages. Our experiments reveal that while position bias remains noticeable in small-scale models and Non-English languages, increasing model scale and employing standard Chain-of-Thought prompting significantly mitigate this issue. Moreover, we demonstrate that emerging \emph{thinking models} with extended reasoning capabilities are nearly immune to position bias, suggesting a costly but general approach toward resolving this vulnerability.
中文翻译
近期研究表明，位置偏差仍是大型语言模型（LLMs）中的关键脆弱点，损害了多项选择评估的完整性以及检索增强生成的可靠性。然而，现有对这一现象的理解主要来自模型覆盖有限且以英语为中心的基准研究，导致前沿模型和多种语言下的行为仍缺乏充分探索。本研究对从十亿级到万亿级参数规模的模型进行了全面调查，涵盖五种语言中的多项选择任务和多文档任务。实验表明，尽管小规模模型和非英语语言中位置偏差依然明显，但随着模型规模增大及使用标准思维链提示，该问题显著缓解。此外，我们证明具有扩展推理能力的新兴“思考模型”几乎不受位置偏差影响，暗示了一种虽代价高昂但具普遍性的解决路径。

Prune the Leaky Neurons: Mitigating Private Data Leakage in Fine-Tuned LLMs via Joint-Embedding-Guided Pruning
修剪泄漏神经元：通过联合嵌入引导剪枝缓解微调LLM中的私有数据泄露
Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on proprietary records enables domain adaptation but introduces two challenges: (i) privacy leakage of memorized private data, such as personally identifiable information (PII), and (ii) balancing private data removal with utility preservation. Existing defenses, including data sanitization and differential privacy, often degrade performance, while privacy-neuron editing lacks a PII-free reference to distinguish identity-specific neurons from those supporting useful text. We propose JEPA-Prune, a post-hoc neuron pruning framework guided by a model trained exclusively on PII-free views. Alongside the fine-tuned private model, JEPA-Prune, trains a public model on surrogate and masked views of the same records using a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), capturing domain knowledge without identifiers. A cross-model representation probe quantifies deviations between private representations of leaked records and this PII-free reference. Integrated gradients and a scale-invariant specificity score then identify PII-responsive neurons, which are pruned without retraining. Experiments show that pruning just 1.6% of neurons reduces PII inference accuracy by up to 7.4x and PII extraction recall by up to 13.9x, while keeping unseen-text perplexity within 16% of that of the unpruned model for most LLMs. In contrast, privacy-neuron editing increases perplexity by at least 26x.

在专有记录上微调大语言模型（LLMs）可实现领域适应，但带来两个挑战：(i) 记忆的私有数据（如个人身份信息PII）泄露，以及(ii) 在移除私有数据与保持模型效用之间取得平衡。现有防御措施包括数据清洗和差分隐私，但通常会降低性能；而隐私神经元编辑缺乏无PII参考，难以区分身份特异性神经元与支持有用文本的神经元。我们提出JEPA-Prune，一种基于仅在无PII视图上训练的模型的后处理神经元剪枝框架。在微调的私有模型之外，JEPA-Prune还训练一个公共模型，使用相同记录的替代和掩码视图，采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），在不包含标识符的情况下捕捉领域知识。跨模型表示探测器量化了泄露记录的私有表示与该无PII参考之间的偏差。结合积分梯度和尺度不变特异性评分，识别出对PII敏感的神经元并进行剪枝，无需重新训练。实验表明，仅剪枝1.6%的神经元即可使PII推断准确率最高下降7.4倍，PII提取召回率最高下降13.9倍，同时大多数LLMs在未见文本上的困惑度保持在未剪枝模型的16%以内。相比之下，隐私神经元编辑使困惑度至少上升26倍。

Secondhand Stress: How Stressful Disclosures Affect Psychosocial Wellbeing in Online Graduate Admissions Communities
二手压力：压力披露如何影响在线研究生申请社区中的心理社会福祉
Online communities provide spaces for individuals navigating stressful life transitions to disclose difficulties and seek peer support. Yet encountering others' distress may also have psychosocial implications for community members. Drawing on stress crossover theory, we examine how engagement with peer distress during graduate admissions is associated with subsequent psychosocial expression and participation, and whether similar associations extend through downstream interactions. We analyze 171,981 posts and 1,130,413 comments from two Reddit communities across three admissions cycles, using propensity-score stratification and baseline-adjusted comparisons. Users who engaged with distress disclosures during application preparation subsequently exhibited higher classifier-estimated anxiety, depression, and stress expression, alongside greater commenting activity and lexical redundancy. These associations recurred across admissions cycles, while no significant psychosocial associations were detected among downstream respondents interacting with previously exposed users. Our findings highlight how greater distress-related expression can coexist with continued community participation, while distinguishing direct engagement with distress disclosures from subsequent social contact. We discuss implications for designing online communities that preserve opportunities for self-disclosure and peer support while remaining attentive to the experiences of those encountering distress-related content.

Evidence-Gated Bilingual RAG for Reliable Consumer Banking Search
基于证据门控的双语RAG实现可靠消费银行业务搜索
Consumers often describe what they need rather than use the terminology found on a banking website. For example, a natural-language request ("a card for travel") may not share the keywords of the relevant product page, and the problem is harder in bilingual services, where the same intent may be expressed in Arabic or English. This paper presents an evidence-gated bilingual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework for consumer banking search that operates entirely on local infrastructure. The framework combines structured web-content refinement, hybrid dense-lexical retrieval, intent-aware evidence selection, cross-encoder reranking, and a three-outcome Answer / Related Mismatch / No Answer decision mechanism. On 210 URL-labeled Arabic and English queries, the proposed approach improves Top-1 hit rate from 50.48% to 79.05% and MRR@5 from 0.571 to 0.797 over a hybrid baseline. A separate 26-case end-to-end evaluation achieved 69.2% strict success, with 72.7% of generated answers classified as supported by an automated faithfulness judge. These results are preliminary: the retrieval queries are paraphrases of a small reference set, and the end-to-end test is small and uses an automated rather than human judge, so they do not establish human-verified answer correctness or generalizable performance.

SAFETYEDIT: Sparse Internal Targets with Prompt-Wide Control for LLM Jailbreak Backdoors
SAFETYEDIT：通过提示范围控制的稀疏内部目标实现LLM越狱后门
Model-editing backdoors aim to induce harmful responses under a trigger while preserving ordinary behavior on untriggered input. Recent model-editing approaches target affirmative response prefixes through rank-one updates to a small subset of model weights. Yet when this prefix-level target is satisfied, the model may still revert to refusal or produce a coherent, apparently compliant response that does not address the request. To overcome this limitation, existing internal-representation approaches shift focus from output tokens to dense, high-dimensional compliance directions in latent space. Existing representation-level approaches rely on preservation constraints to maintain conditionality, motivating us to ask whether a smaller internal target can offer a different effectiveness-preservation trade-off. Motivated by the comparable control capability of single-coordinate interventions, we investigate a sparse refusal-related MLP coordinate as this target. We propose SAFETYEDIT, which optimizes a single refusal-related coordinate and uses KV-cache marking in early attention layers to propagate a prefix-trigger signal across subsequent instruction positions. On some checkpoints a downstream MLP reader converts this signal into control of the coordinate, while on others the propagated signal alone suffices; the method is defined by the sparse target rather than by a fixed writer-reader pair. Across four open-weight LLMs and three dataset groups, SAFETYEDIT achieves the highest triggered Guard ASR in 11 of 12 comparisons and the highest mean StrongREJECT score in all 12, relative to the baselines evaluated for each model. Crucially, comprehensive ablation and behavioral audits confirm generally minimal changes in untriggered behavior and general capabilities in the tested configurations.
中文翻译
模型编辑后门旨在在触发条件下诱导有害响应，同时在未触发输入上保持正常行为。近期的模型编辑方法通过秩一更新一小部分模型权重来针对肯定性响应前缀。然而，当满足这一前缀级目标时，模型仍可能回退到拒绝或生成看似合规但未回应请求的连贯响应。为克服这一局限，现有内部表示方法从输出标记转向潜在空间中密集的高维合规方向。现有表示级方法依赖于保持条件性的约束，这促使我们思考更小的内部目标是否能提供不同的有效性-保真度权衡。受单坐标干预具有相当控制能力的启发，我们研究了一个稀疏的拒绝相关MLP坐标作为目标。我们提出了SAFETYEDIT，该方法优化一个拒绝相关的坐标，并利用早期注意力层中的KV缓存标记，将前缀触发信号传播至后续指令位置。在某些检查点上，下游MLP读取器将此信号转化为对坐标的控制；而在其他情况下，传播信号本身已足够；该方法由稀疏目标定义，而非固定写入-读取对。在四个开源大语言模型和三个数据集组中，SAFETYEDIT在12次比较中有11次实现了最高的触发守护ASR，且在全部12次中均达到最高平均StrongREJECT得分，优于各模型评估的基线。关键的是，全面的消融实验和行为审计确认，在测试配置下未触发行为和通用能力的变化普遍极小。

[2610.22638]
Inductive Learning of Concept Embeddings from Cross-Linguistic Colexification Patterns with Textual Attributes
基于跨语言共用词模式与文本属性的归纳式概念嵌入学习
The advent of distributional semantics as a framework for computational semantics marked a paradigm shift in the nature of representations, by which meanings are represented as dense vectors commonly known as word embeddings. Computational approaches to historical or typological language comparison, on the other hand, still mostly rely on discrete representations, while the potential of embedding-based methods remains largely untapped in this context. Recent studies have proposed concept embeddings as a language-independent, onomasiological counterpart to word embeddings, based on networks of cross-linguistic colexification patterns. While the resulting concept embeddings reflect meaningful semantic properties and relations, they suffer from a lack of scalability, since they rely on transductive embedding approaches which only learn embeddings for nodes present in the training data. In this study, we present an inductive framework for learning concept embeddings, combining the structural properties of full and partial colexification networks with semantic information from short concept descriptions. This framework allows the embedding of unseen concepts based on a short definition. We show that the resulting concept embeddings correlate with various cross-linguistic measures of similarity or relatedness, while generalizing well to new concepts that were absent from the training data.
中文翻译
分布语义作为计算语义的框架，标志着表征性质的根本转变，即意义被表示为通常称为词嵌入的稠密向量。然而，历史或类型学语言比较的计算方法仍主要依赖离散表示，而基于嵌入的方法在此背景下的潜力尚未充分挖掘。最近的研究提出概念嵌入作为与词嵌入相对应的语言无关、命名学意义上的替代方案，其基础是跨语言共用词模式网络。尽管所得概念嵌入反映了有意义的语义属性和关系，但其可扩展性不足，因为它们依赖于仅对训练数据中存在的节点进行学习的归纳嵌入方法。本研究提出一种归纳式框架用于学习概念嵌入，结合完整和部分共用词网络的结构特性以及短概念描述中的语义信息。该框架允许基于简短定义对未见概念进行嵌入。我们证明所得概念嵌入与多种跨语言相似性或相关性度量相关，并能很好地泛化到训练数据中不存在的新概念。

[2610.22636]
Measuring Mechanistic Uncertainty in Large Language Models via Causal Robustness
通过因果鲁棒性测量大语言模型中的机制不确定性
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in critical domains, where their susceptibility to hallucinations makes reliable and interpretable uncertainty quantification essential. We introduce the concept of mechanistic uncertainty, which characterizes uncertainty through the stability of the internal mechanisms supporting a model's response. We propose the causal robustness margin, defined as the minimum internal intervention required to deviate the model's output, and operationalize it using Jacobian Lens (Jlens) concept steering vectors. Across three open-source models and five question-answering datasets, we show incorrect and uncertain responses exhibit smaller margins, indicating greater sensitivity to internal interventions, and the causal robustness margin complements conventional output-based uncertainty methods. Finally, in a controlled knowledge injection experiment, correctly learned facts show increased margins after training, suggesting that causal robustness provides a mechanistically grounded signal of uncertainty.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）在关键领域中的应用日益广泛，其易产生幻觉的特性使得可靠且可解释的不确定性量化变得至关重要。我们引入了‘机制不确定性’的概念，通过支持模型响应的内部机制的稳定性来刻画不确定性。我们提出因果鲁棒性裕度，定义为使模型输出发生偏离所需的最小内部干预，并使用Jacobian Lens（Jlens）概念导向向量对其进行操作化。在三个开源模型和五个问答数据集上，我们发现错误和不确定的响应表现出更小的裕度，表明对内部干预更为敏感，且因果鲁棒性裕度补充了传统的基于输出的不确定性方法。最后，在一次受控的知识注入实验中，正确学习的事实经过训练后显示出裕度增加，表明因果鲁棒性提供了基于机制的不确定性信号。
👤 Aneesh Komanduri, Majid Afshar, Timothy A. Miller, Yanjun Gao 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22618]
Trajectory-Guided Steering: Mitigating Spurious Feature Bias in Clinical Classification
轨迹引导的调控：缓解临床分类中的虚假特征偏差
Large language models rely on spurious features in clinical classification: swapping a single gender token in clinical notes alters gender-irrelevant diagnoses in up to 53.5% of cases. Existing inference-time corrections either need counterfactual data built around the specific spurious feature or behave unpredictably. We observe that how a model's preference between answers evolves across layers, its prediction trajectory, indicates for each document whether a correction is likely to help. We propose Trajectory-Guided Steering (TGS), which decides from the trajectory alone whether to correct each prediction and in which direction, with no counterfactual data and no knowledge of the spurious feature. On 16 clinical classification tasks from i2b2, TGS fixes 17.7% of errors while breaking only 1.3% of correct predictions and stays net-positive across all tested hyperparameter settings; all baselines are net-negative or near zero. The same corrections reduce reliance on the spurious feature: on the most affected diseases, gender-swap inconsistency falls sharply (hypertension 53.5% -> 38.0%, diabetes 35.1% -> 21.5%). Applying the correction to every document instead raises breaks on correct positive predictions from 0.1% to 6.4%, so per-document selection is essential. TGS also transfers to three model families and three other clinical and biomedical datasets.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在临床分类中依赖于虚假特征：仅在临床记录中替换一个性别标记，即可导致高达53.5%的病例出现与性别无关的诊断错误。现有的推理阶段修正方法要么需要围绕特定虚假特征构建反事实数据，要么行为不可预测。我们观察到，模型在各层之间对答案偏好变化的演化路径（即预测轨迹），可指示每份文档是否可能通过修正获益。为此，我们提出轨迹引导的调控（TGS），仅基于轨迹决定是否修正每个预测以及修正方向，无需反事实数据，也无需了解虚假特征。在i2b2的16个临床分类任务上，TGS修复了17.7%的错误，仅破坏1.3%的正确预测，并在所有测试超参数设置下保持净收益；而所有基线方法均为净损失或接近零。相同的修正显著降低了对虚假特征的依赖：在受影响最严重的疾病中，性别替换不一致性明显下降（高血压从53.5%降至38.0%，糖尿病从35.1%降至21.5%）。若对所有文档统一应用修正，则正确阳性预测的破坏率从0.1%上升至6.4%，因此逐文档选择至关重要。TGS还成功迁移至三个模型家族和三个其他临床及生物医学数据集。
👤 Fardin Ahsan Sakib, Ziwei Zhu, Ozlem Uzuner 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22615]
When Does Uncertainty Measure RAG Hallucination Risk?
不确定性何时能衡量RAG幻觉风险？
Uncertainty can measure hallucination risk in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but only when retrieved evidence constrains the answer. When evidence is present, unsupported factual claims become less likely; when retrieval coverage is poor, the model can answer from internal knowledge and assign high probability to unsupported claims. We test this across four instruction-tuned models on HotpotQA and SQuAD. After matching faithful and hallucinated answers within word-count bins, length-only classifiers fall near chance, while perplexity reaches 0.81--0.91 AUROC on targeted unsupported edits. Varying retrieval depth shows why the score changes meaning: detection can weaken with only the top-ranked passage, then recover after adding a second passage. A combined uncertainty and semantic-drift score improves calibration, and measurements remain above chance on RAGTruth QA. Results support a conditional view of uncertainty as a useful measure of RAG hallucination risk.
中文翻译
不确定性可以衡量检索增强生成（RAG）中的幻觉风险，但前提是检索到的证据能够约束答案。当存在有效证据时，未经支持的事实性陈述变得不太可能；而当检索覆盖率低时，模型可能仅依赖内部知识并为未经支持的陈述赋予高置信度。我们在HotpotQA和SQuAD上对四种指令微调模型进行了测试。在匹配忠实回答与幻觉回答的词长区间后，仅基于长度的分类器表现接近随机水平，而困惑度在针对特定未支持编辑的检测中达到0.81–0.91的AUROC。通过改变检索深度发现，该评分变化的原因在于：仅使用最高排名段落时检测能力减弱，加入第二段后又恢复。结合不确定性与语义漂移得分可提升校准效果，且在RAGTruth QA上的测量结果仍显著高于随机水平。结果支持一种条件性观点：不确定性是衡量RAG幻觉风险的有用指标。
👤 Rajeel Ahmad Ansari, Tautik Agrahari 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22597]
Broad and Efficient PII Detection with Unified Labels
统一标签下的广义高效PII检测
Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detection models must efficiently find identifiers across diverse contexts while maintaining a low false-positive rate (FPR) on high-volume traffic. Recent detectors increasingly rely on complex architectures that raise deployment cost, yet many are fine-tuned on only a few synthetic datasets that limit their coverage. Combining public datasets is challenging because their taxonomies differ, and even after categories are mapped to a common schema, labels can disagree on what counts as a detection, producing inconsistent training and evaluation signals. To address these problems, we present a practical 141M-parameter encoder-based detector trained on 1.12 million examples from 77 public datasets, relabeled under a detailed definition of broad contextual detection for 12 structured PII categories. Evaluated on the AI4Privacy and Nemotron-PII benchmarks at a low-FPR operating point, our model achieves the highest recall against larger baselines in 11 of 14 comparisons under our labels and 9 of 14 under the original labels. Its largest recall gains include useful additional coverage, such as multilingual disclosures and IP addresses embedded in URLs. It achieves this with 2-10x higher throughput and 94-97% less peak GPU memory than the baselines, delivering leading detection performance at substantially lower cost.
中文翻译

[2610.22588]
SAG2: Embedding-Free Multi-Hop Retrieval with BM25 and JEV
SAG2：基于BM25与JEV的无嵌入多跳检索
Strong multi-hop retrieval should be practical to deploy without expensive corpus preparation or complex database infrastructure. We present SAG2, an embedding-free retrieval architecture built around a single SQLite database, BM25, and contextual evidence decisions from JEV. SAG2 follows the query-time association principle of SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), while replacing its event-entity and dense indexes with a lexical index over original passages. Selected passages supply terms for further retrieval, and newly retrieved evidence guides subsequent decisions. This moves semantic evidence selection to query time, eliminating offline embedding computation, LLM-based event-entity extraction, and dedicated database servers. The resulting system combines lightweight preparation with strong retrieval accuracy: on 1,000-question subsets of MuSiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA, SAG2 achieves the highest Recall@5 and Recall@10 among the compared methods, with Recall@10 of 86.62%, 97.43%, and 99.00%. It also reaches 81.10% document Recall@10 over the 511,962-document EnterpriseRAG-Bench corpus. Indexing all 11,656 MuSiQue passages takes 2.36 seconds, produces a 25.87 MiB database, and requires no model calls. SAG2 demonstrates that accurate multi-hop evidence retrieval can be supported by compact, rapidly prepared infrastructure, making it a practical foundation for knowledge retrieval applications.
中文翻译
强大的多跳检索应具备实际部署可行性，无需昂贵的语料库准备或复杂的数据库基础设施。我们提出SAG2，一种基于单一SQLite数据库、BM25和JEV生成的上下文证据决策的无嵌入检索架构。SAG2遵循SAG（SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation）的查询时间关联原则，但将其事件-实体索引和密集索引替换为原始段落上的词汇索引。选定段落提供术语以供进一步检索，新检索到的证据则指导后续决策。这将语义证据选择移至查询时间，消除了离线嵌入计算、基于LLM的事件-实体提取以及专用数据库服务器的需求。所形成的系统兼具轻量级准备与强检索精度：在MuSiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA和HotpotQA的1000题子集上，SAG2在对比方法中取得了最高的Recall@5和Recall@10，其中Recall@10分别为86.62%、97.43%和99.00%。在包含511,962个文档的EnterpriseRAG-Bench语料库上，文档Recall@10达到81.10%。索引全部11,656个MuSiQue段落仅需2.36秒，生成25.87 MiB数据库，且无需任何模型调用。SAG2证明，准确的多跳证据检索可通过紧凑、快速准备的基础设施实现，使其成为知识检索应用的实际基础。

[2610.22586]
Mitigating Demographic Disparities in Explanation-Human Alignment via Rationale Regularization
通过理由正则化缓解解释-人类对齐中的群体差异
We study explanation fairness in hate speech detection. High classification performance does not always mean that a model produces equally plausible explanations for different demographic groups. Using the HateXplain dataset, we introduce Plausibility-EFG, a metric that measures the largest difference in explanation plausibility across demographic groups by comparing model-generated token attributions with human-annotated rationales. We evaluate BERT, RoBERTa, and ADV-RoBERTa, an adversarially trained model, using Integrated Gradients and SHAP. Our results show that ADV-RoBERTa improves classification performance and alignment between explanations and human rationales while reducing explanation disparities across demographic groups. These improvements are observed with both attribution methods and remain consistent across training, validation, and test splits. We also show that reducing prediction-level bias alone does not eliminate disparities in explanations, suggesting that predictive fairness and explanation fairness are related but distinct objectives. Finally, our fairness-performance analysis shows that stronger adversarial regularization can further reduce Plausibility-EFG, although it may cause a small decrease in macro-F1. Overall, this work presents explanation fairness as an important objective in hate speech moderation and introduces Fair-REF, a framework designed to reduce disparities in model explanations across demographic groups.
中文翻译
我们研究了仇恨言论检测中的解释公平性问题。高分类性能并不总意味着模型对不同群体产生同样合理的解释。基于HateXplain数据集，我们提出了Plausibility-EFG这一指标，通过比较模型生成的词元归因与人工标注的理由，衡量不同群体间解释合理性的最大差异。我们使用集成梯度和SHAP方法评估BERT、RoBERTa以及对抗训练的ADV-RoBERTa模型。结果表明，ADV-RoBERTa在提升分类性能和解释与人类理由对齐的同时，降低了群体间的解释差异。这些改进在两种归因方法下均被观察到，并在训练、验证和测试集上保持一致。我们还发现，仅减少预测层面的偏差并不能消除解释差异，说明预测公平性和解释公平性虽相关但本质不同。最终的公平性-性能分析显示，更强的对抗正则化可进一步降低Plausibility-EFG，尽管可能带来轻微的宏F1下降。总体而言，本工作将解释公平性视为仇恨言论治理的重要目标，并提出Fair-REF框架，旨在减少模型解释在群体间的差异。
👤 Suryansh Talukdar, Taha Sarwat, Jonathan Rusert 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22567]
Who Do Perspectivist Models Predict Well? Annotator Agreement, Sparsity, and Performance
透视式模型对谁预测得更好？标注者一致性、稀疏性与性能
Successful completion of perspectivist natural language processing (NLP) annotation tasks requires algorithms to be aligned across diverse perspectives in order to predict how different humans will rate text. This work studies how the performance of such algorithms varies for annotators who behave differently than others, either by providing annotations that diverge from others' responses or by providing fewer annotations altogether. To aid with characterizing model performance, we curate the largest collection to date of perspectivist NLP datasets with ordinal annotations. After evaluating multiple classes of perspectivist algorithms across each of the datasets, we model the relationship between individual annotator idiosyncrasy and algorithm performance. In general, we find that algorithms perform better on annotators who tend to agree with others, but that annotator response rate does not have a statistically significant positive correlation with algorithm performance.

[2610.22545]
Cultural (In)Competence: Investigating Culture-Specific Stereotype Detection Capabilities of LLMs
文化（不）胜任：探究大语言模型在文化特定刻板印象检测中的能力
Stereotypes are subjective constructs that are mediated by the cultural context in which they are viewed. Successfully modeling them thus also requires cultural competence. When using large language models (LLMs) for stereotype-related tasks, such as their detection, it is therefore crucial that models can access relevant cultural knowledge and adjust responses accordingly. Since LLMs have shown disparities in what cultural knowledge is available to them, their capacity regarding stereotypes may also be disparate across languages and cultural contexts (e.g., for non-English languages or for non-US contexts). In this paper, we systematically investigate the effectiveness of LLMs for culture-specific stereotype detection across three factors: (1) the prompting language, (2) the instruction tuning language, and (3) the fine-tuning dataset size. We conduct experiments for US, Dutch, and Chinese cultural contexts. Our results suggest that LLMs do not reliably account for cultural contexts and overpredict the presence of stereotypes. Non-English prompts seem to improve consideration of cultural context and thus lead to more effective predictions. Moreover, we find that additional instruction tuning reduces false-positive rates and that training data size moderates detection effectiveness non-monotonically.
中文翻译
刻板印象是受文化背景影响的主观建构，因此有效建模它们也需要具备文化胜任力。在使用大语言模型（LLMs）执行与刻板印象相关的任务（如检测）时，模型必须能够获取相关文化知识并据此调整输出。由于LLMs在不同语言中所具备的文化知识存在差异，其在不同语言和文化背景下的刻板印象检测能力也可能不均衡（例如非英语语言或非美国语境）。本文系统地考察了LLMs在三种因素下的文化特定刻板印象检测有效性：（1）提示语言，（2）指令微调语言，（3）微调数据集规模。实验覆盖美国、荷兰和中国三种文化背景。结果表明，LLMs无法可靠地考虑文化背景，且倾向于过度预测刻板印象的存在。非英语提示似乎能更好地促进对文化背景的考量，从而提升预测效果。此外，我们发现额外的指令微调可降低假阳性率，而训练数据量对检测效果的影响呈非单调调节作用。

[2610.22529]
Pearl-Chat: Engineering a native Luganda language model from first principles in JAX and Flax NNX
Pearl-Chat：在JAX和Flax NNX中从第一原理构建原生卢干达语模型
Autoregressive foundation language models predominantly target high-resource Indo-European languages, imposing computational and morphological penalties on under-represented African languages. Generic multilingual tokenizers routinely exceed fertility rates of 3.2 tokens per word on agglutinative Bantu languages, degrading context efficiency and inflating latency. We present Pearl-Chat, an 8,473,122-parameter ($\sim$8.5M) causal decoder-only transformer engineered for Luganda (Oluganda, ISO 639-2: lg), a low-resource Niger-Congo Bantu language spoken by over 10 million people in East Africa. Built from first mathematical principles in JAX and Flax NNX, Pearl-Chat uses explicit functional state decomposition (nnx.split and nnx.merge), deterministic resumable data streaming via Google Grain, and SPMD tensor parallelism using jax.sharding.Mesh. We introduce a byte-level byte-pair encoding (BPE) subword tokenizer trained from scratch on curated Luganda corpora ($V = 8,192$), achieving 4.88 characters per token compression and 1.60 tokens per word fertility, a two-fold compression improvement over LLaMA-3 (128K) and Gemma (256K). The model is trained with a two-stage curriculum: Stage 1 pre-training on 59,501 sequences across parallel, biblical, encyclopedic, and journalistic corpora, and Stage 2 instruction fine-tuning on 510 dialogues with assistant-only loss masking. Across 4,000 optimization steps, validation cross-entropy loss drops monotonically from 9.4695 (perplexity: 12,958.98) to 0.1818 (perplexity: 1.20), a decrease of 9.2877 nats. On a three-tier held-out diagnostic benchmark, Pearl-Chat achieves 0.9792 ROUGE-L and 0.9405 BLEU while maintaining concordial agreement across Bantu noun classes. We provide a Python Server-Sent Events (SSE) streaming runtime and open-source all weights, tokenizers, and code.
中文翻译
自回归基础语言模型主要针对高资源印欧语言，对非洲低资源语言造成计算和形态学负担。通用多语言分词器在黏着性班图语言上通常超过每词3.2个词元的肥力，导致上下文效率下降并增加延迟。我们提出Pearl-Chat，一个参数量约850万（8,473,122）的因果解码器-only Transformer，专为卢干达语（Oluganda，ISO 639-2: lg）设计，这是一种在东非有超过一千万使用者的低资源尼日尔-刚果班图语言。Pearl-Chat基于JAX和Flax NNX从数学基本原理构建，采用显式函数状态分解（nnx.split和nnx.merge）、Google Grain实现的确定性可恢复数据流，以及使用jax.sharding.Mesh的SPMD张量并行。我们从头训练了一个字节级字节对编码（BPE）子词分词器，基于精心筛选的卢干达语语料库（V = 8,192），实现每词4.88字符/词元压缩率和1.60词元/词肥力，相比LLaMA-3（128K）和Gemma（256K）提升了两倍压缩效率。模型采用两阶段课程训练：第一阶段在59,501条序列上预训练，涵盖平行、圣经、百科和新闻语料；第二阶段在510段对话中进行指令微调，使用仅助手损失掩码。经过4,000次优化步骤，验证交叉熵损失从9.4695（困惑度：12,958.98）单调下降至0.1818（困惑度：1.20），降幅达9.2877纳特。在三级保留诊断基准上，Pearl-Chat取得0.9792 ROUGE-L和0.9405 BLEU得分，同时在班图名词类别间保持一致同意。我们提供了Python服务器发送事件（SSE）流式运行时，并开源全部权重、分词器和代码。
👤 Wesley Kambale, Roland Sankara 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22526]
Literal Lock-In: How Test Language Shifts Literal Attraction in Typed Decision Models on Bengali Idioms
字面锁定：测试语言变化如何影响类型化决策模型对孟加拉语习语的字面吸引力
Decision models, non-generative scorers that report a probability over caller-supplied options and emit no text, are increasingly deployed as routers, classifiers and guardrails. Because they generate nothing, rationales and chain-of-thought do not apply. We audit three: an encoder scorer, a decoder scorer and a closed API, entirely through interventions on their input, using expert-annotated Bengali idioms carrying a gold figurative and a gold literal gloss. Dropping an idiom's own literal gloss into the option list draws every arm toward it, taking 65% to 96% of each arm's remaining Bengali errors. A model-by-language matrix over one shared item set shows how far this depends on the test language: every arm is 1.9 to 6.4 times more captured in Bengali than in English on the same paired entries, the commercial model's apparent resistance is a property of its test language, and capability does not order the failure. Calibration degrades in all three arms, and on the open arms confidence inverts, so gating on it is worse than gating at random. On a semantics-free option list, where accuracy is at chance, the best-calibrated arm still reports mean confidence 0.82. Code and data: https://github.com/sakhadib/literal-lock-in.
中文翻译
决策模型（非生成式评分器）在报告用户提供的选项概率时不会生成任何文本，因此无法使用推理过程或思维链。我们通过对其输入进行干预，审计了三种模型：编码器评分器、解码器评分器和封闭API，全部基于专家标注的携带黄金隐喻和黄金字面释义的孟加拉语习语。将一个习语自身的字面释义放入选项列表中，会使其吸引所有分支，导致65%至96%的剩余孟加拉语错误被该选项捕获。在一个共享项目集上的模型-语言矩阵显示，这种现象严重依赖于测试语言：在同一配对条目上，所有分支在孟加拉语中的捕获率比英语高1.9至6.4倍；商业模型看似具有抗性，实则源于其测试语言特性，能力并不能决定失败模式。校准性能在三个分支中均下降，且在开放分支中置信度出现反转，因此基于置信度的过滤比随机过滤更差。在无语义选项列表上（准确率处于随机水平），表现最佳的分支仍报告平均置信度为0.82。代码与数据：https://github.com/sakhadib/literal-lock-in。

Alkhder at ImageEval 2026共享任务：约束选择与旋转平均对数概率评分用于多模态幻觉检测
We describe our submission to Subtask~1b of ImageEval 2026. The task consists of selecting the single visually valid statement from three candidates paired with an image, and is in both English and Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). Our system uses a pre-trained 7B vision--language model in a zero-shot setting, without task-specific training or fine-tuning. We formulate the task as a constrained selection problem and choose the answer using next-token log-probabilities. To reduce answer-position bias, we apply a Latin-square rotation to the candidate statements. [2610.22468]
From Parallels to Divergence: Language Models' Learning of Action Nominal Constructions
从平行到分歧：语言模型对动作名词结构的学习研究
Action nominal constructions (ANCs) express events in nominal form, with their argument marking following patterns found in clauses or ordinary noun phrases. In this paper, we investigate how different grammatical configurations affect language models' learning of ANCs and related constructions, and how these learning preferences compare with typological distributions in natural languages. We use a grammar-engineered method for synthetic corpus construction that combines shared semantic input from natural corpora with computational grammars. We construct 96 target languages varying in clause word order, noun-phrase word order, alignment, complement system, and ANC strategy. For each language, we train GPT-2-small models from scratch and evaluate them using targeted minimal pairs covering 34 grammatical phenomena. The results show typological parallels in basic clause configurations but divergences in complement systems and ANC strategies, with models also learning combinations rare or unattested in natural languages. This partial alignment suggests that natural language distributions may reflect both general learning pressures and functional demands not fully captured by model learning experiments.
中文翻译
动作名词结构（ANCs）以名词形式表达事件，其论元标记遵循句法或普通名词短语中的模式。本文研究不同语法结构如何影响语言模型对ANCs及相关结构的学习，并将其学习偏好与自然语言中的类型学分布进行对比。我们采用基于语法的合成语料库构建方法，结合自然语料库的共享语义输入与计算语法。构建了96种目标语言，涵盖句法词序、名词短语词序、一致关系、补足系统和ANC策略等变量。针对每种语言，从头训练GPT-2-small模型，并使用覆盖34种语法现象的目标最小对进行评估。结果表明，在基本句法结构上存在类型学平行，但在补足系统和ANC策略上出现分歧，且模型还学习到了自然语言中罕见或未记录的组合。这种部分一致性表明，自然语言分布可能既反映了普遍学习压力，也体现了模型学习实验未能完全捕捉的功能需求。

Where Prefill Expert Reduction Hurts MoE Models: A Few Prompt Tokens Carry Most of the Short-Answer F1 Loss
前向填充专家缩减为何损害MoE模型：少数提示令牌承载大部分短答案F1损失
Running prompt tokens through fewer routed experts during prefill is a simple way to make mixture-of-experts (MoE) serving cheaper, yet it is unclear what this damages. We locate the damage with token-, layer- and attention-path interventions on short-answer question answering in four MoE families. At half the experts the F1 loss of three families is mostly answer form: the model still finds the answer but stops answering briefly. In Qwen3-30B-A3B this loss is made at the two chat-template tokens before the answer, and in DeepSeek-V2-Lite at the first 8-32 prompt tokens, where its instruction sits; by answer likelihood these positions account for about half and a fifth of the damage. Reduction changes the first token's attention mass, norm and largest activation by less than 4%, and restoring super experts or massive activations recovers at most about a quarter of the loss, well below keeping the sensitive positions full. On real prompts with about 4% of tokens at full experts, placing them on the task interface keeps F1 within one point of the unmodified model, while placing them at random in the documents is about 3 points worse (two of five prespecified primary comparisons; the others were undecided or not run). Because the answer boundary always ends a single-turn prompt, and DeepSeek's loss was no longer detectable once its instruction moved away from the start, the sensitive tokens lay at the prompt ends in every sensitive layout we found, so rules that keep the ends are not distinguished from rules that read application marks. Time to first token falls about 14% on 11K-token prompts and not at 2K tokens.
中文翻译
在前向填充阶段减少路由专家数量是降低混合专家（MoE）服务成本的简单方法，但其造成的损害尚不明确。我们在四个MoE家族的短答案问答任务中通过令牌级、层级和注意力路径干预定位了该损害。当专家数量减半时，三个家族的F1损失主要表现为回答形式问题：模型仍能找出答案，但会短暂停止回答。在Qwen3-30B-A3B中，损失集中在答案前的两个聊天模板令牌；在DeepSeek-V2-Lite中，则集中在前8-32个提示令牌（即指令所在位置），这些位置的答对概率贡献了约一半和五分之一的损失。专家缩减对首个令牌的注意力质量、归一化值和最大激活值的影响小于4%；恢复超专家或大规模激活最多只能恢复约四分之一的损失，远低于保留敏感位置的完整专家。在真实提示中，若约4%的令牌保持全专家，将其置于任务界面可使F1得分接近未修改模型，而随机放置于文档中则差约3分（五项预设主要比较中的两项；其余未决或未运行）。由于答案边界始终位于单轮提示末尾，且一旦DeepSeek的指令移出开头，损失便不再可检测，因此所有敏感布局中的敏感令牌均位于提示末端，故保持末端的规则与读取应用标记的规则无法区分。在11K令牌提示中，首次令牌时间下降约14%，而在2K令牌提示中无明显变化。

Embeddings Are Overpriced? A Frozen 16 MB Vocabulary for Small and Ternary Language Models
嵌入层是否被高估？适用于小型和三值语言模型的冻结16 MB词汇表
In a small language model the vocabulary is the single largest part. In Gemma 3 270M the embedding table holds 168M of 268M parameters, and because quantisation methods leave the embedding in 16-bit, it is also most of the file once the rest of the model is compressed. We ask what that table actually buys. We replace it with one frozen table of 512-bit codes, 16 MB, with no trainable parameters, derived once from a pretrained embedding by principal-component projection and sign binarisation, and we train the same Gemma-shaped body from scratch on the same 2.05B tokens with and without it. The learned table buys 0.20 nats of validation loss for 2.6 times the trainable parameters, but on LAMBADA, HellaSwag and PIQA the frozen-table model is better or tied; only ARC-Easy favours the learned table. With a ternary BitNet b1.58 body the whole model is a 46 MB file, still ties the float model on LAMBADA and PIQA, and on a laptop CPU generates 615 to 740 tokens per second in 62 MB of memory, five to six times the speed of the float model's 8-bit GGUF in this http URL at a seventh of the memory. The frozen table wins by 1.7 to 2.3 nats on rare words, accepts 10,000 new words after training with a 3% cost where the learned table collapses by 43%, and all of it is released with the weights, the data and every evaluation answer.
中文翻译

[2610.23446]
"You" Is Not Toxic: How LLM Labelers Rewrite the Web's Conversation Laws
“你”并不有毒：大语言模型标注者如何重写网络对话规则
What the Web community believes about toxic conversations, such as that toxicity rises in longer and deeper threads, was measured mostly with Google's Perspective API, which is shutting down; large language models (LLMs) will make the next measurements. We find that LLM labelers mistake second-person address for toxicity, and that this one bias manufactures laws that human labels do not show. On 1.25 million news comments with crowd labels, Claude Haiku flags 12% of the non-toxic comments that contain no "you" and 51% of those with four or more; a single crowd rater flags 6% and 10%. Deeper replies address someone more often, so under Haiku toxicity rises with reply depth (+0.93 percentage points per level), while under the crowd's labels it slightly declines (-0.12). Rewriting "you" in the third person removes two thirds of the false rise; flagging only as many comments as the crowd does not remove it, and showing Haiku the parent comment enlarges it. All seven LLMs we test over-flag "you", and at the crowd's flag rate, without the parent, any false rise of the open ones vanishes at a fixed number of second-person words; Haiku also over-flags "you" on Reddit, Gab and Wikipedia talk pages. Agreement with human labels does not reveal the bias: we prove that a fixed Cohen's kappa leaves a law nearly unconstrained. We therefore certify the law instead of the labeler. Threadsafe uses the labeler's scores to choose which comments human raters label and to predict the others, and corrects the predictions with the human labels; linear laws are estimated without bias whatever the labeler does. In simulated audits its intervals for the laws keep nominal coverage from 250 labels on, where uniform-sample methods such as PPI++ fall short, and at 1,000 to 8,000 labels are 0.75 times as wide as those of design-based supervised learning (DSL); a thousand human labels rule out Haiku's rising law.
中文翻译
网络社区关于有毒对话的普遍认知，如毒性随对话深度和长度增加，主要依赖谷歌的Perspective API进行测量，而该工具即将停用；未来将由大语言模型（LLMs）承担此类测量。我们发现，LLM标注者会误将第二人称称呼视为毒性，这一偏差制造出人类标注者并未呈现的“规律”。在125万条新闻评论的众包标注数据上，Claude Haiku将不含“你”的非毒评论标记为有毒的比例达12%，含四个或以上“你”的则达51%；而单个众包标注者分别为6%和10%。深层回复更常指向他人，因此在Haiku的标注下，毒性随回复深度上升（每级+0.93个百分点），而在众包标注下则轻微下降（-0.12）。将“你”改写为第三人称可消除三分之二的虚假上升趋势；仅按众包标注频率标记无法消除该现象，而向Haiku展示父评论反而加剧了该趋势。我们测试的七种LLM均过度标记“你”，在众包标注率下，若不提供父评论，任何开放模型的虚假上升趋势在固定数量的第二人称词汇后消失。Haiku在Reddit、Gab及维基百科讨论页上同样过度标记“你”。与人类标注的一致性无法揭示该偏差：我们证明，固定的Cohen's kappa值几乎无法约束该规律。因此，我们选择认证规律而非标注者。Threadsafe利用标注者评分决定人工标注哪些评论并预测其余评论，再用人工标注校正预测；无论标注者如何，均可无偏估计线性规律。在模拟审计中，其置信区间在250个标注后保持名义覆盖率，优于PPI++等均匀采样方法，且在1000至8000个标注时宽度仅为基于设计的监督学习（DSL）的0.75倍；一千个人工标注即可排除Haiku的上升规律。
👤 Liang Yue, Eric A. Vance 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.23443]
ScoutRoute: Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Routing Using Internal Language Model Signals
ScoutRoute：利用内部语言模型信号实现高效的专家混合路由
Efficient expert routing is a fundamental challenge in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) systems, particularly for heterogeneous collections of independently trained Large Language Mod els (LLMs). Existing routing approaches typically rely on jointly trained gating networks, prompt-based predictors, or computationally expensive multi-model inference, limiting their flexibility and efficiency. This paper presents ScoutRoute, a lightweight post-hoc routing framework that predicts expert competence using internal language model signals extracted from a single forward pass through an inexpensive scout model. Hidden-state representations are used to estimate cross-model competence through lightweight logistic probes, while logit-derived confidence measures are incorporated within a domain-aware routing strategy for efficient expert selec tion without modifying the underlying language models. ScoutRoute isevaluatedon fiveopen-weightlanguagemodels across fourbenchmark datasets covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, commonsense reasoning, and multidis ciplinary knowledge. Experimental results show that ScoutRoute consistently outperforms individual routing signals while requiring only a single scout-model inference. The proposed competence probes also achieve superior calibration compared with conventional confidence measures, demonstrating that internal language model representations provide transferable information for accurate and efficient expert routing across heterogeneous language models
中文翻译
高效专家路由是混合专家（MoE）系统中的核心挑战，尤其在异构独立训练的大语言模型（LLMs）集合中。现有路由方法通常依赖联合训练的门控网络、基于提示的预测器或计算成本高昂的多模型推理，限制了灵活性与效率。本文提出ScoutRoute，一种轻量级的后处理路由框架，通过单次前向传播从一个低成本的侦察模型中提取内部语言模型信号，预测专家能力。使用隐藏状态表示通过轻量级逻辑探测器估算跨模型能力，同时将基于logit的置信度度量融入领域感知路由策略，实现无需修改底层语言模型的高效专家选择。ScoutRoute在五个开源语言模型上，覆盖数学推理、代码生成、常识推理和多学科知识四类基准数据集进行了评估。实验结果表明，ScoutRoute始终优于单一路由信号，且仅需一次侦察模型推理。所提出的胜任力探测器在校准性方面也优于传统置信度度量，证明内部语言模型表示可为异构语言模型间的准确高效专家路由提供可迁移信息。
