[2610.23149]
Can AI interview? Comparing AI-led to Human-led Chat Interviews
AI能进行面试吗？比较AI主导与人类主导的聊天面试
Large language model chatbots have been proposed as AI-interviewers for collecting qualitative interview data at scale, potentially bridging social science's longstanding divide between the breadth of surveys and the depth of qualitative interviews. This paper provides the first direct empirical comparison of data quality in AI-led and human-led interviews. In a randomized experiment (N = 41), university students were interviewed via synchronous text chat by either trained non-expert interviewers or a locally hosted AI system; we evaluate exposure, specificity, and relevance. Median interview duration was similar across conditions, but AI-interviewers asked more than twice as many questions per interview and elicited roughly 65 percent more respondent text per interview. Per-question responses were longer with human interviewers, though not after multiple-testing correction; only AI-condition participants skipped questions. Responses were almost uniformly on-topic in both conditions; for specificity we detect no difference, but the design cannot exclude one of up to three quarters of a point. These results were obtained with a small, locally hosted open-weight model (7B parameters), which addresses concerns about data security, GDPR compliance, and scientific reproducibility. Our findings support the viability of AI-interviewing for large-N qualitative data collection.
中文翻译
大型语言模型聊天机器人被提议作为AI面试官，以大规模收集定性访谈数据，可能弥合社会科学中问卷广度与定性访谈深度之间的长期分歧。本文提供了首次直接的实证比较，评估AI主导与人类主导面试的数据质量。在一项随机实验中（N = 41），大学生通过同步文本聊天接受由受训非专家面试官或本地部署的AI系统进行的访谈；我们评估了暴露程度、具体性和相关性。各条件下的平均访谈时长相似，但AI面试官每场访谈提问数量超过两倍，且每场访谈中受访者生成的文本量高出约65%。虽然人类面试官的每问题回应更长，但在多重检验校正后差异不显著；仅AI组参与者跳过了问题。两种条件下回应几乎全部紧扣主题；在具体性方面未检测到差异，但设计无法排除最多四分之三的分数差异。这些结果基于一个小型、本地部署的开源模型（70亿参数），解决了数据安全、GDPR合规性和科学可复现性的担忧。研究结果支持AI面试在大规模定性数据收集中的可行性。
👤 Semra Guven, Tobias Gårdhus, Andreas Bjerre-Nielsen, Anna Rogers, Hjalmar Bang Carlsen 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.23122]
LED: Improving Masked Diffusion Language Models by Lookahead Editing
LED：通过前瞻编辑改进掩码扩散语言模型
Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, enabling non-autoregressive text generation through parallel decoding. Existing dLLMs can be broadly categorized into two families: masked diffusion models (MDMs) and uniform diffusion models (UDMs) In this work, we theoretically show that MDMs are easier to optimize due to their mask-based transition schedule but have lower computational expressivity, whereas UDMs enable iterative state revision and greater expressivity but are substantially harder to train. This trade-off raises a natural question: can we obtain the expressivity of iterative revision without giving up the simple, easy-to-optimize transition schedule of MDMs? Motivated by this analysis, we propose Lookahead Editing (\ours{}), which augments masked diffusion decoding with a rewritable proposal state. At each decoding step, the model performs several lookahead updates to iteratively refine future predictions in this proposal state before committing tokens to the masked sequence. This design introduces a rewritable proposal that uses additional compute to improve expressivity while keeping the simple mask-based transition schedule of MDMs. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-8B to obtain LED-\texttt{7B} and LED-\texttt{8B}, respectively. Across eight benchmarks, LED SFT yields average gains of $+11.4$ and $+7.7$ points over standard MDM SFT. Moreover, using SFT alone, LED-\texttt{8B} outperforms LLaDA2.1-16B Q-mode, which is trained with continued pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning.
中文翻译
扩散型大语言模型（dLLMs）作为自回归语言模型的有前景替代方案，通过并行解码实现非自回归文本生成。现有dLLMs大致可分为两类：掩码扩散模型（MDMs）和均匀扩散模型（UDMs）。本文从理论上证明，MDMs由于基于掩码的转移调度更容易优化，但计算表达能力较低；而UDMs支持迭代状态修正和更高表达能力，但训练难度显著增加。这一权衡引发了一个自然问题：能否在不放弃MDMs简单易优化转移调度的前提下，获得迭代修正的表达能力？受此分析启发，我们提出前瞻编辑（Lookahead Editing, exttt{ours}），通过可重写的提议状态增强掩码扩散解码。在每个解码步骤中，模型执行多次前瞻更新，迭代优化提议状态中的未来预测，再将标记提交至掩码序列。该设计引入可重写的提议状态，利用额外计算提升表达能力，同时保持MDMs的简单掩码转移调度。我们对Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-8B进行微调，分别得到LED- exttt{7B}和LED- exttt{8B}。在八个基准测试中，LED SFT相比标准MDM SFT平均提升+11.4和+7.7分。此外，仅使用SFT，LED- exttt{8B}的表现优于经过持续预训练、监督微调和强化学习训练的LLaDA2.1-16B Q模式。
👤 Yuyan Zhou, Zeyuan Yang, Yuxuan Song, Zhicheng Cai, Hongli Yu et al. 📅 2026/10/16 🔗 arXiv
[2610.23030]
ECHO: Replacing the Compliance Proxy with Response Harmfulness in Optimization-Based Jailbreak Attack
ECHO：用响应有害性替代合规代理进行优化型越狱攻击
Despite extensive safety alignment, large language models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Existing optimization-based attacks share a single objective: maximize a token-level proxy of victim compliance---such as the likelihood of an affirmative prefix---computed on the prompt rather than the decoded reply. This proxy cannot distinguish a cautious hedge from a fully actionable harmful answer, leaving the search blind to what the model actually says. We propose ECHO (Evaluation-Conditioned Harmfulness Optimization), which replaces the compliance proxy with response-level, continuous harmfulness scores from HarmSig, a 1.7B regressor grounded in actual harmful capability. ECHO couples HarmSig with five word-level perturbation operators and Keyword-Anchored Beam Search with global top-m memory, under a strict black-box threat model. Across five open-weight victims on AdvBench, ECHO achieves up to 0.898 StrongREJECT mean score and 89.23% HarmBench success. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .
中文翻译
尽管进行了广泛的安全对齐，大语言模型仍易受越狱攻击。现有优化型攻击采用单一目标：最大化基于提示的受害者合规性代理——如肯定前缀的可能性——而非解码后的回复。该代理无法区分谨慎回避与完全可执行的有害回答，导致搜索过程对模型实际输出视而不见。我们提出ECHO（评估条件有害性优化），用来自HarmSig（一个17亿参数的基于实际有害能力的回归器）的连续响应级有害性评分替代合规代理。ECHO将HarmSig与五种词级扰动算子及关键词锚定束搜索结合，采用严格的黑盒威胁模型。在AdvBench上的五个开源权重受害者中，ECHO达到最高0.898的StrongREJECT均分和89.23%的HarmBench成功率。代码已公开。

[2610.22832]
Too Emotional to Be Fake? Intra-Utterance Emotion Shifts Expose Failure Modes in Speech Deepfake Detectors
情绪太真实，不可能是假的？话语内情绪转变暴露语音深度伪造检测器的失效模式
Expressive speech synthesis can now change emotion within a single utterance, yet its impact on deepfake detection remains unclear. We introduce EmoShift, a benchmark comparing emotion-stable (AA) and emotion-changing (AB) speech to examine these effects while accounting for static emotion identity and acoustic confounds. It comprises 7,240 bona fide utterances from four source datasets and 9,720 synthetic utterances generated using four expressive TTS systems. Across seven detectors, we find that emotion changes can make synthetic speech appear more genuine, increasing spoof miss rates by up to 25.3 percentage points on OMG-Emotion at fixed thresholds. Conversely, emotion-changing bona fide speech increases false-alarm rates by up to 24.4 percentage points on IEMOCAP. Further analyses reveal detector-specific sensitivities to emotion trajectories, not fully explained by static emotion identity or splicing artifacts. These findings underscore the importance of emotion dynamics in deepfake detection.

Reading the News Without Hindsight: NoLBERT Topics and the Cross-Section of Stock Returns
无事后之见地阅读新闻：NoLBERT主题与股票收益横截面
Language models that read financial news are usually trained on text from the same years used to test them, so a backtest can reward hindsight. We build news topics from NoLBERT, an encoder pretrained only on text published between 1976 and 1995, adapted into a sentence encoder by contrastive fine-tuning on passages of the same article, with no labels or returns. We compare it with LDA, MiniLM and FinBERT readers on 1.26 million articles from 2012 to 2024, convert each reader's topics into attention shocks and stock exposures, and forecast the returns of the 1,000 largest US stocks with eight models at three rebalancing frequencies. Models are fitted on 2013-2019, choices are made on 2020-2021, and 2022-2024 is held out. The experiment with the best validation score loses money at every frequency. Measuring attention shocks against the past 63 trading days instead of 5 raises the test rank IC of every reader. NoLBERT gives the most stable topics and forecasts about as well as LDA. An equal-weight combination of all readers and models earns a monthly net Sharpe ratio of 1.12, significant against zero before adjusting for the search but not against a price-only benchmark.

[2610.22659]
Exploring The Empirical Privacy Landscape of Synthetic Data
合成数据的实证隐私景观探索
Methods for training language models on sensitive data differ in how they balance capability gains against the risk of leaking private information. In this paper, we present a controlled comparison of data-level and training-level interventions for navigating the capability-privacy tradeoff on synthetic medical records with canaries to probe for leakage. Direct finetuning performs best on capability metrics but leads to the most privacy leakage, while DP-SGD reduces leakage at substantial cost to capabilities. Generating synthetic data from these records offers a more favorable balance, particularly when guided by a uniqueness-aware instruction to replace private details. Relative to naive paraphrasing, privacy-aware rewriting improves privacy protection across a variety of different rewriting models. We also find that more capable rewriting models generally reduce the risks of downstream privacy leakage, although held-out fit does not improve monotonically and increased test-time compute yields inconsistent gains. Overall, these results highlight privacy-aware rewriting as a promising direction for achieving a more favorable trade-off between capabilities and empirical privacy compared to other training-level methods, and our evaluation framework supports comparisons of future models and methods.
中文翻译
在敏感数据上训练语言模型的方法在能力提升与泄露私人信息风险之间存在不同的权衡方式。本文通过在带有“哨兵”以探测泄露的合成医疗记录上，对数据级和训练级干预进行受控比较，探索能力-隐私权衡问题。直接微调在能力指标上表现最佳，但导致最严重的隐私泄露；而DP-SGD虽减少了泄露，但代价是显著降低能力。从这些记录生成合成数据提供了更优的平衡，尤其在使用关注唯一性的指令替换私密细节时效果更佳。相较于简单的改写，隐私意识改写在多种不同改写模型中均提升了隐私保护水平。我们还发现，更强大的改写模型通常能降低下游隐私泄露风险，尽管保留拟合度并未单调提升，且增加测试时计算量带来的收益也不一致。总体而言，这些结果凸显了隐私意识改写作为实现能力与实证隐私之间更优权衡的有前景方向，我们的评估框架也支持对未来模型和方法的比较。
👤 Zihao Zhao, Avi Schwarzschild, Casey Meehan, Katherine Lee, Zhili Feng et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22455]
High prevalence of antagonizing rhetoric in trending topics on Twitter/X
推特/X热门话题中对抗性修辞的高发性
Disagreement between political opponents is a central element of democracy. This disagreement can be voiced in different ways. An opponent can be antagonized, i.e. portrayed as an enemy that needs to be overcome, or they can be criticized, i.e. treated as a legitimate adversary with whom one can engage. While the latter is a fundamental part of democratic exchange, the former can be detrimental to public discourse. Political disagreement increasingly takes place on social media, where negativity towards political out-groups has been found to drive engagement. However, existing research has largely treated such negativity as a single dimension, leaving open whether it is antagonization or criticism that circulates on these platforms. This work addresses this gap by introducing a computational approach to detect these two types of rhetoric in text and identify its targets. On a corpus of over 17 million tweets from trending topics in the German Twittersphere, the method reveals that about a third of all tweets contain antagonizing rhetoric. Comparing tweets from trending topics to a random sample shows that the discourse in trending topics is systematically more antagonizing than ordinary Twitter communication. Combining antagonization detection with a large-scale estimation of users' political leaning, we find that right-leaning users employ antagonizing rhetoric significantly more than their left-leaning counterparts. Furthermore, both forms of oppositional rhetoric are strongly amplified within political camps, with multipliers retweeting them up to twice as often as tweets without oppositional rhetoric. Finally, patterns of antagonized targets reveal how different political camps construct their out-groups, with the right-wing populist party AfD and the Green party as the central antagonists of the opposing camps.
中文翻译
政治对手之间的分歧是民主的核心要素。这种分歧可以通过不同方式表达：一方可以被敌意化，即被描绘为需要战胜的敌人；也可以被批评，即作为可对话的合法对手对待。后者是民主交流的基础，而前者可能损害公共讨论。政治分歧越来越多地发生在社交媒体上，已有研究发现对政治外群体的负面情绪能驱动互动。然而，现有研究大多将此类负面情绪视为单一维度，未明确区分平台上传播的是敌意化还是批评性言论。本文提出一种计算方法，用于检测文本中的这两种修辞类型并识别其目标对象。在超过1700万条德语推特热门话题数据上，该方法显示约三分之一的推文包含对抗性修辞。对比热门话题与随机抽样推文发现，热门话题中的 discourse 系统性地更具对抗性。结合对抗性修辞检测与大规模用户政治倾向估计，我们发现右翼倾向用户比左翼用户更频繁使用对抗性修辞。此外，两种对立修辞形式在政治阵营内部均被显著放大，多倍转发次数可达无对立修辞推文的两倍。对抗性目标的模式揭示了不同政治阵营如何构建其外群体，其中右翼民粹政党AfD和绿党是对方阵营的主要攻击对象。

Revealing the Impact of Visual Text Style on Attribute-based Descriptions Produced by Large Visual Language Models
视觉文本风格对大型视觉语言模型生成的基于属性描述的影响研究
When the visual style of text is considered, a wide variety can be observed in font, color, and size. However, when a word is read, its meaning is independent of the style in which it has been written or rendered. In this paper, we investigate whether, and how, the style in which a word is visualized in an image impacts the description that a Large Visual Language Model (LVLM) provides for the concept to which that word refers. Specifically, we investigate how functional text styles (readability-oriented, e.g., black sans-serif) versus decorative styles (display-oriented, e.g., colored cursive/script) affect LVLMs' descriptions of a concept in terms of the attributes of that concept. Our experiments study the situation in which the LVLM is able to correctly identify the concept referred to by a visual text, i.e., by a word or words rendered as an image, and in which the visual text style should not influence the attribute-based description that the LVLM produces. Our experimental results reveal that even when the concept is correctly identified, text style influences the model's attribute-based descriptions of the concept. Our findings demonstrate non-trivial style leakage from text style into semantic inference and motivate style-aware evaluation and mitigation for LVLM-based multimedia systems.
中文翻译
当考虑文本的视觉风格时，字体、颜色和大小等方面呈现出广泛的变化。然而，当阅读一个单词时，其含义与其书写或渲染的风格无关。本文研究了大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）在描述其所指概念时，文本在图像中呈现的视觉风格是否以及如何产生影响。具体而言，我们探讨了功能性文本风格（以可读性为导向，如黑色无衬线字体）与装饰性文本风格（以展示为导向，如彩色手写体或草书）对LVLM在描述概念属性方面的影响。实验设定在LVLM能够正确识别由图像呈现的词语所指概念的前提下进行，此时视觉文本风格本不应影响LVLM生成的基于属性的描述。实验结果表明，即使概念被正确识别，文本风格仍会影响模型对概念属性的描述。研究发现揭示了文本风格信息向语义推理中的非平凡泄露现象，强调了在基于LVLM的多媒体系统中开展风格感知评估与缓解策略的重要性。
👤 Xiaomeng Wang, Martha Larson, Zhengyu Zhao 📅 2026-04-30 🔗 arXiv
